Tab se, halanki, kuch samajik pranalion aur ideologically ne aisi paristhitiyan aur ek aisi sanskriti paida ki hai jo un logon ke hiton ko surakshit karne ke liye specialisation ko majboot karti hai jinki yeh seva karti hai. Inmein European feudalism (kisaan aur landlord dono ke liye), African aur Pacific tribalism (specialist tribal clans ka matlab tha ki aapka trade aapke clan aur vanshavali ke anusar pehle se tay tha), Indian caste system (vyaktiyon ko unka 'jeevan ka uddeshya' virasat mein mila, jisne badle mein unke peshe ko tay kiya) aur sabse haal hi mein Western industrialisation, bureaucracy aur corporate aur academic specialisation ('division of labour', jo baad mein poori duniya mein phael gaya jise West ne gulam/colonise banaya) shamil hain.

Modern specialisation ki darshanik shuruat (philosophical origins) post-Enlightenment Western intellectual paradigm mein paayi ja sakti hai jisne gyaan ki vyapakta ko poore roop mein amanageable samjha. Isliye padhai ke prati ek behadd critical, reductionist drishtikon, jiski shuruat French philosopher René Descartes ne ki thi, ne intellectual specialisation ki taraf jhukav ko aage badhaya, jisne pehle ke daur se polymath ki prakriti ko badal diya. Is baat ko Diderot ki *Encyclopédie* ke 'gens de lettres' article mein swikar kiya gaya tha, jisne ghoshna ki, *‘la science universelle n’est plus à la portée de l’homme’* (Universal knowledge ab insaan ki pahonch mein nahi hai). Isne us baat ko chinhit kiya jise historian Peter Burke ‘har kshetra ke gyaan se kai kshetron ke gyaan tak [polymath ka] ek bauddhika peeche hatna (intellectual retreat)’ sandarbhit karte hain. Charles van Doren ne apni *History of Knowledge* mein Enlightenment ke dauran specialisation ke uday ke liye ek aur vyakhya ka sujhaav diya, jiske baare mein unhone kaha ki yeh Renaissance ke baad polymathy se moh-bhang (disillusionment) se juda tha.

> Renaissance ka kamyab ‘Renaissance Men’ produce karne mein asafal rehna bina notice kiye nahi gaya. Agar Leonardo, Pico, Bacon jaise log aur kai doosre lagbhag utne hi prasiddh log har cheez ke baare mein sab kuch jaan-ne ke apne maane gaye sapne mein kamyab nahi ho sake, toh chhote mardon ko koshish karne ki dharana nahi banani chahiye. Vikalp swayam-spasht (self-evident) ho gaya: ek kshetra mein mahaarat haasil karein jabki doosre apne kshetron mein mahaarat haasil karein. Poora karne mein bahut aasan, is raaste ne ek zyada aaramdayak shaikshanik samuday (academic community) ki taraf badhaya. Ab ek kshetra mein ek adhikari ko sirf apne kshetra ke experts ke sath muqabla karne ki zaroorat thi.

Is sthiti ki saaf nirarthakta (apparent futility) ne kai intellectuals ko ek niche area ka daava karne ke liye prerit kiya jise unhone mahsoos kiya ki yeh hi ekmatra tareeqa tha jisse woh pehchan (identity), uddeshya (purpose) — aur kai mamlon mein ek pesha (vocation) ka ahasas viksit kar sakte the. Is haqeeqat ne, iske parinaamswarup ubharne wale vishayon aur up-vishayon (disciplines and sub-disciplines) ki sankhya mein ghatatmak badhotari (exponential rise) ke sath milkar, use paida kiya jise ab 'knowledge ka crisis' ya 'information anxiety' kaha jata hai. Asal mein Enlightenment ke mukhya bauddhika projects mein se ek 'information ko order, codify aur classify karna' tha, jisne encyclopaedias ke uday ko badhava diya.

1800s tak intellectual specialisation is tarah ek haqeeqat ban gaya aur gyaan ke alag-alag vishay universities mein academic departments ke roop mein sansthagath (institutionalised) kiye ja rahe the. 'Discipline' shabd — jo control ya restraint ki sainy dharana (military notion) se import kiya gaya tha — academic context mein ab sakhti se roke gaye bauddhika kshetron (tightly restrained intellectual domains) ko sandarbhit karne ke liye upayog kiya jaane laga. Knowledge management ka yeh model gulam banaye gaye ilaqon mein export kiya ja raha tha (sanskriti, shiksha pranali, shasan tantra aur pūñjīvādī arthvyavastha ki tarah hi), jahan yeh tezi se norm ban raha tha. Europe mein, amateur societies aur specialised journals ke uday ne, 19th century mein universities ke research centres ke sath-sath teaching institutions mein badlav ke sath milkar is andolan ko aur majboot kiya. Knowledge ka khandikaran (compartmentalisation) poore zoor-shor par tha; jismein har khand par ek ivory tower priest dwara monopoly ki ja rahi thi.

Jaise 18th century ke ant ne Europe aur North America mein intellectual specialisation ki shuruat ko chinhit kiya, 19th century ke ant ne duniya bhar mein professional ya occupational specialisation ki shuruat ko chinhit kiya. Is ghatna ke vikas ko do shaktishali sansthaon ki sthapna dwara utprerit (catalysed) kiya gaya tha: government bureaucracy aur corporation. Yeh kramashah imperialism aur industrialisation dwara laye gaye the; dono ne labor ke division par nirbhar ek dhancha laagu kiya; aur dono ko Western (European aur phir American) hegemonic system dwara vishwa स्तर par phelaya gaya tha jisne, Marx ke shabdon mein, 'apni khud ki chhavi ke baad ek duniya' (a world after its own image) banane ki koshish ki.

Pramukh vicharkon, vishesh roop se Franz Kafka, Karl Marx aur Max Weber jaise logon ne state bureaucrats aur industrial workers ke specialisation (aur isliye 'dehumanisation') par prasiddh roop se tippani ki. Marx ne bureaucracy aur corporation ko tulnatmak pratiroop (comparable counterparts) ke roop mein dekha — ek hi sansthagath dharana ke state aur private versions — aur afsos ke sath tippani ki ki unhone 'doctor, lawyer, priest, poet, science ke aadmi ko apne paid wage-labourers mein badal diya.' Unki Alienation Theory ne samjaya ki kaise utpadan prakriya (production process) ki specialised aur dohrayi jaane wali prakriti workers ko 'aatmik aur bhaautik roop se ek machine ki sthiti mein depressed' hone ka kaaran banti hai. Specialisation ka kaaran, unhone tarka diya, labor ke division ki ek neeti thi, jo factory se aage phael gayi aur ek samajik norm ban gayi.

> Division of labour na sirf aarthik, balki samaj ke har doosre kshetra par seva karta hai, aur har jagah men ko specialise aur sort karne ke us sabhi ko gherne wale system ki buniyaad rakhta hai, doosri saari kshamtaon ki keemat par ek aadmi mein ek hi kshamta ka vikas.

Weber, jinhone theoretical roop se sangathan ke sabse kushal roop ke roop mein bureaucracy ka paksh liya, ne phir bhi vyakti par iske seemit karne wale prabhav ko pehchana. 'Specialised kaam tak seemit hona, insaan ki Faustian universality ke tyaag ke sath jise yeh aahwan karta hai,' unhone kaha 'modern duniya mein kisi bhi moolyavan kaam ki ek shart hai.' Europe (vishesh roop se British) colonialism ke duniya bhar mein phaelne ke sath, bureaucracy ka yeh system (aur is tarah iska division of labour) zyadatar colonies mein export kiya gaya tha, sabse vishesh roop se India, Kenya, Malaysia, South Africa, Australia aur beshak, Americas mein. Jabki specialised bureaucracy ka yeh roop 19th aur 20th centuries mein vishesh roop se ek European (aur isliye ek vyapak aupniveshik) ghatna tha, hum jaante hain ki kahin aur aur itihaas ke alag-alag points par state bureaucracies ke sath hamesha aisa nahi tha. Praacheen tribal chiefdoms aur Chinese imperial dynasties ke bureaucrats, udaharan ke liye, generalists ke roop mein prasiddh the aur unmein se kai, jaise Zhang Heng, Shen Kuo aur Su Song, vaastav mein ullekhniya polymaths the.

19th century ke ant mein modern corporation ke uday ne kaam ki prakriti aur manav jeevan-shaili par aur isliye polymath ke bhagya par gehra prabhav daala. Shuruat mein Royal charters ke sath private entities ke roop mein sthapit, woh tezi se trading aur investing organisations ban gaye, jo 14th Amendment (mool roop se African American mukt gulamon ke adhikaaron ki raksha ke liye design kiya gaya) ke baad, vyakti ke adhikaaron ko dharan kar chuke the aur shareholder returns ko kisi bhi doosri cheez se upar rakhne ke liye kanooni roop se bandhe the. Unhone 20th century ki shuruat mein viksit nayi krantikari technologies ko commercialise kiya, jaise telephone, phonograph, electricity aur aviation, aur mass production ka ek system banaya — jise 1913 mein Ford mass production car plant ki sthapna dwara prateekit kiya gaya aur Frederick Taylor dwara shuru kiye gaye labor management ke 'Efficiency School' dwara suvidhajanak banaya gaya — jisne labor ke mechanisation aur en masse specialisation ke uday mein ek naye daur ka aagaz kiya.

Ise shaikshanik pranali (educational system) dwara samarthan diya gaya tha, jisne in aarthik taqaton dwara zaroori resources ki poorti ki aur is tarah ek specialised samaj ko suvidhajanak banane aur banaye rakhne ke liye mukhya ban gaya. Europe ke post-industrialisation education model se behtar iska koi pradarshan nahi hai. 20th century se pehle, vishesh roop se Europe aur United States mein lekin kahin aur bhi, formal education kuch chune hue logon ka visheshadhikar (privilege) thi. Industrial Revolution ke baad, industrial production ki zarooraton ko poora karne ke liye ek mass education system introduced kiya gaya tha. Durbhagya se, halanki, is vyapak shiksha ki aalgaav-kaari (alienating) prakriti bauddhika ekta (intellectual unity), sanshleshan (synthesis) aur isliye samajh ki keemat par aayi.

Curriculum ko factory labourers produce karne ke liye design kiya gaya tha jo zyada se zyada instruction manuals padh sakein, aur production line ke ek vishesh kshetra par specialise kar sakein. Isliye vishayon ko compartmentalised kiya gaya tha aur ek doosre se alag padhaya jata tha, jismein students ko khud factory conveyor belt par products ki tarah treat kiya jata tha, jo process ke dauran alag-alag points par incremental input praapt karte the. Bachhon ko 'curriculum subjects' ke roop mein package kiye gaye gyaan ke in jebon ke beech sambandhon ko dekhne ke liye encourage nahi kiya gaya tha. British philosopher of education Alfred North Whitehead, jo industrialised Britain ke charam ke dauran rahe, ne pehchana ki shiksha ka yeh drishtikon bachhon ko har vishay ki prasangikta (relevance) ko samajhne se rok raha tha — unke apne jeevan ke liye bhi, lekin vyapak duniya ke liye bhi. Unki chinta ko niche diye gaye gadyansh mein sabse behtareen dhang se sankshipt kiya gaya tha:

> Is ekal ekta (single unity) ke bajaye, hum bachhon ko Algebra offer karte hain, jisse kuch nahi nikalta; Geometry, jisse kuch nahi nikalta; Science, jisse kuch nahi nikalta; History, jisse kuch nahi nikalta; ek do Bhashayein, jinpar kabhi mahaarat haasil nahi hui; aur aakhir mein, sabse ubaao, Literature, jise Shakespeare ke naatakon dwara pratinidhitv kiya jata hai, philological notes aur plot ya character ke sankshipt vishleshan ke sath jise mool roop se yaad mein samarpit kiya jana hai. Kya aisi list ke baare mein kaha ja sakta hai ki yeh Life ka pratinidhitv karti hai, jaisa ki ise jeevit rehne ke beech mein jaana jata hai? Iske baare mein jo sabse achha kaha ja sakta hai woh yeh hai ki yeh vishay-soochi (table of contents) ki ek tezi hai jise koi devta dimaag mein chala sakta hai jabki woh ek duniya banane ki soch raha tha, aur abhi tak yeh tay nahi kiya hai ki ise ek sath kaise rakha jaye.

20th century ke mod tak, phir, duniya ke teen sabse prabhavshali sansthaon — academia, government aur corporation — har ek ne labor ke sakht division ko apna liya tha, jeevan ke har kshetra mein hyper-specialisation ki ek nayi sanskriti sthapit ki thi; ek aisa jhukav jo aaj tak norm ban jayega.

Kai millenniums mein socio-psychological conditioning ki is mehnat-bhari prakriya ke kaaran, ab logon ke dimaag mein jaankari ke ghatatmak guna (exponential multiplication) aur 'kaam' ki ek vishesh dharana ke sath milkar, humne ek atyadhik specialised samaj viksit kiya hai jo logon ko lagbhag vishesh roop se unke 'trade', 'occupation', ya 'field' dwara pehchanne par tula hua lagta hai. Aaj lagbhag kisi bhi samajik ya peshevar gathering mein, log iske aadhar par decision/judgments banane ke liye ubaal rahe hain. Us decisive sawaal tak pahonchne ki ek automatic majboori hoti hai: ‘Toh, aap kya karte hain?’ Aur woh ek seedhe jawab ki umeed karte hain.

Psychologically, hum kisi ko ‘an electrician-musician-mother-of-six who was once a physician-athlete with a love for poetry’ ke bajaye ‘electrician’ ke roop mein sandarbhit karna zyada aasan paate hain. Yeh alag-alag samajon mein surnames ke banne ke tareeqe mein pratibimbit hota hai. Britain mein, udaharan ke liye, kai modern surnames (jaise Carpenter, Mason, Taylor, Harper, Smith, Piper, aur ityaadi) medieval feudalism ki virasat hain jahan logon ko unke trade ya occupation dwara sandarbhit kiya jata tha, jaisa ki India mein bhi tha (Bandukwala, ‘hathiyaron wala banda’, ya Lightwalla, ‘bijli wala banda’, aur ityaadi) aur kahin aur bhi. Stigmatisation ek sangyanatmak pravritti (cognitive tendency) hai, jo aksar prevailing socioeconomic system dwara ek samajik samjhoute (social convention) mein sansthagath ki jaati hai.

Toh ek baar jab kisi vyakti se koi kalank/stigma jud jata hai, toh us vyakti ke liye duniya ko yeh samjhana behadd mushkil ho jata hai ki uski zindagi ke doosre, barabar abhinna pehlu bhi hain. Asal mein use aksar khud ko samjhane mein musibat hoti hai. Aaj log doosron ko dibbon (boxes) mein rakhne ke liye majboor mahsoos karte hain, jinhe woh phir sakhti se band karte hain aur saaf taur par label karte hain. Label kiye gaye logon ke liye (jo hum sab mool roop se ban jaate hain) aavantit dibbe (allocated box) se baahar nikalna aur doosre mein pravesh karne ki koshish karna behadd mushkil ho jata hai. Yahan tak ki agar koi aisa karne mein saksham hai, toh log apne labels ke sath bahut kanjoos hain; woh shayad hi ek se zyada jaari karte hain. Artistic polymath Billy Childish, ek prasiddh kavi, novelist, painter aur musician ne samaj dwara unhe ek dhabbe/pigeonhole mein daalne ki jaari koshishon ke baare mein baat ki:

> Main ek loose cannon hoon. Press ke anusar main pehle ek musician hua karta tha. Ab mujhe musician banne ki anumati nahi hai, mujhe ek artist banna padega. Main ek 'purva musician' hoon bhale hi maine sirf pichle saal hi 7 albums produce kiye hon! British Art Show mere paintings ko andar nahi aane dega, ise meri poems hona padega. Aur theek usi samay meri poetry ko art ke roop mein rakha gaya tha, Poetry Society ne poocha ki kya woh mere magazine ke cover ke roop mein mere paintings ka upayog kar sakte hain! Art world mujhe ek musician ke roop mein dekhega, music world mujhe ek painter samjhega — yeh aisa tha, 'aap ise rakh lo . . . hum nahi chahte ki woh hamare aangan mein peshab kare.'

Yeh rukawat sabhi multitalented ya multi-ambitioned logon ko samna karni padti hai, chahe woh fashion models hon jo acting mein transition karne ki koshish karte hain, singers jo ek business venture shuru karte hain, ya jab scientists art ya literature mein apna haath aazmate hain. Publishers ek writer ko ek nishchit prakar ke roop mein 'brand' karna chahte hain aur ise encourage karenge; art dealers chahte hain ki artists 'unke' style mein kaam karna jaari rakhein taaki pehchanne yogya ban sakein; investors chahenge ki unke entrepreneurs 'apne sector se tike rahein.' Sirf ek novelist hona hi kaafi specialised nahi hai; aapko ek genre-specific novelist hona chahiye jo ek vishesh style se tika rahe. Stephen King ke kadd ke bahumukhi lekhakon ne pigeonhole na hone ki koshish mein kai chhadm-naamon (pseudonyms) ke tehat likha hai. Employers khud multitalented logon ko hire karne ke liye bahut nervous hote hain.

Yeh pravritti, jo dimaag ki schematic organisation process ka kaaran aur utpad (product) dono hai, is dharana par aadharit hai ki (kam se kam ek maana gaya) ekagrachitt focus (single-minded focus) behtar utpadakta (productivity), kushalta (efficiency) aur isliye bade aarthik inaam ke barabar hai. Isliye samaj ka ek nirantar khandikaran (compartmentalisation) ek duschakra (vicious circle) paida karta hai, jismein har koi apne khud ke 'field bubble' mein rehta hai — lawyers, investment bankers, athletes aur musicians har ek ya toh doosre se irshya karte hain ya unhe neecha dikhate hain; koi bhi doosre se relate nahi kar sakta. Har koi apne field ko jeeta hai, khata hai, sota hai, peeta hai, yeh unke jaan-ne aur hone ka tareeqa ban jata hai, yeh unhe aur unki chetna ko paribhashit karna shuru kar deta hai aur woh har ek apni khud ki bhasha, apna jargon, samajik ghera, yahan tak ki apna mazaak ka ahasas viksit karte hain.

Is bubble-creation ko predictive analytics ki nayi technology dwara aur badhava diya ja raha hai, jise lagbhag sabhi online businesses dwara ek prakar ki 'digital colonialism' sthapit karne mein niyukt kiya gaya hai. Hum digital aur social media platforms ke zariya apne vyavhaar ke baare mein jaankari ke dher dene mein khush hain, lekin badle mein hamara data ik इकट्ठा kiya ja raha hai aur aakhirkar un algorithms ko rachne ke liye upayog kiya ja raha hai jo humein batate hain ki hum kya chahte hain ya zaroorat hai hamare apne instincts se bhi tezi se. YouTube ko hi lein, udaharan ke liye — agar aap signed in hain toh sirf aapke maujooda dekhne ki aadatton se jude videos hi recommend kiye jaate hain. Isi tarah, Amazon sirf aapke maujooda interests se jude books hi recommend karta hai (aapki khoj aur khareedne ki aadatton dwara pradarshit).

Machine learning par aadharit yeh jatil algorithms kushal recommendation ke ek system ka bhram de sakte hain, lekin vaastav mein advertisers lagatar apne 'target market' ki talash mein rehte hain aur isliye maang karenge ki digital marketplaces aur social media platforms ke paas ek saaf taur par shrenibaddh grahak aadhar (categorised customer base) ho taaki woh ek behadd targeted phir bhi alag vigyapan abhiyan (advertising campaign) racha sakein. Aisa karne mein, hamari digital upasthiti (digital presence) ko bhi pigeonhole kiya ja raha hai. Toh jabki hum daava karte hain ki duniya hamari ungliyon par hai, hum vaastav mein jo kuch bhi karte hain woh yeh hai ki khud ko isse band kar lete hain. Yeh Big Data aur psychographics ka daur hai, jo Enlightenment project ki tarah, jaankari ki badi matra mein vyavastha aur codification lane ka ek prayas hai, aur is tarah gyaan (aur vyaktiyon) ko suvidhajanak dibbon (boxes) mein rakh raha hai.

---

## The Modern Education Crisis (MODERN EDUCATION KA SANKAT)

Current education system saaf taur par kaam nahi kar raha hai, kam se kam aadaraniya British educationalist Ken Robinson ke anusar. Yeh isliye hai kyunki yeh behadd outdated (purana) hai; yeh abhi bhi us model par aadharit hai jise Victorian Britain ne install kiya tha, jisne ‘linearity, conformity aur standardization’ ki sanskriti ko badhava diya, jabki aaj hamare samne ek alag duniya hai, ek aasi duniya jo ‘organic, adaptable aur diverse’ hai. Yeh incongruence (anukoolta ki kami) students ki intellectual aur professional prospects ko prabhavit karti hai. Toh bachhon ke sath robots ki tarah vyavhaar karna 21st-century job market ke bhi anukool nahi hai. Oxford University ke Future of Humanity Institute ke Anders Sandberg kehte hain, ‘Educational institutions ko logon ko industrial age ki tarah machine mein cogs (purgay) banne ke liye train karne ki zaroorat nahi hai — machines bahut sasti hongi . . . unhe logon ko zyada jatil, ill-defined jobs se nipatne ke liye train karna chahiye.’

Education system mein yeh supply-demand mismatch samjhata hai ki school mein asli gyaan aur samajh ki pyaas unquenched (pyaasi) kyun chhut jaati hai aur students isliye adult ke roop mein baad mein apni jeevan mein ek (pehle se hi unfulfilling) job par apne commute ke dauran elementary-level popular history ya popular science books padhkar basics ko phir se dekhne ki ek urjit (pukaar) mahsoos karte hain — vaastav mein yeh so-called ‘educated class’ ke beech aisi books ki popularity mein haal hi mein hue uchaal ko samjhata hai. Lekin adults ko bachhon ke roop mein in popular books dwara educated (ya ‘entertained’ bhi) hona chahiye tha, bajaye iske ki woh apne bachpan mein sleepwalk (neend mein chalte) karein aur phir suddenly adults ke roop mein ek somnolent state (soyi hui sthiti) se is baat par jaagein ki unki practical aur professional mehnat ke pare life ke fascinatings aur important pehlu hain. Zyadatar toh jaagte bhi nahi. Bristol University mein educational psychologists Shafi aur Rose dwara kiye gaye research ne dikhaya ki kai mature students ne yeh mahsoos nahi kiya ki unki initial education ne koi excitement ya education ki samajh bhi instill (bhi) ki hai; unhe education ki value ko realize karne aur parinaamswarup life mein baad mein is par wapas lautne se pehle ‘life’ ko experience karne ke liye wait karna pada.

Student aur modern education system ke beech yeh saaf disconnect uska parinaam hai jise Whitehead ne ‘inert ideas’ (nishkriya vichar) kaha tha — compartmentalised, fragmented information jo bina kisi unifying framework ke school mein students par phenki jaati hai. Parinaamswarup, students na sirf is baat ko samajhne mein kam kshamatawan hain ki alag-alag classes mein unhe transmit kiye gaye gyaan ke yeh tukde ek doosre se kaise relevant hain, balki sabse mahatvapurna baat yeh hai ki woh unki apni lives se kaise relevant hain. Wahan simply koi context nahi hai aur isliye koi internalisation nahi hai. Yeh kathin sthiti (predicament) aaj tak jaari hai. Bachhe classrooms mein baithe hain, lectures sun rahe hain aur books padh rahe hain yeh sochte hue ki geometry ya medieval history ya plate tectonics unke liye kya relevance rakhta hai. Kya yeh unhe corporate executive ya government clerk ke roop mein ek ‘respectable’ job paane mein madad karne ja raha hai? Ya yeh simply ek torturous initiation ceremony hai jisse kisi ko umar aane (coming of age) se pehle guzarna zaroori hai?

Yeh disjointed education, jaisa ki yeh ek factory ke model par aadharit hai, phir unke system se guzarne par pyramid specialisation ki ek prakriya dwara aur badtar (exacerbated) ho jaati hai. Duniya bhar ke students ko bahut jaldi specialise karne ke liye encourage kiya ja raha hai, aksar majboor kiya ja raha hai. Parinaamswarup, multitalented bachhon ko aksar uska samna karna padta hai jise psychologists ‘multipotentiality’ kehte hain — bahut jaldi specialise karne (yaani multiple passions ke beech chunav karne) ki majboori ke parinaamswarup multitalented pupils dwara sahne jaane wali frustration, confusion aur anxiety ki ek sthiti. Ek ashadharan general intelligence wale kai child prodigies ko tezi se apni intellectual capacities ko exclusively ek vishesh kshetra mein channelise karne ke liye encourage (lagbhag majboor) kiya jata hai. Parents aur teachers khud ko samjhate hain ki yeh poori tarah se natural hai. Parinaamswarup, polymathic prodigies (ya ‘multipotentialites’) aksar aam taur par child prodigies jaisa hi bhagya (fate) samna karte hain — woh shayad hi kabhi apni potential ko poora karte hain aur aksar adults ke roop mein expectations se chhoot jaate hain. Yeh child depression ka ek mukhya kaaran bhi hai.

Cognitive scientist, educationalist aur Cognitive Flexibility Theory ke developer, Rand Spiro, is baat ki pushti karte hain ki schools bachhe ki polymathic prakriti ko dabane mein shamil hain:

> Bachhe cognitively bahut flexible hote hain; yeh multiple choice tasks, regimental learning, aur subjects ke compartmentalization ke sath school hai jisne us flexibility, us creativity, single disciplinary boundaries ke baahar duniya ko dekhne ki us aantarik kshamta ko jala kar raakh (scorched) kar diya hai . . . ise polymathically dekhein.

Yeh koi hairani ki baat nahi hai ki yeh bachhe, vocational choices banane ke liye kam-sajja (ill-equipped), ek aise system dwara baha diye jaate hain jo insaano ko corporate machine mein sirf cogs (purgay) ke roop mein treat karta hai.

---
Yahan aapke diye gaye **Employee Disillusionment** section ke poore text ka ek-ek line ka aur exact paragraph-wise Hinglish translation hai, bina kisi line ya shabd ko chhode:

---

## Employee Disillusionment (EMPLOYEE KA MOH-BHANG / NIRASHA)

Duniya ki aabadi ka ek bada hissa ek aise peshe (occupation) mein majboor (ya anichha se dhoondha gaya) hai jo unhe vyaktigat (personally) ya aarthik (financially) roop se poora nahi karta. Zyadatar log un paristhitiyon ke gulam ban jaate hain jismein woh khud ko paate hain. Aisa lagta hai ki swatantra ichha (**free will**) sirf ek bhram (illusion) hai. Yeh hamare samay ki sabse durbhagya-purna haqeeqaton mein se ek hai; manav garima (**human dignity**) par ek dukhad chot aur manav kshamta (**human potential**) ka ek apradhik dabaav (criminal suppression).

Ajeevak specialists ke aisa banne ka ek kaaran yeh hai ki woh zindagi mein shuruat mein hi ek vishisht peshevar chunav (professional choice) karte hain — ya toh isliye kyunki us samay usme ruchi thi lekin zyadatar kai samajik aur aarthik paristhitiyon ke kaaran — jise jaari rakhne ke liye woh majboor hain kyunki yeh unhe paribhashit karne laga hai. Yeh ek na-tootne wala chakra hai; aapka mool kaushal (original skill) chahe jo bhi ho, aapko (samaj dwara aur ironical roop se aapke apne dwara) ek hi dhabbe mein baant (**typecast**) diya jayega aur uske baad phansaya jayega. Udaharan ke liye, ek employer kisi employee ke application mein use dekhega jo ek 'core skill' ya field ke sabse paas lagta hai, aamtaur par ek academic qualification ya work experience ke roop mein. Agla employer bhi wahi karta hai, aur agla bhi, aur isi tarah aage bhi. Is tarah job paane ke chances tab sabse zyada hote hain jab applicant kisi ek visheshata (specialty) par ek exclusive aur a-dig focus pradarshit karta hai.

Yeh ek gehri tarah se sansthagath sanskriti (**entrenched culture**) hai, ek adishya taqat jo kisi ko ek sankuchit hote hue gaddhe mein aur gehra aur gehra khinchti hai, jisse kuch samay baad baahar nikalna lagbhag asambhav ho jata hai. Yeh ek prakar ki gulami hai, ek an-kaha manav bandhan (**unspoken human bondage**). Sabse behtar sthiti mein yeh employee ke mind mein ek swikriti paida karta hai ki yeh ajeevak specialisation hi survive karne aur progress karne ka akela tareeqa hai, lekin aksar system ke sath poore moh-bhang (total disillusionment) ka ek ahasas paida karta hai.

Yeh moh-bhang, shayad ironical roop se, aaj 'developed' duniya mein un gareeb samajon ki tulna mein tarkasangat roop se zyada hai jinse ise sabse aam taur par joda jata hai. Labor ka division, jise Marx ne 'vyaktiyon ko ek vishisht pukaar se baandhna' kaha tha, abhi bhi status quo hai, jo logon ko uske andar phansata hai jise Weber ne 'iron cage' (lohe ka pinjra) kaha tha. Halanki West mein kaam ka paridrishya 1970s mein Studs Terkel dwara alag-alag kshetron ke niraash workers ke sath baatchit publish karne ke baad se kaafi badla hai, aam janta ke beech asuvidha, asantosh aur moh-bhang ka aam ahasas bana hua hai. Vikas-sheel deshon (developing countries) mein manual ya blue-collar kaam ke apekshakrit haal hi mein hue niryaat (exportation) ke kaaran, viksit deshon (developed countries) mein zyadatar workforce ab ya toh berozgar hai ya white-collar (desk) jobs mein hai.

Jabki is desk-job culture ka uday kuch tareeqon se vyaktiyon aur arthvyavasthaon ke liye labhdayak raha hai (jaise saaf taur par samajik pragati ka prateek — yeh dharana ki desk ke peeche baithna kisi tarah physical labor se zyada aadaraniya lagta hai), sachai yeh hai ki isne ek manasik aur bhaautik roop se niraash workforce paida kiya hai. Work Foundation mein Centre for Workforce Effectiveness ke director, Professor Steve Bevan ke anusar, 'Baithna naya smoking hai.' 'Aap jitne zyada nishkriya (sedentary) honge aapki health ke liye utna hi badtar hoga.' Yeh na sirf sharir ki gatividhi (physical movement) ke liye ek praakritik manav pukaar ko rokta hai (jaisa ki Ido Portal aur Edwin le Corre ki physical training philosophies dwara zor diya gaya hai), iske vyapak arthvyavastha par bhi parinaam hote hain.

U.K. mein, udaharan ke liye, U.K. Office for National Statistics (ONS) ke anusar, pichle saal peeth, gardan aur manspeshiyon ki samasyaon ke kaaran kaam ke lagbhag 31 million din khogaye — inmein se bahut kam physical activity se lagi chot ka parinaam the. Yeh ek hi jagah tike rehna (staying put) hai, jo pata chalta hai ki, kisi bhi doosri bimari ki tulna mein zyada aur lambi anupasthitiyon (absences) ka kaaran banta hai. Yeh hairani ki baat nahi hai ki itihaas mein bahut kam (agar koi) polymaths ne itni nishkriya zindagi jee — yahan tak ki intellectuals ne bhi nahi; zyadatar vaastav mein dynamic scholar-adventurers the.

Shayad aur bhi mahatvapurna baat yeh hai ki desk-job culture, jise bade spiritualist Jiddu Krishnamurti ne ek 'bhayanak rat-race' aur ek 'asahniya qaid' (intolerable imprisonment) kaha tha, ke employees par gambhir manasik prabhav hote hain. Aaj logon ki majority saaf taur par apne maujooda peshe se niraash aur na-khush hai. U.K. mein ek haal hi ke survey ke anusar, sirf 20 percent log kaam par khush hain (Roth and Harter, 2010), ek aisa aankda jo 1987 mein 60 percent se tezi se gira hai. Is asantosh ka kaafi lena-dena us stimulation ke level se hai jo logon ko unke jobs se milta hai. 2008 ke ek survey mein, U.K. workforce ke aadhe se zyada ne swikar kiya ki unke paas kaam par stimulation ki kami hai, jismein sirf 10 percent ne bataya ki woh high level ka stimulation experience karte hain. 60 percent se zyada workers usme sach mein shamil (engaged) nahi hain jo woh karte hain (Towers Perrin/Gallup). Employee engagement North America mein kuch samay se 30 percent ke mark ke aas-paas mandra raha hai, aur ek prasiddh roop se disengaged millennial workforce ke sath (jinmein se 87 percent personal development ko work objective ke roop mein top priority par rakhte hain), employers yeh samajhne ke liye sangharsh kar rahe hain ki employee potential ko kaise retain aur maximise kiya jaye.

Asal mein, 'employee engagement' (ya uski kami) ab duniya bhar mein ek ghatna ban raha hai, aur ise naapne ke liye baar-baar surveys kiye ja rahe hain. Jabki inmein se zyadatar adhyayan Europe aur United States mein kiye jaate hain, yeh dikhane ke liye sabhoot hain ki yeh ek vishwa-stariya (worldwide) jhukav hai. Accenture dwara kiye gaye ek haal hi ke adhyayan ke anusar jisne Europe, Africa, Middle East, South America, North America aur Asia ke 18 deshon se 36,000 professionals ke ek sample ka survey kiya, lagbhag aadhe ne mahsoos kiya ki unhe unke maujooda roles mein mahatvapurna chunauti nahi di ja rahi thi, bhale hi zyadatar apne skills aur capabilities ke baare mein confident the.

Yeh frustration ek hi career ki ek-roopta (monotony) se utna hi juda hai jitna ki job ya employer se. School of Life ke ek adhyayan ke anusar, lagbhag 60 percent employees ek alag career chunte agar woh phir se shuru kar sakte, hummein se 20 percent ka manna hai ki hamare paas kabhi aisa role nahi raha jo humein suit karta ho aur 30 percent employees mahsoos karte hain ki unki taqat kisi doosre career ke liye behtar suit karegi.

Jab companies downsize karti hain, toh woh aksar bekaar hue employees ko agli steps par salah aur margdarshan dene ke liye 'outplacement consultants' ki sevaon ka upayog karti hain. Kuch outplacement programmes ke hisse ke roop mein, insaan ke andaruni career sapnon ko zahir karne ke liye manasik prayog (psychological experiments) kiye jaate hain taaki uske agle career steps ko sunishchit kiya ja sake. Jo aksar paaya jata hai, bina kisi hairani ke, woh vyakti ke asli career aur chaahe gaye career ke beech ek bada antar (disparity) hai. Accountants chahte the ki woh magicians hote, web-designers chahte the ki woh musicians hote, office managers chahte the ki woh professional athletes hote, aur ityaadi. Ek kadve pachtave (bitter regret) ka ahasas hai; ki unhe anichha se ek be-reham dhara dwara khule samundar ki nirarthakta mein baha diya gaya hai, jiske aage unhe aakhirkar khud ko samarpit kar dena padega.

Phir bhi seekhne, grow karne ki aantarik manav ichha humein kabhi poori tarah se nahi chhodti. Log apne jobs ke alawa doosre maamlon par chintan karne ke mauqe ke liye taras rahe hain. Oxford philosopher Anders Sandberg, jinhone haal hi mein ek insurance company ke sath ek project kiya tha, ne zaroor yeh paaya: 'City ke skyscrapers ke logon se baat karte waqt mera anubhav yeh hai ki woh philosophy par baat karne ke liye itne khush hote hain, unhe apne rozmarra ke kaam ke baahar sochne ka koi bahana nahi milta . . . yahan tak ki hum kya kar rahe hain, hum yeh kyun kar rahe hain jaisi cheezein bhi?' Asal mein, duniya ka sabse bada open-source encyclopaedia iska ek pradarshan ho sakta hai. Wikipedia ke founder, Jimmy Wales sujhalte hain ki Wikipedia ka vikas is baat ka sabhoot hai ki jo log apne jobs mein 'specialists' maane jaate hain, unke paas aksar kai doosre interests hote hain — unhe bas unhe jaari rakhne ke liye ek mauqe ki zaroorat hoti hai:

> Wikipedia ke kaam karne ka ek kaaran yeh hai ki ek vyapak, multi-disciplinary talent lupt (extinct) nahi hua hai. Ek cheez jo hum kaafi dekhte hain woh yeh hai ki log apne professional kshetron ke baahar adbhut kaam kar rahe hain; archetype daadhi wala maths professor hai jo Elizabethan poetry ya world war history ke baare mein likh raha hai. Yeh pata chalta hai ki academia mein specialise karne ka itna pressure hone ke baavjood hamare paas abhi bhi sarv-gun-sampanna intellectuals hain aur vaastav mein Wikipedia ke phalne-phoolne ka ek kaaran yeh hai ki yeh uske liye ek outlet pradan karta hai.

Jabki Wikipedia kai vidwan multitalented logon ke liye ek bauddhika outlet zaroor pradan karta hai, haqeeqat yeh hai ki zyadatar log exclusively kaam par apna samay samarpit karne ke liye majboor mahsoos karte hain, bhale hi andar gehrai mein woh jaante hon ki unke paas seekhne, yogdan dene, vyakt karne ke liye aur bhi bahut kuch hai.

---

## Work-Life Imbalance (WORK-LIFE KA ASANTULAN)

Duniya bhar ke top 100 newspapers aur magazines ke ek haal hi ke LexisNexis survey ne ‘work-life balance’ par articles ki sankhya mein ek naatakiya badhotari (**dramatic rise**) ko zahir kiya — 1986 se 1996 tak ke dashak mein 32 se badhkar ak अकेले 2007 mein chonkane wale 1,674 articles! 21st-century ki employment system se is moh-bhang (disillusionment) aur parinaamswarup aatm-santhushti ke khali-pan (void in fulfilment) ne logon ko kaam se baahar, kahin aur vividhta (**variety**) aur stimulation ki behadd talash karne ke liye majboor kiya hai, aksar khud ke liye alag-alag chunautiyan set karte hue, chahe woh ‘white-collar boxing’ ho, marathon ya triathlon daudna ho, ya kisi cooking ya painting club mein shamil hona ho, ya yahan tak ki reserve army ke liye sign up karna ho. *Future of Work* ke lekhak Richard Donkin tarka dete hain, ‘Log apni zindagi mein kuch santulan (balance) dobara haasil karne ke mauqe ke liye taras rahe hain, lekin sarkar-en aur companies is rozmarra ke sangharsh ke prati aankhein band kiye (**blinkered**) lagti hain.’ Standard 9-to-5 corporate work lifestyle se aam asantosh *Four-Hour Week* aur *How to be Everything* jaise bestselling self-help books ki adbhut popularity mein bhi saaf dikhta hai jo vaikalpik kaam ki jeevan-shailiyon (alternative work lifestyles) ko encourage karti hain.

Phir bhi extra-vocational activities (kaam se itar gatividhiyon) ki zaroorat par dikhawa (lip-service) karne ke baavjood, zyadatar jobs haqeeqat mein ek samarpit specialisation ko encourage karti hain aur vividhta (variety) ki praakritik manav pukaar ko rokti (inhibit) hain. Shayad yahi wajah hai ki Aristotle ne kaha tha ki ‘sabhee paid jobs dimaag ko soshit (absorb) aur degrade karti hain’ aur Ibn Khaldun ne dekha ki paramparik rozgar rozi-roti kamane ke sabse ‘apmaan-janak tareeqon (**humiliating ways**) mein se ek hai.’ Kya technology ka vikas manav labor ke ghanton ko kam karne ke liye nahi tha, taaki doosre shauqon ke liye samay nikala ja sake jisse behtar, zyada sarv-gun-sampanna (more rounded) manav banaye ja sakein? Kahi na kahi is raaste par ise bhula diya gaya hai, nazar-andaz kar diya gaya hai, ya simply hamari chetna (consciousness) se chhipakar rakha gaya hai.

Jabki ‘pragati’ (**progress**) ki junooni khoj ne (ya iski maujooda paribhasha ke anusar zyada sahi kaho toh, aarthik aur bhaautik labh) vaastav mein technology mein ek abhootpoorv (unprecedented) pragati ko aage badhaya hai, yeh kai tareeqon se manav aazadi aur vividhta ki keemat par aaya hai. Manav labor ke ghante mahatvapurna roop se badh gaye hain, tarkasangat roop se manav itihaas ke kisi bhi daur ki tulna mein record levels tak. ‘Kahin se bhi kaam karein’ (**work from anywhere**) ki jeevan-shaili, jo internet aur mobile technology ki badoulat bahut popular hui hai, ke paas work-life balance ko badhane ka potential tha lekin iske bajaye isne vyaktigat jeevan aur aaram ke samay par kaam ke atikraman (encroachment) ko aur badtar (exacerbated) kar diya hai. Apni buniyaadi bhaautik zarooraton ke poora hone ke baavjood, log vaastav mein zyada kaam kar rahe hain aur kam jee rahe hain. Jaise ki media aur technology writer Douglas Rushkoff ne ise rakha: ‘Humein zyada samay dene ke bajaye, technologies hamesha on rehti hain, market ki seva karne ke liye hamare samay aur energy ko nichodti (**drainers**) hain.’

Aaj ‘kaam’ (work) ko jis tareeqe se dekha jata hai woh us vishesh philosophy ka kaafi karzdar hai jisne chaar sau saalon se zyada samay se Western samaj par dabddaba banaye rakha hai. Protestant work ethic is vishwas ko rakhti hai ki kaam ek mehnat/kathinayee (**toil**) hai — ek aisi cheez jise hum na karna pasand karenge lekin hum jaante hain ki humein phir bhi zaroor karna chahiye, kyunki usme hi mukti (**salvation**) nihit hai: iske poora hone mein ek sadgun (virtue) hai. Yeh wahi mindset hai jo, *History of Work* ke lekhak Richard Donkin ke anusar, ‘kaam ke baare mein koi confusion nahi chhodti, kyunki kaam ko shrenibaddh (categorised), pigeonholed aur paribhashit kiya gaya hai.’ Parinaamswarup, aaj zyadatar logon ke dimaag mein sthapit (enshrined) kaam ki dharana yeh hai: woh gatividhi jismein koi apna zyadatar samay bitata hai aur jiske parinaamswarup survive karne aur ik इकट्ठा karne ke liye zaroori aarthik muavza (financial remuneration) milta hai. Is dharana ko aakhirkar chunauti dene ka samay aa gaya hai.

---

## Survival (JEEVIT REHNA / AATMA-RAKSHA)

‘Specialist ke jeevan-shaili (way of life) mein ek gambhir pareshani (snag) hai,’ Desmond Morris ne apni bestseller, *The Naked Ape* mein kaha, jo insaan aur jaanwar ke vyavhaar ki tulna karti hai. ‘Sab kuch theek rehta hai jab tak vishesh survival device kaam karta hai, lekin agar paryavaran (environment) mein koi bada badlav aata hai toh specialist akela/asaahay (stranded) chhoot jata hai.’ Toh, udaharan ke liye, koala poori tarah se eucalyptus ke patton par jeevit rehta hai aur sirf ek nishchit jalvaayu (climate) ke andar hi survive karta hai, vishesh roop se Eastern Australia ke woodlands mein. Yeh ek specialist hai jo din mein bees (twenty) ghante tak sota hai. Doosri taraf, raccoon ek generalist hai. Uski operational intelligence use ek praakritik range rakhne ki anumati deti hai jismein North aur Central America ka zyadatar hissa shamil hai, aur yeh ek omnivorous (sarva-ahaari) jaanwar hai jo berries, kide-makode, ande aur chhote jaanwar kha sakta hai. Raccoons ki aabadi acchi hai jabki koala endangered (vilupt hone ke kagar par) ho raha hai. Mudda yeh hai ki jab sthitiyan badalti hain, toh vyapak kshamtaon aur behtar lachilepan (flexibility) wali prajatiyan (species) adapt karne mein saksham hoti hain, jabki sankuchit-focussed specialists, kam ya bina kisi vikalp ka samna karte hue, vilupt hone ke prati vulnerable (sugamya) ho jaate hain. Yeh siddhant insaano par bhi barabar laagu hota hai. Asal mein jaise Morris zor dete hain, ‘naked ape’ (yaani insaan) sabhi mein sabse kam non-specialised, adaptive, opportunistic jaanwar hai.

Aaj, ek yeh galat dharana (misconception) ki survival ke liye specialisation ek zaroorat hai, mukhya narrative ke roop mein haavi hai. Yeh ek aise sawaal-niya (questionable) parisar (premise) par aadharit narrative hai ki hum insaano ke roop mein aantarik roop se competitive (prat प्रतिस्पर्धाatmak) hain. Modern Western paradigm se pehle, doosri vishwa-drishtiyon (jaise African *Ubuntu* philosophy — ‘Main hoon kyunki hum hain’) ne manav ke sahyog-kari (co-operative), ekrat (cohesive) paksh par dhyan kendrit kiya, jise 19th-century ke Russian evolutionist Peter Kropotkin aur haal hi mein genome expert Matt Ridley ne pushti ki ki yeh manav swabhav ka utna hi gehra hissa hai jitna ki vyaktigat swarth (individualistic selfishness) ho sakta hai. Polymaths aamtaur par competition se kam aur ‘swayam’ (self) ko viksit karne ki ek andaruni pukaar se zyada sanchalit the, aur woh bhi zaroori roop se ek doosre ke mukable ya kisi doosre ki keemat par nahi.

Malthusian idea ki aabadi resources se aage badh rahi hai, Herbert Spencer ke Social Darwinism aur ‘survival of the fittest’ ki dharana ke sath milkar ek atyadhik-competitive mindset ke nirmaan mein bada yogdan diya hai jisne badle mein colonialism dwara badhava diye gaye shoshan-kaari culture mein murt roop liya aur baad mein kai badi corporations dwara popular banaya gaya. Asal mein, atyadhik competition ki sanskriti hi hai jisne labor ke division ko utprerit (catalysed) kiya hai aur ‘specialist’ ke jhooth (myth) ko phelaya hai, jisse specialisation ka vikas hua hai. Isne logon ka ek aisa culture paida kiya hai jo vicharon ko jodne ke bajaye unki raksha karte hain, jisne beshak aage specialisation ko badhaya hai.

Toh haavi dharana — na sirf aaj balki poore itihaas mein kai samajon mein — yeh hai ki ek tikau aamdani (sustainable income) ya aarthik suraksha (economic security) sirf ek samay mein ek specialist occupation ke sath hi surakshit ki ja sakti hai. Professionally, ek se zyada job, field ya interest ki khoj aarthik aatmhatya (financial suicide) ka paryay ban gayi hai. Non-specialist ke sath ek nakaratmak kalank (negative stigma) jod diya gaya hai, jiske ‘samay barbaad karne wali gatividhi’ ko uski rozi-roti (livelihood) ki keemat par aata hua dekha jata hai. Polymath ko aarthik roop se dooba hua jeev (financially doomed creature) kehkar neech dikhana duniya ki kai sanskritiyon ki kahavaton ki bhashaon (proverbial linguistics) mein pakka baitha hua ek abhyas hai.

Aayein Eastern Europe ki aam kahavaton ko lein, udaharan ke liye: Poles ubharte hue polymath ko ek aise vyakti ke roop mein sandarbhit karte hain jiske paas ‘saat trades hain, aathva — gareebi,’ Estonians ‘nau trades, dasva — bhookh,’ jabki Czech Republic mein woh kehte hain ‘nau crafts, dasva aata hai dukh,’ aur Lithuania mein ki, ‘jab aapke paas nau trades hote hain, toh aapka dasva bhookhmari hai.’ Yeh East Asian cultures mein bhi paaya ja sakta hai: Koreans kehte hain, ‘baarah talents wale aadmi ke paas dinner ke liye khane ko kuch nahi hota’ aur Japanese polymath ko ‘skilful lekin gareeb’ sandarbhit karte hain. Yahan tak ki jin samajon ne kuch sabse bade polymaths produce kiye woh bhi waisa hi sochte hain: Greeks kehte hain, ‘woh jo bahut saare crafts jaanta hai ek khali ghar mein rehta hai’ aur Italians, da Vinci, Michelangelo, Alberti aur Bernini ke vanshaj, is nasal ko ‘har cheez ka expert, kisi ka master nahi’ ke roop mein dekhne lage hain. Jabki upar di gayi idioms unki mool bhashaon se translate ki gayi hain, jismein arth zyada nuanced hone ki sambhavna hai, buniyaadi vichar wahi hai jo ‘jack of all trades, master of none’ ka hai aur polymath ki survive karne, ik इकट्ठा karne aur pradan karne ki kshamta ke aas-paas jaari snak-pan (cynicism) ka sanket hai.

Is dharana ko sakht sodhan (serious revision) ki zaroorat hai. Occupational diversification, jo polymath ka ek aam nishana hai, vaastav mein aksar survival ka sabse pakka saadhan (surest means) hai. Ek kathin aarthik vatavaran ke dauran, log atyadhik vulnerable mahsoos karte hain agar unka vishesh field workforce ki apni zaroorat mein sikud raha ho. Jaise Yuval Noah Harari ne apni haal hi ki book *21 Lessons for the 21st Century* mein nishkarsh nikala hai, aane wale dashakon ke liye aavashyak career badlavon ke prati anukoolta (adaptability) ek aavashyak survival strategy hogi. Toh skills ki ek zyada vividh range ka matlab hai ki vyakti kshetron ki ek range mein rozgar haasil karne ki apni kshamta ke prati aatmatv-vishwasu (confident) hai. Yeh vyakti ko ek bada sashaktikaran (empowerment) ka ahasas de sakta hai jo aakhirkar badi utpadakta (productivity) aur kushalta (efficiency) ki taraf le jata hai. Isliye education ko aisi skills viksit karne par dhyan kendrit karna chahiye jinhe ek se zyada domain par laagu kiya ja sake ya kam se kam transferable ho, vishesh roop se kyunki jismein koi aaj specialise kar sakta hai woh kal purana (out of date) ho sakta hai, un aarthik, rajneetik aur takneeki roop se sthir samayon (volatile times) ko dekhte hue jismein hum abhi khud ko paate hain.

Jaise deshon (economies) ko ehsaas ho raha hai ki ek aarthik kshetra mein specialisation samajhdari nahi hai aur diversification sabse behtar neeti hai, vyaktiyon ko bhi ise samajhna chahiye. Iske alawa ek vyakti ki polymathy ka vyapak samaj ke liye ek mahatvapurna aarthik labh ho sakta hai. Princeton economist Ed Glaeser ne urban city labour markets mein specialisation aur diversity ka adhyayan kiya aur nishkarsh nikala ki yeh labour ki badi diversity (specialisation ke bajaye) hai jo aarthik vikas ki taraf le jaati hai, simply kyunki industries ke beech zyada skills aur knowledge spillover ho sakta hai. Yeh mahatvapurna hai kyunki skills ka ek vividh set hone ka matlab hai ki labour market arthvyavastha ki badalti zarooraton ke anusar market ki maangon par uchit roop se prati-kriya de sakta hai, vishesh roop se agar education ko aarthik kamyabi se juda rehna hai.

Iske alawa, ‘job for life’ ka model vilupt (extinct) ho raha hai. Redundancy ka khatra badh raha hai aur promotion ke chances ghat rahe hain. Business ya arts mein kaam shayad hi kabhi ek pakki aamdani pradan karta hai. Sports aur military mein careers (aur isliye aarthik suraksha) vishesh roop se kam samay tak tikte hain. Aur woh peshe jinhe paramparik roop se sthir maana jata tha ab aise nahi rahe. Careers adviser ke roop mein pachees (twenty five) saal ke anubhav ke baad, Katherine Brooks nishkarsh nikalati hain ki ek specialist occupation vyakti ki aarthik suraksha ke liye simply kaafi nahi hai. Multiple careers, woh sujhati hain, koi luxury nahi balki ek zaroorat hain, vishesh roop se vartaman samay mein. ‘Aaj ke job market ka chaos ka matlab hai ki job seekhers ko flexible hona chahiye aur apni talents ko vividh settings mein apnaya jana chahiye. Is economy mein, aap sach mein sirf ek career plan par focus nahi kar sakte. Aapko plan B, C aur shayad D par bhi ek sath vichar karne ki zaroorat hai. Aapko Possible Lives ki ek vividhta par vichar karne ki zaroorat hai.’ Yeh samajhdari lagta hai, apne andon (eggs) ko kai tokriyon (baskets) mein rakhna. Apne khatre par specialise karein.

---

## Twenty-First-Century Complexity (IKKEESVEIN SADI KI JATILTA)

Sadaiv jeevit rehne (plain survival) ke pare, specialisation hamari bauddhika (intellectually) aur adhyatmik (spiritually) roop se viksit hone ki kshamta ko bhi rokti hai. Yaani, yeh hamari vaastavikta (reality) ki samajh ko seemit ya dhundhla karti hai, jo ki aasan kaala aur safed (black and white) nahi hai, balki kalpana se pare jatil (inconceivably complex) hai. Lambe samay se maani gayi dwidhaayein/dichotomies (church vs. state, religion vs. reason, good vs. evil, communism vs. capitalism, civilised vs. barbarians, unity vs. multiplicity, micro vs. macro, right brain vs. left brain, science vs. art aur ityaadi) sabhi vaastav mein aisi rachnaayein (constructs) hain jo duniya ke ek vishesh Western anubhav aur samajh se nikalte hain. Doosri sanskritiyon ki apni dwidhaayein hain, jaise unke paas apne peshevar aur bauddhika kshetra hain. Woh hamari vaastavikta ke pehluon ko unki apni samajh ke anusar alag tarah se samoohit (group) karte hain (bhale hi kai, jaise Yin-Yang, gyaan aur dwidha ke prati apne drishtikon mein zyada samagra/holistic hain).

Jaise astronaut Story Musgrave ne antariksh se dekha, ‘prakriti sirf curves (ghumaav) banati hai. Insaan lines (rekhaayein) khinchta hai.’ Gyaan ka 'fields' mein junooni khandikaran (obsessive compartmentalisation), jhoothi dwidhaon ki tarah hi, duniya ka ek galat margdarshit saralikaran (misguided simplification) hai; yeh iski ek zyada sachi samajh ko sankuchit karta hai. Jaise French philosopher aur complexity theory ke pita Edgar Morin ne kaha, mukhya vaastaviktaayein aksar krutrim roop se banaye gaye 'disciplines' ke beech ki dararon se nikal jaati hain, jaise ek bhasha se doosri bhasha mein anuvaad karte waqt arth aksar 'translation mein khogata' hai. Ek bhasha, kisi kshetra (field) ki tarah, ek khandit prism hai jiske zariya hum vaastavikta ko mahsoos karte hain — yeh khud vaastavikta ke poore hisse ka pratinidhitv nahi karti.

West mein, aisa khandikaran ek vishesh bauddhika kriya-vidhi (intellectual methodology) ki virasat hai. Ek poorn 'vyavastha' (order) ki behadd talash mein, vaigyanikon aur darshanikon ne (vishesh roop se West mein Descartes ke baad se) hamari vaastavikta ki aantarik jatilta ko saralta mein badal diya, mathematics aur reductive soch ka upayog karke 'praniyon aur vastuon ko vighatit' (disintegrate) karne ke liye. Isne poore samaj ke hyper-specialisation ko badhava diya. Jaise Morin ne kaha, 'hyper-specialisation ne vaastavikta ke jatil taane-baane ko faad diya aur tukde-tukde kar diya, aur is vishwas ko badhava diya ki vaastavikta par thopa gaya khandikaran hi khud vaastavikta tha.' Psychiatrist aur *The Divided Brain* ke lekhak, Iain McGilchrist ke anusar, itihaas ke dauran hyper-specialised samaj un paradigms se jude hain jinka left brain thinking (structural, linear formations ke liye zimmedar hemisphere) par ek aakarshak/bhaari focus tha. Apni thesis mein, woh us prakar ke samaj ka varnan karte hain jise aisa mindset aamtaur par banata hai:

> . . . gyaan ka ek badhta hua specialisation aur technicalisation . . . koi iske seemit focus ke baahar kisi bhi cheez ke prati ek kharij karne wale ravaiye ki umeed karega . . . darshanik roop se, duniya ko khandikaran dwara chinhit kiya jayega, iske nivaasiyon ko aisa lag raha hoga jaise tukdon aur hisson ka ek sangrah aapatat: yadrakshik roop se (randomly) ek sath phenka gaya hai . . . ek takneeki roop se sanchalit aur amlachahi (bureaucratically) roop se prashasit samaj paida kar raha hai.

Yeh beshak is baat ka ek uchit varnan hai ki aaj cheezein kaafi hadd tak kaise hain. Itihaas ke dauran sangyanatmak, samajik aur shaikshanik conditioning dwara paida hue gyaan ke atyadhik khandikaran ko aaj ikkeesvein sadi ki ghatna dwara ek naye level par le jaya gaya hai jise 'Information Explosion' ke roop mein jaana jata hai — uplabdh jaankari mein abhootpoorv badhotari aur ise manage karne aur samajhne mein hone wali kathinai ke kaaran paida hui ek paheli ya 'anxiety'.

Aaj, hamari duniya ki badhti jatilta ne manavta ko Cartesian technique of deduction ke zariya unmein se har ek ki behtar samajh viksit karne ke liye iske vividh pehluon ko alag-thalag karne par majboor kiya hai. 'Field isolationism' ki yeh sanskriti European Enlightenment ke baad apne charam par pahonchi (Diderot ki *Encyclopaedia* dwara prateekit, jisne gyaan ko codify kiya, halanki ironical roop se Diderot khud dwara nahi jo ek polymath the), aur tab se aupniveshik prabhav ke zariya poori duniya mein project ki gayi hai, students, scholars aur industrial workers (sabhee vyavaharik roop se bina aatma ki machines ki tarah kaam karne ke liye trained) ke jaari specialisation mein murt roop le rahi hai.

Morin ne humein is baat ki yaad dilayi ki 'brāhmāṇḍa (cosmos) ek poorn machine nahi hai balki ek sath sangathan aur visangathan (organization and disorganization) ki ek prakriya hai.' Asal mein, ikkeesvein sadi mein jeevan ne hamare samne is jatilta ke level ko zahir kiya hai, aur modern dimaag ko ise behtar dhang se samajhne ke liye khud ko train karna chahiye. Durbhagya se hamari maujooda educational systems humein is chunauti ke liye paryapt roop se sajjit nahi karti hain, jaise pehle samjaya gaya hai. Cognitive scientist aur educationalist Rand Spiro humein yaad dilate hain ki 'zyadatar jeevan vaastav mein multiplication tables ya basic physics seekhna nahi hai . . . zyadatar jeevan jatil ya ill-structured domains se nipatna hai jinhe adaptive flexibility ki zaroorat hoti hai. Yeh rozmarra ke jeevan mein apvaad (exception) ke bajaye zyada norm hai.' Spiro schema theory (manasik prakriya jo jaankari ko shreniyon mein samoohit karti hai) ke khilaf hain aur iske bajaye ek cognitive flexibility theory (CFT) ka prastav rakhte hain, jo domains ko jatil aur 'ill-structured' ke roop mein pehchanti hai (saaf parameters ke sath unke apne saral roop se paribhashit worlds ke vipreet) isliye samajhne ke liye ek nishchit lachilepan (ya polymathy) ki maang karti hai.

Durbhagya se, saralta aur jatilta dono ka upayog political, commercial aur intellectual elite dwara aam logon ko agyaanata ki sthiti mein rakhne ke liye vaicharik auzaron ke roop mein vividh roop se kiya gaya hai.

Darshanik roop se, polymathic mindset duniya ki ek badi, sarv-gun-sampanna, aapas mein judi hui tasveer ki anumati deta hai, jo us asambandhit tareeqe ke liye ek ilaj (antidote) ke roop mein kaam karta hai jismein zyadatar log (aam log aur so-called intellectuals dono) vartaman mein ise dekhte hain. Polymath aur philosopher Seyyed Hossein Nasr aise thinkers ke liye ek tatkaalik zaroorat ko vyakt karte hain:

> Polymaths ka astitva (ya uski kami) ke bahut mahatvapurna samajik parinaam hote hain. Koi bhi samaj poore ki vision ke bina jee nahi sakta aur survive nahi kar sakta. Polymath ek aisi seva pradan karta hai jo lambe samay mein kisi sabhyata ke survival ke liye poori tarah se aavashyak hai. Toh modern culture dwara polymaths ko 'poo-pooing' karne ke bajaye jaisa lagta hai ki woh karte hain, humein hamesha God ka shukraguzar hona chahiye ki kuch log aise hain jo polymaths banne mein saksham hain. Warna har cheez ek doosre se alag ho jayegi jaise bina kisi ekrat karne wale siddhant ke sharir ke organs, jiske bina sharir bikhar jayega.

---

## Machine Intelligence and the Relevance of Humans (MACHINE INTELLIGENCE AUR INSAANO KI PRASANGIKTA)

Insaani dimaag — jaise ki yeh ek-aayami (**one-dimensionally**) sochne ke liye conditioned hai — aisi jatilta ko kaise samajh sakta hai? Is chunauti ke liye behtar equipped banne ke liye khud ko poshit karne ke tareeqon ko viksit karne ke bajaye, insaani dimaag filhaal is uddeshya ke liye machines banane mein vyast lagta hai.

Is kshetra mein beshak badi pragati ho rahi hai. Artificial intelligence (AI) pehle se hi zyadatar dimaag-ko-chunauti-dene-wale khelon jaise chess, backgammon, quiz contests aur scrabble mein insaano ko hara sakta hai. IBM ke Watson ko jatil vikasatmak algorithms ka upayog karke viksit kiya gaya hai, jo ise organic roop se seekhne ki anumati deta hai aur iski seekhne ki prakriya ko zyada insaani banata hai. Iske alawa, 'whole-brain emulation' ya 'mind uploading' biological dimaag ke computational structure ko scan karne aur sakhti se model karne ke zariya intelligent software banane par kendrit ek program hai. Yeh ek jaari project hai jo machine intelligence ke liye gehre parinaamon ka vaada karta hai.

Human level machine intelligence — jab machines so-called Turing Test ko pass kar lengi — ke baare mein zyadatar AI researchers dwara 21st century ke andar haasil hone ki bhavishyavani ki gayi hai. Kuch agle 20 saalon ke andar iske aane ki bhavishyavani karenge. Aur phir, ek aisa point hai jispar machines vaastav mein ruchi ke sabhi domains mein insaano ke sangyanatmak pradarshan (cognitive performance) se kaafi aage nikal jayengi. Vartaman mein, machine intelligence sirf domain-specific hai, lekin aakhirkar, Oxford ke Future of Humanity Institute ke Nick Bostrom ke anusar, in 'super-intelligent' systems ke paas general intelligence ka ek superhuman level hoga. Ray Kurzweil is point ko technological 'singularity' ke roop mein sandarbhit karte hain.

Toh insaano ke liye iske kya nikarth (implications) honge? Ek cheez jo pehle se hi ho rahi hai woh hai job automation. Oxford ke Frey aur Osborne dwara kiye gaye research ke anusar jo 702 occupations aur aane wale dashakon mein unke computerised hone ki sambhavna ka mulyankan karta hai, 47 percent sabhi American jobs khatre mein hain. Historian aur futurist Harari ise achhe se rakhte hain: '19th century ne working class paida kiya, aane wali century "useless class" paida karegi — bina kisi sainy ya aarthik karya ke billions log. Unki zindagi ko arth dena bhavishya ki badi chunauti hoga.' Toh aparyapt (inadequate), mehangi, dispensable machine substitutes hone ke bajaye, insaan apni zindagi mein arth paayenge (aur samaj unmein arth paayega) agar woh apni khud ki, irreplaceable human uniqueness sthapit karne par dhyan kendrit karte hain. Aisi uniqueness polymath ka nishana hai.

Agar insaan typewriters होते, toh hum bahut pehle aprachalit (obsolete) ho gaye hote. Yahan tak ki agar hum latest iPhone hote, toh hum ek do saalon ke andar dispensable ho jaate. Agar, halanki, hum ek Bach symphony, ek Van Gogh painting ya ek Shakespearean play hote, toh hum samay ki kasauti par khare utarte. Lekin kya ho agar insaan — technology aur art dono ke rachiye — dono se bhi zyada uche nirmaan (elevated creations) hote? Kya ho agar hum anukaran-heen (inimitable), aavashyak (indispensable) hote? Humne jo banayi hain unki tarah super-effective machines banne ki koshish karne ke bajaye, kya ho agar hum apni prakriti ko ek anant roop se nuanced ubharte hue pranali (emergent system), kala ki ek shaandar kriti aur, shayad, ek hi mein ek adhyatmik jeev ke roop mein pehchanein?

AI aakhirkar humein jaankari ko ik इकट्ठा karne aur baantne ke bojh se mukt kar dega. Yeh technical specialisation ke domain ko jeet lega. Knowledge ke bhavishya ke liye biological mind ki prasangikta (relevance) phir vividh gyaanon ka upayog karna — unhe milana, curate karna, fuse karna aur jodna — ek anokhi insaani samajh aur gyaan ko rachna hona chahiye. Pramukh neuroscientist Miguel Nicolelis zor dete hain, insaani dimaag anukaran karne ke liye simplemente bahut a-bhavishyavani-yogy (unpredictable) hai. 'Koi bhi Turing machine yeh predict nahi kar sakti ki ek dimaag kya karega. . . hum technology ko apne hisse ke roop mein absorb karenge, technology humein kabhi absorb nahi karegi; yeh simply asambhav hai.' Yeh jaan-ne ke baad, humein ehsaas hona chahiye ki superintelligence ke prospects chahe jo bhi hon, insaani dimaag zindagi ke liye bina kisi vivad ke arthpoorn hai — agar hum sirf iski uniqueness ko phir se khoj sakein.

Kya bhavishya mein koi machine da Vinci ki *Notebooks*, Ibn Sina ki *Kitab al Shifa* ya Marx ki *Das Kapital* jaisa koi kaam produce kar sakti hai? Kya yeh Apple jaisa koi sangathan bana sakti hai ya Black Consciousness jaisa koi andolan prerit kar sakti hai? Yeh pehal-en art, spirituality aur samajik nyay (social justice) se inspired thin — aur jab tak insaan machine programs mein insaani dimaag ke aise anukaran-heen, rahasyamayi aur shresth (transcendent) tatvon ko code karna nahi seekhte, insaan hamesha apna anokha mulya banaye rakhenge.

Bhaley hi hum superintelligent machines ki apariharyata (inevitability) ko maan lein, phir bhi ek lead period hai jiske dauran insaano ko apna sabse mahatvapurna, sabse bhagya-poorn yogdan dena chahiye. Oxford University ke Future of Humanity Institute ke Anders Sandberg ek aashawadi chetawani-data (hopeful warner) hain: 'kam se kam agle kuch dashakon ke liye jab tak machines insaano se zyada smart nahi ho jaatin, human polymath samaj ke liye bahut mahatvapurna hoga.' Sandberg is daur ke dauran polymaths ke mahatva par zor dete hain, 'Jo jobs spasht roop se paribhashit kiye ja sakte hain woh automation se khatre mein hain. Jo jobs paribhashit karne mein kathin hain woh vaastav mein kaafi surakshit hain. Polymaths saaf taur par pichhle wale hain.' Polymaths, woh kehte hain, bhavishya ke liye mahatvapurna hain kyunki 'na sirf woh un jobs ko karne mein achhe hain jinka koi uchit varnan nahi hai, balki woh, iske alawa, aise jobs ka aavishkar karne mein bhi achhe hain.'

Na sirf specialisation sach ko samajhne ka ek bekaar tareeqa ban raha hai, balki yeh survival ke liye bhi ek kharab neeti hai — chahe vyakti ke liye ho, kisi sangathan ke liye ho, kisi samaj ke liye ho ya vaastav mein ek poori prajati (species) ke liye ho. Seedhe shabdon mein, phir, *Homo sapiens* agli do sadiyon ke andar nasht hone ka gambhir risk uthate hain jab tak ki dimaag ko polymaths ke ek vanguard — agar poori peedhi nahi toh — ko insaano ko uddeshya ka ek ahasas dene ki anumati dene ke liye recondition nahi kiya jata. Machine intelligence, aur shayad Kurzweilian singularity bhi mandra rahi ho sakti hai, lekin kam se kam kuch dashak hain jiske dauran manav jati apni kaal-atheeth (timeless) uniqueness ko phir se khojne, aur phir se sthapit karne ke liye gehri khudaai kar sakti hai. Specialisation is uddeshya ke liye nishchit roop se koi samajhdari bhari neeti nahi hai — iske ulat yeh insaano ko a-manaviyakrit (dehumanising) kar raha hai, humein ek aise daur mein mechanise kar raha hai jahan machines ke sath muqabla karna simply vyarth hai. Doosre shabdon mein, yeh humein kisi bhi asli uddeshya se mukt kar raha hai.

Specialisation, phir, koi 'diya hua' (given) nahi hai. Yeh humein sirf un logon dwara ek system ke roop mein 'diya gaya' hai jinki yeh seva karta hai — atyadhik alpasankhyak (extreme minority). Hum mein se baaki simply maan lete hain ki ise aage badhne ka raasta hona chahiye. Hum bina kisi sawaal ke sarkar, business aur media ke purohit-varg (priesthood) dwara khilaye gaye haavi bhashan (dominant spiel) par vishwas karte hain. Asli sach, jaisa ki hum ab jaanne aaye hain, yeh hai ki specialisation kisi shaap (curse) se kam nahi hai, kyunki yeh manav aatm-santhushti ko rok raha hai, rachnatmakta (creativity) ko ghot raha hai, survival ke mauqon ko seemit kar raha hai, bauddhika agyaanata aur kattarta (intellectual ignorance and bigotry) ko badhava de raha hai aur zyada aam taur par logon ko ek bahut hi ek-aayami, rangheen jeevan anubhav de raha hai. Yeh, kaafi saaf taur par, insaani aatma ko maar raha hai, insaani anubhav ko roak raha hai aur iske alawa hamare astitva ko hi khatre mein daal raha hai. Is durbhagya-purna haqeeqat se khud ko azaad (emancipate) karne ke liye, humein apne dimaag ko recondition karna chahiye. Humein har ek ko apne aadi-kaaleen aatman (**primordial self**) — aantarik polymath — par wapas lautna chahiye aur vikas aur pragati ke liye ek vaikalpik raasta apnaya jana chahiye.

---

# Chapter 5 - Reconditioning the Mind (CHAPTER 5: DIMAAG KO RECONDITION KARNA)

Specialisation se infected (sankramit) system ko neeche lane ka pehla step apne dimaag ko detoxify karna hai.

Hum sabhi ek aise aantarik swabhav (innate disposition) ke sath paida hote hain jo curious (jigyaasu), creative aur versatile (bahumukhi) hota hai. Homogeny (ek-roopta), order (vyavastha) aur conformity (anuroopta) ke sath junooni, samaj phir har dimaag ko uske asli self ko bhulaane ke liye condition karta hai. Yeh thopa gaya bhulapan (inflicted amnesia) hamare khatre par aata hai — vyaktiyon ke roop mein aur ek prajati (species) ke roop mein. Aakhirkar, ek aparyapt roop se trained mechanistic specialists ke roop mein hamara kya mulya hoga superintelligent machines ki duniya mein jo aasaani se humein out-specialise (specialisation mein peeche chhod) sakti hain? Hamara conditioned, ek-aayami (one-dimensional) dimaag ikkeesvein sadi ki chunautiyon ki jatilta (complexity) ko samajhne mein kaise kshamatawan hoga? Hum khud ko un unnecessary biases (fuzool ke pakshpaaton) ki vividhta se kaise azaad (emancipate) kar sakte hain jo hamare decisions, morals aur opinions ko aakar dete hain? Shayad sabse mahatvapurna baat, jatil jeevon ke roop mein jo poori tarah se aatm-siddhi (self-actualisation) ki talash karte hain, hum apni khud ki bahu-pakshiya potential (bahu-pakshiya kshamta) ko kaise poora kar sakte hain? Iske liye humein apne andar ke polymath ko phir se khojna hoga.

Paramparik gyaan (traditional wisdom), modern cognitive science aur itihaas ke dauran polymaths ke jeevan aur vicharon ke sabak ke sanshleshan (synthesis) ke zariya, hum polymath ke gyaanamak safar (epistemological journey) ka chart bana sakte hain, jise phir apne dimaag ko recondition karne ke liye internalise kiya ja sakta hai. Aisi yatra ke chhah (six) mukhya components hain:

1. **Individuality** — Khud ko samajhna (Understanding oneself);
2. **Curiosity** — Nirantar, aseemit jaanch (Continuous, boundless enquiry);
3. **Intelligence** — Vividh kshamtaon ko poshit karna, abhyas karna aur anukoolit karna (Nurturing, exercising and optimising various abilities);
4. **Versatility** — Gyaan aur anubhav ke alag-alag kshetron ke beech bina kisi rukawat ke aage-peeche badhna (Moving seamlessly between different spheres of knowledge and experience);
5. **Creativity** — Ek rachnatmak parinaam ke liye aapatat: alag-alag kshetron ko jodna aur synthesise karna (Connecting and synthesising seemingly disparate fields for a creative outcome);
6. **Unity** — Zyada spashtata aur poore ki vision ke liye gyaan ki vividh dhararaon ko ekrat karna (Unifying various strands of knowledge for greater clarity and vision of the whole).

Har manav jeev ke paas — kisi na kisi hadd tak — unity ka ek ahasas, versatility ka kaushal, connections banane ki kshamta, intelligence ki ek degree, creativity ke moments, ek aantarik curiosity aur apni individuality ki ek awareness hoti hai. Jab in visheshataon ko adbhut synchronicity ke sath unlock kiya jata hai, toh polymath ka dimaag jaag uthta hai. Toh agar hum in (aapas mein gehre se jude hue) visheshataon mein se har ek ko apni chetna (consciousness), hamare mindset, jeevan ke prati hamare drishtikon mein shaamil (assimilate) kar sakte hain, toh yeh ek majboot buniyaad ki anumati dega jahan se ek polymathic life ko launch kiya ja sake.

---

## Individuality (VYAKTI-TV / ANUTTA-TA)

> Insaan kuch bhi kar sakta hai agar woh sirf itni ichha kare.
> — Leone Battista Alberti

Aapne shayad kabhi Arthur Alfred Lynch ke baare mein nahi suna hoga, bhale hi unhone modern history ki sabse swashbuckling (sahasik) zindagiyon mein se ek jee ho. Woh 1860s ke Australia mein paida hue the, jahan unhone ek civil engineer ke roop mein qualify aur practice kiya lekin baad mein journalist banne ke liye London chale gaye, pehle *National Reformer* ke liye, aur phir *Daily Mail* ke liye Paris correspondent ke roop mein. Bade adventure ki tapan (yearning) mein, woh *Reformer* ke liye Second Boer War ko cover karne ke liye war correspondent ke roop mein South Africa ke liye nikal gaye. Wahan, unhone Boers ke prati hamdardi viksit ki, aur Pretoria mein General Louis Botha ke sath ek meeting ke baad woh ek soldier ke roop mein Boer side mein shamil ho gaye. Colonel ki rank diye jaane par, unhone Second Irish Brigade khadi ki, jismein Irishmen, Cape colonists aur doosre log shamil the jinhone British occupation ka virodh kiya.

Lekin jaldi hi unhe British dwara capture kar liya gaya aur jail mein daal diya gaya, jinhone unhe mrityudand (death sentence) sunaya. Kismat ke ek mod par, unhe maaf (pardon) kar diya gaya aur baad mein World War I ke dauran unhone New British Army ke liye volunteer kiya, ek private 10th Battalion Royal Munster Fusiliers khadi ki. London mein rehne ke dauran, woh kisi tarah ek physician ke roop mein qualify aur practice karne mein kamyab rahe, aur yahan tak ki England aur Ireland dono ke constituencies ke liye parliament mein chune gaye. Vyapak yatra karne ke baad, Lynch kai bhashaon mein fluent bhi bane aur novels aur autobiography samet vyapak roop se likha, saath hi physiology, philosophy aur physics jaise vividh vaigyanik vishayon par critiques bhi, jinmein Einstein ki General Theory of Relativity ki ek bold critique shamil thi.

Zyadatar polymaths ki tarah, Lynch ne apni individuality ki ek nirantar khoj aur apni asli potential ko haasil karne ke prati ek samarpan ko pradarshit kiya. Humein sabhi ko apni individuality sthapit karne ke liye ek introspective (aatm-manthan) yatra se shuruat karni chahiye. Apni mool anokhapann (**Einzigkeit**) ko explore karne mein, aapko phir dhare ke vipreet (against the grain) jaane ke liye taiyar rehna hoga — jahan zaroori ho formal, traditional, official tareeqon ko kharij karein aur parinaamon ko sahne ke liye taiyar rahein. Iske parinaamswarup hua algaav/marginalisation aapko aap jo kuch bhi karte hain usme jitna ho sake aatm-nirbhar (self-sufficient) banne ke liye sanchalit karega. Tab, aur tabhi, aap apne anukooltam self (optimal self) ko aage badhane ke liye taiyar honge — ek aisa optimum jo kisi aur ke bajaye aap dwara set kiya gaya hai. Is andaruni yatra ko phir ek baahari yatra mein badalna chahiye.

---

### Self-Realisation (Aatm-Siddhi / Aatm-Gyaan)

> Har koi polymath banne ke liye nahi bana hota. Lekin un logon ke liye jinki kai kshetron mein ruchi aur jhukav hai, polymathy ek arth mein khud ke prati sachha hona hai.
> — Seyyed Hossein Nasr

Plato ne ek baar pratipaadit (postulated) kiya tha ki gyaan aantarik (innate) hota hai aur hum yaadasht (recollection) aur phir se khojne (rediscovery) ke zariya iske prati jagruk hote hain. Asal mein, sach ke liye Greek shabd *aletheia* ('cheezein jo bhulayi na gayi hon') tha aur 'educate' shabd ka mool arth 'us cheez ko baahar nikalna tha jo andar hai.' Self ko khojna aur viksit karna polymath ka mukhya uddeshya hai.

Individuality Self par ek focus ki taraf ishara karti hai — ek prakar ka swarth (selfishness): lekin ek aisa jo doosron ki keemat par vyaktigat hiton ka peecha nahi karta balki iske bajaye samajik, bauddhika aur adhyatmik aazadi ki taraf aatm-manthan yatra par focus karta hai. 'Self' ko yahan isliye 'ego' se alag kiya jana chahiye — vaastav mein Self ko ego se mukt karna millennia se Eastern philosophy ka dhyan raha hai. Asli polymaths ke paas aamtaur par yeh antar karne ki kshamta hoti hai.

Individuality mool roop se manav garima (**human dignity**) ko bahal karne, pehchanne aur realize karne ke sath-sath free will ke samarthan ka ek sawaal hai. Pakistani kavi aur polymath Muhammad Iqbal ke liye, individuality (*khudi*) ke bhaautik aur adhyatmik dono arth the: ‘Khudi ko kar buland itna ke har taqdeer se pehle Khuda bande se khud poochhe bata teri raza kya hai?’

Self, beshak, sirf kaviyon dwara romanticise kiya gaya ek adhyatmik concept nahi hai balki ek neurobiological fact hai — yeh ek genetic uniqueness ki taraf ishara karta hai jiske sath hum mein se har ek paida hua hai. Jaise neuroscientist David Eagleman humein yaad dilate hain:

> Hum mein se har ek apni khud ki trajectory par hai — hamare genes aur hamare anubhavon dwara sanchalit — aur parinaamswarup har dimaag ki ek alag andaruni zindagi hoti hai. Dimaag snow-flakes jitne hi anokhe hote hain . . . jaise-jaise aapke trillions naye connections nirantar bante hain aur phir se bante hain, vishisht pattern ka matlab hai ki aap jaisa koi kabhi astitva mein nahi raha hai ya kabhi dobara astitva mein nahi aayega. Aapke chetan jagrukta (conscious awareness) ka anubhav aapke liye anokha hai.

Hamari genetic banaavat (genome) aur neural structure (connectome), yaani, hamare astitva ke liye anokhe hain. Ise phir se khojna, poshit karna aur nirdeshit karna mahatvapurna hai. Medieval Europe mein silent reading ka uday (congregation mein scripture ko zor se gaane ke paramparik riwaaj ke vipreet), udaharan ke liye, Western duniya mein aisi individuality ko viksit hone ki anumati deta hai, jabki yeh millennia se Eastern meditative traditions ka ek abhinna aayaam raha hai. Koi iske liye chahe jaise bhi badhe, simply apni uniqueness ke prati chetan aur ashwast (convinced) dono hona pehla step hai. Ek baar jab aap samajh jaate hain ki aap kaun hain, toh aap is sthiti mein honge ki samajh sakein ki aap kya ho sakte hain.

Alberti ki sixteenth-century ki ghoshna ki ‘insaan kuch bhi kar sakta hai agar woh sirf itni ichha kare’ ne us prakar ki individuality ki buniyaad rakhi jo European Renaissance ke dauran polymathy ke ek poore daur ko inspire karegi aur bhaavi ‘Uomo Universale’ ke liye mantra ban gayi. Haqeeqat, halanki, yeh hai ki hum mein se har ek ko kuch taqaton aur kamzoriyon, talents aur akshamtaon, chunautiyon aur mauqon ke sath samppan kiya gaya hai. Hum unhe kuch hadd tak control kar sakte hain — vaastav mein hamesha logon ki bina kisi cheez se mauqe banane aur kamzoriyon ko taqaton mein badalne ki adbhut kahaniyan hoti hain (Irishman Christy Brown ke adbhut case par vichar karein jise cerebral palsy tha aur woh sirf apne baayein pair ka upayog kar sakta tha, lekin ek prasiddh kavi, novelist aur playwright ke sath-sath ek painter bhi bana). Phir bhi hum bhaautik aur bauddhika kshamtaon wale mamooli insaan hain jo shaandar hain lekin seemit hain. Toh humein jo haath mila hai humein use sabse behtareen dhang se khelna hoga. Asli polymath ke liye, aatmakankshi aatm-vishwas aur vaastavikta ke ahasas ke beech sahi santulan banana mukhya hai. Jaise Teddy Roosevelt ne kaha: ‘apni aankhein taaron par rakhein aur apne pair zameen par.’

British educationalist Ken Robinson zor dete hain ki kisi vyakti ka focus un kshetron par hona chahiye jahan talent ya kshamta passion ya ichha se milti hai; yeh is intersection par hai, jaisa ki baar-baar sabit hua hai, jahan kamyabi pakti hai. Jo log multiple fields mein is talent-passion connection ko khojte hain unhe un sabhi ko aage badhana chahiye. Jab Nathan Myhrvold Microsoft ke chief technology officer the, unhone wildlife photography ko bhi aage badhaya, nights mein ek chef ke roop mein kaam kiya aur ek culinary degree lene ke liye samay nikala. Microsoft chhodne ke baad, woh vaigyanik anusandhan par laute, apni doosri startup company ki sthapna ki aur prachur roop se aavishkar karna shuru kiya. TED dwara ‘professional jack of all trades’ kahe gaye, unhone 2007 mein TED Conference mein khud ke prati sachha hone aur apni kai visheshataon ko apnane ke mahatva par baat ki:

> Apni zindagi mein apna passion dhoondhne aur apni saari energy ko uspar kendrit karne mein ek zabardast matra mein gyaan hai, lekin main kabhi aisa nahi kar paaya hoon. Haan mere paas passion hoga, lekin phir kuch aur hoga, phir kuch aur. Lambe samay tak maine isse ladai ki; maine socha mujhe mehnat se lag jana chahiye. Lekin aakhirkar maine socha ki mujhe main jo hoon usse ladna nahi chahiye, balki ise apnana chahiye.

Myhrvold apne aantarik swabhav par wapas laute aur apni individuality ko phir se khoja. Aisa karte hue, woh apne anukooltam self (optimal self) ko aage badhane mein saksham the.

Self, beshak ek pravahi (fluid) concept hai jise aatm-manthan ke sath-sath anubhavon ke prati prati-kriya dwara khoja jata hai. Juli Crockett, boxer-philosopher-singer-playwright, ne samjaya ki kaise iski pehchan ne unhe aakhirkar multiple fields mein shreshthta haasil karne ki taraf badhaya: ‘Main khud ke sath apne rishte ko khula rakhne ki koshish karti hoon, aur khud ko granted nahi leti. Khud ko "jaan-ne" ka naatak nahi karti, aur isliye main apni seemaon ko nahi jaanti.’ Asal mein, *Working Identity* ki lekhika Herminia Ibarra ne ishara kiya ki kisi ki identity aur potential ko alag-alag careers mein ghusne aur har anubhav ke prati apni prati-kriya ka mulyankan karke prayog aur galti (trial and error) ke zariya sabse behtareen dhang se khoja jata hai.

---

### Against the Grain (Dhare Ke Vipreet)

> Agar aap hamesha normal banne ki koshish kar rahe hain, toh aap kabhi nahi jaan paayenge ki aap kitne adbhut ho sakte hain.
> — Maya Angelou

Edward Heron-Allen ne aam nineteenth-century Englishman se alag socha aur kaam kiya. Unhone World War I ke dauran intelligence unit ke sath ek soldier ke roop mein shuruat ki aur unki war journals baad mein publish hui thin. Lekin interestingly, ek aise samay mein jab non-specialist ko derogatory roop se ek 'amateur' ke roop mein brand kiya jata tha, unhone projects ki ek aisi range ko aage badhane ka faisla kiya jo asambandhit lagte the. Sabse pehle, unhone handmade violins banaye aur violin-making ki ek kitaab publish ki jo ek sau saal se zyada samay tak print mein rahi. Woh ek expert palmist (hast-rekha shastri) bhi bane, unhone kai kitaabein likhin aur poore United States mein is vishay par kai lectures diye. Phir unhone law mein qualify aur practice karne ka faisla kiya. Ek scientist ke roop mein, microscopy aur biology mein unki safaltaon aur 'foraminifera' (shells wale single-celled protists) mein adhyayan ka matlab tha ki vaigyanik sansthaon ke paas unhe swikar karne ke alawa koi vikalp nahi tha. Woh aakhirkar Royal Society mein chune gaye.

Lynch ki tarah, Heron-Allen ne bhi apne careers ke baahar history, Buddhist philosophy par kitaabon aur yahan tak ki asparagus par ek treatise samet kai vishayon par likhne ke liye samay nikala. Ek oriental linguist ke roop mein, unhone Turkish aur Persian ka adhyayan kiya, aur Persian kavi Omar Khayyam ki kavitaon ka anuvaad kiya. Isse bhi zyada, woh ek kushal novelist bhi the, jinhone 'Christopher Blayre' ke chhadm-naam (pseudonym) ke tehat horror aur science fiction genres mein kai novels aur chhoti kahaniyan publish kin.

Heron-Allen ek swatantra aatma (free spirit) the, aur unhone apne passions aur jhukavon ka palan kiya, bhale hi isne unhe paramparik halkon se alag-thalag kar diya ho. Jaise ki, unhe, kai sabse bade polymaths ki tarah, ek 'eccentric' (sanki) kaha gaya; woh aamtaur par apmaan-janak label jo aksar un logon ko diya jata hai jo 'normality' ko chunauti dete hain ya usse bhatakte hain. Specialisation ke dhare ke vipreet (against the grain) jana — baad wala, beshak status quo tha — kai logon dwara samajik standards ke anusar a-paramparik aur 'odd' vyavhaar maana jata tha. Toh single-field specialisation ke norm hone par, un polymaths ne automatic roop se yeh label kamaya.

Psychologists dwara eccentricity par kiye gaye sabse vyapak adhyayan ke anusar, eccentrics ko poori tarah se khush, tarkasangat log kaha jata hai jo simply dhyan aur kriya mein majority se alag hain. Woh apne samaj ki majority se ek alag wavelength par hain; aur aksar acchi wajah se. Beshak, asli genius aur 'madness' ke beech ek majboot sambandh dikhane ke liye adhyayan hue hain. Lekin hum ab jaante hain ki autism aur synaesthesia jaise mental health conditions aksar ek ashadharan intelligence aur creativity ko unlock karte hain. Kisi bhi maamle mein, French thinker Foucault ne humein yaad dilaya ki 'madness' ka upayog aksar elites dwara un logon ko vilify aur discredit karne ke liye kiya jata hai jinhe unke dwara ek aisa drishtikon ya vyavhaar rakhne wala maana jata hai jo 'unacceptable' hai. Aam dharana ke vipreet, halanki, eccentricity madness nahi hai.

Interestingly halanki, eccentricity polymath ki ek aam (bhale hi aavashyak na ho) vyaktitva visheshata (personality trait) hai. Clinical psychologists dwara rekhankit 'eccentric' ki 18 vishisht visheshataon mein se, sabse mahatvapurna — jismein ek nonconformist attitude; idealistic; creative; teevra curiosity; shauk ke sath ek khush junoon; behadd intelligent; apni uniqueness ka shuruati gyaan shamil hain — polymath se mel khati hain. Isliye yeh hairani ki baat nahi hai ki itihaas ke dauran kai (agar sabhi nahi) polymaths — jinmein Benjamin Franklin, Leonardo da Vinci, Isaac Asimov aur Ludwig Wittgenstein jaise log shamil hain — ko ya toh unke samkalinon dwara ya unki mrityu ke baad, eccentrics ke roop mein jaana jata tha.

Individualism ka ek mukhya pehlu sthapit kattartaon (established orthodoxies) ko chunauti dena hai.
