Yeh mindset mevaad roop se kisi bhi aur har cheez ke lagatar sawaal uthane ki taraf le jata hai, adhikaar ki vaidhta (legitimacy of authority) aur haavi aarthik siddhanton se lekar dharam-granth ki vyakhya aur vaigyanik sachaiyon ki maanyata tak. Lekin koi bhi jo standard samajik aur bauddhika jhukav ka palan nahi karta hai use majority dwara alag-thalag (marginalised) kiye jaane ki sambhavna hai.

Phir bhi aise polymaths ne apne drishtikon ke prati cynical aur apmaan-janak ravaiye par thoda sa dhyan diya. Woh apne aantarik swabhav ke prati sachhe rahe aur samajik dabaav ke aage jhukne se inkaar kar diya. Theroux ko quote karne ke liye, woh kabhi 'doosre drummer ki taal par march karne' se nahi dare. Woh apni khud ki duniya mein rahe, apne drishtikon aur tareeqon ke sath, aur gyaan ki khoj aur aam taur par aatm-vikas ke prati apne drishtikon ke baare mein ek aatm-vishwas rakhte the.

Aise sanskritik paradigms the jahan aisi individuality aksar vyakt ki jaati thi. Italian Renaissance ke dauran, Burckhardt ke anusar, 'koi bhi pratyaksh hone se, doosron se alag dikhne se nahi darta tha; mard zididipane se apni zindagi ke raaste aur apne vyaktitva ke kanoonon ka palan karte the.' Kisi ke liye bhi kuch bhi sambhav tha. Nayi skills seekhna, aapke mool peshe se na jude kisi yadrakshik (random) vishay par ek treatise likhna polymath ke liye koi samasya nahi thi, bhale hi yeh pradhan sanskriti na ho.

---

### Self-Sufficiency (Aatm-Nirbharta)

1830 mein, American Transcendentalist Ralph Waldo Emerson ne apna prabhavshali essay *Self Reliance* publish kiya jismein unhone logon ko conformity se bachne aur apne instincts aur ideas ka palan karne ka aagrah kiya. Unhone logon ko andar ke genius ko khojne aur apne aatm-mulya (self-worth) ko realize karne ke liye encourage kiya. Millennia pehle, Plato ke samkalin Hippias of Elis ne *auterkeia* (ya autarky) ke sadgun ko padhaya aur pradarshit kiya — apni zarooraton ko poora karne mein saksham hone ke zariya aatm-nirbharta aur doosre logon se aazadi ki ek naitikta — jise unhone padhaya aur encourage bhi kiya. Iske practical aur intellectual dono parinaam the.

Manav jeevan entities ke ek nishchit samooh dwara suvidhajanak banaya (aur tarkasangat roop se niyantrit) jata hai: machines, organisations, accessories, log, praakritik aur adhyatmik kanoon, imaratein, khana aur drugs. Sahi hone ke liye, hum rozmarra ke aadhar par alag-alag 'facilitators' par nirbhar karte hain — computers, gadgets, corporations, government bodies, handbags, kapde, doctors, vehicles aur doosre upayogita ke saman. Hum unjaane vishwas (inadvertent trust) ki duniya mein jee rahe hain. Is roopak-atmak (allegorical) 'trust contract' ne duniya bhar mein ek aamtaur par santusht aur udaseen samaj ke nirmaan mein bada yogdan diya hai.

Hum hamari suraksha karne ke liye security agencies par, door-daraaz ke ilaqon mein kharab na hone ke liye vehicles par, hamare saman ke liye sahi keematein lene ke liye corporations par, aasaani se kaam karte rehne ke liye laptops aur computers par, hamare dard ko kam karne ke liye prescribed drugs par, sadkon par ek sabhya tareeqe se vyavhaar karne ke liye logon par, emergency sthitiyon mein kaam karne ke liye mobile phones par, hamare upar na girne wali imaraton ko design aur construct karne ke liye architects par, hamari zindagi bachane ke liye surgeons par vishwas karte hain. 'Over-trusting' mein, hum santusht ho jaate hain aur bina jaane apne dimaag ko zaroori rozmarra ki jaanch se a-ksham (disable) kar dete hain. Aisa karne mein, hum na sirf zyada se zyada seekhne ki apni aantarik pravritti ko sankuchit karte hain (ek aisa instinct jo bachhon mein sabse zyada dikhta hai, lekin durbhagya se aur fuzool mein adults ke beech gayab ho raha hai), balki badle mein logon, machines aur organisations ko apni aazadi (autonomy) samarpit kar dete hain, jo is tarah hamari lives ko control karte hain. Isliye, polymathise karne ka matlab hai aisi aakarshak nirbharta se khud ko azaad karna aur, ek arth mein, sachi aazadi ka peecha karna. Un a-bhavishyavani-yogy tatvon par vishwas karne ke bajaye, kisi ko (Emerson ko quote karne ke liye) 'Khud Par Vishwas' (Trust Thyself) karna chahiye.

Polymaths ne hamesha practical aur intellectual gyaan ke liye standard education systems par apni nirbharta ko kam se kam kiya hai. Woh freethinkers ya 'freedoers' ke roop mein aaye hain. Asal mein, polymath aur educationalist Hamlet Isakhanli ne 'self-education, seekhne ki ajeevak ichha, ek majboot ichha-shakti aur endurance' ko polymath banne ke sabse mahatvapurna steps ke roop mein rekhankit kiya. Phir yeh hairani ki baat nahi hai ki itihaas ke dauran zyadatar polymaths autodidacts (swayam-sikhaye-hue) rahe hain. Le Corbusier, Coward, Edison, Leibniz, Goethe, Franklin, Leonardo aur Tagore jaise polymaths apne dwara kiye gaye lagbhag har kaam mein self-taught the.

Autodidacts, 'woh log jo khud ko padhana pasand karte hain ya kisi na kisi tareeqe se non-teaching sthitiyon se gyaan haasil karna pasand karte hain' standard educational systems ki seemaon ko pehchante hain aur aazad roop se uska peecha karte hain jise woh apne liye ruchi aur mulya ka maante hain. Chahe yeh ek Indian market trader ho jo practical upayog ke liye jatil arithmetic seekh raha hai aur laagu kar raha hai ya ek French freethinker ho jo State ke khilaf vidroh ke roop mein gyaan ki talash kar raha hai, autodidacts bauddhika aur samajik aazadi ki taraf sangharsh ke hisse ke roop mein apni individuality ko realize karte hain aur exercise karte hain. Aisa karne mein, woh apne liye aseemit sambhavnaon ko kholte hain, bhale hi shuruat mein aisa lage jaise woh dhare ke vipreet (swimming against the current) taair rahe hain.

Aaj zyadatar sabse vidwan log paramparik roop se 'intellectual' halkon se nahi aate — zyadatar ke paas basic education bhi nahi hai, college degrees ki baat toh door hai. Ironical roop se, encyclopaedic gyaan aaj Colombo ke tuk-tuk driver, Liberia ke chhote business owner aur Ulaanbaatar ke shoemaker mein paaye jaane ki sambhavna modern West ke ek 'highly educated professional' se zyada hai. Bas ek Wall Street derivatives trader ya botany ke professor ko Erbil ke ek taxi driver ya Khartoum ke ek barber ke sath ek room mein kai vishayon par debate karne ke liye ek sath rakhein aur result dekhein. Yeh bhala kaise sambhav hai? Yeh wahi kaaran hai kyunki ek yuva Mike Tyson, street brawling 'thug' ek ameer public school ke kid se behtar boxer hoga jisne sabse behtareen kit khareedi, sabse behtareen trainer hire kiya aur saari paramparik techniques seekhin. Tyson ko apne shot ke liye ladna pada, woh zyada bhookha, aseemit aur intuitive hai jabki 'manufactured' boxer mechanistic, orthodox, spoilt aur atyadhik calculative hai. Bina shaikshanik aur samajik visheshadhikaron wale log ek majboot curiosity aur ek prakar ka raw samarpan viksit karne ki pravritti rakhte hain, jaise early twentieth-century United States mein ek black athlete ko team ke liye chune jaane ke liye apne white counter-part se 10 guna behtar hona padta tha. Hinta (inferiority) aur utpeedan (oppression) vaastav mein aksar ek shaktishali motivator ke roop mein kaam kar sakte hain, jaisa ki humne Paul Robeson, José Rizal aur Che Guevara jaise polymathic krantikariyon se dekha hai.

---

### Human Optimality (Manav Anukoolta)

Apni poori zindagi mein, Leon Battista Alberti apne anukooltam self (optimal self) banne se chintit the. Mukhya roop se fifteenth-century humanist philosopher ke roop mein humein jaane-maane, Alberti ka *De iciarchia* ('On the Man of Excellence and Ruler of His Family') courtier polymath ke ubharte hue vishwa-drishtikon ko paribhashit karne mein ek mukhya text bana. Unki prasiddh ghoshna ‘insaan sabhi cheezein kar sakta hai agar woh sirf unki ichha kare’ humanist movement ke liye mantra ban gayi. Aur unhone nishchit roop se uska abhyas kiya jo unhone updesh diya.

Alberti ne pratishthit University of Bologna mein law ka adhyayan kiya aur jab doctor of canon law ke roop mein graduate hue, toh unhe Rome mein Papal Chancery mein secretary niyukt kiya gaya, jahan unhe santon aur shahidon ki paramparik zindagiyon ko phir se likhne ke liye commission kiya gaya tha.

Visual art mein apni ruchi ka palan karte hue, unhone Donatello aur Brunelleschi jaise pramukh artists se salah li, aur mukhya treatise, *On Painting* ko poora kiya, jo art mein pehli baar perspective ke siddhant ko introduced karega. Phir unhe Pope ka architectural adviser niyukt kiya gaya aur unki *Ten Books on Architecture* Renaissance architecture ki bible ban gayi, jisne unhe 'Florentine Vitruvius' ka title dilaya. Unhone geography par ek treatise bhi likha jisne ek zameen ke ilaqe ka survey aur mapping karne ke kanoon set kiye aur grammar par ek kitaab jo yeh pradarshit karne ki koshish karti thi ki Tuscan vernacular literary upayog ke liye utni hi uchit thi jitni Latin thi. Cryptography par unke kaam mein pehli jaane-maani frequency table aur cipher wheel (ise bhi Alberti ka aavishkar maana jata hai) ka upayog karke coding ki pehli polyalphabetic system shamil thi.

Alberti jaise polymaths ne apne anukooltam astitva ki sthiti (optimal state of being) ko paane ke liye lagatar prayas kiya. Optimality kisi ki potential ka sabse poorn realization hai; yeh ek bhramatmak 'perfection' ka peecha karne se alag hai. Maslow ne kaha ki 'jo ek aadmi ho sakta hai, use woh hona chahiye' aur yeh ki koi aatm-siddhi ki sthiti tabhi haasil karta hai jab 'koi woh sab kuch ban jata hai jo woh banne mein kshamatawan hai.' Yeh ek aantarik manasik swabhav (innate psychological disposition) hai, aur ek aisa jise sahi paristhitiyon mein activate kiya ja sakta hai. Toh bhale hi hum ek kshetra mein atyadhik kamyab ban jaate hain, hum tab tak santusht nahi hote jab tak hum woh sab kuch poora nahi kar lete jo hamare liye sambhav hai. Is gyaan ke sath ki hum aantarik roop se bahumukhi hain, jo kuch bhi sambhav hai use poora karne ka prayas karna swabhavik roop se multiple talents aur interests ki khoj aur unhe faldaayi banane mein shamil hai. Yeh wahi lagta hai jisne Paul Robeson ko inspire kiya, jinhone apne pita se 'adhikatam manav aatm-siddhi' (maximum human fulfilment) ka idea seekha — ki zindagi mein kamyabi ko paise aur vyaktigat pragati ke sandarbh mein nahi naapa jana chahiye, balki goal apni khud ki potential ka sabse ucha vikas hona chahiye.

Itihaas mein aisi jaghein aur daur the jahan aisa mindset aam tha, jaise Alberti ka Renaissance Italy. Us daur ke pramukh historian Jacob Burckhardt ne kaha ki isne 'pehle individuality ko sabse ucha vikas diya, aur phir vyakti ko sabhi roopon mein aur sabhi sthitiyon mein khud ke sabse utsahi aur vyapak adhyayan ki taraf le gaya.' Yahan aisa laga ki God se insaan par hi dhyan ka ek achanak badlav hua tha, jo artist dwara self-portraiture aur writer dwara autobiography ki achanak badhotari dwara uchit roop se pratinidhitv kiya gaya tha. Isne badle mein sakht vyaktigat vikas ke ek daur ko utprerit kiya: 'bade mardon' ki jeevniyon ko padhne ka ek junoon tha, gentlemanly manners ki ek sukshma perfection, sansarik gyaan ki ek anant khoj, aur kalaatmak aur saahityik hiton ka ek jaari sanrakshan. Manav potential mein vishwas ek aadarshwadi level par pahonch gaya aur optimality ultimate goal ban gaya.

Giovanni Pico della Mirandola, khud ek polymath aur Renaissance Humanism ke mukhya instigators mein se ek, ne apne prabhavshali essay *Oration on the Dignity of Man* mein kaha ki 'Usme [insaan mein] sabhi cheezein hain . . . toh use sabhi cheezein banne dein, sabhi cheezein samajhne dein aur is tarah ek devta banne dein.' Jabki kuch log ise ek atishayokti (overstatement) maan sakte hain, unke sandesh ka saar yeh hai ki hum sabhi ke paas ek an-chhua potential hai jo realize hone ke liye ubaal raha hai, aur is optimality ko aage badhana khud ke sath nyay karna hai. Aur iske liye, woh ishara karte hain, humein kisi par ya kisi cheez par nirbhar nahi hona chahiye. Bade psychologist Carl Rogers ke liye, human optimality incongruence ke antar ko band karne se aayi — ek vyakti jo hai aur potential roop se jo ho sakta hai uske beech. Rogers ke anusar, 'achi zindagi' 'poori tarah se kaam karne wale vyakti' (fully functioning person) dwara jee jaati hai: 'Achi zindagi ki yeh prakriya, mujhe yakin hai, kamzor dil walon ke liye zindagi nahi hai. Isme kisi ki potentialities ka zyada se zyada banne ka khinchav aur vikas shamil hai. Isme hone ka sahas shamil hai. Iska matlab hai khud ko poori tarah se zindagi ki dhara mein launch karna.'

---

## Curiosity (JIGYAASA)

> Hum aage badhte rehte hain, naye darwaze kholte hain aur nayi cheezein karte hain, kyunki hum curious hain aur curiosity humein naye raaston par le jaati rehti hai.
> — Walt Disney

---

### A Natural Trait (Ek Praakritik Gun)

> Gyaan ki ichha se zyada praakritik koi ichha nahi hai. Hum har us saadhan ki koshish karte hain jo humein is tak le ja sakta hai.
> — Michel de Montaigne

Eleventh-century ke Persian prodigy Al-Biruni ek ajeevak bauddhika explorer (intellectual explorer) the. Unke paas ek vyapak-aadhrit shiksha (broad-based education) thi aur unhone vishesh roop se astronomy aur mathematics mein shreshthta haasil ki, jiske liye unhone apne kishoravastha (teens) mein hi saara jaana-maana gyaan haasil kar liya tha. Jaisa ki riwaaj tha, unhone theology ke bhaashan par bahut shuruat mein hi mahaarat haasil kar li thi. Woh Mahmud of Ghazni ke darbar mein ek adviser aur ambassador bane, jahan dharmik tyoharon ke kaalanukram (chronography) par unki pehli kitaab ek shauqeen mathematician aur astronomer ke sath-sath ek kushal historian, anthropologist aur theologian ke roop mein unki pratishtha ko pakka karegi.

Lekin anthropology aur philosophy mein unka mukhya yogdan tab aaya jab unhe Mahmud of Ghazni ke darbar mein le jaya gaya aur Mahmud dwara aaj ke modern-day Pakistan ke ilaqe ki jeet ke dauran bandi banaye gaye Indians ke sampark mein laya gaya. Indian riwaajon, bhasha aur abhyas par likhne ke sath-sath, Al-Biruni Hindus ke dharam ka sachhi tarah se manoranjan karne wale pehle Muslim philosophers mein se ek the, jinhe aamtaur par bahudeovadi kafir (polytheistic infidels) ke roop mein dekha jata tha, aur unhone Hinduism ko ek jatil darshanik vishwa-drishti (philosophical worldview) ke roop mein gambhir jaanch ke yogya maana. Yeh Indian history, science, culture, philosophy, language aur theology par unke 600-page ke masterpiece mein charam par pahoncha, jiska title saral roop se *The India* tha.

Unka agla bada karya, *Qanun*, Ptolemy ke *Almagest* ka ek mahatvapurna vikas tha aur Biruni mineralogy, pharmacology, botany aur medicine jaise vividh vaigyanik vishayon par lagbhag 140 treatises produce karenge (anuvaad ke sath-sath mool yogdan bhi). Jis cheez ne unhe poori zindagi sanchalit kiya woh ek asantusht/a-tript (insatiable) curiosity thi, jo prakriti mein vyapak thi aur unhe jaanch ke vividh raaston par le gayi.

Curiosity, jo Al-Biruni jaise polymath ka ek aavashyak driver hai, hamari biology ke sath-sath hamari chetna mein bhi samahit hai. Sociologists ke beech ek bhaari aam-sehamati hai ki curiosity manav sthiti ke buniyaadi gunon mein se ek hai — ek praakritik swabhav jo class, race ya gender ke parwah kiye bina sabhi insaano mein maujood hai. Iska samarthan evolutionary biology dwara kiya jata hai, jisne baar-baar sabit kiya hai ki insaano ko curious hone ke liye genetically program kiya gaya hai. Primates, udaharan ke liye, trapdoor ke doosri taraf kya hai yeh khojne ke liye zyada samay aur mehnat se kaam karenge, usse bhi zyada jitna woh khane ya sex ke liye prayas karenge. Asal mein, zoologist Desmond Morris, manav aur jaanwar ke vyavhaar ke ek prasiddh scholar (prasangik roop se ek kamyab surrealist painter bhi), ne apni 1967 ki bestseller *The Naked Ape* mein nishkarsh nikala ki ‘sabhi mammals ke paas ek majboot khoj-parak pukaar (exploratory urge) hoti hai,’ aur insaan un sabhi mein sabse zyada jigyaasu (inquisitive) hain:

> Sabhi yuva bandar jigyaasu hote hain, lekin unke adult banne par unki curiosity ki teevrata (intensity) ghatne lagti hai. Hamare [insaano] sath, bachkanaji ki jigyaasa hamare vayask (mature) saalon mein majboot aur faili hui hoti hai. Hum kabhi jaanch karna band nahi karte. Hum kabhi is baat se santusht nahi hote ki hum guzara karne ke liye kaafi jaante hain. Har sawaal jiska hum jawab dete hain ek doosre sawaal ki taraf le jata hai. Yeh hamari prajati ka sabse bada survival trick ban gaya hai.

Behavioural scientist aur neuroeconomics ke professor, George Loewenstein ke anusar, curiosity simply ek aisi pukaar hai jo tab paida hoti hai jab hum 'jo hum jaante hain aur jo hum jaan-na chahte hain' ke beech ek antar (gap) mahsoos karte hain. Is gap ke bhavnatmak parinaam hote hain: yeh ek manasik khujli (mental itch), dimaag par ek macchar ke kaatne jaisa lagta hai. Hum naye gyaan ki talash karte hain kyunki hum khujli ko us tarah se khujlate hain. Pehle, yeh isliye hai kyunki dimaag ke paas aspashtata (ambiguity) ya anishchitata ke liye ek praakritik na-pasandgi hoti hai; aur curiosity wahi hai jo ise door karne ke liye activate hoti hai. Doosra, aparyapt optical (ya us maamle mein, koi bhi) stimulation dimaag ko automatic roop se aaram ki sthitiyon ka 'anukooltam santulan' (optimal balance of arousal states) haasil karne ke liye ubaav (boredom) se baahar nikalne ka raasta dhoondhne ke liye majboor karti hai.

Abstract jaankari ke liye hamari ichha, jo mool roop se curiosity ka kaaran hai, ek dopaminergic craving ke roop mein shuru hoti hai, jo usi aadi-kaaleen raaste (primal pathway) mein nihit hai 'jo sex, drugs aur rock and roll par bhi prati-kriya deta hai.' Yeh koi hairani ki baat nahi hai ki Aristotle ne ghoshna ki ki 'sabhi aadmi swabhav se gyaan ki ichha karte hain' aur Leonardo ne nishkarsh nikala ki 'seekhna kabhi dimaag ko nahi thakata.' Charles van Doren ne apni *History of Knowledge* mein is manav gun ki taqat aur kaal-atheethta ko rekhankit kiya:

> Jaan-ne ki ichha, jab aapko ehsaas hota hai ki aap nahi jaante, sarvabhoomik hai aur shayad a-pratibandhit (irresistible) hai. Yeh manavta ka mool laalach (original temptation) tha, aur koi bhi aadmi ya aurat, aur vishesh roop se koi bachha, lambe samay tak is par vijay nahi pa sakta. Lekin yeh ek aisi ichha hai, jaisa ki Shakespeare ne kaha tha, jo us cheez se badhti hai jis par ise khilaya jata hai. Gyaan ki pyaas ko bujhana asambhav hai. Aur aap jitne zyada intelligent honge yeh utna hi zyada hoga.

Leonardo Da Vinci ke pramukh expert, Martin Kemp, pushti karte hain ki curiosity ek polymath ka trademark hai:

> Leonardo ka dimaag ek aisa dimaag hai jo bachhe ki tarah poori tarah se curious hai — aisa kyun hota hai, main kya dekh raha hoon, main ise kaise samajh sakta hoon, aur agar aap us prakar ki bachkanaji curiosity ko zabardast bauddhika taqat ke sath milate hain, toh aapko kuch bahut hi prabhavshali (potent) milta hai.

Gyaan ke prati Islamic drishtikon udaharan-swaroop hai. Muhammad logon ko ‘gohd se lekar qabr tak gyaan ki talash karne’ aur ‘gyaan ka peecha karne ke liye jahan bhi aap ise paayein’ encourage karne ke liye jaane jaate the, yeh batate hue ki ‘gyaan ki talash karna har Muslim par ek kartavya hai’ aur vaastav mein ‘uske liye jo gyaan ki talash ke raaste par nikalta hai, Allah uske liye jannat ka raasta aasan kar dega.’ Unke baare mein yeh bhi kaha jata hai ki unhone ghoshna ki thi ki ‘ek scholar ki siyahi ek shahid ke khoon se zyada pavitra hai’ aur ‘sochne ka ek ghanta sattar saal ki ibadat ke barabar hai’ aur ‘ek padha-likha aadmi shaitan ko ek hazaar agyaani ibadat-garon se zyada takleef deta hai.’ Muhammad worldly (*ulum aqliyya*) aur religious (*ulum naqliyya*) dono gyaan ko sandarbhit kar rahe the; ek doosre ke bina paryapt nahi tha.

Intelligence aur curiosity ke beech ka sambandh mahatvapurna hai, lekin yeh tarka diya ja sakta hai ki curiosity shayad kamyabi ka mukhya driver hai. Asal mein, Einstein hi the jinhone prasiddh roop se ghoshna ki thi: ‘Mere paas koi vishesh talent nahi hai. Main sirf junooni roop se curious (passionately curious) hoon.’ Ek praakritik manav pravritti hone ke sath-sath, curiosity ya doosre shabdon mein ‘gyaan ki pyaas’ ko millennia se vividh sanskritiyon, dharamon aur darshanon ke zariya encourage kiya gaya hai.

Lekin intelligence aur creativity ki tarah, curiosity dimaag mein do raaston mein se ek le sakti hai. Pehla gehrai (depth) ka hai, jiske dwara vyakti kisi vishesh vishay mein gehra aur gehra jaanch karta hai, jo ki us specialist ke liye typical hai jo pyramid ke top tak ek linear raasta lene ke liye ubaal raha hai. Doosra, jo aseemit chaudai (boundless breadth) ka hai — aur jahan pyramid maujood bhi nahi hai — woh ek polymath ka raasta hai. Polymath vyapak roop se curious hota hai; manav-nirmit disciplinary boundaries unke dimaag ko kisi ek vishesh kshetra se nahi baandh sakti. Woh ek investigative journalist ya ek detective ki tarah jaanch ki ek rekha ka peecha karta hai, aur ek khula dimaag banaye rakhta hai chahe us sawaal ke liye unhe biomimicry seekhna pade ya plumbing, astrophysics ya masonry. Jaisa ki Montaigne ne kaha, ‘us vyakti ko jo gyaan ki talash mein hai ise wahin se pakadne dein jahan yeh pada hai.’

Multiple (sambandhit aur aapatat: asambandhit) ghatnaon ke baare mein sawaal poochhna ek curious mind ka trademark hai, Paul Robeson ne kam umar se hi ‘seekhne ke liye ek pyaar, iski poori poornata mein sach ke liye ek nirantar khoj’ viksit kar li thi. Asal mein bachha ultimate enquirer, pre-polymath hai, aur yadyapi adults us curiosity ko nahi khote hain jo ek aadi-kaaleen manav gun lagti hai, unki curiosity quality aur type mein kharab ho jaati hai. Woh *why* ke bajaye *how* ke sath zyada vyast ho jaate hain; uski samajh ke bajaye jaankari ke sath zyada chintit ho jaate hain.

Critical thinkers jaise polymathic philosophers ek zyada jatil aur vyavasthit tareeqe se bachhe ki virasat ko jaari rakhte hain — jaise 'eccentric' artists, hobbyists aur 'trivia buffs' ek isi tarah ke khelne wale tareeqe se karte hain — jabki zyadatar doosre adults rozmarra ki zindagi ki vishesh vyavahariktaon (practicalities) mein vyast rehte hain. Adults sochte hain ki woh jaante hain ki unhe kya jaan-ne ki zaroorat hai aur jaise ki woh tezi se band-dimaag (closed-minded) ho jaate hain. Woh apne hairani ke ahasas (sense of wonder) ko kho dete hain.

Yeh pravritti saalon se myths, parables aur proverbs ke vikas ke zariya dimaag mein pakki ho gayi hai jo curiosity ke khatron ki chetawani dete hain. Pandora’s Box parable aur yeh idea ki ‘curiosity killed the cat’ is baat ko darshate hain ki samaj mein curiosity ko kis apmaan-janak tareeqe se dekha gaya hai. Is sanskriti ki jadein institutional elitism aur janta se gyaan ko chhipane mein hain, iski virasat ek aisa dimaag hai jo ‘apne kaam se kaam rakhne’ ke liye conditioned hai.

---

### Sources of Knowledge (Gyaan Ke Srot)

> Gyaan jaanne-wale ke mode ke anusar hota hai.
> — Aristotle

Sach mein curious polymath is baat se jagruk (cognisant) hota hai ki ‘gyaan’ jis tareeqe se insaan ise samajhte hain mukhya sroton ki vividhta se aa sakta hai, jinmein se kai aapas mein ativyapit (overlap) hote hain. Yeh primary pools aur faculties hain jinse hum antar-drishti (insight) aur samajh khinchte hain — ise jaankari ke sroton (sources of information) se uljhaya nahi jana chahiye.

Ek mahatvapurna srot jise poore itihaas mein vyapak roop se swikar aur aadar kiya gaya hai woh manav gawahi (human testimony) hai. Chahe African folk tales, Arabian poetry, samajik baatchit, television, modern university lectures aur TED Talks ke roop mein maukhik (orally) aaye, ya praacheen tablets, printed treatises aur modern-day computer screens ke likhit parampara ke zariya aage badhaya gaya ho, jiske paas gyaan hai usse doosron tak gyaan ka pahonchna jinke paas nahi hai shayad gyaan ke transfer, arjan aur ik इकट्ठा hone ki sabse badi prakriyaon mein se ek hai jise insaan ne kabhi jaana hai.

Testimonial knowledge (ya jaankari) kaafi hadd tak vishwas par aadharit hoti hai lekin kuch hadd tak intuition aur rationality par bhi taaki ise samjha, parakha aur context mein rakha ja sake. Is tarah, khud manav rationality — bahu kam ya bina testimonial knowledge ke — gyaan ka ek mahatvapurna srot ban sakti hai. Gyaan arjan ka yeh roop shayad darshanikon (philosophers) dwara sabse behtareen dhang se pradarshit kiya jata hai, jo astitva, prakriti aur naitikta par vividh sawaal uthane ke liye apni tarka-shaktiyon (powers of reasoning) ka upayog karte hain aur aisa karke apne dimaag ko naye sambhavnaon aur nishkarshon ki ek shrinkhla ke liye kholte hain. Buddha, Socrates aur Confucius jaise sach mein original thinkers darshanikon ke behtareen udaharan the jinhone testimonial knowledge ke bajaye rational ya intuitive gyaan par kam nirbharta dikhayi.

Lekin manav rationality gyaan ke ek aur srot se bhi sanchalit hoti hai, jo perceptual experience se aati hai, jo seedhe manav indriyon (drishti, shravan, sparsh, sugandh aur swad) dwara haasil ki jaati hai. Jaise Leonardo ne kaha, ‘hamare saare gyaan ki shuruat hamari dharanaon (perceptions) mein hoti hai.’ Yeh ‘empirical’ gyaan, halanki, sirf kshanik (momentary) hota hai jab tak ki ise nirantar memory mein store na kiya jaye aur general samajh ko badhane ke liye doosre gyaan ke sath fuse na kiya jaye. Is tarah hamari apni vyaktigat yaadasht-en (memories) — aur unke fusion se nikalne wali antar-drishtiyan — apne aap mein gyaan ke ek mukhya srot ke roop mein kaam kar sakti hain.

Jabki testimony, reason, experience aur memory par zyadatar modern Western epistemologists dwara gyaan ke mukhya sroton ke roop mein sehamati vyakt ki gayi hai, doosri paramparaon ke paas apne epistemic frameworks hain (udaharan ke liye, Hindu yogic tradition ke paas hi gyaan ke 16 atyadhik nuanced srot hain). Zyada aam taur par, duniya bhar mein alag-alag paramparaon mein swikar kiye gaye mahatvapurna sroton mein esoteric (jaise Steiner dwara unki *Knowledge of Higher Worlds* mein varnit); revelatory ( scripture jaise Quran, Bible, Zend Avesta aur Vedas ke zariya samvaadit divine wisdom); linguistic (har bhasha soch ke ek nishchit mode ka pratinidhitv karti hai); cultural (alag-alag mulya, naitikta aur abhyas jaan-ne ke ek tareeqe ko prabhavit kar sakte hain); artistic (sangeet ya painting ki ‘bhasha’) shamil hain. Doosron mein intuitive (Kahneman ke dual process model ka system 1); genetic (Carl Jung ki genetic memory theory); synesthetic (ek indriya ke parinaam ko doosri indriya ke zariya anubhav karna); emotional (hamari ‘irrational’ bhavnaayein humein apne aur duniya ke baare mein bahut kuch sikhaye sakti hain); intoxicant (drug-induced creativity, jo yugon se sabhi kshetron mein anant geniuses dwara pradarshit ki gayi hai); natural (podhon, jaanwaron aur cosmos ki epistemology); technological (human aur machine intelligence ka bhavi fusion hamari chetna ke mode ko plain biology se aage badhane ke liye set hai); sexual (kuch Hindu traditions anubhavatmak badlav ko utprerit karne ke liye sex ki taqat mein vishwas rakhte hain); aur interactive (dialectics) shamil ho sakte hain.

Jaise Hawaiian kahavat hai: ‘Saara gyaan ek hi school mein nahi seekha jata.’ Alag-alag sroton ka alag-alag uddeshyon ke liye mulya hota hai, lekin simply yeh samajhna ki woh vividh hain aur context ke aadhar par sambhavid roop se barabar maanyata aur taqat ke hain ek mahatvapurna antar-drishti hai. Aatmakankshi polymath ko apne gyaan ko multiple sroton se khinchne ki koshish karni chahiye aur aisa karne mein woh automatic roop se khud ko gyaan ke vividh 'fields' ke beech paayega.

---

### The Limitations of Mind (Dimaag Ki Seemaayein)

> Jise kabhi vastu-parak duniya (objective world) kaha jata tha woh ek prakar ka Rorschach inkblot hai, jismein har sanskriti, science aur religion ki har pranali, vyaktitva ka har prakar, ek aisa arth padhta hai jo dhabbe ke aakar aur rang se sirf door se hi nikalta hai.
> — Lewis Mumford

A.J. Jacobs, *Esquire* ke editor, ek swayam-swikar kiye gaye human guinea pig hain. Apni zindagi ke pehluon ko sudharne ke ek mission par, unhone khud ko atyadhik jeevan-shaili ke prayogon (extreme lifestyle experiments) ki ek shrinkhla se guzara jismein apni zindagi ko India mein ek team ko outsource karne, ek saal poori tarah se Bible ke anusar jeene, atyadhik sharirik poornata (bodily perfection) ka peecha karne, ek global family reunion aayojit karne aur Founding Fathers ke sabhi 110 samajik kanoonon ka palan karne ke daur shamil hain. Unhone har anubhav par ek kitaab likhi hai. Ek din, bauddhika roop se nishkriya (inert) mahsoos karte hue, unhone A se Z tak poori *Encyclopaedia Britannica* (lagbhag 44 million words!) padhne mein ek saal bitakar 'know-it-all' banne ki koshish karne ka faisla kiya. Task poora karne par, woh ek a-pratyashit (unexpected) realization par aaye: ‘Main har cheez ke baare mein kaafi kam nishchayatmak (less conclusive) ho gaya hoon,’ unhone kaha. ‘Sirf ek cheez hai jiske baare mein mujhe yakin hai, aur woh yeh hai ki nishchitata (certainty) ek khatarnak cheez hai. Hum sabhi ko zyada ittefaq se (in probabilities) baat karne ki zaroorat hai. Humein apne gyaan ki seemaon ko swikar karne ki zaroorat hai.’ Jacobs ka nishkarsh humein ek sadiyon purani samajh ki yaad dilata hai.

Itihaas ke sabse samajhdar dimaag hamesha is baat par zor dene ke liye peeda mein rahe hain ki ek insaani dimaag ek hi jeevan mein kitna samajh sakta hai uski seemaon ko samajhna kitna mahatvapurna hai. Third-century ke Chinese sage Ko Hung ne ghoshna ki ki ‘jo koi jaanta hai woh uski tulna mein bahut kam hai jo woh nahi jaanta. Cheezon ki ek badi vividhta hai.’ Ali ibn Abi Talib, Prophet Muhammad ke cousin aur Islamic Empire ke chauthe Caliph, ne jo kaha woh lagbhag ek samaan tha: ‘sach mein padha-likha aadmi woh hai jo samajhta hai ki jo woh jaanta hai woh uski tulna mein bahut kam hai jo woh nahi jaanta.’

Aur Chinese philosopher Confucius ke nishkarshon ke beech ke stark antar ka kya, jinhone kaha tha ki ‘asli gyaan apni agyaanata ki hadd ko jaan-na hai’; fifteenth-century ke German philosopher Nicolas of Cusa, jinhone kaha tha ki ‘ek aadmi apni agyaanata ko jitna behtar jaan paaye ga, uski padhai utni hi badi hogi’; Indian philosopher Swami Ramdas, jinhone kaha ‘jab aap jaante hain ki aap kuch nahi jaante, tab aap sab kuch jaante hain.’ Woh sabhi saaf taur par Taoist Lao Tzu ki ghoshna ko dohraate hain ki ‘samajhdar aadmi woh hai, jo jaanta hai ki woh kya nahi jaanta.’

Is namra karne wale sach (humbling fact) ki ek asli pehchan polymath ka aavashyak nishana hai. Professor David E. Cooper, *World Philosophies* ke lekhak jismein unhone poore itihaas mein duniya ki kai (agar sabhi nahi) darshanik paramparaon ka adhyayan kiya, us mahatvapurna realization ki baat karte hain jo multiple philosophical perspectives ki khoj se aati hai.

> Itihaas mein kai perspectives ko explore karne ka ek gun namrata (humility) ka sabak hai jo yeh sikhata hai. Inki agyaanata logon ko yeh sochne deti hai ki woh kisi cheez ko sochne wale pehle vyakti hain, jabki vaastav mein sadiyon pehle iska andaza laga liya gaya tha.

Vaastavikta ko samajhne mein hamare dimaag ki seemitata (limitedness) sirf Eastern aur pre-modern philosophers ka tarka nahi hai. Modern neuroscience zahir karti hai ki insaani dimaag sirf hamari indriya dharanaon (sense perceptions) ke anusar vaastavikta ko pakadne mein saksham hai, jinaki vaastav mein electromagnetic spectrum ke ek-dasvein trillinth tak hi pahonch hai (hum sirf light rays dekhte hain, aur gamma, X-ray, radio, Wi-Fi ityaadi jaise doosri rays ko capture karne ke liye machines ki zaroorat hoti hai). Jaise bestselling neuroscientist David Eagleman pushti karte hain, ‘Vaastavikta ka hamara anubhav hamari biology dwara sankuchit hai. . . hamare dimaag duniya ka bas ek chhota sa hissa sample kar rahe hain.’ Doosre jaanwar vaastavikta ke un tukdon ko dekh sakte hain jo hum nahi dekh sakte (saanp infrared dekhte hain aur madhumakhiyaan ultraviolet, udaharan ke liye). Insaani dimaag ka apna *umwelt* (German shabd 'aaspas' ke liye jise vaigyanik is context mein upayog karte hain) hai, jo ek sath ashadharan roop se antar-drishti-purna aur niraashajanak roop se seemit hai.

Jabki kuch logon ke liye yeh haqeeqat gyaan ki khoj ko ek niraashajanak aur vyarth abhyas banati hai, polymath ke liye, jo khule dimaag wala hai, yeh multiple worlds ke astitva ke sabhoot ke roop mein kaam karti hai, jinmein se har ek ko samjha aur ekrat kiya jana hai. Woh samajhta hai ki kaise manav soch aur gyaan ka raasta aksar itna ek-aayami aur un kai cognitive aur cultural biases se dhak-ka hua hai jo hamari soch ko prabhavit (ya seemit) karte hain. Yeh ek shaktishali, anant curiosity ko utprerit karta hai, jo polymath ko ek sachha talash-gar (true seeker) banata hai.

---

### Multi-Perspectivism (Bahu-Drishtikon-Vaad)

Polymaths is tarah zyada vastu-parak (objective) banne ki talash karte hain — yaani, apne *umwelt* ka vistar karne ke liye. Woh bauddhika jaanch ke ek aise tareeqe mein shamil hote hain jismein multiple perspectives ko khojna, aage badhana, anubhav karna aur jaan-na shamil hai aur phir unhe ek aise tareeqe se synthesise (fuse aur context mein rakhna) karna shamil hai jo duniya ki ek zyada poori, zyada nishpakshtam tasveer ki anumati deta hai. Science ke philosopher E.O. Wilson ise vaastavikta ko ughadne ki sabse behtareen methodology ke roop mein sujhalte hain:

> Sirf [disciplinary] boundaries ke aar-paar pravahita (fluency) duniya ka ek saaf drishya pradan karegi jaisa ki yeh vaastav mein hai, na ki ideologies aur religious dogmas ke lens ke zariya dekha gaya ya tatkaalik zaroorat ke prati alp-drishti (myopic) prati-kriya dwara aadeshit. . . . Ek santulit drishtikon disciplines ka tukdon mein adhyayan karke haasil nahi kiya ja sakta balki unke beech consilience (mel) ki khoj ke zariya haasil kiya ja sakta hai.

Gyaan aur anubhav ke jitne zyada kshetron ko hum ik इकट्ठा kar sakte hain aur apne repertoire mein jod sakte hain, utne hi zyada perspectives hum ughad sakte hain aur duniya ke apne khud ke zyada sarv-gun-sampanna, zyada samriddh drishtikon ko banane (ya kam se kam soochit karne) ke liye synthesise kar sakte hain. Yeh epistemic unity hi hai jo kisi ki sthiti ko objectivity ke ek uche status tak pahonchne ki anumati deti hai. Yeh na sirf asli hamdardi aur samajh ke liye pravritti ko badhati hai (aaj ke bahuvadi, aapas mein jude, global samaj mein jiski bahut zaroorat hai) balki ek prakar ki samajik aur bauddhika aazadi ke liye bhi. Yeh manav sthiti ki ek zyada samagra samajh haasil karne ka, ya sansehp mein, ek asli shiksha paane ka ek tareeqa hai.

Yeh saaf lagta hai, lekin hairani ki baat hai ki bahut kam log iska abhyas karte hain — sivaay un kuch sach mein polymathic minds wale logon ke. Yeh wahi hai jahan sabse bade intellectuals bhi (ya mujhe kehna chahiye, vishesh roop se) chhoot jaate hain. Phir bhi ise Ancient India mein samjha gaya tha jahan Jainist philosophers ne soch ke ek mode ko viksit kiya jise *Anekantavada* kaha jata tha, jisne alag-alag perspectives ke astitva, sarahana aur sambhavid maanyata ko padhaya. Prasiddh ‘haathi aur andhe aadmi’ (elephant and the blind men) ki taamsheel (analogy) aksar ise pradarshit karne ke liye di jaati hai, lekin hum ek aur ka upayog kar sakte hain jo hamare samay se mel khaye: ‘shehar’ aur iske ‘nivaasi.’

Twenty-First-Century London jaise ek dynamic, multi-dimensional shehar par vichar karein. Iske nivaasi unke samajik, ethnic, peshevar, bhaugolik aur shaikshanik background ke sandarbh mein alag-alag hain. In karakon par nirbhar karte hue, har nivaasi ka shehar ka apna anubhav hota hai. London khud ek nishchit vaastavikta rakhta hai, lekin logon ki iski dharana unke iske anokhe anubhavon ke aadhar par alag-alag hoti hai. Is tarah shehar ka ek zyada vividh anubhav nivaasi ko iski vaastavikta ke zyada paas layega, jabki ek ek-aayami anubhav kisi bhi objectivity ka daava nahi kar sakta.

Jaisa ki gyaan itna anant roop se vyapak hai, koi bhi door se intelligent aur chatur dimaag apni hypothesis ke paksh mein ek aakarshak tarka rachte mein saksham hai. Chahe kitna bhi ‘gyaani’ ho, koi bhi chaturai se mutthi bhar ‘tathyon’ ya raayon ko chun sakta hai — hamesha manav gyaan ke vishaal samundar mein ek boond — aur manav dimaag ki zaroori roop se aanshik, seemit rationality dwara sahayata praapt (rationality khud vyaktigat intuition ki ek chhalang mein nihit hai) unhe behadd vishwasniya dhang se prastut kar sakta hai.

Lekin zindagi nuanced hai — agar sabhi cheezein sach mein vichar ki jaayein toh zyadatar raayein aur faisle pahonchna lagbhag asambhav hoga. Aur jaisa ki zindagi itni chhoti hai, insaan (ego aur survival instinct dwara) aam taur par logon, sthitiyon aur gyaan ko saral banane, stereotype karne, shrenibaddh karne, maan lene aur judge karne ke liye majboor mahsoos karta hai. Hamare paas apne faisle lene aur apni raayein rachne ke liye sakht time limits hain. Yeh ek kathin sthiti (predicament) hai. Yahi wajah hai ki Leonardo ne kaha tha ‘sabse badi dhokhadhari jisse aadmi peedit hote hain woh unki apni raayein hain.’ Is tarah kai, agar zyadatar nahi, polymaths ne woh liya jise philosopher David E. Cooper ‘syncretic’ attitude ke roop mein rekhankit karte hain: yeh vishwas ki ‘sach ka raasta kai, kai perspectives ko ek sath ik इकट्ठा karna hai, jinse ek aam core ubhrega, jahan "sach" nihit hai.’

Is baat ko dhyan mein rakhte hue, aaram ke ilaqon (comfort zones) mein apariharyata roop se basne se pehle, jitna ho sake lambe samay tak sarv-gun-sampanna bane rehna mahatvapurna hai. Raay-on ki tarah hi, kisi maamle par ek pakka judgment banane se pehle jitna ho sake lambe samay tak khule dimaag wala bane rehna mahatvapurna hai. Jaise aapke faisle behtar soochit hote hain (aur isliye sach ke zyada paas) agar ek khule dimaag aur gyaan, vichar aur multiple factors ke prabhavshali upayog ke sath kiye jaayein, aapke ultimate astitva mein zyada tatva hoga aur aapki raayein zyada antar-drishti-purna hongi agar aapke jeevan ke anubhav (aur isliye gyaan) paryapt roop se vividh rahe hon. Is tarah, polymathy ki khoj sambhavid roop se hamare cognitive biases ko kam karne ka sabse prabhavshali tareeqa hai.

Yahi wajah hai ki do namra polymaths ke beech ek debate do ego-driven specialists ke beech ki tulna mein zyada faldaayi hone ki sambhavna hai. Udaharan ke liye, science vs. religion, capitalism vs. socialism, liberalism vs. conservatism aur ityaadi par aaj ki badi debates aapas ki samajh ki taraf thodi pragati karti hain kyunki har pratiyogita apne khud ke kshetra mein apni visheshata par nirbhar karti hai opponent par iski samajh ki kami ka aarop lagane ke liye. Yahi wajah hai ki Marilyn vos Savant, jinhe duniya ke sabse intelligent dimaagon mein se ek maana jata hai, sujhati hain: ‘polymathy ke bina, hum mulyankan ke paryapt saadhanon ke bina doosron ke prabhavon, judgments aur vishwason ko swikar karne ke liye shraapit hain.’ Is tarah debaters do poori tarah se alag wavelengths par shamil ho rahe hain, kyunki unki ruchi apni sthiti ko chunauti dene ke bajaye use banaye rakhne mein hai (asal mein maujooda paradigm mein sabse aasan rozi-roti perspectives ko explore karne ke bajaye unka bachao karne se banti hai). Yeh unhe alag tarah se sochne par majboor karta hai.

Yahi wajah hai ki Edward de Bono ki Parallel Thinking method (unki bestselling book *Six Thinking Hats* dwara popular banayi gayi), jo logon ke ek group ko samuhik roop se multiple perspectives mein rehne ke liye encourage karti hai, creative problem solving aur dispute resolution ke maamlon mein itni prabhavshali rahi hai. Socrates ke dialectic method ki tarah, yeh samuhik jaanch ki prakriya se egos ko baahar nikalati hai.

---

### Open-Mindedness (Khula-Dimaag-Pan)

> Seekhna badi aatmaon ko badhata hai.
> — Namibian proverb

Ek sach mein curious mind hamesha ek khula dimaag hota hai. Zyadatar log zindagi mein shuruat mein hi ek vishesh raay banate hain ya kuch faisle lete hain, jismein kisi kaaran se (rational se zyada emotional) woh ek majboot vishwas viksit karte hain. Is ‘take’ ne kamyabi ya ek vishesh sthiti ya pratishtha dilayi ho sakti hai, aur is par U-turn ya 90° turn lena aksar professional suicide ya, sabse badtar, ego par ek chot ka matlab ho sakta hai. Yeh pravritti kam se kam aanshik roop se samjhati hai ki professional aur intellectual specialisation status quo kyun bani hui hai.

Aisa band-dimaag-pan, ya naye ideas par vichar karne ki an-ichha, dimaag ke aspashtata (ambiguity) ke liye praakritik na-pasandgi mein paida hoti hai, jise woh aksar explore karne ke bajaye andhe dhang se khatam karne ki talash karta hai. Aspashtata kuch logon ke liye curiosity ko utprerit kar sakti hai lekin doosre jo tatkaalik nishchitata ko pasand karte hain jaanch ki prakriya ko poori tarah se rok dete hain. Zyadatar log aage ki jaankari ki prakriya aur judgment ko khatam karne ke liye tatkaalik roop se ek jawab ki maang karte hain, bhale hi woh jawab sahi ya sabse behtareen jawab na ho. Cognitive scientists ke liye is prakriya ko Need for Cognitive Closure (NFCC) ke roop mein jaana jata hai.

Iske alawa, dimaag automatic cognitive procedure dwara psychological compartmentalisation ko sunishchit karne ke liye viksit hua hai jise schematic organisation ke roop mein jaana jata hai. Yeh apne aaspas ke vatavaran mein dhancha dhoondhne ke liye concepts ko ek sath samoohit karke vishleshan se kriya tak tezi se badhne ka ek kushal tareeqa hai. Jabki yeh schemata ek point par basic survival ke liye mahatvapurna the, woh modern duniya mein vyapak aur rachnatmak roop se sochne ki kisi ki kshamta ko rokte hain. Hum dharanaon par itni aasaani se sawaal nahi uthate; hum nishkarshon par tezi se koodte hain aur kisi vishesh sthiti ke baare mein sochne ke vaikalpik tareeqon mein rukawatein paida karte hain.

Desmond Morris, manav aur jaanwar ke vyavhaar ke observer, ne dekha ki insaano ko neophilic aur neophobic personalities mein alag kiya ja sakta hai. Pehle wale naye se pyaar karte hain aur doosre wale isse darte hain. Yeh sabse zyada khule dimaag wale, neophilic thinkers hain jo polymaths mein viksit hote hain kyunki woh wahi hain jo kisi an-khoje sawaal par ek rash, un-soochit judgment banane se pehle use poori tarah se explore karne ke liye majboor mahsoos karte hain. Polymath ke vyaktitva ka vishleshan karne wale psychologists Five Factor Model ke ‘openness’ facet par ek ucha score dekhenge.

Khula-dimaag-pan bahut aksar a-nirnayakta (indecisiveness) ke roop mein kharij kar diya jata hai. Log aadatand maan lete hain ki khule-dimaag-pan ka matlab zoridaar nishkarsh nikalne se hichkichahat ya parhez hai. Lekin sach mein curious mind ke liye, khula-dimaag-pan simply ek swabhav hai, jaanch ka ek vyavasthit tareeqa, zindagi ke prati ek bauddhika drishtikon bajaye iske ki anivaarya ulhan aur a-nishchayatakta ki sthiti ho. Asal mein, khula-dimaag-pan aksar zyada spashtata pradan kar sakta hai agar koi sirf dhairya se sach ka peecha karne ke liye taiyar ho. Yeh drishtikon eleventh-century ke Persian polymath Al-Ghazali dwara sabse behtareen dhang se vyakt kiya gaya tha jab unhone apni bauddhika (aur adhyatmik) odyssey ka chart banaya:

> Mujhe Yakeen ke sath pata tha ki koi bhi kisi bhi science mein jo kharab hai use tab tak nahi pehchan sakta jab tak ki uske paas us science ke sabse door ke ilaqon ki aisi pakad na ho ki woh us science ke siddhanton mein nipun sabse padhe-likhe logon ke barabar ho; phir use usse bhi aage nikalna chahiye aur usse bhi badi uanchai paani chahiye taaki woh un jatil gehraiyon se jagruk ho jaye jo science ke swikrut master ki samajh se pare rahi hain. Tab, aur tabhi yeh sambhav hoga ki jis kharabi ka woh aarop lagata hai woh sach mein aisi hi dekhi jayegi.

Isi tarah ke andaaz mein, Socratic Dialogues ka aadhar yeh tha ki na toh unhone aur na hi unke interlocutor ne yeh maana ki woh kuch jaante hain — yeh zaroori roop se ek khule dimaag wali, ego-free bauddhika dialectic thi jo charcha ke sabhi vishayon ko langh gayi, aur aisa karte hue, samajh ke un levels tak pahonchi jahan doosre nahi pahonch sake. ‘Akeli asli samajhdari yeh jaan-ne mein hai ki aap kuch nahi jaante,’ unhone zor diya.

Lekin khule dimaag wala bane rehna aasan nahi hai. Jaise French Renaissance essayist aur polymath Michel de Montaigne ne kaha: ‘Sach mein Aristotle ki tarah baat karna aur Caesar ki tarah jeena Socrates ki tarah baat karne aur Socrates ki tarah jeene dono ki tulna mein kahin zyada aasan hai.’ Yaani, is meethe santosh mein aaram karna kahin zyada aasan hai ki koi sach ke kabze mein hai bajaye iske ki sach ki khoj mein lagatar mehnat ki jaye, yeh pehchante hue ki koi ek talash-gar hai aur anishchit kal tak rahega.

---

### Knowledge for Life (Jeevan Ke Liye Gyaan)

> Ek manav jeev ko diaper badalne, hamle ki yojana banane, suar kaatne, imarat design karne, jahaz chalane, sonnet likhne, accounts balance karne, deewar banane, haddi set karne, marte hue ko dhasa dene, aadesh lene, aadesh dene, sahyog karne, akele kaam karne, equation solve karne, naye prashn ka vishleshan karne, khaad phenkne, computer program karne, swadishth khana banane, kushal roop se ladne, shaan se marne mein saksham hona chahiye. Specialisation kide-makodon ke liye hai.
> — Robert A. Heinlein

Alag-alag samajon ke paas, is baat par nirbhar karte hue ki unhone kya mahsoos kiya ki zindagi ka uddeshya mool roop se kya tha, is baat ki ek alag dharana rahi hai ki ‘upayogi’ gyaan kya hai. Confucians ne gyaan haasil karne ko naitik charitra (moral character) ko sudharne ke saadhan ke roop mein dekha; Hindus aur Muslims ne paramparik roop se ise Divine ko jaan-ne ke saadhan ke roop mein dekha; prehistoric hunter-gatherers ne ise ek kathin, vipreet vatavaran mein survive karne ke saadhan ke roop mein dekha. Modern manav ke liye, gyaan ko strategic aur operational mein baanta ja sakta hai. Pehla woh gyaan hai jo ultimate objective ki taraf sabse behtareen dhang se aage badhne ke liye zaroori hai, woh chahe jo bhi ho. Yeh badi tasveer ko samajhna aur hamari lives ko context mein rakhna hai — cosmos mein hamari jagah, hamari lives par macro-level decisions aur policies ka prabhav, hamara adhyatmik aur naitik astitva, hamare career objectives, family planning aur ityaadi. Polymaths ke liye, yeh inmein se kai ek sath honge.

Baad wala (operational) us gyaan ko sandarbhit karta hai jo ek practical, rozmarra ke level par ek achhi tarah se vichar ki gayi zindagi jeene ke liye zaroori hai. Yeh ek functional gyaan hai, ek aisi cheez jo aamtaur par 'practical generalist' ke paas hoti hai ya uske dwara khoji jaati hai, jo un sabhi buniyaadi karakon ki pehchan karta hai jo uski zindagi ko prabhavit kar rahe honge, chahe kitne bhi vyapak hon, aur unse nipatne mein saksham hone ke liye khud ko usi anusar shikshit karta hai. Yeh us prakar ka hai jise *The Knowledge: How to Rebuild our World from Scratch* aur *The New York Times Practical Guide to Practically Everything* jaisi kitaabon mein rekhankit kiya gaya hai.

Strategic aur operational dono gyaan ke prabhavshali arjan ke liye jo zaroori hai woh hai alert hone ki ek chetan sthiti (conscious state of alertness); nishkriyata (passiveness), santosh (complacency) aur udaseenata (apathy) ke trance se ek poori cognitive sharpness ki sthiti mein jaagna. Yeh optimal dimaag kshamta ka upayog hai, yaani, right aur left brain hemispheres ke sath-sath conscious aur unconscious systems ka prabhavshali synchronicity.

Ek shuruat ke liye, general knowledge ko sirf aaramdayak trivia ke roop mein nahi dekha jana chahiye. Sahi general knowledge — jiska vyakti ke liye ek practical mulya (utility) hai aur uske liye vyaktigat hai — rozmarra ki zindagi ke sudhar ke liye aavashyak hai, aur vaastav mein ek tezi se jatil hoti duniya mein vyapak survival ke liye aksar ek zaroorat hai. Beshak, hamare control se baahar kai cheezein hain chahe hum kitna bhi ek enquiring mind ka abhyas karein aur ek aatm-nirbhar jeevan-shaili ko niyukt karne ki talash karein. Hum apni lives ke aaspas ki har cheez ke baare mein sach tak kabhi nahi pahonch paayenge, na hi hum un sabhi auzaron ko produce ya manufacture karne mein saksham honge jinaki humein yatra ke dauran zaroorat hogi, jaise Ibn Khaldun ne humein unke fourteenth-century masterpiece, *Muqaddimah* mein yaad dilaya tha.

Prakriti ka prabhav, ek ke liye, ek aisi cheez hai jo lagbhag poori tarah se hamare control se baahar hai. Interdependence ki ek degree hamesha apariharyata hoti hai aur kuch contexts mein, vaanchhnaiya (desirable) hoti hai. Halanki, hum mein se har ek kam se kam apni lives ke buniyaadi pehluon ke apne gyaan ko badha sakta hai; jis tareeqe se sangathan, prakriti, machines, arthvyavasthaayein, imaratein aur aam taur par log kaam karte hain aur humein prabhavit karte hain, udaharan ke liye, mahatvapurna jaankari hai. Isse bhi behtar, humein yeh samajhna chahiye ki yeh hamari apni lives mein kaise fit baithte hain aur prabhavit karte hain, chahe kitne bhi seedhe ya apraatyaksh tareeqe se ho. Iska matlab hai ki economics, politics, science, philosophy, psychology, religion, history aur mathematics jaise vividh kshetron ka hamara gyaan hamesha badhta rehna chahiye. Hamare paas jitna zyada gyaan hoga, aur jitna behtar hum ise apni vyaktigat zarooraton ke anusar ek sath lane aur laagu karne mein saksham honge, hamare chunav aur faisle utne hi behtar soochit honge (chahe rationally kiye gaye hon ya instinctively).

Lekin jaise Stephen Wolfram, polymath aur *Mathematica* ke designer, kehte hain, ‘aapko kisi cheez ke baare mein seekhne ke liye ek kaaran ki zaroorat hoti hai.’ Aksar, kaaran simply zindagi hai; ya astitva bhi. Do painkillers ke beech tatva mein antar, ya curry ki shuruat, aawaas par sarkar ki neeti ya East Africa ki sexual philosophy jaise saral tathya, udaharan ke liye, kisi ke mood, aamdani, swasthya, rishton aur aam pradarshan mein sudhar kar sakte hain. Dusri taraf, santosh aur agyaanata hamari zindagi ki quality ko kam karte hain aur control mein rehne ki hamari kshamta ko rokate hain. Agyaanata ek kalpanik anand (mythical bliss) hai.

Michel de Montaigne ne kaha, ‘akela gyaan jise main dhoondhta hoon woh hai jo mujhe batata hai ki khud ko kaise jaan-na hai, aur mujhe sikhata hai ki achhi tarah se kaise marna hai aur achhi tarah se kaise jeena hai.’ Aur yeh mindset wahi hai jisne unhe ek polymath banaya. Duniya ke woh hisse jo hamari lives ko prabhavit kar sakte hain aur karte hain saaf taur par multiple aur vividh hain. Isliye hamara gyaan bhi aisa hi hona chahiye. Car chalana, khana banana, computer operate karna, apne pyaron se pyaar karna — yeh sabhi rozmarra ke prayas hain jo bahut alag hain, jinhe alag-alag prakar ke gyaan, cognitive skills aur intelligence domains ki zaroorat hoti hai — sabhi hamari lives mein ek din banane ke liye ekrat ho jaate hain. Phir bhi humein unhe seekhne mein koi jhijhak nahi hoti kyunki hum swikar karte hain ki woh hamare survival ko suvidhajanak banate hain aur humein ek behtar din jeene mein saksham banate hain. Phir, academic aur professional 'fields' ke beech badhna vivadaaspad kyun dekha jata hai?

Aavashyak gyaan aur cognitive skills ke is chhote portfolio ko duniya ke un doosre pehluon ko shamil karne ke liye vistarit kiya jana chahiye jo aamtaur par hamari lives ko prabhavit karte hain (ya kisi point par prabhavit karne ki sambhavna hai). Humein seekhna chahiye, udaharan ke liye (kam se kam basic) lawyer, accountant, doctor, handyman, computer engineer, entertainer, soldier ki skills kyunki legal, financial, medical, gharelu, technical aur survival chunautiyan modern life mein typical hain. Warna hum santusht ho jaate hain aur 'experts' rakhne ke luxuries par nirbhar karte hain, sabse basic samasyaon ke liye bhi fuzool mein unke reham par jeete hain. Yeh mehenga sabit ho sakta hai, financial sense mein aur doosri tarah se bhi. Log ab iske prati jaag rahe honge: *Lifehacker* jaise blogs ki badhti popularity, jiska uddeshya practical maamlon ki ek vyapak range par rozmarra ke gyaan ko share karna hai, logon dwara jeevan ke liye apne gyaan ko badhane ki ek bhookh ko pradarshit karti hai.

## Intelligence (BUDDHIMATTA / CHATURTA)

> Ek pratibhashali vyakti kabhi bhi sirf ek kshetra mein pratibhashali nahi hota.
> — Hamlet Isakhanli

William Sidis America ke sabse jaane-maane child prodigies mein se ek the. Unke baare mein kaha jata hai ki unka IQ daraj itihaas mein sabse zyada tha — woh reportedly 18 mahine ki umar mein *New York Times* padh sakte the aur 8 saal ke hone tak unhone khud ko aath bhashayein (Latin, Greek, French, Russian, German, Hebrew, Turkish aur Armenian) sikhaye thin. Bachpan mein unki ashadharan intelligence ke kaaran, unke pita ne Harvard University mein unke admission ke liye apply kiya. Yadyapi shuruat mein unki umar ke aadhar par kharij kar diya gaya tha, unhe do saal baad 11 saal ki umar mein swikar kar liya gaya. Unhone 16 saal ki umar mein *summa cum laude* ke sath graduation kiya, yahan tak ki advanced mathematics par lectures diye aur phir Harvard Law School chale gaye. Unhone baad mein cosmology (space ke doosre ilaqon mein thermodynamics ke liye vaikalpik siddhanton ka sujhaav dete hue), Native American history aur anthropology (unki *The Tribes and the States* mein Americas ka 100,000 saal ka itihaas), philology (unhone 'Vandergood' naam ki ek bhasha banayi) aur transportation systems (unhone streetcar transfers par ek treatise likha) jaise vividh vishayon par likha. Woh ek popular celebrity bane, lekin lagatar ekant (seclusion) aur privacy ki talash karte rahe. Woh 52 saal ki umar mein mar gaye lekin unke paas kai additional fields ko aage badhane ka potential aur intention dono tha.

Ashadharan intelligence ek genius ka trademark hai; aur jaise Sidis ka case pradarshit karta hai, asli genius aseemit hota hai. Otago University ke psychologist Geoffrey White ne khoja ki ‘typical genius interests ki range mein aur . . . ability ki range mein typical college graduate ko peeche chhod deta hai.’ Samaj ne Sidis ko dhabbe mein baantne ki koshish ki; pehle ek mathematician ke roop mein, phir ek lawyer ke roop mein, phir ek linguist ke roop mein, phir ek historian ke roop mein — aur har baar unhone baahar nikalne aur doosre kshetra ko aage badhane ka prayas kiya. Parinaamswarup, unhe ek eccentric kaha gaya. Sach mein, Sidis ek samajik roop se utpeedit polymath the. Unki kahani dikhati hai ki IQ sort ki paramparik intelligence bhi — woh type jisse hum aamtaur par, lekin kabhi-kabhi galat roop se, geniuses ko jodte hain — polymathise karne ki pravritti ke sath ek sambandh (correlation) rakhti hai.

Is baat ki pushti American psychologist Keith Simonton dwara ki jaati hai, jo zor dete hain ki ek ashadharan roop se uchi intelligence polymath se majboot roop se judi hoti hai: ‘IQ na sirf badhti hui shohrat se juda hai, balki behtar versatility jaise assets se bhi juda hai. Jitna ucha IQ hoga, utne hi zyada domains mein ek vyakti kamyab ho sakta hai.’ Kaaran, woh kehte hain, yeh hai ki ‘zyada bauddhika resources (intellectual wherewithal) ke sath, woh dilettante ke kharab karne wale bhatkaav ka risk uthaye bina zyada enterprises mein shamil ho sakte the.’ Yaani, behtar bauddhika kshamta wala vyakti kam intelligence wale vyakti ki tulna mein apne bauddhika resources ko zyada domains mein zyada udaarta se baantne mein saksham hota hai, jo isliye har ek khoj ke liye intelligence ke seemit aavantan (limited allocation) ke kaaran multiple fields mein koi mahatvapurna yogdan dene ki kam sambhavna rakhta hai. Marilyn vos Savant, jo ek samay duniya ke sabse uche IQ ki dharak thin, sehamat hain: ‘Mujhe lagta hai ki ek general intelligence versatility se kaafi hadd tak judi hai.’

King’s College London mein behavioural genetics ke professor Robert Plomin samjhate hain ki aisa kyun ho sakta hai: ‘Agar aap zyada smart hain, toh aap zyada strategically sochte hain, role chahe jo bhi ho,’ woh kehte hain. ‘Idea yeh hai ki, agar aap bahut smart hain, toh aap apne cards ko behtar khelte hain.’ Buzan ki *Book of Genius* ke anusar, jismein ‘genius guru’ un visheshataon ka mulyankan karta hai jinhe woh ab tak ke top 100 sabse bade geniuses maanta hai, polymathy aur genius ke beech ka sambandh chonkane wala hai. Unke top 20 geniuses sabhi ke paas 90 percent se upar polymathy scores hain; aur iske alawa 90 percent se upar polymathy wale zyadatar logo ke paas 90 percent se upar IQ bhi hai. Yeh saaf nahi hai ki unhone yeh ganana kaise ki, lekin mukhya nishkarsh yeh hai ki ek majboot sambandh hai.

Lekin curiosity ki tarah, intelligence polymathy ko suvidhajanak bana sakti hai ya rok sakti hai, is baat par nirbhar karte hue ki yeh intellect ko lambvat (vertically) modti hai ya kshitij (horizontally). Ek uchi intelligence, udaharan ke liye, kisi ko ek vishesh kshetra mein qaid kar sakti hai agar intelligence ka upayog explore karne aur jodna ke bajaye nishkarsh nikalne aur vighatit karne ke liye kiya jata hai. Aaj ke samaj mein, yeh wahi disha hai jise sabse zyada encourage kiya jata hai. Intelligence sach ki khoj ke liye utni hi prabhavshali hai jitni jhooth ka bachao karne ke liye. Yeh dimaag ko chouda karne ke liye utni hi prabhavshali hai jitni ek band dimaag ko majboot karne ke liye. Yadyapi praakritik jhukav intelligence ka upayog multiple domains mein kiye jaane ke liye hai — hum yeh jaante hain kyunki Sidis jaise child prodigies ne hamesha multiple fields mein brilliance pradarshit ki hai agar unhe sirf anumati di gayi thi — ise maujooda education aur professional systems mein encourage nahi kiya jata hai.

---

### General vs. Multiple Intelligences (Aam Ya Bahu-Buddhi)

> Manav buddhi bahumukhi aur bahu-aayami hai — yeh kai roopon mein aati hai.
> — Ken Robinson

Anna Maria van Schurman ashadharan roop se intelligent thin. Mardon ki duniya mein ek mahila, woh 18th-century Netherlands mein apne mukhya karya *Whether a Christian Woman Should Be Educated and Other Writings from her Intellectual Circle* ke liye jaane jaati thin, jisne unhe apne samay ki pramukh feminist intellectuals mein se ek bana diya.

Unhone University of Utrecht se law mein degree ke sath graduation kiya (woh university mein akele mahila student thin aur parde ke peeche se lectures mein shamil hoti thin). Ek theologian ke roop mein, unhone *De vitae humanae termino* samet kai works produce kiye, manav jeevan ke ant mein God aur physician ki kramashah bhoomikaon par, aur naye ubharte hue Labadie Protestant movement ke liye ek pramukh member aur writer banin.

Schurman ek ashadharan roop se multi-talented artist bhi thin — ek engraver, glassmaker, sculptor (wax, wood aur ivory), portrait painter aur calligrapher, unke kai works aaj bhi astitva mein hain. Unke paas bhashaon ke liye bhi ek shauk tha aur woh 14 bhashaon mein nipun maani jaati thin, jinmein Latin, Greek, Hebrew, Arabic, Syriac, Aramaic aur Ethiopian shamil hain. Unhone kai bhashaon mein kavitaayein bhi likhin, aur unka sangrah *Opuscula hebraea, graeca, latina, gallica, prosaica et metrica* (‘Little works in Hebrew, Greek, Latin and French, prose and poetry’) us samay ke scholars dwara vyapak roop se padha gaya tha.

Schurman saaf taur par multiple talents wali mahila thin. Lekin kya yeh ek ashadharan roop se uchi general intelligence se nikale the, ya unke paas simply un kshetron se judi kuch intelligences thin jinmein unhone shreshthta haasil ki? Ek lambi bahas ki kya intelligence sabhi kshetron (ya cognitive tasks) ke liye aam hai ya kya intelligence ke multiple types hain, har ek alag-alag cognitive abilities ka pratinidhitv karta hai, ne psychologists ko dashakon se ghere rakha hai.

Theory of General Intelligence sabse pehle early twentieth century mein aaya, jise English psychologist Charles Spearman dwara viksit kiya gaya tha, jinhone apne research se dekha ki school ke vishayon mein bachhon ki performance ratings jo asambandhit lagti thin, positively correlated thin. Unhone tarka diya ki in correlations ne ek aantarik general cognitive ability ke prabhav ko darshaya jo sabhi prakar ke mental tests par performance mein pravesh karti thi. Unhone nishkarsh nikala ki saari cognitive performance ko ek ekal (core) general ability factor (jise unhone *g* label diya) aur ek badi sankhya mein narrow task-specific ability factors ke sandarbh mein avadharnakrit (conceptualised) kiya ja sakta hai.

Is theory ko baad mein ek aise model mein viksit kiya gaya hai jo cognitive abilities ko ek teen-level hierarchy ke roop mein pratinidhitv karta hai, jahan hierarchy ke bottom par narrow factors ki ek badi sankhya hoti hai, intermediate level par broad, zyada general factors ka ek mutthi bhar hissa hota hai, aur apex par ek ekal factor, *g* factor hota hai, jo sabhi cognitive tasks ke liye aam variance ko pratinidhitv karta hai. Agar yeh theory sahi hai, toh yeh sujhatta hai ki polymaths aise vyakti hain jinke paas ek ashadharan general intelligence hai, jo badle mein unhe versatile hone aur vividh (theoretically sabhi) domains mein shreshthta haasil karne ki anumati deta hai, bhale hi unhe alag-alag cognitive abilities ki zaroorat ho. Iske alawa diversity khud kisi ki general intelligence ko badha sakti hai. University of Toronto mein ek haal hi ke adhyayan ke anusar, udaharan ke liye, drum lessons lene ke baad chhah-saal ke bachhon ke IQ test scores mein mahatvapurna sudhar hua.

1980s mein Howard Gardner dwara shuru ki gayi Theory of Multiple Intelligences (MI) ne intelligence par pehle ke narratives ko chunauti di. Gardner ne tarka diya ki ek sarv-samaveshi general intelligence hone ke bajaye, vaastav mein intelligence ke alag-alag types hain, jo sabhi ek vyakti ke paas alag-alag hadd tak hote hain. Unhone pehchana ki intelligence vividh roopon mein aa sakti hai, na ki sirf apne paramparik IQ roop mein, aur tarka diya ki kisi vyakti ki vividh abilities ko swikar aur celebrate kiya jana chahiye.

Unki multiple intelligences mein musical-rhythmic, visual-spatial, verbal-linguistic, logical-mathematical aur bodily-kinaesthetic shamil hain. Toh udaharan ke liye agar kisi ke paas high visual, musical, mathematical aur kinaesthetic intelligences hain, toh woh music, art, mathematics aur sports mein shreshthta haasil karne ke liye ek majboot sthiti mein hain — chahe ek sath hon ya ek ke baad ek. ‘[Polymathy] MI theory ke sath poori tarah se anukool hai,’ Gardner zor dete hain. ‘Kuch log kai fields mein talented honge, doosre itne bhagyashali nahi hain.’

Multiple intelligences hain ya nahi, mudda yeh hai ki intelligence ka upayog ya toh iske vividh roopon mein kiya jana chahiye ya vividh disciplines par laagu kiya jana chahiye. Mamla chahe jo bhi ho, jaise Ken Robinson kehte hain, ‘Intelligence hamari kai sanskritik dharanaon dwara vishwas kiye jaane ki tulna mein kaafi zyada samriddh, zyada vividh, zyada nuanced aur aakarshak hai.’

---

### Critical Thinking and Common Sense (Gambhir Soch Aur Aam Samajh)

Iran mein Shi'a Islamic tradition ke ek Grand Ayatollah, Muhammad al-Husaini Al Shirazi, 20th century ke sabse prachur lekhakon mein se ek bane (1200 se zyada works publish karte hue) aur unhone law, economics, theology, sociology, history, philosophy aur politics mein mahatvapurna yogdan diya. Koi information age mein aise kaarname ki kalpana kar sakta hai, lekin Shirazi ne internet ke aavishkar se theek pehle kaam kiya. Woh apni ungliyon par duniya ka gyaan hue bina vishayon ki itni vyapak range par kuch gambhir kehne mein kaise saksham the?

Al Shirazi ki critically sochne ki ashadharan kshamta ne har ek vishay par gehra gyaan rakhne ki zaroorat ko visthapit (superseded) kar diya. Critical thinking truth ko khojne aur dharanaon par sawaal uthane ke liye intelligence aur reason ka ek vyavasthit upayog hai. Yeh logic aur sabhoot ki kasauti par pehle se maujood tathyon ko parakhna hai. Curiosity aur creativity ki tarah, yeh ek aisa gun hai jo sabhi disciplines mein sarvabhoomik roop se laagu hota hai. Immanuel Kant ne tarka diya ki 'reason' ek ultimate value ke roop mein kaam karta hai jo disciplinary divisions ko langhta hai aur 'har cheez ka mulyankan karne ke liye aazad' hona chahiye.

Kant se do millennia pehle, Aristotle ne critical thinking ko polymath ka sabse shaktishali feature ke roop mein ishara kiya tha. Unhone adhyayan ke kisi diye gaye kshetra mein do prakar ki kushalta ke beech ke antar par zor diya; ek 'vishay ka vaigyanik gyaan' hona aur doosra 'iske sath ek shaikshanik parichay' hona. Jabki pehla saaf hai — aur jiske liye curiosity aur gyaan ka arjan zaroori hai — doosra 'upayog kiye gaye tareeqe ke achhe ya bure hone ke baare mein ek uchit achanak judgment banane ki kshamta' ya ek prakar ki common sense ko sandarbhit karta hai jo sabhi kshetron mein laagu ho sakti hai.

Yeh common sense critical thinking ka saar hai. Lekin agar us tareeqe se upayog na kiya jaye jaisa Aristotle ne kalpana ki thi, toh critical thinking drishtikon reductionist tareeqon ki taraf bhi le ja sakta hai aur lagatar specialisation ka kaaran ban sakta hai. Toh unhone nishkarsh nikala ki 'sarvabhoomik shiksha ke aadmi' (man of universal education) ki zaroorat hai — jo type 'gyaan ki sabhi ya lagbhag sabhi shakhaon mein critical' ho. Woh us generalist ko sandarbhit karte hain jo gyaan ki multiple fields ki samajh mein yogdan dene ke liye gehre gyaan se zyada ek sarvabhoomik roop se laagu hone wali critical thinking par nirbhar karta hai. Beshak Aristotle khud is idea ko murt roop dete the.

Tareeqe chahe jo bhi hon, ek sach mein critical mind — jaisa ki Aristotle ke 'man of universal education' ke paas tha — khud ko lagbhag kisi bhi kshetra mein laagu kar sakta hai. Critical thinking ke ek champion aur khud kai vishayon par lekhak, Ziauddin Sardar kehte hain ki aisa tareeqa kisi bhi intelligent vyakti ko so-called 'specialist' fields mein pravesh karne ki anumati de sakta hai:

> Ek baar jab jargon, jo baahar walon ko uljhane ke liye design kiya gaya hai, ko hata diya jata hai toh koi ek aisa methodology aur thought process paata hai jise koi bhi mastery kar sakta hai jo ise samajhne ke liye dridh-sankalp hai. Is sambandh mein, asli intellectual ek polymath hai: uska basic tool ek tez dimaag aur ek transdisciplinary methodology hai jo kisi bhi discipline, kisi bhi subject, manav gyaan ke kisi bhi segment ko ughad kar rakh sakti hai.

Polymaths so-called 'experts' dwara kahi gayi baaton ko granted nahi lete. Jabki unke paas kisi diye gaye kshetra mein specialised knowledge nahi raha ho sakta hai, woh kshetra ke apne paas maujood seemit anubhav aur gyaan ka sabse behtareen upayog karne mein saksham hote hain aur iski apni samajh ko anukoolit (optimise) karne ke liye apni intelligence aur critical thinking faculties ko niyukt karte hain. Iske alawa, woh pehchante hain ki multiple perspectives (ya fields) mein jaanch empirical objectivity ke liye sabse logical aur intelligent raasta hai.

---

### Social and Emotional Intelligence (Samajik Aur Bhavnatmak Buddhi)

> Apni talents ko mat chhipao, woh upayog ke liye banaye gaye the, Chhaya mein sundial kya hai.
> — Benjamin Franklin

Artificial Intelligence (AI) jald hi Turing Test pass kar lega, jiske baad woh 'common sense' sthapit karne mein insaano ko mat dene mein saksham ho sakta hai. Lekin insaani intelligence ke aise pehlu hain jo (kam se kam abhi tak) anukaran-heen (inimitable) lagte hain.

Social Intelligence jatil samajik rishton aur vatavaranon ko prabhavshali dhang se navigate aur negotiate karne ki kshamta hai. Emotional intelligence apni bhavnaon ke prati jagruk hone, control karne aur vyakt karne, aur antravyakti rishton (interpersonal relationships) ko samajhdari aur hamdardi se sambhalne ki kshamta hai. Woh non-programmable 'unique selling points' hain, agar aap chahein, jo manav prajati ko computers par ek gambhir prati-sparddhi labh (competitive advantage) dete hain. Oxford researchers Frey aur Osborne ne apne article *The Future of Employment* mein nirdisht kiya, ‘social aur emotional intelligence ko automate nahi kiya ja sakta lekin motor skills ko zyada aasaani se kiya ja sakta hai.’ Polymaths ke liye mahatvapurna roop se, in intelligences ko multiple domains mein laagu kiya ja sakta hai (ya kam se kam kiya jana chahiye).

---

## Versatility (BAHUMUKHTA / VIVIDHTA)

> Jo cheez kisi vyakti ko versatile (bahumukhi) banati hai woh hai apne khud ke hiton (interests) ko seemit karne se inkaar karna.
> — Raymond Tallis

American ‘million-dollar baby’ Juli Crockett ek undefeated professional boxer, ek playwright aur theatre director, ek ordained minister, ek record-selling band mein lead singer/songwriter, philosophy ki ek Ph.D. scholar aur ek international corporation mein ek business executive rahi hain. Scottish Pakistani Azeem Ibrahim British Army mein ek soldier, Scots rich list mein listed ek financial entrepreneur, political science mein ek Harvard scholar, Pakistani government ke education adviser, ek theological think-tank ke founder, aur kai humanitarian initiatives ke patron rahe hain. Crockett aur Ibrahim dono ne aise alag-alag kshetron mein tezi se shreshthta haasil ki hai kyunki kisi bhi doosri talent se zyada, unke paas ek mukhya gun (core trait) ek ashadharan level par hai: versatility (bahumukhta).

Curiosity aur intelligence polymath ke liye mahatvapurna gun hain, lekin inmein se har ek ko ya toh kshitij (horizontally - vyapak jaanch) ya lambvat (vertically - specialisation ki taraf) modaa ja sakta hai. Aaj ka hyper-specialised samaj saaf taur par baad wale ko encourage karta hai, toh aatmakankshi polymath ke liye, aatm-vikas ke in auzaron mein jo cheez zaroor jodi jaani chahiye woh hai versatility.

Versatility polymathic mind ki buniyaadi visheshata ya ‘core competency’ hai — yeh wahi hai jo polymath ko doosre prakar ke geniuses se alag karti hai. Versatility hamesha polymath ka paryay nahi hoti, lekin — doosre polymathic attributes jaise creativity, general intelligence aur critical thinking ke vipreet — yeh iski ek aavashyak shart (essential prerequisite) aur zaroori component hai. Latin *versatilis* se aate hue, iska shabdash (literally) anuvaad ‘aasaani se mudna’ (turning easily) hota hai, lekin English mein ise ‘kushal roop se kai cheezein karne mein saksham hona’ ya ‘vividh upayog ya kai karya hona’ ke roop mein paribhashit kiya jata hai. Saral shabdon mein, yeh vividh, aapatat: asambandhit domains ke beech bina kisi rukawat ke badhne ki kshamta hai.

Aaj, versatility ek aisa skill hai jiske baare mein music, film aur sport ke kshetron mein sabse zyada baat ki jaati hai, lekin yeh beshak manav jeevan ke har kshetra mein pradarshit hota hai. Yeh insaano mein ek aantarik gun hai aur vaastav mein ek aisa gun hai jise hamare (vyapak roop se maane gaye) aam purvajon tak khoja ja sakta hai. Meethe aaloo ko saaf karne ke sath-sath anajon ko alag karne ke tareeqon ka aavishkar karke, 1950s ke Capuchin monkey, Imo ne primatologists ko pradarshit kiya ki apes ke paas multiple, alag-alag cognitive abilities hoti hain jinhe woh prabhavshali dhang se aage-peeche shift karne mein saksham hote hain.

---

### Switching Lives (Zindagiyon Ko Badalna)

> Insaan vividh, bahu-roopi aur hamesha badalane wali prakriti ka ek jeevit praani hai.
> — Mesopotamian saying
> *(from Oration on the Dignity of Man)*

Cheng Man-ch’ing ne twentieth century mein West mein Chinese culture ko introduced karne mein sabse badi bhoomikaon mein se ek nibhayi. Unhe martial arts, medicine, painting, poetry aur calligraphy mein unki uplabdhiyon ke liye ‘Master of the Five Arts’ ke roop mein jaana jata tha. Woh apne career mein shuruat mein hi ek prasiddh doctor (physician) bane, pehle swatantra roop se abhyas karte hue, aur phir aakhirkar Taiwan mein rehne ke dauran Chinese statesman Chiang Kai-shek ke vyaktigat doctor bane. Woh traditional Chinese herbal medicine ke sath-sath Western pharmacology dono mein achhi tarah se nipun the.

Apne poore career ke dauran, ‘Professor Cheng’ jaisa ki woh jaane gaye, ne vividh kalaroopon mein bhi shreshthta haasil ki, vishesh roop se *san chueh* ki praacheen Chinese parampara ka palan karte hue ‘Three Perfections’ (painting, poetry aur calligraphy) mein. Unki pehli kavitaayein 18 saal ki umar mein publish hui thin, aur 19 saal ki umar mein woh Yu-Wen University mein poetry ke professor bane. Unhone shuruat mein aamdani ke ek srot ke roop mein painting shuru ki aur phir apne samay ke bade masters ke tehat kaafi adhyayan ke baad, unhe Shanghai School of Fine Arts ka head niyukt kiya gaya jahan unhone painting aur calligraphy padhayi (unke students mein baad mein Chinese Premier Chiang Kai-shek ki patni shamil thin). Unka kaam Paris aur New York ke sath-sath poore China mein pradarshit kiya gaya tha, aur woh College for Chinese Arts and Culture ke ek founder bane.

Woh shayad West mein *tai chi* ko popular banane ke liye sabse behtareen dhang se jaane jaate hain — traditional Chinese martial art jo apne sharirik, manasik aur adhyatmik labhon dono ke liye jaana jata hai. Unhone *tai chi* par adhyayan kiya, abhyas kiya, padhaya aur likha, aur popular Yang-style form ki shuruat ki. Woh ek mystic aur philosopher bhi the, jinki *Tao Te Ching*, *I Ching* aur Confucius ke *Analects* jaise Chinese classical texts par commentary ko ek darshanik treatise: *Essays on Man and Culture* mein compile kiya gaya tha.

Upar di gayi zyadatar cheezon ko ek sath karte hue; Cheng saaf taur par versatility ki kala ke master the. Yeh skill, jo aaj ek fashionable competency ban gaya hai, jise duniya ke kai employers dwara dhoondha jata hai, ek nishchit mindset ki maang karta hai. Chahe chetan roop se ya praakritik roop se, ek vyakti ke paas lagatar badlav ke prati ek khula-pan — aur vaastav mein kabhi-kabhi ek laalach — hona chahiye. Lekin shayad isse bhi zyada mahatvapurna aam taur par badlav ki apariharyata (inevitability of change) ki swikriti hai, us tareeqe se alag nahi jis tareeqe se Buddhist *anatta* (a-nittayata/impermanence) ke drishtikon ka samarthan karta hai, jo duniya aur uski vastuon ko ek dynamic system ke hisse ke roop mein dekhta hai jismein har cheez aasthayi aur naashvan hai.

Beshak ‘badlav’ koi adhyatmik concept nahi balki vaastav mein ek bhaautik haqeeqat hai. Har chaar mahine mein aapke red blood cells poori tarah se badal jaate hain, aur aapki skin ke cells har kuch hafton mein badal jaate hain. Lagbhag saat saalon ke andar aapke sharir ka har atom doosre atoms dwara replace kar diya jayega. Bhaautik roop se, aap lagatar ek naye aap hain. Agar badlav ko zindagi ki haqeeqat ke roop mein swikar kiya jata hai aur iske alawa is roop mein apnaya jata hai, toh kisi ke aapekshik aasaani ke sath zindagi ke alag-alag kshetron aur charanon mein badhne, ya unke beech switch karne ki zyada sambhavna hoti hai.

Badlav ke sath vividhta (variety) aati hai. ‘Agar aap specialised nahi hain toh yeh dimaag ko bahut zyada aazadi deta hai . . . aap khud par rukawatein nahi laga rahe hain, jiska matlab hai ki aap bahut zyada tezi se aur pravahi (fluidly) badh sakte hain,’ artistic polymath Billy Childish kehte hain, jinhe, kai logon ki tarah, lagatar stimulation ki zaroorat hoti hai. Vyakti kabhi-kabhi simply multiple ‘lives’ jeene mein mulya aur santosht paata hai jise woh (chetan ya a-chetan roop se) ek doosre se poori tarah se alag treat karta hai. Aatm-siddhi alternative lives jeene aur multiple identities apnane se haasil hoti hai (chahe ek sath ho ya ek ke baad ek); apne kalpanaon ko sirf sapne dekhne ke bajaye unhe jeene se. Kisi interdisciplinary agenda ki zaroorat nahi hai — kshetron ke beech sambandhon ko dekhe jaane ya banaye jaane ki zaroorat nahi hai — har ‘life’ ya ‘facet’ apne aap mein, alag-thalag roop se paryapt roop se yogya hai. ‘[Polymathy] yeh swikar karna hai ki aap har cheez ko ek chhatri ke neeche nahi la sakte,’ Gayatri Spivak kehti hain, ‘aapko jugglery karni padegi; yeh jaadu ki tarah hai, naachne ki tarah hai. Ek adventure ki tarah.’

Crockett aur Ibrahim apne vividh projects ko compartmentalise karte hain; unke beech daily, kabhi-kabhi hourly aadhar par prabhavshali dhang se switch karte hue. Jabki alag-alag duniyaon ke beech lagatar switch karne ka kaam ek aakarshak roop se faldayi jeevan-shaili sabit ho sakta hai, iska manasik taazgi (psychological rejuvenation) ka prabhav bhi ho sakta hai. Kuch, udaharan ke liye, zindagiyon ke beech is switch ko recovery ke ek vaikalpik tareeqe ke roop mein upayog karte hain. Jab poochha gaya ki kya ya kab woh vaastav mein apne projects ki anant list se break lete hain, Tim Ferriss ne yeh kehkar jawab diya, ‘Main sabse behtareen tab recover karta hoon jab main apna focus switch karta hoon; toh kuch na karne ke bajaye main kisi aisi cheez par focus karunga jo usse poori tarah se na-judi ho jis par main focus kar raha tha.’ Yeh circuit training workout ke peeche ka wahi concept hai — poora ghanta, rest-free, full-body exercise session jise ek aise tareeqe se banaya gaya hai jo sharir ke alag-alag hisson ko thodi der aaram karne ki anumati deta hai, jabki doosre hisse kaam kar rahe hote hain. ‘Badlav aaram jitna hi achha hota hai,’ jaise kahavat hai.

Is tarah apni zindagi ke ek pehlu se doosre mein pravesh karne ke liye (chahe aasthayi ya beech-beech mein hi sahi) ‘bhagne’ ki yeh sambhavna kisi vyakti par ek taaza karne wala prabhav daal sakti hai, jo aksar badi kul santushti aur utpadakta (productivity) ki anumati deti hai. Is tarah dekha jaye toh, switching ka upayog monomathic, specialised life ke sath sabse badi samasya se nipatne ke liye kiya ja sakta hai: ghati hui aamdani/labh (diminishing returns). David Eastburn ne American Philosophical Society mein apne lecture mein ise prasiddh roop se samjaya: ‘jaise aap kisi vishisht gatividhi ko aage badhate hain, jodi gayi santushti aakhirkar ghat jaati hai. Kisi point par aapko kuch alag karne se zyada additional santushti milti hai.’ Ek economist ke roop mein, woh marginal utility ke concept aur plateau effect ko sandarbhit kar rahe the, jo na sirf arthvyavasthaon aur sangathanon par laagu hota hai, balki vyaktiyon par bhi.

‘Trick,’ Eastburn sujhalte hain, ‘gatividhiyon ke ek mishran ke zariya kul santushti ko adhikatam (maximize) karna hai.’ Yahi wajah hai ki kuch vyakti, Tim Ferriss ke anusar, ‘sankhipt adhyayan ko tezi se ghatte labhon ke point tak le jaate hain, usse aage nahi.’ Woh tarka dete hain ki ‘focused generalist jo do saal tak vyavasthit roop se Japanese padhta hai versus specialist jo 10 saal tak Japanese padhta hai, us tatkalikta (urgency) ki kami ke sath jo un logon ke liye typical hai jo daava karte hain ki kisi cheez ko "seekhne mein poori zindagi lagti hai", ke beech shayad 5 percent samajh ka antar hai.’ Ise aamtaur par 80/20 principle ke roop mein sandarbhit kiya jata hai.

Azeri polymath Hamlet Isakhanli mahsoos karte hain ki dimaag ke liye zaroorat aur junoon ke maamlon ke beech switch karna swasth hai: ‘Aapni gatividhi ko samay-samay par badalna, apne dil ki gehraiyon se kisi ichha ko khinchna aur ise zindagi mein lana sambhav hai.’ Toh asambandhit tasks ke beech koodna tunnel-visioned nirantarata se aane wale diminishing returns ki sambhavna ko rokti hai. Yeh moolta (originality) ke ek baar-baar injection ki anumati deti hai, jo ya toh kisi doosre field/task se praapt antar-drishtiyon se aayegi ya simply kisi ek kshetra mein atyadhik kaam na karne ki 'taazgi' se. Ek aur fitness training analogy ka upayog karne ke liye, kehne ke liye, running ya bench-pressing ke ek progressive program ke baad, 'block' ko door karne aur performance ko badhane ke liye ek nishchit point ke baad poori tarah se alag exercise ke sath sharir ko 'shock' dena mahatvapurna hai. Kai lekhakon ne writer's block ko door karne ke liye bhi is tareeqe ka upayog kiya hai.
