Versatile vyakti dwara aisi 'multi-tasking' unhe anukooltam aur lagatar alert rakhti hai, jisse kul performance badhti hai. *Harvard Business Review* mein published ek haal hi ke Cass Business School study ne multitasking ke paksh mein tarka diya, aur paaya ki 'polychronic executives' (multi-taskers) behtar information brokers, tezi se decision-makers hain aur vaastav mein unke doosre saathiyon ki tulna mein unka aarthik pradarshan (financial performance) zyada hai. Jab poochha gaya ki woh multi-task kyun karte hain, toh executives mein se ek ne samjaya: ‘hum vividhta (variety) ka anand lete hain; woh lagatar switching, zyada kathinai se kendrit (concentrate) hone ki zaroorat ki chunauti.’

TED Conferences ke curator, Chris Anderson, sehamat hain ki ‘switching’ ek mahatvapurna bauddhika tareeqa hai. Ideas ko explore karne ke sabse prabhavshali tareeqe par salah dete waqt, apne khud ke TED Talk ke dauran, unhone vividhta ke zariya dimaag ko taaza karne aur vistarit karne ke mahatva ko samjaya. Asal mein woh TED ki kamyabi ka shrey is visheshata ko dete hain:

> Vishayon ke beech badalte rahein . . . dimaag ke ek hi hisse mein jaane wale bahut zyada talks mat rakhein. Dimaag ko ek manspeshi (muscle) ke roop mein sochein . . . agar aapke paas bahut zyada analytical ya bahut zyada inspirational ityaadi hai, toh dimaag ka woh hissa thak jata hai . . . TED ke kaam karne ka kaaran yeh hai ki aap ise milate hain. . . . Kuch musical, kuch visual, kuch inspirational, kuch analytical ityaadi rakhein. Yeh dimaag ko kholne deta hai. . .

Bruce Lee 32 saal ki umar mein mar gaye, lekin tab tak unhone ek genius martial artist aur actor ke roop mein duniya bhar mein shohrat kamayi thi. Lekin us aadmi mein aur bhi bahut kuch tha. Woh ek gehre thinker aur kavi the. Unke paas versatility ki ek philosophy thi, jo Kung Fu ka saar thi. ‘Aapko paani ki tarah aakar-heen, roop-heen hona chahiye,’ unhone zor diya. ‘Jab aap paani ko ek cup mein daalte hain, toh yeh cup ban jata hai. Jab aap paani ko ek bottle mein daalte hain, toh yeh bottle ban jata hai. Jab aap paani ko ek teapot mein daalte hain, toh yeh teapot ban jata hai. Paani tapak sakta hai aur yeh crash kar sakta hai . . . Paani ki tarah bano, mere dost!’ Shayad yeh wahi tareeqa tha jisne unhe nunchucks ko paddle ke roop mein upayog karke ek Chinese ping-pong champion ke sath rally karne ki anumati di!

Darshanik roop se, har switch ke sath, polymath ‘jaan-ne aur hone ke roopon’ ke beech ek epistemological shift se guzarta hai. Vaigyanik roop se, ise cognitive aur neural processes ke zariya samjaya ja sakta hai.

---

### Cognitive Shifting and Neuroplasticity (Sangyanatmak Badlav Aur Neuroplasticity)

James Cook (prasiddh 'Captain Cook') ko eighteenth century mein Royal Society dwara South Pacific ki pehli yatra karne ke liye commission kiya gaya tha. Unki team ko Venus dwara Sun ke eclipse ka observation karna tha, lekin beshak, chhipa hua agenda ek aupniveshik (colonial) tha: fabled dakshini mahadweep (southern continent) ko dhoondhna aur uspar daava karna. Is baat se jagruk ki Polynesians itihaas roop se Southern Pacific ke sabse behtareen samudri navigators the, Cook ne navigation, meteorology, language, culture, cartography aur astronomy ke maamlon par apni madad ke liye ek sthaniye polymath ko recruit kiya.

Tupaia ek Tahitian linguist, orator, priest aur politician the jinke paas likhne ya mapmaking ka koi pehle ka gyaan nahi tha, lekin unhone Pacific ka ek aisa chart banaya jismein Polynesia ke har mukhya group ko shamil kiya gaya aur Marquesas se Rotuma aur Fiji tak 4000 km se zyada phala hua tha. Woh ship ke translator bhi the, jo Maori, Javanese aur Tahitians ke sath samvaad karne mein saksham the, aur ek pratibhashali artist bhi the, jo unhone varnan kiya use darshane ke liye jeevant tasveerein banate the. South Pacific ke logon aur sanskritiyon par jaankari ka ek mahatvapurna srot, unhe unke biographer dwara ‘Pacific ka pehla anthropologist’ bhi bataya gaya hai.

Tupaia ke paas saaf taur par ‘code-switching’ ke liye ek ashadharan talent thi — alag-alag prakar ke gyaan, skills aur vatavaranon ke beech prabhavshali badlav (shifting). Yeh ek prakar ki cognitive ability hai jo aamtaur par linguists se judi hoti hain jinhe multiple bhashaon ke beech baar-baar switch karna padta hai, actors ke sath jo vividh characters mein andar-baahar hote hain, ya un vyaktiyon ke sath jinki multiple samajik aur sanskritik pehchan hoti hai aur unhe jahan woh hain aur jinke sath woh samvaad karte hain uske anusar sochne aur hone ke apne mode ko adjust karna padta hai. Computer par multiple program windows, browser tabs aur documents ke beech switch karne ka kaam shayad is concept ka ek microcosm hai. Ya shayad 21st century ke liye ek behtar analogy augmented reality ho sakti hai.

Cognitive shifting ek aisi prakriya hai jise kai meditative traditions mein encourage kiya jata hai aur se guzara jata hai, aur ek aisa jiska psychologists aur neuroscientists dwara lagatar adhyayan kiya jata hai taaki asambandhit tasks ke beech switch karne ki dimaag ki kshamta ko behtar dhang se samjha ja sake. Yeh dimaag ke executive function ka ek component hai: vishisht dimaag ilaqe jo activate hote hain jab koi vyakti multiple asambandhit tasks ke beech switch karta hai, jinmein prefrontal cortex, basal ganglia, anterior cingulate cortex aur posterior parietal cortex shamil hain. Yeh kshetr samuhik roop se polymath ki ek sthiti ya cognitive skill se doosri mein swatantra roop se badhne aur lachile dhang se sochne aur apne focus ko prabhavshali dhang se phir se nirdeshit karke badli hui sthiti par uchit prati-kriya dene ki kshamta ke liye zimmedar hain.

Hamare dimaag lagatar badlav se nipatne mein saksham hone ke liye biologically program kiye gaye hain. Hamare neurons aur unke synapses lagatar badlav ki sthiti mein hain — connections dynamic hain, apna size aur strength aur location badal rahe hain; ban rahe hain aur un-form ho rahe hain. Yeh prakriya, jise neuroplasticity ke roop mein jaana jata hai, tab hoti hai jab hamare neurons aur neural networks naye jaankari, sensory stimulation, vikas, kshati (damage) ya dysfunction ke jawab mein apne connections aur vyavhaar ko badalte hain. Bade badlav ke samay dimaag ke cellular ya neural networks ka tezi se badlav ya reorganisation ho sakta hai.

Jaise hum naye anubhav haasil karte hain, baar-baar upayog kiye jaane wale synapses (ek aisa dhancha jo ek neuron ko doosre ko electrical ya chemical signal pass karne ki anumati deta hai) majboot hote hain jabki an-upayog kiye gaye synapses kamzor ho jaate hain. Aakhirkar, an-upayog kiye gaye synapses ek prakriya mein poori tarah se khatam ho jaate hain jise synaptic pruning ke roop mein jaana jata hai, jo neural connections ke kushal networks ko peeche chhod deta hai. Yeh prakriya bachpan ke vikas aur sharirik chot ke dauran sabse zyada sakriya hoti hai, lekin yeh hamari poori zindagi ke dauran naye jaankari ya nayi paristhitiyon ke anusar adjust hone ke liye ek reaction mechanism ke roop mein bani rehti hai. Toh neuroscientist David Eagleman humein yaad dilate hain ki badlav koi aisi cheez nahi hai jo hamare liye kathin honi chahiye, kyunki yeh hamare dimaag ke andar automatic aur lagatar ho raha hai:

> Aapka dimaag ek a-dayalu shape-shifter hai, lagatar apni khud ki circuitry ko rewiring kar raha hai, aur kyunki aapke anubhav itne anokhe hain isliye aapke neural networks mein vishaal, decoded patterns bhi hain. Kyunki woh aapki poori zindagi badalte rehte hain, aapki identity ek chalta hua target hai; yeh kabhi ek endpoint par nahi pahonchti . . . hum fixed nahi hain. Gohd se qabr tak, hum vikas ke kaam (works in progress) hain.

Jaise Oxford neuroscientist Anders Sandberg kehte hain, sabse sakht vyaktiyon ke paas bhi ek frontal lobe hota hai jo unhe apna dimaag badalne ki anumati deta hai, ‘. . . insaano ke baare mein sabse cool phir bhi sabse daravani cheezon mein se ek yeh hai ki aap sirf ek sentence kehkar unki zindagi badal sakte hain . . . yeh billiyon ke sath kaam nahi karta!’

Jaise science writer Leonard Mlodinow ne apni haal hi ki book *Elastic: Flexible Thinking in a Time of Change* mein bataya, sochne ke tareeqe jo versatility, adaptability, openness aur resilience ko poshit karte hain — yaani, ‘elastic thinking’ — wahi hain jo humein tezi se badalane wale is daur mein samriddh hone ki anumati denge jo 21st-century life ke lagbhag har pehlu ko badal raha hai.

---

### Multifacetedness (Bahu-Pakshiyata)

> Har din humein kam se kam ek chhota geet sunna chahiye, ek achhi kavita padhni chahiye, ek shaandar tasveer dekhni chahiye, aur, agar sambhav ho, toh kuch samajhdari bhare shabd bolne chahiye.
> — Johann Wolfgang von Goethe

Matrakci Nasuh sixteenth century mein Ottoman Navy mein shamil hue aur unhone apni vilakshan talwarbaazi (swordsmanship) ke liye tezi se pehchan haasil ki. Woh Empire ke pramukh yoddhaon mein se ek bane, saath hi ek weapons instructor, master bladesmith, combat training par ek writer (*Tuhfetül Guzât*) aur Matrak naam ke ek martial sport ke inventor (isliye unka naam) bane.

Lekin martial arts unke kai pehluon (multiple facets) mein se sirf ek tha. Sabse pehle, Nasuh ek mathematical scholar bhi the jinke geometry par kaam samet *Cemâlü’l-Küttâb* aur *Kemalü’l-Hisâb* ko Sultan Selim dwara kaafi saraha gaya tha — unhone multiplication methods (*Umdet-ul Hisab* mein darshaye gaye) ka aavishkar bhi kiya jo pehle sirf European Renaissance scholars ko shrey diye jaate the. Isse bhi zyada, Nasuh ek prasiddh painter aur miniaturist bhi the, aur unka detailed cityscapes aur epic battle scenes ka vishisht ‘Matrakci style’ aaj bhi Turkey ki pratishthit galleries aur museums mein pradarshit hai.

Nasuh ne khud ko apne samay ke pramukh historians mein se ek ke roop mein bhi sabit kiya, 1520 se 1543 tak ke daur ko cover karte hue *Mecmaü’t-Tevârih* aur *Suleymannâme* jaise masterpieces produce kiye, saath hi Suleiman ke Iran campaign par ek treatise jiska title *Fetihname-i Karabuǧdan* tha. Ek soldier, martial artist, polyglot, painter, mathematician aur historian ke roop mein, unhone apne kai pehluon ko pradarshit kiya: intellectual, physical aur creative.

Itihaas ke kai polymaths ki tarah, Nasuh apne kai pehluon ko niyukt karne ke liye ek vishwasniya darbari ke roop mein apni sthiti ka upayog karne mein saksham the. Lekin versatility ka pradarshan sirf professional ya intellectual life par hi nahi, balki poori zindagi par laagu hona chahiye. Asal mein, karak aur vichar kisi vyakti ke sarv-gun-sampanna hone ke vikas mein yogdan dete hain: yatra ki vividhta, bhashaon aur sanskritiyon ke sampark mein aana, anubhav kiye gaye moments, hue rishte, mahsoos ki gayi bhavnaayein, ki gayi baatchit, ki gayi soch, padhi gayi kitaabein ya articles, haasil ki gayi qualifications, kiye gaye yogdan aur ityaadi. Yeh asli polymathy ko ek aakarshak roop se jatil aur gehri dharana banata hai, jiska asli maapak lagbhag asambhav hai.

Zyadatar, agar sabhi nahi, log bahumukhi (multifaceted) hote hain. Kis hadd tak har pehlu ka abhyas kiya jata hai aur woh sabhi kaise murt roop lete hain yeh alag-alag hota hai, aur yeh wahi hai jo kisi ki polymathy ke level aur prakriti ko tay karta hai. Traditional Hindu philosophy, udaharan ke liye, vyaktigat aatm-siddhi ko aage badhane ke chaar tareeqon ko sujhati hai: gyaan (*jnana yoga*), kaam (*karma yoga*), pyaar (*bhakti yoga*) aur psycho-physical training (*raja yoga*). Modern life mein, kisi vyakti mein bahu-pakshiyata kai tareeqon se murt roop le sakti hai: unka vyaktitva, aacharan, naitikta, peshe, avocational interests, vyaktigat jeevan, jeevan anubhav, sanskritik drishtikon ya bhashayi background. Ek achha ya kushal jeevansathi, beta, parent, sibling ya lover hona, udaharan ke liye, sabhi aise kaam ya roles hain jinhe aamtaur par granted liya jata hai — lekin inhe sach mein polymathic equation mein shaamil kiya jana chahiye kyunki yeh bhi apne aap mein khoj aur uplabdhi ke alag-alag kshetra hain.

Yeh jaan-ne ke baad, koi bhi, apne tareeqe se apne kai pehluon ko niyukt aur abhyas kar sakta hai: sharirik — chahe sports ya exercise, khana ya sex ke zariya; bauddhika — chahe formal education, self-learning ya gambhir chintan ke ek saral moment ke zariya; adhyatmik — chahe ibadat, mysticism ya chintan ke zariya, prakriti, art ya divine se inspired; rachnatmak — chahe ek masterpiece paint karna ho ya apne bachhon ke sath khelna ho, sangeet sunna ho ya ise compose karna ho; bhavnatmak — mahatvapurna logon ke samne alag-alag tareeqon se apni bhavnaon ko vyakt karke; aur practical — swasthya, finance, logistics, handiwork, samajik rishton aur aam survival ke rozmarra ke maamlon se nipatna. Aisa karne mein, chhipa hua talents ke ughadne ya naye passions ke viksit hone ki sambhavna ghatatmak roop se (exponentially) badhti hai.

Koi in pehluon ko chahe jaise bhi samoohit ya shrenibaddh kare, yeh pehchanna mahatvapurna hai ki woh astitva mein hain, vividh hain aur unhe ek aise tareeqe se poshit kiya jana chahiye jo ek sarv-gun-sampanna charitra aur jeevan anubhav ki anumati deta hai. Ek bahumukhi zindagi, poore vyakti ko uske hisson ke yog se zyada banate hue, ek polymathic hone ki kaafi zyada sambhavna rakhti hai. Yeh vishesh roop se sach hai agar koi apne anubhavon mein vividhta lane ki talash karta hai.

---

### Diversifying Experiences (Anubhavon Mein Vividhta Lana)

British broadcaster Patrick Moore ko ek ‘serial amateur’ ke roop mein jaana jata tha — ek astronomer, chess player, cricketer, golfer, soldier, actor aur novelist. Unhe inmein se kisi bhi gatividhi mein world-expert ke roop mein pehchan nahi mili thi, na hi unhone inmein se kisi mein koi gambhir yogdan diya tha. Lekin unhone ek ashadharan roop se vividh aur rangheen zindagi jee, jaan-ne aur hone ke alaga-alag kshetron mein pravesh kiya, chahe woh sports, military, art, science ya literature ke zariya ho aur is tarah duniya mein ek anokhi sarv-gun-sampanna antar-drishti haasil ki. Isi tarah, American writer George Plimpton ek shauqeen birdwatcher, fireworks enthusiast, actor, journalist, literary critic aur amateur multi-sportsman the jinhone American football, ice hockey, baseball, tennis, boxing aur yahan tak ki bridge aur high-wire circus performing mein professional competitions mein bhaag lene ke apne anubhavon ko record kiya!

'Jacks of all trades' se zyada, Moore aur Plimpton jaise log 'masters of variety' the. Patla phaelna zaroori roop se kisi ko kam polymathic nahi banata. Har anubhav, chahe kitna bhi sankhipt ho, aur vishesh roop se agar yeh kisi aise kshetra mein hai jo vyakti ke liye poori tarah se naya hai, uske sarv-gun-sampanna-pan aur kul vyaktigat, professional, intellectual, physical aur spiritual vikas mein jodta hai. Kya kisi ko skydiving karne se roka jana chahiye sirf isliye kyunki yeh sirf kuch minutes tak tikta hai aur aap ise professionally aage badhane ki sambhavna nahi rakhte hain? Kya kisi ko cultural history par kitaab padhne se roka jana chahiye sirf isliye kyunki woh aage badhkar is par apni kitaab nahi likhne wale hain ya isme Ph.D. ke liye padhai nahi karne wale hain?

Jawab zyadatar log jo denge woh nahi hai; kyunki har learning experience jo kisi ke paas hai uski zindagi mein maayne rakhta hai, aur yeh aksar unke liye ya samaj ke liye kisi bade ya chhote tareeqe se labhdayak sabit ho sakta hai. Sansehp mein, apne anubhavon mein vividhta lana na sirf zindagi ko ashadharan roop se rangheen banata hai, balki ise un tareeqon se bhi samriddh karta hai jo sirf pashchatt-drishti (hindsight) hi bata sakti hai.

Khula-dimaag-pan, vishesh roop se khud ke prati, astitva ki ek mahatvapurna sthiti hai jo swabhavik roop se anubhav ke vividhta ko encourage karti hai. Juli Crockett, jo ek athlete, playwright, musician aur scholar rahi hain, ne ise pehchana: ‘Main khud ko kisi ek cheez ke roop mein "label" na karne ki koshish karti hoon, apni self-perception ko seemit na karne ke liye. Aapko kisi cheez mein achha hone ke liye usme kharab hone ke liye taiyar rehna hoga. Agar aap khud ko un cheezon tak seemit karte hain jinke sath aap "safe" hain aur jaante hain ki aap pehle se hi unme achhe hain, toh yeh ek chhoti list hai. Agar aap kisi bhi cheez ko ek baar try karne, aur kisi cheez mein ausat (mediocre) hone ke liye taiyar hain, toh aap bahut saara saman karenge.’ Lifestyle experimenter A.J. Jacobs, jo apni zindagi ko vividh prayogon ki ek shrinkhla ke roop mein jeete hain, zor dete hain ki ‘aapko hamesha adventures ko haan kehna chahiye, warna aap ek bahut hi ubaao zindagi jiyenge.’

Iske alawa, diversity vyaktigat aatm-siddhi mein ek mahatvapurna bhoomika nibha sakti hai. Kisi ka gyaan aur anubhav jitna zyada vividh hoga, aapke paas antar-drishti-purna faislon aur rachnatmak samadhanon par pahonchne ka utna hi behtar mauqa hoga. Is tarah, ek jack of all trades master of insight ban jata hai. Yeh kisi ke gyaan, skills aur anubhavon ki vividhta hi hai jo vividh kshetron mein, aur vishesh roop se mool kshetra (core field) mein ek anokhi antar-drishti ki anumati deti hai. Ideas aur genius ke moments pehle se maujood vicharon, yaadashton aur cognitive skills ke ek sangrah ke a-chetan fusion se ubharte hain.

Yeh anubhav moments, jobs, hobbies ya sirf ghatnaon ke roop mein aa sakte hain. Hobbies, udaharan ke liye, sirf mamooli shauk nahi hain, balki aapke bauddhika astitva ko aakar dene mein mahatvapurna yogdan deti hain. Woh jigsaw puzzle ka ek abhinna hissa hain jo aap hain. Sabse aksar, woh aapki zindagi ke kisi doosre pehlu ko badhane mein yogdan dengi, kabhi-kabhi aapke bina ehsaas kiye bhi. Humne ise Nobel science prize-winning hobbyists ke genius se dekha hai, saath hi multi-careered business leaders, statesmen aur philosophers se, jinmein se kai apne respective breakthrough ideas mein 'peripheral' gatividhiyon aur purva careers ke prabhav ko swikar karte hain.

Hyper-specialisation ki pradhan sanskriti ke baavjood, ubharte hue polymaths aaj khud ko ek aakarshak khel ke maidaan par paate hain. Modern duniya aapke anubhavon mein vividhta lane ke paryapt mauqe pradan karti hai. Koi bahut zyada aasaani se yatra kar sakta hai, kifayati kitaabein khareed sakta hai, vishayon ki ek range ke baare mein logon ki ek range ke sath baatchit mein shamil ho sakta hai, aur nayi skills seekh sakta hai. Lekin sabse mahatvapurna baat yeh hai ki yeh logon ko yeh samajhne ki anumati deta hai ki duniya kaise aapas mein judi hui hai: arthvyavasthaayein kaise interact karti hain, kaise vishesh sanskritiyan aur philosophies sthaniye politics mein khelne aati hain, kaise art ek mahadweep mein doosre ke sambandh mein viksit hua hai, kaise naitikta alag-alag samajon mein srotit hoti hai, kaise prakriti ko apna maujooda roop lena pada, kaise sports aur businesses hamare tribal instincts ko khila rahe hain, kaise technology ne humein aage badhaya aur phansaya dono hai. Yeh is baat ki behtar samajh pradan kar sakta hai ki sarkar ke alag-alag roop kaise kaam karte hain, ameer aur gareeb ke beech ka antar ghatatmak roop se kaise badha, kaise alag-alag races aur languages ek aam purvaj se ubhari aur English ek chhote north-west European tribal dialect se badhkar ek billion se zyada logon dwara boli jaane wali bhasha ke roop mein kyun ubhari. Sansehp mein, yeh humein zyada chetan zindagiyan jeene ke liye encourage karta hai.

Aam taur par halanki, anubhavon mein vividhta lana simply zindagi ko zyada rangheen, zyada mazedar banata hai. Iske alawa, anubhav ki vividhta khud ke baare mein seekhne ke liye utni hi mahatvapurna hai jitni ki duniya ke baare mein seekhne ke liye. Yeh aatm-manthan yatra (introspective journey) ka ek mahatvapurna hissa hai.

Un logon ke liye bhi jo specialise karne ke liye utsukh hain, anubhavon mein vividhta lane mein paryapt samay bitana is baat ka behtar idea pradan karega ki aakhirkar kis par focus kiya jaye. ‘Aapne pichhle samay mein jo kuch bhi kiya hai use kachra mat samjhein,’ Robert Greene kehte hain, *Mastery* ke lekhak, jo itihaas ke bade masters ke aam gunon mein bestselling jaanch hai. ‘Sabse mamooli jobs bhi aapko aise sabak aur skills sikhayenge jinhe aap baad mein exploit aur combine kar sakte hain.’ Asal mein, itihaas mein kuch samajon ne specialist role ke liye diversity ke mulya ko pehchana: eleventh-century Andalusía mein ek muezzin (aazaan dene wala) ko astronomy, linguistics, philosophy, musicology aur kai doosre vishayon mein achhi tarah se nipun hona padta tha isse pehle ki unhe ek mukhya masjid mein muezzin (ek ultra-specialist position maana jata tha) ke job ke liye vichar kiya ja sake.

Orit Gadiesh, global strategy consulting firm Bain and Company ki chairperson, ek generalist hain jinhone lagbhag har industry mein clients ke sath kaam kiya hai. ‘Aapko un cheezon par "samay barbaad" karne ke liye taiyar rehna hoga jo seedhe taur par aapke kaam se judi nahi hain kyunki aap curious hain,’ woh zor deti hain. ‘Lekin phir aap, kabhi-kabhi a-chetan roop se, unhe wapas usme integrate karne mein saksham hote hain jo aap karte hain.’ Robert Twigger, journalist, adventurer aur *Micromastery* ke lekhak jinhone khud ko duniya bhar mein vividh chunautipurna sthitiyon mein improvising karte hue paaya, sehamat hain: ‘Gyaan ke jitne zyada kshetron ko aap cover karte hain, improvisation ke liye aapke resources utne hi bade honge.’ Yeh isliye hai kyunki vividh anubhav, bhavnaayein aur data a-chetan mein 'incubate' ya 'ferment' hone ke liye chhod diye jaate hain, ek prakar ka anjaane mein hua sanshleshan (synthesis), jab tak ki antar-drishti ki ek chamak ek bade idea ke roop mein astitva mein visphotit na ho jaye.

Sapnon ya doosre subconscious anubhavon ke zariya har kisi dwara anubhav kiya gaya surrealism is baat ka pradarshan hai ki kalpana aur creativity swabhavik roop se manav kshamta ka ek hissa hai. Yeh vicharon, anubhavon aur ideas ki praakritik hybridity ko bhi pradarshit karta hai — manav soch ke alag-alag tatvon ka yadrakshik (random) fusion jo ek nayi vaastavikta ke nirmaan ki taraf le jata hai. Yeh, vaastav mein, wahi hai jo art hai. Kuch log ise adhyatmikta ka srot (ya utpad) bhi maan sakte hain.

---

### A Thought-Action Mentality (Soch-Kriya Manoshtithi)

> Kriya (action) ke bina gyaan paagalpan hai aur gyaan ke bina kriya ahankar (vanity) hai.
> — Al-Ghazali

Anubhav intellect ko badhata hai, aur iska vipreet bhi. 25 saal ki umar mein desh ke sabse yuva MLA ke roop mein, Shrikant Jichkar Indian parliament ke liye chune gaye the, isse pehle ki woh alag-alag government departments ke Minister ke roop mein aur finance, irrigation, tax, transport, power, patents aur planning jaise vividh mudon se nipatne wali vividh committees par seva karein. Kriya ke ek aadmi (man of action) ke roop mein, unhone HAM Radio Association ki sthapna bhi ki, poore India mein flood victims ke liye prabhavshali disaster management par kaam kiya, aur desh ke sabse pramukh priests mein se ek bane.

Yeh unke professional career ke dauran tha jab Jichkar ne ek sath vyapak vidwatta (scholarship) ke raaste ko aage badhaya, 1972 aur 1990 ke beech har summer aur winter mein vividh advanced degree qualifications ke liye exams (kul 42) likhte hue. Unhone aakhirkar 20 postgraduate degrees haasil kin jinmein ek MBBS (medicine), LLM (law), MBA (business) ke sath-sath public administration, sociology, history, philosophy, English literature, political science, archaeology, psychology mein Masters Degrees aur Sanskrit mein a Litt. D. shamil hai — aakhirkar modern history mein sabse zyada academically qualified vyakti bane. Bina kisi hairani ke, unke paas India mein sabse badi vyaktigat libraries mein se ek thi, jismein 52,000 se zyada kitaabein thin.

Jichkar ek doer (karne wale) the, jinhone apni padhai ke dauran sochne ki prakriya ko lagatar shaamil kiya. Ludwig Wittgenstein ne, doosri taraf vipreet drishtikon apnaya: woh ek thinker (sochne wale) the jinhone apni zindagi mein asli kriya (real action) ko shaamil karne ko ek point banaya. Unhone aeronautical engineer ke roop mein apne career ki shuruat ki, lekin jaldi hi apna dhyan mathematics aur vishesh roop se mathematics ki philosophy ki taraf mod diya. Unhone Cambridge mein Bertrand Russell ke tehat adhyayan kiya, jo yuva philosopher ke genius se vishesh roop se prabhavit the. Unke mukhya saahityik yogdan mathematics ki philosophy, language ki philosophy, saath hi logic aur psychology mein the. Yadyapi unka published output unke saathi academics jitna vishaal nahi tha, phir bhi unhe twentieth century ke sabse prabhavshali philosophers mein se ek maana jata hai.

Lekin philosophy, unhone zor diya ‘ek theory nahi balki ek activity hai,’ aur unhone is kaaran se aksar academia se ‘asli duniya’ mein peeche hatna majboor mahsoos kiya. World War I ke dauran unhone Austrian army mein ek officer ke roop mein seva ki aur unke sahas ke liye unhe kai baar sammaanit kiya gaya, aur World War II ke dauran unhone samajik aur manual kaam ki apni pukaar ko santusht karne ke liye London mein ek hospital orderly ke roop mein kaam kiya. Unhone ek teacher aur gardener ke roop mein kaam karte hue bhi samay bitaya. Iske alawa, unhone apni photography (unka kaam haal hi mein London School of Economics aur Cambridge University mein pradarshit kiya gaya hai) aur apni architecture (unhone apna ghar khud design kiya aur banaya) ke liye shohrat haasil ki, ek shaikshanik vishay ke roop mein philosophy ke liye visualisation aur aestheticism ke mahatva par zor dete hue.

Aaj kal ‘thinker’ aur ‘doer’ alag hain — yeh maan liya jata hai ki leaders ke paas chintan karne ka samay nahi hota aur intellectuals ke paas leader ki vyavhrikta (pragmatism) nahi hoti. Lekin Churchill, Smuts aur Roosevelt ki tarah, itihaas ke kuch sabse prabhavshali leaders scholar-statesmen rahe hain jinhone leadership roles mein shamil hone ke sath-sath polymathic intellectuals ke roop mein kaam kiya.

Roman Empire ke charam par, polymaths ne samaj mein sakriya bhoomikaayein nibhayi saath hi vidwatta mein amulya yogdan diya. Ideas ke historian Peter Watson ke anusar, unki ruchi ‘utilitas’ mein thi, ideas ki upayogita, woh taqat jo woh mamlon mein la sakte the. Is tarah woh practical philosophers the — sciences, humanities aur arts mein scholars, lekin jinhone public offices bhi sambhale aur soldiers, jurists, governors, librarians aur politicians ke roop mein samaj mein yogdan diya. Unke darshanik jaanch ne unke professional careers ka samarthan kiya aur iska vipreet bhi. Marcus Tullius Cicero ne ek politician, lawyer aur orator ke roop mein barabar tareef ka anand liya saath hi language, philosophy aur political science ke scholar ke roop mein bhi. Pliny the Elder Vespasian ke shasan ke dauran na sirf ek mahatvapurna statesman aur military leader the, balki *Naturalis Historia* ke lekhak bhi the, jo Roman era se bachi sabse vyapak encyclopaedias mein se ek hai, aur unhone history aur grammar mein bhi yogdan diya.

Isi tarah Islamic duniya mein, polymath ek thinker aur ek doer dono ke roop mein maujood tha — ek aisa drishtikon jo saaf taur par Quran aur uske artikulator se inspired tha. Ziauddin Sardar, jinhone apni kitaab *Reading the Quran* mein Quran ka vyapak adhyayan kiya, nishkarsh nikalte hain: ‘Main Quran ko ek aise text ke roop mein dekhne aaya hoon jo ek sath sochne aur karne ko badhava deta hai.’ Yuddh, shasan aur vyapar jaisi practical vaastaviktaon ke empire (ya empires, jaise woh bane) ke kendra mein hone par, Muslim polymaths samaj mein intellectual roles ke sath-sath practical roles mein bhi shreshthta haasil karne ki pravritti rakhte the — merchants, soldiers, jurists, diplomats, physicians aur imams ke roop mein. Itihaas ke kai sabse bade polymaths — Leonardo, Franklin, Kuo, Robeson, Tagore, Schweitzer, Goethe, Morris, Rizal, Imhotep aur Chen — ne wahi pravritti pradarshit ki.

Is prakar ki polymathy shayad samaj ke liye sabse zyada prabhavshali aur moolyavan hai kyunki yeh intellectual aur experimental versatility dono ko pradarshit karti hai, saath hi gyaan ke vividh sroton ka upayog bhi. Phir bhi kuch samaj thinker aur doer ko alag karne par tule hue hain, aksar ek ko doosre ki keemat par mulya dete hue. Iska ek jeevant udaharan Britain mein Industrial Revolution ke dauran tha, jab bourgeois intellectual aur working class labourer ke beech ek saaf antar tha. British polymath John Ruskin ne prasiddh roop se ispar apni niraasha vyakt ki:

> Hum chahte hain ki ek aadmi hamesha sochta rahe, aur doosra hamesha kaam karta rahe, aur hum ek ko gentleman kehte hain, aur doosre ko operative; jabki workman ko aksar sochta hona chahiye, aur thinker ko aksar kaam karta hona chahiye, aur dono ko gentlemen hona chahiye, sabse behtareen arth mein. Jaisa ki yeh hai, hum dono ko ungentle banate hain, ek irshya karta hai, doosra apne bhai se nafrat karta hai; aur samaj ka vishaal hissa roggrast thinkers aur dukhiaari workers se bana hai. Ab sirf mehnat (labour) se hi soch ko swasth banaya ja sakta hai, aur sirf soch se hi mehnat ko khush banaya ja sakta hai, aur dono ko bina kisi dand ke alag nahi kiya ja sakta.

Duniya is sambandh mein zyada nahi badli hai. Samaj abhi bhi thinkers aur doers ko alag karna pasand karta hai — itna zyada ki logon ke liye kisi bhi category mein girna swabhavik lagta hai. Ek aisa fireman hona kitna aam hai jo art ka historian bhi ho ya ek theologian jo car mechanic ke roop mein bhi kaam karta ho? Kitne intellectuals apne ideas ko laagu karne ke liye corporations aur governments par nirbhar karte hain aur kitne businessmen aur statesman unhe salah dene aur unki kitaabein ghost-write karne ke liye intellectuals par nirbhar karte hain?

Thinker aur doer dono ke status ko barabar kiya jana chahiye, jaisa ki un samayon mein tha jab merchants, musicians aur artisans ko poets, philosophers aur historians jitna hi ucha maana jata tha aur parinaamswarup jab samaj apni sabse jeevant, creative aur polymathic sthiti mein the. Kisi ko yeh pehchanna chahiye ki soch aur kriya dono ka vyaktigat aur samajik vikas ke liye barabar mulya hai; aur ki kisi bhi peshe mein shreshthta haasil karne ke liye alag-alag hadd tak dono ki zaroorat hoti hai.

Chinese philosopher aur polymath Zhu Xi ne kaha ki gyaan aur kriya sach mein intelligent gatividhi ke a-vibhajya (indivisible) components the. ‘Gyaan aur kriya ko hamesha ek doosre ki zaroorat hoti hai,’ unhone kaha. ‘Yeh ek aise vyakti ki tarah hai jo pairon ke bina nahi chal sakta bhale hi uske paas aankhein hon, aur jo aankhon ke bina nahi dekh sakta bhale hi uske paas pair hon.’ Kuch log apni zindagi mein ek stage par ek zyada reflective period se guzarte hain, aur doosre par ek zyada hands-on occupation pasand karte hain.

Sochne aur karne dono ki ek aantarik kshamta hum sabhi mein maujood hai, bhale hi paristhitiyon aur samajik prabhav ke anusar jhukav aur pravritti alag-alag ho sakti hai.

---

### Managing Time (Samay Ka Prabandhan)

> Achhi tarah se bitayi gayi zindagi lambi hoti hai.
> — Leonardo da Vinci

Zindagi chhoti nahi hai. Indian intellectual Khushwant Singh, jo haal hi mein apne 100th saal mein mar gaye, ant tak sakriya the aur unke paas ek lawyer, diplomat, historian, politician, novelist aur journalist ke roop mein kamyab anukramik (sequential) careers the. Singh ke paas ek lambi zindagi thi, aur physician aur transhumanist Terry Grossman ke purvanumanon ke anusar, biotechnology mein hone wali aane wali pragati aane वाले dashakon mein aging process ko kaafi dhima kar degi.

Lekin aaj bhi, achhi sthitiyon mein jeene wale ek swasth vyakti ke paas, ausatun, is planet par lagbhag sattar-paanch (75) saal hote hain. Woh lagbhag 45 ‘operational’ saal hain (yeh maanate hue ki sakriya, professional life 20 se 65 saal tak tikti hai), jo kul milakar 400,000 ghanton se thoda kam hai. Iska ek-tihaai hum sone mein bitate hain, jo humein jaagte samay ke lagbhag 270,000 ghante deta hai. Modern biologists zor dete hain ki humein din mein chhah se aath ghante ki neend ki zaroorat hoti hai, lekin duniya ke kuch sabse kamyab log qasam khate hain ki unhe kaafi kam ki zaroorat hoti hai, aur hum kam neend par prabhavshali dhang se kaam karne ke liye apne sharir ko train kar sakte hain.

Lekin agar hum bahut zyada sote bhi hain, ‘world-class kamyabi haasil karne ke liye 10,000 ghante’ ki theory ke anusar (Malcolm Gladwell dwara popular banayi gayi), hamare paas theoretically abhi bhi 27 alag-alag kshetron mein shreshthta haasil karne ke liye paryapt samay hai! Haqeeqat mein halanki, zyadatar log apni samajik aur parivarik zindagiyon ko samay ki ek mahatvapurna matra aavantit (allocate) karna pasand karte hain. Shayad ise dekhne ka ek zyada vaastavik tareeqa yeh hai ki 10,000 ghante paanch saal ki avadhi mein full-time working hours banate hain (40 hours ko 50 weeks se multiply kiya, phir 5 se multiply kiya). Retirement ki standard age se pehle, ausat vyakti ke paas unki working lives mein paanch saal ke aath periods hote hain. Iska matlab hai ki theoretically koi poori tarah se alag kshetron mein, anukramik roop se, bina kisi ativyapan (overlap) ke aath kamyab careers rakh sakta hai.

Doosre, jaise lifestyle coach Tim Ferriss — 35 saal ki umar se pehle ek record-breaking tango dancer, martial arts mein ek national champion, polyglot bestselling author, aur kamyab businessman ban kar apne point ko sabit karte hue — yahan tak sujhalte hain ki sahi drishtikon ke sath kisi ke liye bhi ‘ek saal ke andar lagbhag kisi bhi kshetra mein world-class banna’ sambhav hai. Method, woh daava karte hain, skill ko deconstruct karna aur ise vyaktigat tareeqe se reconstruct karna hai. Asal mein, Josh Kaufman jaise log sujhalte hain ki kisi bhi skill ko achhe level par seekhne ke liye sirf 20 ghante kaafi hain, kyunki tabhi learning curve apni sabse teevra sthiti mein hota hai. Kisi bhi maamle mein, hum jaante hain ki chhote periods ke andar alag-alag kshetron mein multiple uplabdhiyan José Rizal, Rudolph Fisher, Che Guevara aur Pico Della Mirandola ki zindagiyon se sambhav hain — jin sabhi ki mrityu 40 saal ki umar se pehle ho gayi thi. Mudda yeh hai ki samay, agar sahi dhang se manage kiya jaye, toh hamesha prachur hota hai aur isliye iski kami ke liye banaye gaye koi bhi bahane shayad hi kabhi maanya hote hain. Jaise Leonardo da Vinci ne kaha, ‘samay un logon ke liye kaafi lambe samay tak tikta hai jo iska upayog karte hain.’

---

## Creativity (RACHNATMAKTA / SRJANSHILTA)

> Agar koi ek shabd hai jo creative logon ko doosron se alag banata hai, toh woh hai shabd ‘complexity’ (jatilta). Ek vyakti hone ke bajaye, woh ek jan-samooh (multitude) hain.
> — Mihaly Csikszentmihalyi

Creativity hamesha se manav pragati ki reodh ki haddi (backbone) rahi hai. Toh shayad polymath ka vyakti aur samaj dono ke liye sabse bada mulya yeh hai ki yeh creativity ka kaaran aur utpad (product) dono hai. Ise samajhne ke liye, humein pehle is aam dharana par vijay paani hogi ki creativity sirf arts se judi hai (ya kam se kam isme sabse behtareen dhang se murt hoti hai) aur yeh pehchanna hoga ki yeh zyadatar manav prayason ka ek abhinna hissa hai. Chahe woh ek journalist ho jo ek story racha raha ho, ek businessman jo ek deal negotiate kar raha ho, ek soldier jo action mein saadhan-sampann (resourceful) ho, ek engineer jo ek device ka aavishkar kar raha ho, ek lawyer jo ek case bana raha ho, ek taxi driver jo traffic se bachne ke liye apna route phir se configure kar raha ho, ek humanitarian jo ek charity shuru kar raha ho ya ek plumber jo ek sink ko unblock kar raha ho — log rozmarra ke aadhar par naye tareeqon se naye concepts, sangathan, arts ke works, aur samasyaon ke samadhan banate hain. Asal mein, har an-dekhi method ya breakthrough, chahe macro ya micro scale par ho, chahe vidwatta mein ya vyavaharik jeevan mein, mool roop se ek creative act hai.

Zyadatar, agar sabhi nahi, vyakti ya toh creative hain ya unke paas hone ka potential hai. Jinhe hum aksar ‘artists’ maante hain woh sirf creativity ke instigators ya ‘enablers’ hain; kalpana aur creativity aksar art ke reader, listener ya viewer par chhod di jaati hai. Gabriel García Márquez aur Wassily Kandinsky jaise writers aur artists ka kaam utna hi kalpana ko aahwan (invoke) ya uttejit (provoke) karna hai jitna ki apne works produce karne ke liye apni khud ki kalpanaon ka upayog karna hai. Is tarah, manav jeevan ki bahu-pakshiyata ko dekhte hue, zyadatar vyaktiyon ke apni jeevan-kaal ke dauran jeevan ke vividh kshetron mein creative hone ki sambhavna hai. Zyadatar ke liye, yeh ‘polymathic creativity’ unke dwara khud pehchani bhi nahi jaati hai, doosron ki baat toh door hai, sivaay pehchane gaye polymaths ke ek chhote group ke.

---

### The Creative Force (Rachnatmak Taqat)

> Agar aapke paas ek creative dimaag hai toh aap lagbhag kisi bhi kshetra mein ideas viksit karne ki talash kar sakte hain.
> — Edward de Bono

Hiraga Ginnai nineteenth-century Japan mein paida hue the, unke andar har kaam mein innovate karne ki ek aseemit pukaar thi. Unhone shuruat mein herbal medicines banane ke naye tareeqon ko explore karke shuru kiya. Unke creative genius ne unhe Takamatsu Castle ke herb garden mein pharmacologist ke roop mein ek appointment dilaya. Unhone jaldi hi resign kar diya, ek ronin ban gaye, kyunki unhe laga ki aur bhi bahut si cheezein hain jinpar woh apni creativity laagu kar sakte the. Ginnai ne economics se shuruat ki. Unhone trade imbalances ke baare mein likha aur Japan mein aatm-nirbhar transplant industries ki shuruat ki, aur West se imports ko replace karne ke liye wool manufacturing ke methods viksit kiye, China se imports ko replace karne ke liye pottery aur Dutch olive oil imports par nirbharta kam karne ke liye olive trees ki plantation ki.

Unhone phir apni creativity ko business aur entrepreneurship ki duniya par laagu kiya — metals exports mein Japan ki taqat ko pehchante hue unhone kai business ventures shuru kiye jinmein ek mining business, ek barge service aur ek charcoal wholesale business shamil tha. Ek inventor ke roop mein unhone *erekiteru* naam ka ek electrical generator produce kiya, jo static charge produce karne ke liye foil ke ek tukde ke khilaf glass ke ek insulated cylinder ko ragadkar kaam karta tha.

Ginnai ne phir faisla kiya ki creativity ko arts ke zariya sabse behtareen dhang se vyakt kiya jata hai. Dutch se Western oil painting techniques seekhne ke baad, unhone apne khud ke oils aur dyes viksit kiye jiska upayog unhone *Painting of a Western Woman* (*Seiyō Fujin zu*) samet kai works produce karne ke liye kiya. Woh ek art teacher bhi bane aur aakhirkar fiction likhne mein apna haath aazmaya. Unhone kai novels likhe jinmein *Fūryū shidōken den* shamil hai, jise Japan ki pehli science fiction story ke roop mein quote kiya gaya hai, aur *Hōhiron* (*On Farting*) naam ka ek prasiddh satire.

Ginnai ne humein dikhaya ki creativity — chahe business, academia, arts ya rozmarra ki zindagi ke kshetra mein ho — jab dimaag ke kendra mein sachhi tarah se samahit hoti hai, toh vishayon ki ek vyapak range par laagu ho sakti hai. Woh na toh scientist the na artist na writer na inventor. Woh simply ek creator the. Douglas Hofstadter ne humein yaad dilaya ki ‘zyadatar log Leonardo da Vinci ko ek polymath ke bajaye alag-alag kshetron mein kaam karne wale creator ke roop mein sandarbhit karna pasand karenge.’ Polymath khud ko ek creator ke roop mein dekhta hai, jiski andaruni creative force ek maachis ki tarah hai jo kai mombattiyon ko jala sakti hai. ‘Jo log ek kshetra mein creative hain, woh aantarik roop se polymathic hain,’ cognitive psychologist Rand Spiro kehte hain. Artistic polymath Billy Childish nishchit roop se sehamat hain: ‘Creativity aseemit hai aur kisi bhi cheez par laagu ho sakti hai. Agar aisa nahi hai toh yeh sach nahi hai. Agar aap painting mein us tarah se shamil nahi ho sakte jaise aap cooking ya aam taur par zindagi mein shamil hote hain toh yeh shayad ek sachha raasta nahi hai.’

Polymaths ke andar is creative force ka astitva academic research ke zariya baar-baar pusht kiya gaya hai. 1926 mein, psychologist Catherine Cox ne apne research ke zariya paaya ki vyakti jitna zyada creative tha, unke interests utne hi zyada vividh the. Psychologist Eliot Dole Hutchinson ne zor diya ki asli creative genius ki koi seema nahi hoti. ‘Yeh koi hadsa nahi hai,’ woh vyapak adhyayan ke baad nishkarsh nikalati hain, ‘ki bade dimaagon mein peshe gayab ho jaate hain . . . aise aadmi scientists, artists, musicians nahi hain, jab woh utne hi achhe se kuch aur bhi ho sakte the. Woh creators hain.’

Polymathy aur creativity ke beech yeh sambandh aaj psychologists ke beech tezi se swikar kiya gaya sach ban raha hai, jaise American psychologist Robert Root-Bernstein ne dekha: ‘Psychologists dwara lambe samay se yeh dekha gaya hai ki jo log innovative hote hain woh gatividhiyon ki ek vyapak range mein bhaag lene ki pravritti rakhte hain aur doosre logon ki tulna mein apni gatividhiyon mein ek ucha level ka skill viksit karte hain.’

Intelligence ki tarah hi, psychologists ke beech is baat par lambi bahas rahi hai ki kya creativity ‘domain-general’ hai ya ‘domain-specific’. Pehla drishtikon yeh manta hai ki jo skills creative performance ki taraf le jaate hain woh mool roop se sabhi domains mein wahi, ya kam se kam bahut samaan hain. Yeh is baat ka samarthan karta hai ki hum sabhi ke andar ek aantarik creative force hai. Baad wala — jiska paksh psychologists Baer aur Kaufman ne liya — tarka deta hai ki kisi vishesh domain mein creativity ke liye zaroori cognitive skills us domain ke liye vishisht (specific) hain aur sabhi domains par laagu nahi kiye ja sakte. Phir bhi, woh nishkarsh nikalte hain ki mool roop se har kisi ke paas ‘multi-creative’ potential hai.

---

### Hybridity (Sankarta / Mishran)

> Diversity (vividhta) sach mein khoj aur aavishkar ki prakriya ko samriddh karti hai.
> — Fraser Stoddart, scientist aur Nobel laureate

Hybridity kisi bhi creative output ka kaaran, prakriya aur parinaam hai. Is arth mein, har cheez ek hybrid hai. Avadharnakrit roop se (philosophy aur science dono mein), do ya do se zyada alag, anokhi entities ka fusion ek aur alag, anokhi entity produce karta hai. Yeh haqeeqat ki baad wali fusion se pehle astitva mein nahi thi yeh pushti karti hai ki ise banaya gaya tha aur fusion khud ek creative process thi. Chemistry mein ek creative experiment, udaharan ke liye, mein do ya do se zyada elements shamil hone chahiye: kisi ek chemical element par prayog karne se aap sirf itna hi kar paayenge — aap ise ubaal sakte hain, freeze kar sakte hain, melt kar sakte hain — lekin bas itna hi. Do ya do se zyada elements ko milane se ek poori tarah se naya compound substance ya element banane ki anumati milti hai. Toh hybridity kai, aapatat: asambandhit ghatnaon ke sanshleshan (synthesis) se nikalne wala creative outcome hai.

Yeh species, substances ya ideas par laagu ho sakta hai. Yeh samay ki shuruat se ek jaari prakriya rahi hai, chahe isme atoms, cells, genes, vichar, bhashayein, devices, concepts ya materials shamil hon. 'Hybrid' shabd ka upayog shuruat mein biology ke kshetra mein podhon aur jaanwaron mein 'cross-fertilisation' ya 'cross-pollination' ko sandarbhit karne ke liye kiya gaya tha. 19th century se iska upayog linguistics aur racial theory mein kiya jane laga, jab yeh maujooda bhashaon aur races ke blend se naye bhashaon aur races ke uday ka varnan karta tha. Lekin 'fusion' ya 'synthesis' ke roop mein hybridity ka vichar hamesha duniya bhar ke alag-alag samajon mein kisi na kisi roop mein maujood raha hai.

Toh hybridity ka concept polymathy par kaise laagu hota hai? Manavta ke kuch sabse bade aur sabse prabhavshali ideas apni prakriti se hi hybrid constructs hain. *Ideas That Changed the World* ke lekhak Felipe Fernández-Armesto ne nishkarsh nikala ki mahatvapurna ideas alag-alag kshetron ke alag-alag ideas ke nirantar synthesis ke zariya aate hain: ‘ideas "breed" karte hain,’ woh kehte hain, ‘woh ek doosre ke sampark mein aane par guna (multiply) hote hain.’ Yeh dimaagon ke ek samuday mein ya ek hi dimaag ke andar bhi ho sakta hai.

Knowledge ke kshetron ya 'disciplines' ki hybridity ko aaj interdisciplinarity ke roop mein sandarbhit kiya jata hai. Yeh ek fashionable trend hai, jise modern academic aur professional halkon mein kam se kam dikhawe (lip service) ke zariya badhava diya jata hai. Jaise sochne ke purane tareeqe (ya gyaan ke jeb) baasi, aprasangik, sakht ya bahishkarkari lagne lagte hain, unhe ek naye, hybrid discipline banane ke liye naye configurations aur alliances mein phir se sangathit kiya jata hai jo nayi sthiti ke liye zyada prasangik hai. Chahe dimaagon ke ek group ke beech ho ya ek hi dimaag ke andar, interdisciplinarity ka uddeshya kisi bhi diye gaye prashn ya samasya ko ek multi-dimensional tareeqe se dekhna hai, aur aisa karte hue diye gaye ghatna ki badi samajh ki khoj mein mahatvapurna connections banana hai.

---

### Connections (Sambandh)

> Padhai ka ek hissa doosre par roshni daalta hai.
> — Isaac Barrow

Kamyabi bahut aksar doosre kshetra mein aage badhne ke liye ek kshetra se ideas udhaar lene ke parinaamswarup aayi hai. Yeh art ya science ke liye utna hi sach hai jitna ki vidwatta ya vyavaharik prayason ke liye. Albert Einstein aur Charles Darwin bina kisi vivad ke geniuses hain, phir bhi humne ab khoj liya hai ki woh woh tunnel-visioned specialists nahi the jaisa ki hum aksar maan lete hain. Iske ulat, tathya (aur unke apne swikriti) sujhalte hain ki yeh unke shauk, vividh backgrounds aur additional pursuits ke sath banaye gaye connections the jinne unhe unke respective core disciplines mein breakthrough karne mein saksham banaya. Louis Pasteur ki microbe ki krantikari khoj sirf isliye sambhav hui kyunki Pasteur ke paas crystallography mein bhi ek background tha, jisne unhe microbe ko dekhne ke liye ek microscopic lens ki zaroorat ko samajhne ki anumati di. ‘Polymaths apni gatividhiyon par ek mahatvapurna hadd tak mahaarat haasil karte hain aur unke beech ke buniyaadi connections ko mahsoos karte hain,’ psychologist Robert Root-Bernstein ne dekha.

Baar-baar, innovations ek taaza aankh se ya kisi doosre discipline se aate hain. Prasiddh nineteenth-century English scientist William Rowan Hamilton ek physicist, astronomer aur mathematician the jinhone apne disciplines ke interconnectedness ka upayog har ek mein shreshthta haasil karne ke liye kiya. Is synthesis ke parinaamswarup unhone algebra, classical mechanics aur optics mein bade breakthroughs kiye. ‘Unhone [polymaths ne] vishesh disciplines mein yogdan apne vyapak hiton ke kaaran diya, unke baavjood nahi,’ Root-Bernstein zor dete hain. Aur yeh inhi jaise polymaths hain, woh ashwast hain, jinke hum bhavishya ke sabse bade synthetic breakthroughs ke karzdar honge. Is baat ki pushti historian Felipe Fernández-Armesto dwara ki jaati hai. ‘Kisi ek kshetra ke sabse behtareen thinkers doosron mein bhi achhe hote hain, jo aapas mein samriddh karne wale hote hain.’ Martin Kemp, Leonardo da Vinci par duniya ke pramukh adhikari, pushti karte hain ki ‘polymaths ki jo visheshataayein aapko aamtaur par milengi unmein se ek yeh hai ki woh links dekhte hain jahan hum algaav dekhte hain — Leonardo ke liye har cheez judi hui hai.’

Chahe hum kitna bhi specialised hone ka daava karein, hum creative, novel samadhan dhoondhne ke liye (chahe chetan ya a-chetan roop se) doosre 'fields' se lagatar ideas udhaar le rahe hain. Iska matlab business mein paise kamane aur kamyab hone ke liye ek specialist skill (chahe law, carpentry ya health ho) ka upayog karna ho sakta hai; government policy, scholarly debate ya daily life mein yogdan dene ke liye ek soldier ke roop mein antar-drishtiyan; cooking mein science ka upayog; economics ko samjhane ke liye psychology; aur ityaadi. Skills, gyaan aur anubhavon ki ek vividh range rakhna badi tasveer ko dekhne ki kshamta ko utprerit karta hai — jo badle mein creative breakthrough ki anumati deta hai. Is link ko Steve Jobs dwara uchit roop se samjaya gaya tha, jo shayad twenty-first century ke sabse prabhavshali creator the:

> Creativity sirf cheezon ko jodna hai. Jab aap creative logon se poochhte hain ki unhone koi cheez kaise ki, toh woh thoda guilty mahsoos karte hain kyunki unhone sach mein aisa nahi kiya, unhone bas kuch dekha. Kuch samay baad yeh unke liye saaf laga. Yeh isliye tha kyunki woh apne hue anubhavon ko jodne aur nayi cheezon ko synthesise karne mein saksham the. Aur kaaran jo woh aisa karne mein saksham the woh yeh tha ki unke paas doosre logon ki tulna mein zyada anubhav the ya unhone apne anubhavon par zyada socha tha. Durbhagya se, woh kaafi durlabh vastu hai. Hamari industry mein bahut se logon ke paas bahut vividh anubhav nahi rahe hain. Toh unke paas jodne ke liye paryapt dots nahi hain, aur woh samasya par vyapak drishtikon ke bina behadd linear samadhanon ke sath samapt hote hain. Manav anubhav ki kisi ki samajh jitni vyapak hogi, hamare paas utna hi behtar design hoga.
> — Steve Jobs, *Wired*, February 1995

Ek synthesis, aisa lagta hai, subconscious mein hota hai jo kisi tarah humein aakhirkar creative ideas par pahonchne mein saksham banata hai. Is dharana ko pachas saal se bhi pehle creative advertising mogul James Webb Young dwara unki prasiddh kitaab *A Technique for Producing New Ideas* mein pehchana gaya tha: ‘Ek idea purane tatvon ka naya combination hi hai,’ unhone kaha. . . . ‘Insight alag-alag time scales par operate hone wali dimaag ki sthitiyon aur prakriyaon ki ek shrinkhla ka charam roop hai. . . . Kuch dimaagon ke liye har tathya gyaan ka ek alag tukda hai. Doosron ke liye yeh gyaan ki chain mein ek link hai.’

Yeh nishchit roop se modern interdisciplinary scholar aur intellectual polymath Vaclav Smil ke liye sach hai, jo tarka dete hain ki ‘asli duniya ki jatilta kai perspectives (historical appraisals samet) ki maang karti hai aur kai linkages ko trace karne ki zaroorat rakhti hai.’ Yeh aaj vaigyanik breakthroughs ke aage rehne walon ke liye vishesh roop se sach hai. Scientific polymath Ray Kurzweil hamare daur ki badi takneeki samasyaon ke samadhan ka shrey connections banane ki is kshamta ko dete hain: ‘Tezi se, samasyaon ke samadhan multiple fields ke intersection par paaye jaate hain. Udaharan ke liye, speech recognition mein mere kaam mein speech science, linguistics, mathematical modelling, psycho-acoustics aur computer science shamil the.’

Psychologists ne ise lagatar pehchana hai. Lewis Terman ne paaya ki aise bahut kam vyakti hain jo ek ya doosre kshetron mein ausat se zyada kshamta pradarshit kiye bina ek kshetra mein badi shreshthta haasil karte hain. Roberta Milgram ne paaya ki kisi bhi discipline mein career ki kamyabi IQ, grades, standardised test scores ya inke kisi bhi combination ki tulna mein ek ya zyada bauddhika roop se utprerit aur gahan avocational interests se behtar correlated hai.

Historians ne bhi wahi paaya hai. Historian of science Paul Cranfield ne rekhankit kiya ki mid-nineteenth century mein biophysics ki khoj karne wale group ke beech, udaharan ke liye, har vyakti dwara aage badhaye gaye avocations ki sankhya aur range, uske dwara ki gayi badi khojon ki sankhya, aur ek scientist ke roop mein uski baad ki sthiti ke beech ek seedha sambandh tha. Ek aur historian, Minor Myers ne Renaissance se lekar modern era tak ki kai badi hastiyon ki zindagiyon ka adhyayan kiya aur viksit kshamtaon ki range aur kisi vyakti ke yogdanon ki vividhta aur mahatva ke beech ek sambandh khoja. Unhone nishkarsh nikala ki gyaan aur skill set ki vividhta jitni badi hogi jise ek vyakti integrate kar sakta hai, parinaami novel aur upayogi permutations ki sankhya utni hi badi hogi.

Writer Andrew Robinson ki polymaths Thomas Young, Rabindranath Tagore aur Satyajit Ray ki zindagiyon mein jaanch (unhone har ek ki biographies likhi hain) dikhati hai ki polymathy aur creativity ke beech ek spasht link hai. ‘Ashadharan creators ki ek mahatvapurna sankhya ne ek se zyada domain mein kaam kiya hai’ woh kehte hain, Young aur 20th-century ke vilakshan Englishman Michael Ventris dwara decipherment mein breakthroughs ka udaharan dete hue: ‘Dono mamlon mein — Ventris aur Young — unke decipherment breakthroughs disparate domains ke unke gyaan par nirbhar the, jo unke vidwan rivalon ke paas nahi tha.’ ‘Unke sabse behtareen ideas,’ unhone likha, ‘unki versatility se ubhre.’ Is baat ki pushti educationalist aur creativity thinker Ken Robinson dwara ki jaati hai, jo kehte hain, ‘creativity bhavna aur soch ke beech, aur alag-alag disciplinary boundaries aur ideas ke kshetron ke aar-paar interactions par nirbhar karti hai.’

---

### The Art-Science Intersection (Kala-Vigyaan Ka Chauraha)

> Sabse bade vaigyanik artists bhi hain.
> — Albert Einstein

Shayad interdisciplinarity ke mahatva ka sabse behtareen pradarshan art aur science ke beech ka kaafi hadd tak a-saraha gaya sambandh hai. Asal mein, science ka upayog aksar art mein shreshthta haasil karne ke liye ek zariye ke roop mein kiya jata hai, aur iska vipreet bhi. Art-science intersection se nikalne wali creativity poore itihaas mein saaf rahi hai. Leonardo ne *The Last Supper* jaise apne paintings mein geometrical perfection haasil karne ke liye mathematics ka upayog kiya, jabki Einstein ne apni General Theory of Relativity viksit karte waqt apni kalpana ko badhava dene ke liye apne music (woh violin bajate the) ka upayog kiya. Dono ne apne kaam par in 'baahari' prabhavon ke mahatva par vishesh note kiya. Dono ke beech maana gaya avadharnakrit antar yeh hai ki 'artists' sambhavid duniyaon ke sath kaam karte hain jabki 'scientists' 'asli' duniya mein kaam karne ke liye majboor hain. Sach mein, hum jaante hain ki kalpana ka vaastavikta ke liye ek mulya hai aur vaastavikta ka kalpana ke liye ek mulya hai.

Einstein aur Leonardo jaise geniuses ke liye yeh majboot sambandh kaam karne ka kaaran yeh hai ki science aur art ek doosre ke khali-pan ko bharte hain aur ek doosre ki duniyaon mein springboards ke roop mein kaam karte hain. Asal mein Einstein jaise sabse pioneering aur creative scientists sehamat the ki ‘kalpana gyaan se zyada mahatvapurna hai, kyunki gyaan us sabhi tak seemit hai jo hum ab jaante hain aur samajhte hain jabki kalpana poori duniya ko aur jo kuch bhi kabhi jaan-ne aur samajhne ke liye hoga us sabhi ko samet ti hai.’

American psychologist Bernice Eiduson dwara kiye gaye ek adhyayan ke anusar jiska title *Sigma Xi Survey* tha jo kai Nobel laureates ke testimonials par aadharit tha, zyadatar bade scientists ke paas aksar multiple avocational interests hote hain. 1901 aur 2002 ke beech literature mein Nobel laureates ke gahan vishleshan mein paaya gaya ki bade artists aur writers ke paas aksar multiple avocational interests hote hain. Unhone paaya ki science laureates lab ke baahar atyadhik kamyab the: aadhe se zyada ke paas kam se kam ek artistic avocation tha, aur lagbhag sabhi ke paas chess se lekar kide-makode ik इकट्ठा karne tak ek tikau shauk tha; ek-chauthaai musicians the; aur 18 percent drawing ya painting jaise visual arts ka abhyas karte the. Yeh laureates ausat scientist ki tulna mein gaane, naachne ya acting karne ke 25 guna zyada sambhavna rakhte hain; visual artist hone ke 17 guna zyada; kavita aur literature likhne ke 12 guna zyada; woodwork ya koi doosra craft karne ke 8 guna zyada; musician hone ke 4 guna zyada; aur photographer hone ke do guna zyada sambhavna rakhte hain.

Aise kai udaharan hain. Anatomist Ronald Ross ek ullekhniya novelist, playwright, poet aur painter bhi the; physicist Murray Gell-Mann languages ke itihaas, creative thinking ki psychology, ornithology aur archaeology par bhi ek adhikari hain. Iska matlab hai ki artistic avocation ka general intelligence par prabhav ho sakta hai. Asal mein, Einstein jaise so-called specialists ne yahan tak sujhav diya hai ki unke doosre interests vaastav mein unke mool kshetra mein unke kaam mein yogdan dene aur use badhane mein ek mahatvapurna bhoomika nibhate hain. ‘Main aksar sangeet mein sochta hoon . . .’ unhe kehte hue record kiya gaya hai.

Kai scientific polymaths ne science ki vividh shakhaon ki apni samajh mein yogdan dene ke liye art ka upayog kiya hai. Ismail al-Jazari, 12th-century ke prachur inventor aur engineer, ne apne designs ke sundar miniatures produce kiye, apne prasiddh notebooks mein jaanch aur prastuti dono ke auzar ke roop mein art ka upayog kiya, jiske baare mein kuch log kehte hain ki usne Leonardo da Vinci ko inspire kiya. 18th-century ke English scientist Erasmus Darwin, ek physician, botanist, biologist, cosmologist aur engineer, ne prakriti par apne chintan ko vyakt karne ke liye prasiddh roop se poetry ka upayog kiya. Bengali scientist Jagadish Chandra Bose ne biologist, physicist aur botanist ke roop mein shreshthta haasil ki aur phir vaigyanik khoj ke saadhan ke roop mein storytelling ka upayog kiya, aur aisa karte hue modern science fiction ke pitaon mein se ek bane. Austrian scientist Ernst Haeckel, ek physician, zoologist, biologist aur philosopher of science, ne sundar paintings aur drawings ki ek shrinkhla ke zariya prakriti ki apni sarahana ko vyakt kiya. Isi tarah, Afro-American George Washington Carver — jinhe *Time* magazine dwara 1941 mein plastics, paints, dyes aur gasoline samet unke kai krishi aavishkaron ke liye 'Black Leonardo' kaha gaya tha — ne ek plant painter ke roop mein apne pehle ke career ke zariya botany ki apni samajh ko badhaya. Spanish neuroscientist aur Nobel laureate Santiago Ramón y Cajal neurons ke apne adbhut drawings ke liye barabar jaane jaate hain. American Samuel Morse barabar matra mein ek inventor aur painter the. Yeh list chalti rehti hai.

Isi tarah, kai artists ne artistic masterpieces produce karne ke liye vaigyanik concepts ka upayog kiya hai. Jazz musician John Coltrane ka mathematical 'Tone Circle' model ek behtareen udaharan hai. Salvador Dalí ne *The Great Masturbator* aur *Galatea of the Spheres* jaise ashadharan paintings produce karne ke liye Freudian psychoanalysis aur Einsteinian atomic physics ke apne jatil gyaan ka upayog kiya. Desmond Morris, modern surrealist painting ke ek aur mahatvapurna vyakti, ne apne atyadhik saraye gaye artwork ko soochit karne ke liye zoology mein apni mahaarat ka upayog kiya. ‘Agar mere paintings kuch aur nahi karte, toh woh yeh pradarshit karne ka kaam karenge ki aise titles ("science" aur "art") bhramak hain,’ woh kehte hain. ‘Painting sirf ek craft nahi hai, yeh vyaktigat research ka ek roop hai. . . . Vaastav mein aaj log scientists ya artists nahi hain . . . woh explorers ya non-explorers hain, aur unki explorations ka context doosre darje ke mahatva ka hai.’

Art historian Kenneth Clark ne dekha ki art aur science dono mool roop se ek hi kalpana-atmak sroton se ubharte hain: ‘Art aur science . . . waise nahi hain jaisa maana jata tha, do vipreet gatividhiyan, balki vaastav mein manav dimaag ki kai ek hi kshamtaon par nirbhar karti hain. Aakhri raste par, har ek kalpana par nirbhar karta hai. Artist aur scientist dono hi dhundhle dhang se samjhe gaye ideas ko murt roop dene ki koshish kar rahe hain.’ Is baat ki pushti ek haal hi ke psychological study dwara ki jaati hai, jo nishkarsh nikalata hai: ‘scientists aur artists aksar apne creative kaam ki aadatton ka ek hi tareeqon se, ek hi bhasha ka upayog karke varnan karte hain, aur aam, transdisciplinary mental toolkits par nirbhar karte hain jinmein observe karna, imagine karna, abstract karna, pattern banana, body thinking, empathise karna aur ityaadi shamil hain.’

Industrial revolution ke disciplinary aur professional compartmentalisation ke baad se art aur science ko wapas ek sath lane ki zaroorat ko, jaise pehle zikr kiya gaya tha, scientist-novelist C.P. Snow dwara unke 1959 ke ‘The Two Cultures’ lectures mein prasiddh roop se rekhankit kiya gaya tha, jisne art (humanities samet) aur science ke a-swasth divergence ke khilaf chetaya tha. 1990 mein, artist-inventor-psychologist Todd Siler ne do duniyaon ke beech ke interconnectedness ki pehchan mein ‘ArtScience’ shabd racha, halanki na toh yeh shabd aur na hi yeh concept poori tarah se chala. Haal hi mein, Google ke CEO Eric Schmidt, jo duniya ke sabse creative aur prabhavshali sangathanon mein se ek hai, ne zor diya ki modern duniya mein technological innovation ko badhava dene ke liye is sambandh ko phir se jagane ki zaroorat hai. ‘Humein art aur science ko wapas ek sath lane ki zaroorat hai,’ unhone British technologists se ek haal hi ke bhashan mein kaha. ‘Victorian era ke gloriyous dinon ko yaad karein. Yeh ek aisa samay tha jab wahi log poetry likhte the aur bridges banate the.’

---

### Brain Chemistry (Dimaag Ki Chemistry)

Jeong Yak-yong, Korea ke King Jeongjo ke 18th-century ke government aide, ne ek civil engineer ke roop mein seva ki (unhone modern Suwon mein Hwaseong Fortress design kiya) aur phir Gyeonggi provinces ke liye special envoy ke roop mein. Woh ek vilakshan kavi bhi the jinhone nau saal ki umar mein hi likhna shuru kar diya tha, aur jiski poetry ne 19th-century ke tea revival mein ek mukhya bhoomika nibhayi. Lekin Catholics ka utpeedan karne wale naye regime ke baad, Yak-yong ko exile (desh-nikala) mein majboor hona pada. Wahan, unke baare mein kaha jata hai ki unhone politics, philosophy aur economy se lekar natural sciences, medicine aur music tak ke vishayon par 500 volumes likhe. Exile se wapas lautne ke baad, unhone jurisprudence (*Heumheumsinseo*), linguistics (*Aeongakbi*), diplomacy (*Sadekoryesanbo*), shasan ki kala (*Mongminsimseo*), aur administration (*Gyeongsesiryeong*) par apne sabse mahatvapurna works publish kiye. Apni zindagi mein alag-alag points par aur alag-alag paristhitiyon ke dauran khud ko phir se invent karne ki unki kshamta humein dikhati hai ki Yak-yong ke dimaag mein ek ashadharan 'plasticity' thi.

Dimaag mein lagbhag ek sau billion neurons hote hain, har ek ke paas ek hazaar synapses hote hain, aur har ek ek doosre ke sath lagbhag anant tareeqon se interact karta hai (kuch logon ka andaza hai ki interconnections ki sankhya lagbhag ek sau trillion hai!). Yeh na sirf manav soch ki pure kshamta ko pradarshit karta hai balki iski anant vividhta ko bhi. British psychiatrist Iain McGilchrist dimaag ke anant interconnectedness ko samjhate hain:

> Dimaag ek ekal, ekrat, atyadhik dynamic system hai. Dimaag mein kahin bhi hone wali ghatnaayein doosre ilaqon se judi hoti hain, aur unke liye sambhavid parinaam hote hain, jo us shuruati ghatna par prati-kriya de sakte hain, ise phelaye sakti hain, badha sakti hain ya viksit kar sakti hain, ya vaikalpik roop se ise kisi tareeqe se thik kar sakti hain, ise rok sakti hain, ya santulan (equilibrium) ko phir se sthapit karne ka prayas kar sakti hain. Koi 'bits' nahi hain, sirf networks hain, pathways ka ek lagbhag anant array.

Agar hum in interconnections ko ek prati second ki dar se ginna shuru karein toh aap ab se tees million saal baad bhi gin rahe honge, aur agar unhe suljhaya jaye, toh dhaga itna lambaa hoga ki Prithvi ke do baar chakkar laga sake! Aisa hamare sochne ke tareeqon ka anant-pan hai.

Interconnectivity ke sath-sath, hamare dimaag mein (jaise pehle varnit hai) plasticity bhi hoti hai; baad wala na sirf versatility ko samjhata hai, balki hybridity ko bhi. Dimaag ki chemistry aur structure plasticity ke zariya anubhav dwara bhaautik roop se badal jaate hain — dimaag koi fixed mass nahi hai jo vyavhaar ko aakar deta hai; vyavhaar bhi dimaag ko aakar deta hai. Hamare pichhle zyadatar anubhav hamari chetan memory se khogaye hain, lekin woh anubhav hamari lives ko un tareeqon se aakar dena jaari rakhte hain jinhe contemporary cognitive science samajhne ki koshish karti hai. Hamare anubhavon ki vividhta isliye tay karti hai ki hum kaun hain — hum apne anubhavon aur apne dwara ik इकट्ठा kiye gaye gyaan ka utpad ban jaate hain, shayad waise hi jaise Hindu karma ke concept mein vishwās karte hain (jabki baad wala multiple lifetimes ke system ko sandarbhit karta hai, yeh concept ek hi life par bhi utna hi laagu hota hai). University of Sussex mein Neuroscience ke Professor Michael O'Shea iski pushti karte hain:

> Neurons ek electronic circuit mein solder joints ke biological equivalent ke sath ek doosre se nahi jude hote. Joints fixed nahi hote balki hamare tezi se badalane wale vatavaran mein sabse haal ke anubhavon ke anusar lagatar adjust hote hain. Is tarah vyavhaar hamare tezi se badalane wale vatavaran mein sabse haal ke anubhavon ke anusar lagatar adjust hota hai.

Neuroscientists dwara neural plasticity ke roop mein sandarbhit is prakriya ke polymathy ke liye gehre parinaam hote hain. ‘Sirf ek cheez karna galati ho sakti hai,’ Harvard Medical School mein neurology ke professor Alvaro Pascual-Leone kehte hain. ‘Agar aap multiple cheezon ka abhyas karte hain toh aap vaastav mein unmein se kisi ek cheez mein behtar ho jaate hain.’ Kisi vishisht intellectual discipline, professional career, shauk ko aage badhana ya sirf ek vishesh anubhav rakhna, woh ishara karte hain, na sirf kisi poori tarah se alag cheez ke aage badhne ya shamil hone par ek nishchit prabhav daalega, balki us prabhav ke labhdayak hone ki bhi sambhavna hai. Jab koi vyakti pehle wale se alag kshetra ko aage badhane ke liye switch karta hai, toh uske neurons pehle se alag-thalag ilaqon ke beech naye pathways banate hain. Hamare skills jitne zyada vividh honge, yaani, upayog mein aane wale neural pathways utne hi zyada vividh honge.

*The Medici Effect* ke lekhak Frans Johansson kehte hain ki hamara dimaag ‘ek aisi jagah hai jahan alag-alag cultures, domains, aur disciplines ek hi point ki taraf ek sath behte hain. Woh connect hote hain, sthapit concepts ko takrane aur combine hone ki anumati dete hain, aakhirkar naye, krantikari ideas ka ek jan-samooh banate hain.’ Modern research ne pradarshit kiya hai ki dimaag naye anubhavon ke anusar adjust hone, naye jaankari seekhne aur naye memories banane ke liye naye neural pathways banana aur maujooda ko badalna jaari rakhta hai. Neuroscientist Donald Hebb ke anusar, ‘Dimaag reorganise hota hai. Alag-alag ilaqe sambhal sakte hain aur yeh wahi tasks karne ke liye alag-alag pathways, alag-alag networks ka upayog kar sakta hai. Toh potential wahan hai.’ Jaise-jaise hum bade hote hain, naye cheezein try karne ke bajaye jo hamare dimaag ko chunauti dengi, jo hum pehle se jaante hain use majboot karne ki ek aam pravritti hoti hai.

Creativity, aisa lagta hai, polymathy ko poshit karti hai, aur iska vipreet bhi. ‘Polymathy creativity ka samarthan karti hai,’ cognitive psychologist Rand Spiro kehte hain, ‘kyunki creativity ko aap jis domain par kaam kar rahe hain usse baahar se ideas, analogies, patterns aur perspectives ki zaroorat hoti hai.’ Yeh humein wapas right-left brain dichotomy par le aata hai. Kai neuroscientists creativity ka shrey inferior superior temporal gyrus ko dete hain. Ek creative moment ke dauran, left hemisphere shayad hi prati-kriya deta hai lekin right zyada active ho jata hai, gamma waves mein ek chonkane wali badhotari dikhata hai. Left hemisphere par brain cells ke paas chhote dendrites hote hain, jo paas se jaankari khinchne ke liye upayogi hote hain, lekin right par cells kaafi aage tak shakhaayein nikalte hain aur door ke asambandhit ideas ko ek sath khinchte hain.

Dimaag ke ashadharan bauddhika aur creative potential ke baare mein aage ke ughadana chetna ki badli hui sthitiyon (altered states of consciousness) aur genius ke mukti se aate hain jo neurological conditions, spiritual experiences, psychedelic drugs aur cognitive enhancers (‘smart drugs’) se nikalte lagte hain. Sudden savant syndrome aur Ayahuascan art ke cases humein sambhavnaon ki yaad dilate hain, lekin aksar ek achhi tarah se vichar ki gayi shiksha ka dimaag par ek transformational prabhav ho sakta hai.

---

## Unity (EKTA / EKATMATIKTA)

> Main bilkul bhi kisi aise vaastavikta (reality) ki kalpana nahi karta jo bina kisi asli ekta (genuine unity) ke ho.
> — Gottfried Leibniz

---

### The Bigger Picture (Badi Tasveer)

Is idea se ek step aage badhna ki kuch kshetron ke beech connections hain, yeh sujhana hai ki sabhi kshetr a-suljhe roop se jude hue hain — jaisa ki Aristotle ke ‘Tree of Life’ aur haal hi mein Fritjof Capra ke ‘Web of Life’ dwara pradarshit kiya gaya hai. *Esquire* ke editor, A.J. Jacobs, jinhone apne ‘lifestyle experiments’ mein se ek ke hisse ke roop mein poori *Encyclopaedia Britannica* padhne ko chuna, ne nishkarsh nikala ki: ‘har cheez six-degrees-of-separation game ke ek worldwide version ki tarah judi hui hai.’ Edwin Hubble ne pehchana ki gyaan ka anubhav aur khandikaran simply ek manav-nirmit prakriya hai, jabki vaastavikta vaastav mein ek ekrat poora (one unified whole) hai. ‘Apni paanch indriyon se sajjit,’ unhone kaha, ‘insaan apne aaspas ke brāhmāṇḍa ko explore karta hai aur is adventure ko Science kehta hai.’

Kshetron aur disciplines ke khandikaran aur branding ke sath manav ka yeh junoon — jo aanshik roop se ‘information explosion’ dwara utprerit hai — samaj dwara aur is tarah manav dimaag dwara ek apekshakrit haal hi ka apnaav hai. European Enlightenment se pehle, disciplinary boundaries sakhti se fixed nahi thin aur isliye ‘bhatakne ke khatron’ ke bina padhai ke multiple fields ko aage badhana aasan tha. Asal mein ‘bhatakne’ jaisa koi vichar nahi tha — ek aisi pehchan maujood thi ki cosmos mein har cheez a-suljhe roop se ek aise tareeqe se judi hui thi jiske liye iske multiple pehluon ki jaanch ki zaroorat thi. Jeevan aur soch ke prati yeh samagra (holistic) drishtikon pehle ke polymaths ke liye norm maana jata tha ‘jinke liye gyaanon ki ekta, ek aam ped par shakhaayein, cosmos ki ekta ko darshati thi.’

Manav itihaas mein har samaj ki haavi philosophy ne alag-alag kaaranon se hi sahi, polymathy ko encourage karne mein kuch bhoomika nibhayi hai. Lekin time aur place ke parwah kiye bina, in sabhi vishwa-drishtiyon mein se ek aam dhaga guzarta hai. Yeh dhaga holistic drishtikon hai — ek aisa jo Ancient Egyptian, Greek, Roman, Christian, European Renaissance aur West African Yoruba philosophies mein, saath hi Confucian, Taoist, Islamic, Hindu, Polynesian aur Mayan cosmological frameworks mein paaya ja sakta hai. Science ke historian aur Islamic philosopher Seyyed Hossein Nasr iski pushti karte hain:

> Koi keh sakta hai ki sabhi Islamic sciences — aur, zyada aam taur par kahein toh, sabhi medieval aur ancient cosmological sciences — ka uddeshya astitva mein maujood sabhi cheezon ki ekta aur antar-sambandh (interrelatedness) ko dikhana hai, taaki, cosmos ki ekta par chintan karte hue, insaan ko Divine Principle ki ekta ki taraf le jaya ja sake, jiski tasveer Nature ki ekta hai.

Udaharan ke liye, Tawheed ka Islamic concept, jo God ke ekal-pan (oneness) aur cosmos ki ekta ki taraf ishara karta hai, wahi tha jisne Muslim polymathy ke ek poore daur ko prerit kiya. Nasr spasht karte hain:

> Arabic mein Tawheed na sirf ekta balki ‘ek banana’ — integration (ekikaran) ka matlab rakhta hai. Toh integration Islamic Revelation ke sabse mahatvapurna parinaamon mein se ek tha. Quran khud aur iska ekta ka sandesh hi kaaran hai ki classical Islamic civilisation ne hamesha polymathy ke mahatva par zor diya aur yahi kaaran tha ki isne itne saare polymaths produce kiye.

Jabki Muslims vaastav mein *muwahidun* ya ‘unifiers’ ke roop mein jaane gaye, is Divine Principle ki aisi ekta ko zyadatar samajon mein pehchana gaya tha: Aboriginal tribes ke liye yeh *arungquiltha* tha, Polynesian tribes ke liye *mana*, Mesoamerican tribes ke liye *waken*. Jaise Fritjof Capra, physicist aur *Tao of Physics* ke lekhak pushti karte hain:

> Sabhi ghatnaon ka buniyaadi antar-sambandh (interconnectedness), jo polymaths jo systemic thinkers hain unki mukhya antar-drishti hai, Hinduism aur Buddhism se lekar Taoism tak Eastern spiritual traditions ki bhi buniyaadi antar-drishti hai.

Thirteenth-century ke Christian philosopher aur polymath Thomas Aquinas ne varnan kiya ki kaise theology hamare gyaan ke sabhi kshetron ko ek sath la sakti hai, ‘God ke apne gyaan ka ek jhalak pradan karti hai, jo har cheez ka ekal aur saral drishya hai.’ Isse prabhavit hokar, Cambridge scholar Keith Eyeons, *The Theology of Everything* ke lekhak, samjhate hain ki Divine Unity ko Christian polymaths dwara mool srot ke roop mein kaise dekha gaya tha, aur kaise usne swabhavik roop se har cheez ke antar-sambandh ka nishkarsh nikala:

> God brāhmāṇḍa ke un tarkasangat dhanchon (rational structures) ka srot hai jinaki jaanch vaigyanik karte hain. God ke paas ek mahima aur ek sundarta bhi hai jo aanshik roop se srishti (creation) ke zariya dekhi jaati hai. Iske alawa, God pyaar hai, aur Trinity mein Christian vishwas sujhatta hai ki God ki teen-guna prakriti ke andar pyaar ke rishte hain. Isliye manav rishte aur samuday us divine consciousness ke charitra ka kuch hissa darshate hain jo brāhmāṇḍa ko aakar deta hai. Un darshanik vicharon ko milane se ishara milta hai ki, udaharan ke liye, physics, art, aur dosti sabhi jude hue hain.

Chahe Leonardo da Vinci, hamare quintessential polymath, ne apne Christian vishwason ke kaaran wahi drishtikon rakha ya nahi, yeh saaf nahi hai. Hum jaante hain, halanki, ki woh East ke holistic ideas mein ruchi rakhte the. Leonardo par duniya ke pramukh expert Martin Kemp ne iski pushti ki:

> Leonardo ne doosri sanskritiyon ke baare mein poochhtachh karne ke liye samudri kaptanon se baat ki. Woh vishesh roop se un zyada holistic philosophies mein ruchi rakhte the jo aksar European specialised soch ke baahar ki soch ko darshati thin, aur aisi soch jismein empirical data ki sakht prakriyaayein kam prachalit aur kam haavi thin.

Yeh tarka dete hue ki har cheez a-suljhe roop se judi hui hai, Leonardo ne sakhti se vishwas kiya ki ek discipline ko kai doosron ki majboot samajh ke bina poori tarah se nahi samjha ja sakta. Unhone aksar painting, music, poetry, philosophy aur science ke beech ke buniyaadi connections ki taraf ishara kiya. ‘Woh jo painting se nafrat karta hai woh na toh philosophy se aur na hi nature se pyaar karta hai’ unhone kaha, ‘music ko painting ki behan kaha ja sakta hai’ aur ‘agar poetry moral philosophy se nipatti hai, toh painting ka lena-dena natural philosophy se hai.’ Leonardo ke anusar, poori duniya (aur iska gyaan), ek bada (Italian!) parivar hai. Asal mein, yeh isliye hai kyunki woh cheezon ko categories mein nahi dekhte the ki unke notes itne sporadic lagte hain — woh vishayon ke beech swabhavik roop se switch karte the kyunki woh har cheez ko juda hua dekhte the. Jaise Kemp kehte hain:

> Leonardo ek prakar ke pathological lateral thinker the . . . toh anatomy ko explore karte waqt, woh dil, paani ki gati ko dekh rahe honge . . . Aur jab paani ki gati ko explore kar rahe honge, toh woh baalon ke ghumaav ke baare mein soch rahe honge, aur ityaadi — in sambandhit hiton ka ek anant ghumaav hoga, aur is saari vividhta ke neeche ek aam cheez hai, ek kaaran aur prabhav.

Lekin holistic drishtikon kisi bhi tarah se praacheen, premodern philosophies aur religious cosmologies tak hi seemit nahi hai. Yeh modern scientific paradigm ka bhi ek feature hai. E.O. Wilson, jinhe kai log duniya ke pramukh vaigyanikon mein se ek aur reason aur scientific method ka champion maante hain, khud apni kitaab *Consilience* mein gyaan ki ekta ka aagrah karte hain. ‘Gyaan ki ek ekrat pranali (united system),’ woh tarka dete hain, ‘vaastavikta ke abhi tak an-khoje kshetron ko pehchanne ka sabse pakka saadhan hai.’ E.O. Wilson ke anusar, yeh vishwa-drishti manav chetna ki praakritik sthiti hai. Woh zor dete hain ki gyaan ka ekikaran (unification) ‘un prernaon ko santusht karta hai jo manav swabhav ke sarahniya paksh se uthti hain’ aur vaastav mein ‘intellect ko ultimate purpose deta hai.’

Modern Western science aur philosophy ke itihaas mein duniya ke kai sabse bade thinkers ne is drishtikon ki upayogita (aur kabhi-kabhi aavashyakta) par tippani ki hai. Phir se, isme koi hairani ki baat nahi hai ki inmein se kai vaastav mein polymaths the. Goethe ne nature ko ‘ek bada samanjasyapoorn poora’ dekha aur Humboldt ke paas ‘Globe ko ek bade poore ke roop mein dekhne ki aadat’ thi. Buckminster Fuller ne duniya ko ek ekal entity ya ek ‘unfragmented whole’ ke roop mein rekhankit kiya aur iski absolute ekta mein aur parinaamswarup ‘comprehensivist’ — ek aisa vyakti jiske paas cosmos ki zyada sarv-gun-sampanna samajh hone ki sambhavna hai — ki zaroorat mein ek majboot vishwas vyakt kiya. Yeh soch ka ek aisa tareeqa hai jo duniya ki har cheez ko ek hi field ke hisse ke roop mein treat karta hai jiske andar har cheez aapas mein judi hui hai.

Yadyapi holism Hindu cosmology mein apni jadein rakhne wali ek praacheen philosophy hai, yeh sadiyon se Western soch ka ek aavashyak pehlu rahi hai (Kant, Spinoza, Hegel aur Nietzsche isse kaafi prabhavit the). Asal mein ‘holism’ ek darshanik shabd ke roop mein kisi aur ne nahi balki twentieth century ke ek prasiddh polymath, Jan Smuts ne racha tha, jinhone apni kitaab *Holism and Evolution* (1927) mein sabhi cheezon aur gyaan ki ekta ka aagrah kiya tha. Yeh us cheez ki taraf ishara karta hai jise vaigyanik, artists aur philosophers lambe samay se ek ‘vanishing point’ maante aaye hain — darshanik parinaamon ke sath ek geometric dharana, jahan hamare jaanch, gyaan aur samajh ke sabhi vishesh kshetra aakhirkar ekrit (converge) hote hain.

Kai aapatat: alag-alag kshetron ki a-vibhajyata (inseparability) par yeh zor aaj ke kai polymaths dwara abhi bhi saajha kiya jata hai, chahe scientific hon ya artistic. ‘Main har cheez ko juda hua dekhta hoon, aur main connections dhoondhne ke liye mukhya roop se prerit hoon kyunki algaav mein mujhe kuch bhi samajh nahi aata,’ philosopher aur polymath, Roger Scruton kehte hain. ‘Kai vaigyanik ise swikar karte hain. Na keval aap biology ko physics ke kanoonon ke sandarbh mein dekhe bina nahi samajh sakte, physics ke kanoonon ko khud biology mein unke upayog ke sandarbh mein samjha jana chahiye.’

Jab acting, poetry, music aur painting mein se unke favorite ke baare mein poochha gaya, toh artistic polymath Viggo Mortensen ne jawab diya: ‘Main sach mein unhe alag nahi karta; woh sabhi ek hi cheez hain.’ Ek aur artistic polymath Billy Childish isi tarah mahsoos karte hain: ‘Main khud ko ek writer ya painter ya musician ke roop mein pehchan nahi deta, balki aatm-siddhi ke raaste par ek vyakti ke roop mein. Mere liye yeh ek adhyatmik jeevan raasta hai.’ Aise vyaktiyon ke liye creativity bhi swabhavik roop se aati hai. David Stewart, woh musician jise aksar ek modern ‘Renaissance man’ ke roop mein varnit kiya jata hai, ne kaha, ‘Log box ke baahar sochne ki baat karte hain? Khair, mere liye, maine kabhi ek box dekha bhi nahi.’

---

### Context (Sandarbh)

> Cheezein aapas ki nirbharta (mutual dependence) se apna astitva aur prakriti nikaalti hain aur apne aap mein kuch nahi hain.
> — Nagarjuna, Buddhist philosopher (150–250)

Ekta se gehre se juda hua context (sandarbh) hai. Context kisi vishesh vastu ya ghatna par uske aaspas ki badi tasveer ke sambandh mein vichar karna hai, mool vastu ko behtar dhang se samajhne ke drishtikon se. Context ko pakadne ke liye, kisi ko (paas ke aur door ke) sambandhit ghatnaon ki ek vividhta ki jaanch aur vichar karne ki zaroorat hoti hai — jo ek 360°, multi-dimensional vishleshan ki anumati deta hai. Is drishtikon ka mahatva, jo polymath ka ek trademark hai, Hegel dwara vakaalat kiya gaya tha aur phir Dewey dwara rekhankit kiya gaya jinhone maana ki context ki upeksha (neglect) sabse gambhir galati hai jo philosophers karte hain. Lekin yeh galati nishchit roop se philosophers tak hi seemit nahi hai. Polymath Rabindranath Tagore school ke bachhon ke ek sentence ko samajhne ki taamsheel (analogy) pradan karte hain:
