> Bachhe, jab woh alphabet ke har alag akshar ko seekhna shuru karte hain, toh isme koi anand nahi paate, kyunki woh lesson ke asli uddeshya ko chhod dete hain; vaastav mein, jabki akshar sirf apne aap mein aur alag-thalag cheezon ke roop mein hamara dhyan khinchte hain, woh humein thaka dete hain. Woh hamare liye anand ka srot tabhi bante hain jab woh words aur sentences mein milte hain aur ek idea ko vyakt karte hain.

Contextual agyaanata ke khatron ko jeevan ke har kshetra mein anant roop se pradarshit kiya ja sakta hai. Kitaabon, bhashanon aur dharmik granthon se anant quotes, udaharan ke liye, behadd anadi-purna (ya kabhi-kabhi chaturai) se nikaale gaye hain aur context se baahar upayog kiye gaye hain, jisse gambhir galatfahmiyan hui hain jinake aksar vinashkari parinaam hue hain. Ek bade sonnet se nikaale gaye ek hi verse ke arth-heen-pan (ya bhramak-pan) par vichar karein; ya ek bade novel se ek chapter par ya iski poori theory se ek equation par; ya ek symphony se ek note par; ya ek bade painting ke ek square inch par. ‘Kuch bhi waisa nahi hai jaisa yeh hai sivaay us context ke jismein yeh sthit hai,’ Iain McGilchrist kehte hain, ‘ise baahar nikaalein aur yeh apni prakriti badal leta hai.’ Vastuon ko unke vatavaran se alag karne ki is pravritti ko philosopher Edgar Morin ne ‘andhi intelligence’ ke roop mein sandarbhit kiya.

Kuch Darwinists daava karte hain ki insaani dimaag ne zaroori roop se ek specialised, focused pravritti viksit ki kyunki isne natural selection ki prakriya ke dauran survival mein madad ki. Lekin is dharana par phir se sochne ki zaroorat hai. Asli survival ke liye na sirf haath mein maujood khatre ya mauqe ki asli samajh ki zaroorat hoti hai, balki aaspas ke aur sambandhit sabhi khatron aur mauqon ki bhi. Yaani, survival ke liye, dimaag ka right hemisphere survival ke liye utna hi mahatvapurna hai jitna ki left. Badi tasveer se alag-thalag har khatre ka ilaj karna aksar aatmghati hota.

Polymaths samajhte hain ki survive karne ka matlab samajhna hai aur sach mein samajhne ke liye kisi bhi diye gaye vishay ya sthiti ke contextual, holistic mulyankan ki zaroorat hoti hai. Isme atyadhik dhyan ki bhi zaroorat hoti hai, aur dimaag ka right hemisphere — jo contextual aur holistic soch ke liye zimmedar hai — cognitive scientists ke anusar, kul dhyan ke chaar-paanchvein hisse ko control karta hai. McGilchrist jungle mein jaanwaron ka udaharan dete hain: jab woh kha rahe hote hain toh unhe khane par dhyan kendrit rakhna chahiye, lekin unhe ek sath apne aaspas ke vatavaran ke baare mein bhi satark (vigilant) rehna chahiye — unke dimaag ka left hemisphere khane par sankuchit focus ke liye zimmedar hai aur right dhyan ke ek vyapak roop ke liye zimmedar hai (udaharan ke liye predators aur mates ko dekhna).

Toh survival ke liye, teevra focus aur contextual soch dono ki barabar matra mein zaroorat hoti hai. Asal mein aam ke bajaye vishesh ke sath, overall rules ke bajaye tathyon ke sath hamara maujooda junoon, statistician aur philosopher Nassim Nicholas Taleb ke anusar, mukhya kaaranon mein se ek hai kyunki hum 'Black Swans' (a-sambhavid phir bhi apariharyata atyadhik ghatnaayein jaise market crashes aur terrorist attacks) ke liye a-tai-yaar rehte hain.

---

### Systems Thinking (Pranali Soch)

> Sabhi cheezon se barabar pyaar karein: brāhmāṇḍa Ek hai.
> — Hui Ssu

Apne gyaan mein vividhta lana ek cheez hai. Ise ekrat karna, poore ki vision lane ke liye ek kushal synthesis karna doosri cheez hai. Nicolaus Copernicus, jinka heliocentric revolution sarvabhoomik shiksha ki ek prakriya se paida hua tha, apne daur ke ubharte hue 'specialist' astronomers ke tareeqe se niraash hue:

> Unke sath yeh aisa hai jaise koi artist vividh models se apne tasveeron ke liye haath, pair, sar, aur doosre sadasyon ko ik इकट्ठा kare, har hissa behtareen dhang se banaya gaya ho, lekin ek hi sharir se na juda ho, aur kyunki woh kisi bhi tarah se ek doosre se mel nahi khate, parinaam aadmi ke bajaye ek rakshas (monster) hoga.

Renaissance ke baad se, Western thinkers ne mukhya roop se science aur philosophy ke prati reductionist drishtikon ko apna liya, jiski shuruat mukhya roop se French philosopher René Descartes ne ki thi. Cartesian tareeqe ne duniya ko vyaktigat buniyaadon, nishchit building blocks ke sandarbh mein dekha jinhe reductionist vishleshan ke zariya sabse behtareen dhang se samjha ja sakta tha. 300 saalon tak is drishtikon ne vividh natural phenomena ki jaanch aur vyakhya karne mein ek lamba raasta tay kiya tha.

Lekin twentieth century ki shuruat tak vaigyanikon ke ek group ne ehsaas kiya ki sciences mein gyaan tezi se khandit (fragmented) ho raha tha, jisse log sabhi natural phenomena ke beech ke aantarik connections aur ekta ko kho rahe the. Scientific thinkers ki is nasal ne holistic soch ke traditional, pre-Enlightenment mode par wapas lautne ki talash ki, jise unhone Systems Thinking (Soviet polymath Alexander Bogdanov dwara viksit aur Austrian-American biologist Ludwig von Bertalanffy dwara popular banaya gaya) naamak ek scientific framework mein viksit kiya. Is naye scientific paradigm ne ek naye ecological movement (jiski soch ko 'deep ecology' ke roop mein varnit kiya gaya tha) ko inspire kiya, jiska James Lovelock ka krantikari Earth ka Gaia Theory sabse popular pradarshan hai. Asal mein United States mein ecological movement ke pioneers mein se ek, Barry Commoner hi the jinhone zor diya tha ki ‘har cheez har doosri cheez se judi hui hai.’

Systems Thinking, is movement ke pramukh jeevant samarthakon mein se ek Fritjof Capra ke anusar, mukhya roop se ‘connectedness, relationships aur context’ ko sandarbhit karta hai. Iska premise yeh hai ki poore ki prakriti hamesha iske hisson ke mamooli yog se alag hoti hai aur vastuon ke beech ke rishte primary hain aur vastuyein khud secondary (kyunki vastuyein khud networks ke alawa kuch nahi hain, bade networks mein samahit). Toh gyaan ek ‘building’ nahi balki ek ‘network’ hai, systems thinkers ke anusar.

Capra ne modern Western science ko behtar dhang se samajhne ke liye Eastern philosophy ke tatvon ko import karke Systems Thinking mein yogdan diya, yaani unki 1975 ki bestseller *The Tao of Physics* ke zariya. Leonardo da Vinci ki soch mein unke adhyayan ne — jinhe unhone original systems thinker ke roop mein ughada — polymathy ke sath iske link ki pushti ki. Aaj, Systems Thinking ke practical mulya ko kai kshetron mein pehchana gaya hai aur iske siddhanton ko business aur government ke leaders aur managers dwara laagu aur apnaya gaya hai saath hi economics, ecology, philosophy aur doosre kai intellectual disciplines ke andar bhi.

---

### Whole Brain Thinking (Poora Dimaag Soch)

> Ek poore dimaag ke vikas ke liye yeh siddhant hain: Art ki science ka adhyayan karein. Science ki art ka adhyayan karein. . . . Ehsaas karein ki har cheez har doosri cheez se judi hui hai.
> — Leonardo da Vinci

‘Holistic thinking’ ka idea kai modern materialist scientists ke beech bhaon uthane mein kabhi asafal nahi hota jo ise New Age spiritualism ke ‘philosophical mumbo jumbo’ se apmaan-janak roop se jodne mein tezi dikhate hain. Lekin ironical roop se yeh modern neuroscience hai jo soch ke is mode ke liye sabse behtareen vyakhya pradan karti hai. Dimaag ke right hemisphere ki uske pratiroop, left, ke muqable bhoomika ko samajhkar hi holistic, contextual soch ke mahatva ko poori tarah se saraha ja sakta hai. Right hemisphere ka swabhav ‘cheezon ko ek poore ke roop mein aur unke sabhi jatil aapas ki nirbharta mein dekhna’ hai jabki left hemisphere ka swabhav ‘sankuchit roop se aur algaav mein dekhna’ hai. ‘Left hisson ke baare mein gyaan pradan karta hai jabki right poore ki samajhdari pradan karta hai’ psychiatrist Iain McGilchrist kehte hain.

Iske alawa, kai neuropsychological studies ke anusar, right hemisphere dhyan ke sabhi prakaron (vigilance, alertness, sustained attention, aur divided attention) ke liye zimmedar dikhaya gaya hai sivaay ‘focused attention’ ke jo left hemisphere ka kshetra hai. Iska matlab hai ki right ‘vyapak, global aur flexible attention’ ke liye zimmedar hai jabki left ‘local, sankuchit roop se focused attention’ ke sandarbh mein haavi hai. McGilchrist ne tarka diya hai (apne masterpiece *The Master and his Emissary* mein) ki holistically sochne ki hamari kshamtaayein Western history mein alag-alag points ke dauran alag-alag thin, aur un dauron ke dauran apne sabse uche level par thin jinhe humne sabse zyada creative aur intellectually productive maana (yaani, Classical Greece, Renaissance Europe aur Enlightenment).

1950s mein, prasiddh British thinker Isaiah Berlin ne Leo Tolstoy ke intellectual framework ka ek vishleshan publish kiya. Aisa karte hue, unhone itihaas ke bade thinkers ko do aam prakaron mein baanta, foxes aur hedgehogs, aur jaanch ki ki Tolstoy dono mein se kisne fit baithte the. Hedgehog, unhone samjaya, ‘har cheez ko ek hi central vision se jodta hai, ek system kam ya zyada coherent ya articulate, uske sandarbh mein jo woh samajhta, sochta aur mahsoos karta hai.’ Yeh ek aisa system hai jo ‘ek ekal, sarvabhoomik, sangathankari siddhant hai jiske sandarbh mein ak अकेले woh sab jo woh hain aur kehte hain mahatva rakhta hai.’ Fox, doosri taraf, ‘kai uddeshyon ka peecha karta hai, aksar asambandhit aur yahan tak ki virodhabhasi, jude hue, agar bilkul bhi hon, toh sirf kisi de facto tareeqe se, kisi manasik ya sharirik kaaran se, kisi naitik ya saundaryatmak siddhant dwara na jude hue. Unki soch bikhari hui ya phali hui hoti hai, kai levels par badhti hui, anubhavon aur vastuon ki ek vishaal vividhta ke saar ko pakadti hui.’ Sansehp mein, hedgehog ‘ek badi cheez jaanta hai’ jabki fox ‘kai chhoti cheezein jaanta hai.’ Lekin Berlin ka nishkarsh hai jo mahatvapurna hai. Unhone paaya ki Tolstoy dono mein se kisi bhi category mein saaf taur par fit nahi baithte the; woh na toh generalist the na specialist. Woh vaastav mein dono the.

Sabak yeh hai ki specialise karne aur polymathise karne ki pravritti dono, kuch hadd tak, hum sabhi mein aantarik hain; aur sabse behtareen polymaths ek sath dono karne ki kshamta pradarshit karte hain. Woh sarvabhoomikta (universality) aur visheshata (particularity) ko ek dwidha ke roop mein nahi dekhte, balki iske bajaye purak drishtikonon ke roop mein dekhte hain jo vaastavikta को iske sabse saaf roop mein ughadne ke liye Yin-Yang ki tarah ek sath kaam karte hain. Yeh upar set kiye gaye jaanch ke tareeqe ke liye zaroori manoshtithi hai: ek do-step prakriya jismein pehle ek ‘diverse immersion’ ki zaroorat hoti hai aur phir poore ko samajhne ke liye ‘dots ko jodne’ dwara anusaran kiya jata hai. Toh logical reasoning (jo specialisation ko poshit karti hai) aur holistic intuition (generalist ka nishana) dono prakriya mein barabar mahatvapurna hain.

Yahi wajah hai ki dimaag ke right aur left hemispheres dono manav soch mein barabar mulya jodte hain (halanki McGilchrist ke anusar pehla Master hai aur doosra uska Emissary) aur yeh tab hota hai jab woh ek sath kaam karte hain ki polymathic potential anukooltam roop se realize hoti hai. Sabse behtareen painters ne detail par jatil roop se focus karne ke sath-sath poori tasveer ko visualize karne aur anupaatikta (proportionality) sunishchit karne ke liye beech-beech mein peeche hatne ki zaroorat ko samjha. Ek writer ke liye har section, chapter aur paragraph par teevra roop se focus karna utna hi mahatvapurna hai taaki yeh apna vazan utha sake, jitna ki poore karya ki nirantarata, connectivity aur ekta sunishchit karna.

Bade Lebanese poet Khalil Gibran dwara di gayi ek sundar taamsheel ko apnane ke liye, jabki lute par multiple strings alag ho sakti hain, har ek apne khud ke note ke sath barabar mahatvapurna aur har ek ko sukshma fine tuning ki zaroorat hoti hai, yeh tabhi hota hai jab woh poore samanjasya (harmony) mein ek sath kaampte hain ki sundar sangeet hamare kaanon mein mukt hota hai. Yeh ek aisa siddhant tha jise artists aur scientists dono dwara pehchana gaya tha. Henri Poincaré, jinhe sabhi mathematical fields mein unki mahaarat ke liye ‘last universalist’ ke roop mein jaana jata hai, ne kaha:

> Yeh vividh hisson ka samanjasya hai, unki samamiti (symmetry), unka khush santulan; ek shabd mein yeh woh sab hai jo vyavastha laata hai, woh sab jo ekta deta hai, jo humein saaf taur par dekhne aur ek sath ensemble aur details dono ko samajhne ki anumati deta hai.

Neuropsychologist aur Nobel laureate Roger Wolcott Sperry pushti karte hain ki yeh ‘jab dimaag poora hota hai, left aur right hemispheres ki ekrat chetna alag-alag hemispheres ke vyaktigat gunon ke yog se zyada hoti hai.’ Sperry ka varnan dikhata hai ki asli polymath ke dimaag mein, right aur left hemispheres ko purak dhang se barabar upayog mein laya jata hai taaki un creative breakthroughs ko mukt kiya ja sake jinke liye woh lambe samay se prasiddh rahe hain.

McGilchrist ka manna hai ki yeh santulan Western history mein Renaissance aur Enlightenment dauron ke dauran sabse behtareen dhang se haasil kiya gaya tha, jo us daur se mel khata hai jab West mein polymathy apne charam par thi. Woh sujhalte hain ki Greeks, Romans aur Renaissance ki sanskritiyan ‘aisi thin jahan do hemispheres ke "takes" ke beech ek faldaayi santulan tha . . . yeh dekha jata hai ki duniya ke ek pehlu ko samajhne ke liye humein jitne ho sake utne doosron ko samajhne ki zaroorat hai, kyunki right hemisphere ka zor cheezon ko judi hui dekhna hai, na ki compartmentalised.’

Aaj, hum jatilta ke ek naye paradigm mein pravesh kar rahe hain jismein left par a-nupaatik focus (jise hum sadiyon se anubhav kar rahe hain) se ek poore dimaag ki taraf soch mein badlav ki zaroorat hai, jo dono hemispheres ka synergetically aur synchronistically upayog karta hai. Mahatvapurna roop se, humein ek aisi education system, ek professional vatavaran aur ek haavi sanskriti ki zaroorat hai jo ise prabhavshali dhang se poshit karti hai.

---

### Genuine Specialists (Asli Specialists)

> Unke sath yeh aisa hai jaise koi artist vividh models se apne tasveeron ke liye haath, pair, sar, aur doosre sadasyon ko ik इकट्ठा kare, har hissa behtareen dhang se banaya gaya ho, lekin ek hi sharir se na juda ho, aur kyunki woh kisi bhi tarah se ek doosre se mel nahi khate, parinaam aadmi ke bajaye ek rakshas (monster) hoga.
> — Nicolaus Copernicus

Yeh bahut aksar maan liya jata hai ki jab geniuses ‘zone’ mein aate hain toh woh tunnel-visioned ho jaate hain, haath mein maujood vishesh task par focus karne ke liye sabhi peripheral bhatkaav ko rok dete hain. Sach yeh hai ki yeh ‘zone’ vaastav mein woh point hai jis par genius har pehlu mein absolute ekta aur samanjasya paata hai — central ya peripheral — jo haath mein maujood task se door se bhi juda ho, chahe woh baseball game mein ek pitcher ho, apne laptop par ek writer ho ya apni violin par ek musician ho. Yeh wahi hai jise Adler ek sarv-shreshth aadat banane ke liye multiple skills seekhne ke roop mein varnit karte hain: ‘Shuruat mein seekhne wala khud par aur alag-alag act mein apni skill par dhyan deta hai, jab acts ne poore performance ki skill mein apna algaav kho diya hai toh seekhne wala aakhirkar us goal par dhyan de sakta hai jise unki haasil ki gayi technique unhe karne mein saksham banati hai.’ Woh padhne ki kala ko sandarbhit kar rahe the, lekin yeh kisi bhi ‘specialist’ task par laagu hota hai. Yeh dimaag ki ek anukooltam sthiti hai jise psychologist Mihaly Csikszentmihalyi ‘flow’ ke roop mein sandarbhit karte hain — jo ab neuroscientists dwara conscious mind (transient hypofrontality) ki dhimi gati ke zariya unconscious mind ke muqable anubhav kiye jaane wale poore ekta ke ahasas ke roop mein samjaya gaya hai.

Jabki yeh ‘flow state’ kaafi hadd tak alag-alag kshetron mein ucha pradarshan karne wale, elite practitioners aur thinkers se juda hai, ise har kisi dwara rozmarra ki zindagi ke moments mein bhi anubhav kiya jata hai. Car chalana, udaharan ke liye, ek act ke roop mein dekha jata hai, lekin vaastav mein yeh tasks ki ek vividhta hai jiske liye multiple cognitive abilities ki zaroorat hoti hai jo ignition ghumane ke point par sabhi poore samanjasya mein ek sath aate hain. Driver dwara mahsoos ki gayi yeh subconscious, instinctive ekta polymath dwara mahsoos ki gayi ekta ke samaan hai jo driving ko ‘aam taur par zindagi’ aur ‘fields’ ko iske kai pehluon (mirror, gearstick, clutch, brake, accelerator, steering wheel) ke roop mein dekhta hai.

Toh yadyapi polymaths practical aur sangathnatmak uddeshyon ke liye is duniya ke alag-alag pehluon ko segment kar sakte hain, aisa sabhi segments ke sakhti se jude hone aur aakhirkar poore ko banane ki sarahana karte hue kiya jata hai. Woh poore ko iske hisson ke yog se bada dekhte hain. Dhyan dilaye jaane par yeh ek saaf dharana lag sakti hai, lekin ek atyadhik compartmentalised duniya mein, ise kaafi aasaani se kho diya jata hai aur shayad hi kabhi chintan kiya jata hai.

Kul milakar, jo log bahut patla phaelne ki chetawani dete hain woh is baat ko nazar-andaz karte hain ki diversity vaastav mein specialisation ke muqable kul gyaan aur intelligence ko badha sakti hai, jo us hadd ko sankuchit karti hai jis tak koi pragati kar sakta hai — poore ke roop mein bhi, aur kisi diye gaye discipline ke andar bhi. Mamooli thinker ke liye, poore ke hisse hamesha uske hisson ka yog honge. Polymath ke liye, vividh hisse, jab synthesise kiye jaate hain, ek creative outcome ko mukt karte hain jo apariharyata roop se poore ke size aur tatva ko badha dega. Creativity guru Dave Trott ne is concept par apni bestseller *One Plus One Equals Three* aadharit ki.

---

### Navigating the Information Age (Information Age Ko Navigate Karna)

> Jo seekhta hai lekin sochta nahi woh khogaya hai!
> — Confucius

Poore ki ek saaf vision rakhna chunautipurna hai, vishesh roop se ab uplabdh jaankari ki atyadhik vishaal matra ke sath. Aakhirkar, hum mein se har ek rozmarra lagbhag 100,000 words ki jaankari leta hai. Chunauti us cheez ko chunne mein saksham hona hai jo hum mein se har ek ke liye mulya ka ho sakta hai aur cheezon ko vishesh roop se hamari lives ke context mein aur vyapak duniya mein ek aise tareeqe se rakhna hai jo hamare liye maayne rakhta hai. Yeh wahi hai jise Edward de Bono ‘mulya banane ke liye sochne’ ke roop mein sandarbhit karte hain. Is tarah, hum sabhi ko, kuch hadd tak, polymaths banna chahiye, jinke liye hamari poori duniya hamara core field hai, aur astitva ka har pehlu jo iske liye maayne rakhta hai use explore kiya jana chahiye aur ise iski poornata mein samajhne ke liye upayog kiya jana chahiye.

‘Hum jaankari mein doob rahe hain, jabki wisdom ke liye bhookhe hain,’ E.O. Wilson kehte hain, jo aaj ke science ke pramukh philosophers mein se ek hain. ‘Duniya ab se synthesizers dwara chalayi jayegi, jo log sahi samay par sahi jaankari ko ek sath rakhne, iske baare mein critically sochne, aur mahatvapurna chunavon ko samajhdari se banane mein saksham hain.’ Peter Burke, Europe mein polymathy ke vikas par duniya ke pramukh expert, wahi nishkarsh nikalte hain:

> Specialisation ke ek daur mein, generalists ki zaroorat pehle se kahin zyada hai — na sirf synthesis ke liye, badi tasveer ko paint karne ke liye, balki vishleshan ke liye bhi, kyunki ‘gap ka dhyan rakhne’ aur un gyaanon ki taraf dhyan khinchne ke liye ek polymath lagta hai jo doosri tarah se disciplines ke beech ki jagahon mein gayab ho sakte hain, jaisa ki woh vartaman mein paribhashit aur sangathit hain.

French philosopher Edgar Morin, Complexity Theory ke pita, ne kaha:

> Humein sochne ke ek aise prakar ki zaroorat hai jo use phir se jodta hai jo asambandhit aur compartmentalised hai, jo ekta ko pehchante hue vividhta ka aadar karta hai, aur jo aapas ki nirbhartaon ko samajhne ki koshish karta hai. Humein ek radical soch (jo samasyaon ki jad tak pahonchti hai), ek multidimensional soch, aur ek sangathnatmak ya systems soch ki zaroorat hai.

Schools aur universities paramparik roop se gyaan ke prasaar ke kendra rahe hain — iska transfer, yaani lectures aur books ke zariya teacher se student tak. Lekin aaj, ‘information age’ mein jaankari ki uplabdhta ab samasya nahi hai; jaankari ka navigation hamare samay ki chunauti hai. ‘Kyunki har kisi ke paas jaankari ke is tsunami tak pahonch hai isliye yeh sach mein critical ho jata hai ki hum logon ko ise navigate karna sikhayein,’ Wikipedia ke founder Jimmy Wales rekhankit karte hain. ‘Hum kaise jaante hain ki kis par vishwas karna hai? Bahut se logon ko yeh skills nahi sikhayi gayi hain.’ Navigation ke liye soch ki zaroorat hoti hai, aur jaise Edward de Bono ishara karte hain, hamare maujooda educational institutions sochne ki aavashyak skill ko sikhane ki mahatvapurna bhoomika ko poora nahi karte hain. Yeh soch ke zariya hai — woh prakriya jismein deduction, synthesis aur application shamil hai — ki jaankari gyaan ban jaati hai.

Aaj censorship jaankari ko rokna nahi hai, balki aapko ek asambandhit, yadrakshik tareeqe se jaankari se bhar dena hai. Yeh ya toh humein abhibhoot aur uljha deta hai, ya humein poore ko samajhne par haar manne, aur ek vishesh par focus karne par majboor karta hai. Yeh aasaani se band-dimaag wali, sankuchit specialisation ko majboot kar sakta hai jisse hamara samaj pehle se hi peedit hai. ‘Hum Babylon, Alexandria aur ityaadi se hamesha ek global, universal Library ki khoj mein rahe hain,’ multimedia education par focus karne wale psychologist Rand Spiro kehte hain, ‘ab hamare paas yeh internet ke roop mein hai, lekin durbhagya se log ise sochne ki takleef se khud ko bachane ke liye ek band tareeqe se upayog kar rahe hain . . . jabki vaastav mein web vaikalpik drishtikonon ko dhoondhne mein saksham hone mein bahut achha hai.’

Jabki yeh nishchit roop se aapekshik santosh, aalas aur manav soch ke vanchan (deprivation) ki sanskriti ko encourage karta hai (is baat ko sujhane ke liye paryapt research hai), iske labh anant roop se bade ho sakte hain agar har vyakti ise samajhdari se upayog karne mein saksham ho. ‘Modern information age mein, koi sochega ki tezi se jaankari haasil karne ka mauqa polymathy ko aahwan karega,’ Hamlet Isakhanli kehte hain, ‘[yeh] kisi ke liye bhi bade mauqe paida karta hai jo kai cheezein seekhna chahta hai.’ Asal mein ek ubharta hua ‘ideas industry’ jismein conferences, web portals aur podcasts shamil hain jo alag-alag kshetron ke speakers ki ek range ko host karte hain (jaise Idea City, Lift, Big Think, RSA, Intelligence Squared, Joe Rogan Experience, Tim Ferriss, London Real) dimaag ke vistar aur self ke vikas ko encourage karne mein ek mahatvapurna bhoomika nibhate hain.

Chahe theoretical ya experiential ho, jaankari hamari ungliyon par aasaani se uplabdh hai. Koi bhi lagbhag kuch bhi seekh sakta hai: car mechanics, Polynesian philosophy, emergency survival, Russian art ka itihaas, software troubleshooting, mobile phone engineering, Marxist economics, applied pharmacology, gharelu plumbing. Kisi bhi cheez ko ‘Wikihow’ ya simply ‘Google’ karna aasan hai. Web-based jaankari, agar samajhdari se srotit ki jaye, toh kisi ke liye bhi polymathise karne ki anumati de sakti hai. Online srot jaise YouTube (kalpana ki jaane wali har tarah ki videos), Khan Academy (vividh vishayon par tutorials), TED (vividh maamlon par naye ideas), Edge (West ke scientific elite ke ideas), Wikipedia (jiske paas 267 alag-alag bhashaon mein 31 million articles hain), 5 million e-books muft mein uplabdh hone aur har ek newspaper aur magazine online uplabdh hone ki baat toh door hai, humein jaankari ke tsunami se bhar deta hai.

Phir bhi educational institutions gyaan ke ik इकट्ठा hone aur vitran (transfer) se sambandhit apne sadiyon purane role se aage nahi badhe hain taaki ise sabse behtareen dhang se sangathit karne, samajhne aur upayog karne ke tareeqe ko sikhaya ja sake. Critical thinking ki aaj barabar, agar zyada nahi, zaroorat hai pichhle samay ki tulna mein taaki yeh parakha ja sake ki kis jaankari ki zaroorat hai, kab, kis hadd tak aur kis context mein.

---

### Revolution (Kranti)

Manav vikas par sabse aam taur par maani jaane wali narrative ke anusar, do million se 10,000 saal pehle tak, duniya ek hi samay mein kai manav prajatiyon (human species) ka ghar thi: *Homo neanderthalensis*, *Homo erectus*, *Homo soloensis*, *Homo floresiensis*, *Homo rudolfensis*, *Homo ergaster* aur *Homo sapiens*. Ek cognitive revolution wahi tha jisne *sapiens* ko doosre *Homo* genus ke muqable vilupt hone se bachne ki anumati di. Ek bada dimaag, auzaron ka upayog, behtar learning abilities aur jatil samajik dhanchon ne *sapiens* ki tikauta (sustainability) ko sunishchit kiya.

Yeh pehli cognitive revolution thi; doosri abhi tak nahi hui hai. Beshak sanskritik aur bauddhika renaissances hue hain, jaise eleventh-century Cordoba mein, thirteenth-century Timbuktu mein, fifteenth-century Florence mein aur eighteenth-century Paris mein (kai, kai doosron ke beech). Pichhle kuch millennia mein alag-alag points par technological breakthroughs aur spiritual awakenings, saath hi scientific, political aur economic revolutions hue hain. Lekin ek cognitive revolution — ek aisa jo ek poori prajati ki chetna mein badlav lane ke liye dimaag ke neurological structure ko badal deta hai — abhi aana baaki hai. Aur ise is century mein aana chahiye. Bahut kam log is mulyankan se behas karenge ki manavta ek astitva-atmak sankat (existential crisis) se peedit hai, jiske liye tatkaalik samadhan ki zaroorat hai. Aur yeh manav ki sadiyon purani nasal, polymath, hi hai jo ek baar phir chunauti ka samna karne ke liye uthega.

---

# Chapter 6 - An Alternative System (CHAPTER 6: EK VAIKALPIK PRANALI)

Pichhle chapter ne ek cognitive revolution (sangyanatmak kranti) se kam kuch prastavit nahi kiya — system dwara lagatar a-manaviyakaran (perpetual dehumanisation) ki neeti se Self ki ek azaadi (emancipation). Aakhirkar halanki, specialisation ki samasya systemic hai aur isliye iske liye ek systemic samadhan ki zaroorat hogi. Toh yeh section un logon ke dhyan ke liye hai jo us tareeqe ko prabhavit karne ki sthiti mein hain jis tarah se hum samaj, education, kaam aur, mahatvapurna roop se, bhavishya ko mahsoos karte hain aur paribhashit karte hain. Kya hum bhavishya ke control mein mutthi bhar polymaths chahte hain, ya hum aakhirkar sabhi manav jeevon ki polymathic potential ko mukt karna chahte hain?

---

## Society (Samaj)

### A Community of Minds (Dimaagon Ka Ek Samuday)

Itihaas mein badi sabhyataon dwara itne saare polymaths produce kiye jaane ke mukhya kaaranon mein se ek (is tathya ke alawa ki woh wahi hai jahan manav uplabdhi ke record hone ki sabse zyada sambhavna thi) yeh hai ki zyadatar empires (jaise Ottoman, Roman aur British) vividh sanskritiyon, vishwa-drishtiyon, bhashaon aur ideas ke logon ko shamil aur assimilate karne ke liye bade hue. Samajik baatchit ki vividhta jismein ek vyakti shamil hua hota nishchit roop se samajik aur bauddhika hybridity ki ek prakriya ko utprerit karta. Ronald Burt ne apni *Social Origin of Good Ideas* mein wahi nishkarsh nikala:

> Jo log samajik duniyaon ke intersection par rehte hain unke paas achhe ideas hone ka ucha risk hota hai. . . . Doosri tarah se alag-thalag samoohon se jude logon ke sochne aur vyavhaar karne ke vaikalpik tareeqon se parichit hone ki zyada sambhavna hoti hai jo unhe vaikalpik tareeqon ko chunne aur synthesise karne ka vikalp deta hai.

Apni kitaab *The Difference* mein, complexity theorist Scott Page ne zahir kiya ki, vyapak research ke anusar, pragati aur innovation uche IQs wale akele thinkers par kam aur ek sath kaam karne wale aur apni individuality ka faayda uthane wale vividh logon par zyada nirbhar ho sakte hain. Unka kaam dikhata hai ki perspectives ki ek range ko pradarshit karne wale groups samaan-soch wale 'experts' ke groups ko peeche chhod dete hain. Yeh un kaaranon mein se ek hai ki jury justice system ka itna mahatvapurna hissa hai — yeh yadrakshik (randomly) vividh range ke backgrounds ke logon ko ek sath aane, case mein apne perspectives lane aur ek naye insight par pahonchne ke liye ise doosron ke sath milane ki anumati deta hai, ek aisa jiske paas hone mein atyadhik trained, ajeevak specialised lawyer akele kshamatawan nahi hota.

Yeh prakriya, jise Aristotle dwara 'wisdom of the crowd' ke roop mein racha gaya tha, journalism (yeh tay karne ke liye ki news mein kya shamil kiya jaye), politics (jaise Athenian democracy mein sortation), academia (interdisciplinary projects) aur business (cross-domain advisory groups aur ideas ki 'crowd-sourcing') jaise vividh kshetron mein upayog ki jaati hai.

Diversity ka creative prabhav sirf groups par hi laagu nahi hota. Ek hi dimaag ke andar diversity (dimaagon ke ek samuday ke vipreet) ka bhi isi tarah ka creative prabhav ho sakta hai. Nayi sanskritiyon, naye logon, naye ideas aur sochne ke naye tareeqon ke sath baatchit vyaktigat dimaagon ko chetan aur a-chetan levels dono par jeevan ki vividhta ko samajhne aur poori tarah se sarahana karne ki anumati deti hai. Aur is tarah ek group ki diversity kisi ke apne vyaktigat sochne ki prakriyaon ki diversity ko prabhavit karti hai, saath hi iska vipreet bhi. Cognitive scientist Rand Spiro dimaagon ke andar aur aar-paar is sahyog ('collaboration within and across minds') ke baare mein baat karte hain:

> Jo ek ke liye achha hai woh duniya ke liye bhi achha hai, ek doosre ko majboot karta hai . . . groups mein kaam karte hue aap alag-alag perspectives sunte hain, jo aapko ek vyakti ke roop mein alag-alag perspectives deta hai . . . phir badle mein groups ke andar kaam karne ke liye behtar suit hota hai . . . zyada tentacles, connections ke zyada sambhavid points. . . . Aap 'apne aap mein ek group hone' mein behtar ho jaate hain aur is tarah multiple perspectives se ek andaruni dialogue mein shamil hote hain.

Is prakriya ka vaastav mein dimaag par ek nishchit bhaautik prabhav (physical impact) padta hai — hum ab jaante hain ki brain weight, cortical thickness aur neural circuitry samajik baatchit ki matra, prakriti aur prakriti ke anusar badalti aur badalti hai.

Vyakti mein diversity ka aisa sudhar phir badle mein poore samaj ke roop mein diversity mein ek ghatatmak badhotari (exponential rise) ki anumati deta hai, ek positive feedback loop banata hai. Ek multicultural, cosmopolitan vatavaran jismein lagatar aur asli inter-class, cross-field aur cross-cultural baatchit hoti hai isliye logon, ideas aur fields ke beech hybridity ki anumati dene ki sabse zyada sambhavna rakhta hai. Yeh ek aisa vatavaran hai jo swabhavik roop se polymathy ko paalta hai, jab tak ki vyakti ko diversity ko internalise karne ke liye aatm-manthan ke moments rakhne ki anumati di jaati hai, saath hi baahari abhivyakti ke zariya apni andaruni diversity ko murt roop dene ki bhi.

Aise cases hain, halanki, jahan groups par is nirbharta ne vyakti ki polymathise karne ki pravritti ko roka hai. Industrial age se ubharte hue neoliberal economic doctrine ke duniya bhar mein pradhan socioeconomic system ke roop mein hone par, vyakti ko sangathan — yaani corporation — dwara pichhaad diya gaya hai. Badi hastiyon ko hamari duniya ke movers aur shakers ke roop mein badi companies dwara replace kar diya gaya hai. Baad me aane waale nation state ki bhoomika aur prabhav ko pichhaadne ke liye bade hue hain (duniya ki 100 sabse badi economies mein se, zyadatar ab companies hain, deshon nahi), jiske vyaktigat (manav) level par polymathy ke liye gehre parinaam hain. Corporation ne khud ko manaviyakrit (humanised) kar liya hai, uske paas apna khud ka vision aur mission hai saath hi uske apne values ka set hai, aur apne adhikaaron ko dharan karke, vyakti ke kshetra par pravesh kiya hai aur aakhirkar use abhibhoot kar diya hai.

Yeh, gyaan mein ghatatmak badhotari ('information explosion') aur manav ke a-manaviyakaran ke sath milkar matlab rakhta hai ki diversity, interdisciplinarity aur multi-disciplinarity ek vyaktigat ke bajaye ek 'team' ya sangathnatmak vichar ban jaate hain. Technologist Vinnie Mirchandani ne 21st-century corporation par polymath analogy ko laagu karne ke liye ek poori kitaab samarpit ki. Yeh BPs aur GEs hain jo 'New Polymaths' hain; multinational companies jo multiple technologies mein shreshthta haasil karti hain aur alag-alag sectors se multiple talent pools ko synthesise karti hain aaj ke Leonardos aur Ben Franklins hain, woh tarka dete hain.

Isliye saavdhani bartani chahiye, vishesh roop se corporate duniya mein, yeh sunishchit karne ke liye ki communal diversity ke labh dono taraf bahein — vyakti se samuday tak, saath hi iska vipreet bhi. Humein, jaise tech philosopher aur polymath Jaron Lanier chetaate hain, vyakti ki keemat par samuhik (collective) ki mahima nahi karni chahiye.

---

### True Globalisation (Sachi Globalisation)

H.G. Wells, khud ek polymath — ek novelist, draughtsman, biologist aur history aur politics par ek writer — ne 1936 mein 'World Brain' ke concept ko viksit kiya. Unki vishwa-drishti ko dekhte hue, yeh shayad hi hairani ki baat thi. 'Mera vishesh kshetra hamesha generalisation aur synthesis raha hai,' unhone kaha. 'Main alag-thalag ghatnaon aur asambandhit details ko na-pasand karta hoon.' Wells ke ideas hamesha global paimane par the. Unhone duniya ka ek mukhya itihaas likha, World Peace movement ke ek pramukh sadasya the aur World War II ke baad World Government ke liye movement mein shamil hue. Racism aur classism ke ek mukhar virodhi ke roop mein, woh apne samay ke un kuch British intellectuals mein se ek the jinhone imperial prism ke baahar se duniya ko dekha aur duniya ke alag-alag hisson se gyaan ke mahatva ko samjha; history mein uske yogdan ke sath-sath bhavishya ke shanti ke liye uski aavashyakta ko. Unhone ek sach mein vividh phir bhi anupaatik roop se ekrat duniya ki kalpana ki. Ek sachi globalisation.

21st-century globalisation, jisne kuch hadd tak dimaagon ke samuday ko vistarit aur compile karke global hybridity ki prakriya ko suvidhajanak banaya hai, ne vyakti ke liye kai naye duniyaayein kholi hain jo doosri tarah se kalpana se pare hoti. Aaj, aisi vividh baatchit kai roopon mein aur vividh platforms ke zariya aa sakti hai: Skype, email, WhatsApp, social media, blogs, discussion forums aur ityaadi — jahan alag-alag backgrounds ke dimaag ideas ko share aur viksit karne ke liye ek sath aate hain. Yeh ek aisi jagah hai jahan, science writer Matt Ridley ko quote karne ke liye, 'ideas sex karte hain.' Ridley tarka dete hain ki technology mein asli innovations ek collective brain se aate hain; woh is baat par nirbhar karte hain ki log ek doosre se kitne achhe se jude hue hain. Isliye internet samjhata hai ki hum technology innovation mein aise golden age se kyun guzar rahe hain, woh tarka dete hain.

Lekin globalisation manavta ke liye barabar matra mein ek mauqa aur ek khatra dono sabit hua hai. Agar ise sahi dhang se samjha aur navigate kiya jaye, toh yeh gyaan ki talash karne ke liye ek asantusht curiosity wale logon ke liye ek abhootpoorv mauqa prastut kar sakta hai. Ise samaj ko polymathise karne ke liye ek taqat ke roop mein upayog kiya ja sakta hai. Industrial revolution aur imperialism apne sath ek nishchit manoshtithi laaye — mechanistic linearity aur reductionist specialisation ki. Is manoshtithi ne us samay ki zarooraton ko (kam se kam kuch logon ke liye) poora karne mein apna uddeshya poora kiya. Aaj, ek globally integrated information age mein jeete hue, humein ek poori tarah se alag manoshtithi ki zaroorat hai — ek aisi jo fluid, holistic aur interconnected ho. Har vyakti ki chintaayein ab ekal aur seedhi nahi hain; woh bahumukhi aur jatil ho gayi hain.

Sabse mahatvapurna, globalisation ko polymathic fertilisation ke utprerak (catalyst) ke roop mein kaam karne ke liye, ise asli hona chahiye. Koi maan lega (ya kam se kam umeed karega) ki ek 'decolonialised' aur globalised 21st century mein, Great Western Bias ghat gaya hoga. Lekin aisa nahi hua hai. Globalisation duniya ki sabhi ya kai sanskritiyon ka anupaatik fusion nahi hai jaisa ki iske samarthak daava karte hain, balki iske bajaye haavi sanskriti ka atyadhik a-nupaatik phailav ban gaya hai, jo aaj Western hai. Is par chunauti di jaani chahiye. Khatra yeh hai ki aisa globalisation ek ya kuch mukhya 'world languages' (yaani official UN bhashayein) ke prasaar ka kaaran ban sakta hai jismein har cheez ko translate kiya jana hai. Phir bhi history, literature aur philosophy ke kai works sambhavid roop se doosri bhashaon mein behtar uplabdh hain.Isliye 'global village' ka idea bhramatmak hai.

Duniya bhar ki indigenous sanskritiyon, itihaason aur darshanon ka aadar kiya jana chahiye aur unhe zyada gambhirta se liya jana chahiye — vishesh roop se kyunki unmein se kai aantarik roop se holistic aur isliye prakriti se polymathic hain, jo unhe shayad maujooda vatavaran ke liye zyada laagu karta hai. Unhe ek sach mein barabar ka darja diya jana chahiye, na ki suvidha ke anusar sirf tokenise kiya jaye. Jaise-jaise duniya globalise hoti hai, isliye vividh sanskritiyon aur vishwa-drishtiyon ki ek sachi samajh haasil karne ke liye alag-alag bhashayein seekhne ka ek jhukav hona chahiye. Kalpana karein ki kaise, udaharan ke liye, Japanese history ka ek Aztec vivran ya Polynesian philosophy ka ek Malian version ya Chinese literature ka ek Egyptian review na sirf history ki hamari dharana ko phir se aakar de sakta hai, balki humein itni saari cheezon ke baare mein hamari maujooda dharanaon ko sodhit (revise) karne ke liye bhi majboor kar sakta hai.

Is baat ko dhyan mein rakhte hue, jo tezi se mahatvapurna hai woh hai ek global mind ko poshit karne ke liye ek global education ka hona. Iska matlab hai ki bachhon aur yuvaon ko multiple bhashayein (na sirf spashth chunav) padhayi jaani chahiye, aur duniya ke literature, duniya ke cinema, duniya ke art aur duniya ke philosophy se ek aise tareeqe se parichit karaya jana chahiye jo alag-alag samajon ke gyaan ko (kam se kam sambhavid roop se) barabar treat karta hai. Unke paas ek global anubhav bhi hona chahiye — travel, udaharan ke liye, curriculum mein gehre se baitha hua hona chahiye.

Jaise Enlightenment ke dauran European upper classes Grand Tour se guzare, aaj ke yuvaon ko global adventures par nikalne ke liye majboor mahsoos karna chahiye — vishesh roop se kyunki yatra kai logon ke liye bahut aasan ho gayi hai. 'Gap year' mahatvapurna hai lekin paryapt nahi; yeh bachhe ke global integration ki ek poori prakriya ka ek hissa hona chahiye. Yeh kisi ke polymathic vikas ke liye vishesh roop se mahatvapurna hai, kyunki yeh dimaag ko chouda karta hai, gyaan aur anubhav ki hybridity ko badhata hai, individuality ka nirmaan karta hai aur alag-alag perspectives ke sampark mein aane ki anumati deta hai. Faith in the Commonwealth project dwara laagu ki gayi UNESCO ki Global Citizenship Education (GCED) initiative is disha mein ek mahatvapurna step hai.

---

### Art and Culture (Kala Aur Sanskriti)

Chahe 'high culture' ya 'popular culture' ke zariya (T.S. Eliot ne dono ko ek 'poori sanskriti' ke nirmaan ke liye mahatvapurna dekha), hip hop, theatre, poetry, film, novels, music, ya bachhon ki kahaniyon ke zariya, polymathy ko manav sthiti ka ek zyada saraye jaane wala roop hona chahiye. Ek kaafi chonkane wala tathya yeh hai ki polymaths (polymathy ek idea ke roop mein hone ke sath) ko artistic productions aur saahityik works mein depiction ke zariya shayad hi kabhi explore kiya gaya hai.

Udaharan ke liye, jabki polymaths artistic aur saahityik khoj ke liye behtareen muses ke roop mein kaam karte hain — unki lives kisi bhi tulna mein sabse aakarshak, sahasik, preranadaayak aur bahumukhi hone ke kaaran — ullekhniya roop se, is kitaab mein zikr kiye gaye ashadharan polymaths mein se bahut kam (agar koi) kisi bhi mainstream (ya independent bhi) film mein darshaye gaye hain. Sherlock Holmes, James Bond, Jason Bourne, Will Hunting, Forrest Gump aur MacGyver jaise sahasik, genius, yahan tak ki polymathic kalpanik characters ko darshane ke liye filmmakers dwara bhookh ki nishchit roop se koi kami nahi hai. Toh yeh samajh se pare (baffling) hai ki Hollywood ya kisi doosre mainstream cinema sansthan ne Imhotep, Aristotle, Shen Kuo, da Vinci, Franklin, Goethe, Ibn Khaldun, ya doosre ullekhniya polymaths ki asli zindagiyon ka upayog apne screenplays ke aadhar ke roop mein kyun nahi kiya hai, bhale hi har ek nishchit roop se ek sach mein aakarshak, preranadaayak aur manoranjak story pradan karega.

Eric Bana dwara abhinay (starring) aur Edward Zwick dwara produce ki gayi Richard Francis Burton ki mahakavya zindagi ke baad ek Hollywood blockbuster, ya Spike Lee 'Joint' mein Paul Robeson ke roop mein Will Smith ka abhinay, ya shayad Forrest Gump/Apollo 13-style adventure drama mein F. Story Musgrave ke roop mein Tom Hanks ka abhinay nishchit roop se vyapak roop se aakarshak productions honge. Nishchit roop se, Omar Khayyam ke kaarnamon ko darshane wali ek Majid Majidi film, Rudolf Steiner ke multi-dimensional dimaag par ek Werner Herzog film ya Rabindranath Tagore ke kai pehluon ko darshane wali ek Mani Ratnam production mein ek aakarshak shaikshanik anubhav ke sath-sath romanshak manoranjan ke gun honge. Yeh fiction ka sahara liye bina, hone ke ek alag tareeqe ko ughadne ka ek tareeqa hoga.

Aaj, sarakaron aur corporations dwara kaafi hadd tak sankuchit-focussed 'specialists' ka paksh lene ke sath, yeh independent patrons hain jo polymaths ko pehchanne, encourage karne aur sakriya roop se samarthan dene ki pravritti rakhte hain. Asal mein, aaj enterprise ke sabse bade patrons mein se kuch vyakti bane hue hain, yaani ameer philanthropists aur enthusiasts jo kai mamlon mein khud polymathic hain — Bill Gates, Richard Branson, Elon Musk aur Nathan Myhrvold jaise log. Swatantra, aazad roop se kaam karne wale entrepreneurs ke roop mein (jaise ki gulam sarakari adhikariyon aur mechanistic corporate executives ke vipreet), unke vyaktigat vividhta ki taqat aur potential ki sarahana karne ki zyada sambhavna hoti hai, aur woh maujooda riwaajon aur prakriyaon ka palan karne ki zaroorat ke bina, apni marzi se polymath ka samarthan karne mein saksham hote hain.

Art aur culture ki duniya mein, Nasser D. Khalili — 'modern day Medici' jinhe khud kai logon dwara ek polymath sandarbhit kiya jata hai — art, culture, education aur business mein kai interdisciplinary projects ka samarthan kar rahe hain, jinmein Commonwealth ke sath ek initiative shamil hai jo khule-dimaag-pan ko encourage karne aur vikas-sheel deshon mein yuvaon ki bahu-pakshiya potential ko adhikatam (maximize) karne ki talash karti hai.

---

## Education (SHIKSHA / PADHAI)

Pehle, humne is baat ki jaanch ki ki maujooda education system wahin kyun hai jahan yeh hai. Summary ke roop mein, Ken Robinson kuch mukhya karakon ko rekhankit karte hain:

> Academic education mein, specialisation aanshik roop se is samajh ka parinaam hai ki gyaan sirf deductive reasoning aur sabhoot ki prakriya se nikalta hai. Yeh apariharyata roop se cultural kshetra ke doosre ilaqon jaise arts ko sideline karta hai. Isme 19th century mein mass education systems ke vikas dwara tezi aayi hai. Factories ko nishchit cognitive skills ki zaroorat thi, isliye maths aur English ko mahatva diya gaya. Agar aap gyaan ke is vichar ko ek zyada aagyakari (compliant) aur anuroop (conformist) workforce paida karne ki zaroorat ke sath milate hain, toh aapko ek aisa education system milta hai jiska intelligence par ek vishesh drishtikon hai, lekin ek aisa bhi jismein bhavna (emotion) aur abhivyakti (expression) ko nazar-andaz kiya jata hai. Usi daur ke dauran, hamare paas psychiatry, psychoanalysis aur economic theory ka uday hua, jo sabhi duniya ko dekhne ke us tareeqe se nikale the.

---

### Establishing the Purpose of Learning (Seekhne Ke Uddeshya Ko Sthapit Karna)

Gyaan taqat hai — kuch logon ke liye, gulam banane ki taqat; doosron ke liye, mukt karne ki. Yahi wajah hai ki gyaan ki khoj — jise doosri tarah se ‘education’ ke roop mein jaana jata hai — ko samajon ke itihaas mein itna bada mahatva diya gaya hai. Manav itihaas ke zyadatar hisse ke liye, education ka uddeshya kisi ke naitik charitra (moral character) ko badhana, manavta mein sakaratmak roop se yogdan dene ki kshamta ko badhana, saath hi rozmarra ki zindagi ki vaastaviktaon ke liye khud ko anukooltam (optimally) taiyar karna tha. Confucians ne education ko naitik charitra ko sudharne ke saadhan ke roop mein dekha; Hindus aur Muslims ne ise Divine ko jaan-ne ke saadhan ke roop mein dekha; prehistoric hunter-gatherers ne ise ek kathin, vipreet vatavaran mein survive karne ke saadhan ke roop mein dekha. Aristotle ne kaha ki bachhon ko na sirf isliye shikshit kiya jana chahiye kyunki diya gaya vishay ‘upayogi’ ho sakta hai, balki simply isliye kyunki education apne aap mein ek achhi cheez hai.

Is raaste par kahi na kahi, samajik Darwinist ravaiyon ki vyapakta aur capitalist paradigm ke dabddabe ke kaaran, education ko mukhya roop se (aur kabhi-kabhi vishesh roop se) bade bhaautik aur samajik status ke saadhan ke roop mein dekha jaane laga. Hamari maujooda sansthaon aur sanskriti ne humein education par ek value-adding process ke roop mein nirbhar hone ke liye majboor kiya hai jiske baad hum khud ko un employers ko bechte hain jinhe hamari unki kamyabi mein yogdan dene ki kshamta ka aashwasan diya ja sakta hai. Woh ‘value’ sabse aksar is baat se judge ki jaati hai ki hum kitne ‘specialised’ hain. Is arth mein, education sthirta (stability) aur status haasil karne ka ek auzar ban gayi hai.

Lekin aaj, shayad humein seekhne ke asli uddeshya ka phir se mulyankan karne ki zaroorat hai. Is duniya ki ek zyada poori, zyada vyapak samajh viksit kiye bina aap khud ko is duniya mein kahan rakhna hai yeh kaise tay kar sakte hain? Aaj tatkaalik roop se ek aise education system ki zaroorat hai jo curiosity ko (autonomy ko encourage karke), ekta ko (holistic, contextualised learning ko encourage karke) aur creativity ko (multitalented logon par monomathic specialisation na thop kar) encourage kare. Polymath aur educationalist Hamlet Isakhanli ek aise learning environment banane mein vishwās karte hain jo multiple talents aur interests ko encourage karta hai. ‘Haqeeqat mein, koi bhi student kabhi sirf ek kshetra mein interested nahi hota; uske paas kai kshetron aur shauqon ko sametne wala interests ka ek ghera hota hai. Uske liye in kshetron mein kshamtaon ko pradarshit karne ka mauqa paida karna zaroori hai.’

Iske sath, halanki, khud se poochhne ke liye kuch mahatvapurna sawaal aate hain: hum gyaan ki talash kyun kar rahe hain? Hamare liye ‘seekhna’ ya ‘education’ ka kya matlab hai? Uplabdhi, khushi, aatm-siddhi aur kamyabi ka hamara vichar kya hai? Jaisa ki yeh khada hai, humein ek job ke liye taiyar hone ko kaha gaya hai — ek aisa bhi jiske jald hi automate hone ki sambhavna hai! Mahatvapurna roop se, humein shuruat se hi saaf aur imaandar hona chahiye ki ‘gyaan’ ka hamara vichar vaastav mein kya hai. Hum mein se bahut se log autopilot par hain, samaj par nirbhar karte hue humein batane ke liye ki humein kya seekhna chahiye aur kyun. Ek baar jab aisa chintan ho jata hai, toh gyaan haasil karne ke sath-sath share karne ki ek asli bhookh aantarik roop se andar viksit hogi.

---

### Encouraging Creative Autonomy (Rachnatmak Aazadi Ko Badhava Dena)

> Bachhe ek aise vyapak curriculum ke sath sabse behtareen dhang se phalte-phoolte hain jo unke vividh talents ka jashn manata hai.
> — Ken Robinson

Hum maan lete hain ki hamara education system tribal, ‘outdated’ seekhne ke tareeqon se ek lamba raasta tay kar chuka hai, lekin shayad humein un paramparik vicharon mein se kuch ko phir se dekhne ki zaroorat hai. Polymath aur anthropologist Jared Diamond ne apne adhyayanon ke zariya dikhaya hai ki kai traditional, tribal samajon mein educational process, modern Western system se poori tarah se alag hone ke baavjood, aksar aisi sarv-gun-sampannata (roundedness) ke mukt vikas ki anumati deta hai ek aise tareeqe se jo vyaktiyon ko zindagi ki pure vividhta ke liye taiyar karta hai. Unhone Papua New Guinea mein tribal education systems ko dekha: ‘yahan koi formal instruction aur yaad karna nahi, koi classes nahi, koi exams nahi, koi cultural sites (schools) nahi jismein gyaan ke packages, unke context se alag karke, ek vyakti se doosre ko transmit kiye jaate hon.’ Paramparik shiksha mein ‘gyaan’, woh dekhte hain, ‘samajik jeevan se a-vibhajya’ hai.

Yeh mahatvapurna hai kyunki bachhon ko jo padhaya ja raha hai uski prasangikta aur upayogita se relate karne mein saksham hone ki zaroorat hai — unhe unhe transfer kiye ja rahe skill aur gyaan ke asli mulya ko samajhne ki zaroorat hai taaki woh apne aaspas ki duniya ko behtar dhang se samajhne (aur usme survive karne) ke liye ek auzar ke roop mein iska upayog karne mein saksham ho sakein. Vividh ‘fields’ mein pravesh (transcendence) is tarah swabhavik roop se aayega. Italian physician Maria Montessori dwara viksit kiye gaye school system jaise kuch modern school systems aisi educational philosophy ko pratibimbit karte hain.

Asal mein, traditional, tribal samajon mein bachhon ke a-pratibandhit paalan-poshan se creativity aur aam jeevan-taiyaari ke liye bachhon ke khel (childplay) ke mahatva ke baare mein bahut kuch seekha ja sakta hai. Modern samajon mein parents, apne bachhon ki suraksha ke prati atyadhik surakshatmak, khoj aur aam taur par child autonomy ko sankuchit karte hain. Jared Diamond ke Aka Pygmy Firaha Indians aur Western Australia ke Martu jaise traditional samajon mein adhyayan dikhate hain ki multi-age playgroups, creative toy-making aur real-life apprenticeship se bana ek educational framework ek aise vatavaran ki anumati deta hai jahan ‘education aur khel ke beech koi algaav nahi hai’ aur ek bahut hi sakht, creative aur lachila yuva paida karta hai ‘jo yeh vishwās nahi karte ki koi unka kuch karzdar hai.’

Yeh drishtikon ek bachhe mein curiosity ko mukt roop se raaj karne ki anumati deta hai, aur educationalist Ken Robinson ki ghoshna ki pushti karta hai ki ‘agar aap ek bachhe mein curiosity ki chingari jala sakte hain, toh woh bina kisi aage ki sahayata ke seekhenge.’ Is tarah, individuality ko bhi badhava diya jata hai. Agar koi modern samajon mein aisi creative autonomy ke tatvon ko import kar sakta hai, toh iska parinaam ek zyada polymathic bachpan ka vikas hoga. ‘Conventional Mind’ ko apne aadi-kaaleen instincts par wapas lautne ki anumati dene mein ya jaisa ki Robert Greene ise sandarbhit karte hain, ‘Original Mind,’ aisi shiksha adult ko apni poori zindagi ke dauran apne aantarik roop se creative, curious swabhav ke paas rehne ki anumati degi.

---

### Nurturing All-Rounders (Sarv-Gun-Sampanna Logon Ko Poshit Karna)

> Education ghatnaon ke beech ke chhipe connections ko samajhne ki kshamta hai.
> — Václav Havel, Czech playwright aur statesman

Confucius ki educational philosophy ke anusar, jaisa ki *The Great Learning* mein vyakt kiya gaya hai, kisi ko education ko ek jatil, aapas mein jude system ke roop mein treat karna chahiye jiske andar kisi ko santulan ke liye prayas karna chahiye. Unhone tarka diya ki seekhne ka koi bhi ek pehlu doosre se alag nahi hai, aur kisi ke seekhne ke ek hi pehlu ko poshit karne mein asafalta poore roop mein seekhne ki asafalta ki taraf le jayegi.

Kuch millennia aage badhein. University of Jena mein apne inaugural lecture mein, Goethe ke dost Friedrich Schiller ne *Bildung* (ek vyapak-aadhrit shiksha jo student ke aam vikas ka hissa bani) ki dharana ko badhava diya versus *Ausbildung* (specialist education jo students ko ek vishesh peshe ke liye taiyar karti hai). Is movement ke ek baad ke sadasya, Nietzsche ne specialisation ke vikas ke khilaf chetaya, jiske baare mein unhone kaha ki isne scholars ko poore ke drishtikon ko khone par majboor kiya. Unhone tarka diya ki specialist ‘kabhi bhi apni uchit uanchai tak nahi pahonch paayega, woh uanchai jahan se woh sarvekshan kar sakta hai, aaspas dekh sakta hai aur neeche dekh sakta hai.’

Usi movement ke ek aur mahatvapurna sadasya, polymath aur explorer Alexander von Humboldt ne *Wissenschaft* ki dharana ko introduced kiya, jo vyakti ke sarv-gun-sampanna vikas aur sirf dimaag ke bajaye poore vyaktitva ko poshit karne ki zaroorat ko darshata hai. University ka uddeshya, Humboldt ne zor diya, isliye ‘gyaan ke poore body ko kholna aur gyaan ke sabhi siddhanton aur buniyaadon dono ko pratipaadit karna’ hona chahiye. Eighteenth aur nineteenth centuries mein German intellectuals mein se kai pramukh log jaise Hegel aur Kant bhi aisi holistic education ke samarthak the. Karl Marx ne bhi apne *Communist Manifesto* mein yuvaon ke kai pehluon ko poshit karne ke liye vishesh roop se ‘many-sided education’ ka prastav rakha.

Twentieth century mein, ek naya educational movement, ya ‘social pedagogy’ viksit hua jismein teachers ko bachhe ko ek poore vyakti ke roop mein treat karne aur uske kul vikas ka samarthan karne ke liye train kiya gaya tha. Aaj aise schools hain jo holistic शिक्षण aur seekhne par aadharit aise educational model ka palan karte hain (ya kam se kam palan karne ki koshish karte hain). Kuch ne is ethos ko apne curriculum mein samahit kiya hai; Waldorf Schools, udaharan ke liye, Rudolf Steiner dwara intellectual, practical aur artistic seekhne ko badhava dene ke sath-sath ek samajik aur adhyatmik chetna viksit karne ke liye sthapit kiye gaye the. Krishnamurti Schools (Steiner ke samkalin aur saathi theosophist J. Krishnamurti dwara sthapit) ek isi tarah ki philosophy ka palan karte hain, jaise mystic aur modern polymath Sadhguru dwara sthapit Isha schools karte hain. Kitna aakarshak hai ki adhyatmik buniyaadon par sthapit schools is drishtikon ki shuruat kar rahe hain.

School ka curriculum chahe jo bhi ho, schoolteachers (jinmein se kai khud generalists hain) khud schools mein polymathy ko badhava dene mein ek mahatvapurna bhoomika nibha sakte hain. Hamlet Isakhanli ek scenario sujhalte hain:

> Ek aise student ko lena sambhav hai jo natural sciences ko achhe se samajhta hai aur use sangeet sunna sikhana, use sound aur harmony ke baare mein mathematical aur physical theories ke saral tatvon ki yaad dilana, poetry aur music ke beech ke connections par kaam karna, music history mein gote lagana, aur shayad use usi tarah ke projects dena jaise ek composition likhna sambhav hai.

Humein yeh nahi bhoolna chahiye ki alag-alag bachhe seekhne ki prakriya ke alag-alag stages par alag-alag talents viksit karte hain ya pradarshit karte hain. ‘Main aise bachhon ko jaanta hoon jo alag-alag disciplines mein, ya din ke alag-alag samay par, ya bade groups ke bajaye chhote groups mein behtar hain, aur kabhi-kabhi woh apne aap aage badhna chahte hain,’ Ken Robinson kehte hain.

Polymath Nathan Myhrvold, jinhone pehchana ki educational system ke paas quality control (standardised curriculum aur testing ke roop mein) ke sath ek factory ki aantarik visheshataayein hain aur ek acceptable level ki education ke sath jitne ho sake utne students ko graduate karne ka goal hai, ne apne judwaa bachhon ke liye ek custom education system design karne ka faisla kiya.

> Jaisa ki aap umeed karenge, yeh typical schools dwara pradan kiye jaane wale subjects aur teaching styles dono mein kaafi zyada vividh tha. Woh kuch samay ke liye Seattle ke ek majboot school mein gaye, lekin humne unke liye biology jaise kshetron mein jahan unhone shreshthta haasil ki ek university professor dwara private lessons ka bhi intazam kiya. Humne unke coursework se jude kai family trips liye — Greek islands ki yatra jahan Odyssey set thi, udaharan ke liye. Aur ek saal ke liye unhone Vermont mein ek a-paramparik farm-based school mein bhaag liya jahan sabhi students farm par kaam karte hain aur unke paas outdoors mein kai classes hoti hain. Saaf taur par yeh drishtikon zyada eclectic hai — shamil aur mehenga hone ki baat toh door hai — jitna kai families pasand karengi. Lekin mujhe lagta hai ki bachhon ko unke liye khule jeevan ke vividh tareeqon ke prati vyapak sampark dena samajhdari hai jabki kisi bhi passion ya talent ko khilana bhi jo woh shuruat mein dikhate hain.

General Studies, jise ek valid A-Level subject ke roop mein lambe samay se discredit kiya gaya hai, padhaya jana chahiye, jiske andar curriculum mein har subject ke context aur relevance par zor diya jana chahiye — woh sabhi ek doosre se kaise relate karte hain aur woh student ki apni zindagi se kyun relevant hain. Agar is tareeqe se set kiya jaye, toh General Studies ko curriculum mein sabse mahatvapurna subject banna chahiye.

Kisi bhi maamle mein, students ko unki sarv-gun-sampannata (roundedness) ke liye inaam diya jana chahiye. Unka mulyankan unki competence ki diversity ke anusar kiya jana chahiye, na ki sirf har respective subject ke andar unki competence aur gyaan ke anusar. Iske alawa, bhale hi ultimate goal specialise karna ho, phir bhi, jaise psychologists Gutman aur Schoon sujhalte hain, specialisation par ek vistarit moratorium hona chahiye jahan students apne talents aur interests ko explore karne mein zyada samay bitakar sakein.

---

### A 'Higher' Education (Ek 'Uchhatar' Shiksha)

Zyadatar modern education systems ke pyramidal specialisation structure ko dekhte hue, higher education ke andar seekhne ki diversity ko banaye rakhna ek vishaal chunauti hai. Alag-alag dauron aur sabhyataon se higher education ke kuch purane models par wapas lautna upayogi ho sakta hai. Taxila University (aaj ke Pakistan mein), duniya ki sabse purani, ne sunishchit kiya ki adult student medicine, law aur military sciences ke sath-sath elephant lore, archery aur hunting jaise vividh kshetron mein barabar achhe se trained tha. Muslim duniya mein universities aur higher education ke sansthan paramparik roop se polymathic seekhne ke kendra rahe hain, ek holistic educational anubhav pradan karte hue — Cairo mein Al Azhar aur Iran mein Qum iske behtareen udaharan hain. Europe mein, ‘university’ shabd, jo Latin *universitas* (jisaka matlab ‘universal’ ya ‘whole’ hai) se nikalta hai, ishara karta hai ki higher education ko bhi har student ke liye seekhne ke ek poore spectrum ko samet na chahiye. Asal mein, in early medieval universities ke paas unke curriculum ke roop mein *studia generalia* tha.

Aaj, halanki, ajeevak specialists ko order par manufacture kiya ja raha hai jaise woh lagbhag do sadiyon se kiye ja rahe hain. Jaise har vyakti ka praakritik swabhav ek bahumukhi, bahu-aayami hai (is kitaab mein baar-baar pradarshit), kya theoretically ek specialist ke bajaye ek polymath ko ‘manufacture’ karna zyada aasan nahi hona chahiye? Santiago Ramón y Cajal, Medicine/Physiology mein pehle-kabhi Nobel laureate aur modern neuroscience ke founding father (aur apne aap mein ek prasiddh artist), ne ek sarv-gun-sampanna, multi-field education ke labhon ki gawah di:

> Saaf-drishti wale teacher dwara pasand ke kaafi zyada yogya woh students honge jo thode zyada zidddi hain, pehle sthaan ka tiraskar karne wale, ahankar ke laalach ke prati a-sanvedansheel hain, aur jo ashanti kalpana ki prachurta se samppan hokar, literature, art, philosophy aur dimaag aur sharir ke sabhi manoranjanon ke peeche apni energy bitate hain. Unhe door se dekhne wale ke liye, aisa lagta hai jaise woh apni energy ko bikhair rahe hain aur nashat kar rahe hain, jabki vaastav mein, woh unhe channelise aur majboot kar rahe hain. . . .

Modern curriculum medieval universities ke polymathic structure ke sabse paas jo aata hai woh modern liberal arts system hai — United States mein vyapak lekin kahin aur kam. Europe mein shuru hue, ise aaj *Encyclopaedia Britannica* ke anusar paribhashit kiya gaya hai, ‘ek college ya university curriculum jiska uddeshya professional, vocational, ya technical curriculum ke vipreet general knowledge pradan karna aur general intellectual capacities viksit karna hai.’

‘Liberal’ ke sath, Latin mein matlab ‘aazad vyakti ke yogya’, iska uddeshya us dogma se aazad rehna hai jo shuruati specialisation ko gherata hai. Yeh scholar ke liye utna hi sach hai jitna ki student ke liye. Jab Douglas Hofstadter ne Harvard professor E.O. Wilson ko do polymathic professors ke baare mein bataya jo liberal arts colleges mein sthit the, Wilson ne nimnlikhit (paraphrased) ke sath jawab diya:

> Haan, us tarah ke adbhut thinkers aur teachers fancy Ivy League universities ki tulna mein chhote liberal-arts colleges mein paaye jaane ki kaafi zyada sambhavna hai, jo hamesha sirf ‘cutting-edge’ logon ko hire karte hain jinaki gehre ideas mein koi ruchi nahi hoti, balki kisi chhote discipline mein simply supernarrow ‘world-class’ specialists hote hain. Harvard aise ‘world-class’ logon se khacha-khach bhara hua hai, lekin agar koi mujhse poochhe ki unka bachha kahan ek asli shiksha pa sakta hai, toh main hamesha Harvard jaise elite jagah ke bajaye ek chhote (liberal arts) college ki sifarish karunga, jo kaafi zyada snobby aur trendy hai. Aap Harvard jaane se bahut saari pratishtha pa sakte hain, lekin aapko lagbhag utni achhi shiksha nahi milegi.

Kai conventional universities ke paas jeevant ‘extra-curricular activities’ hain jinmein vividh clubs aur societies (sports, arts, books, activism aur ityaadi se sambandhit) shamil hain lekin yeh tathya ki ise ‘extra-curricular’ maana jata hai conservative educational institutions ki aankhon mein sabhi additional pursuits ke peripheral (aur is tarah inferior) status ko darshata hai. United States mein Bachelor of General Studies (BGS) degree uplabdh hai, lekin aisi degrees ko aksar traditional universities aur employers dwara bahut ‘wishy-washy’ aur ‘lack of focus’ hone ke liye kharij kar diya jata hai. U.K. mein, University College London (UCL) un kuch sansthaon mein se ek hai jo liberal arts-style program offer karti hai. Yeh Bachelor of Arts and Science (BASc) degree chalati hai, jo students ko unke adhyayan ke dauran ek multidisciplinary raasta lene ke liye encourage karti hai. Jo log sochte hain ki aisi degrees graduate employers ke sath hichkichahat ka kaaran banti hain unhe apna dhyan haal hi ke Institute for Student Employment (ISE) report ki taraf lana chahiye, jisne paaya ki U.K. mein sirf 26 percent employers applicants par vichar karte waqt actual degree discipline ke sambandhit vishesh zarooraton ko vyakt karte hain.

---

### Self-Learning (Aatm-Shiksha / Swayam-Seekhna)

Lewis Mumford, twentieth century ke sabse aadaraniya intellectuals mein se ek, ko tuberculosis ke kaaran university chhodni padi thi. Phir bhi woh aakhirkar architecture, art, history aur urban planning se lekar technology, literature aur philosophy tak ke vishayon par ek world authority banne mein kamyab rahe. Unhone inmein se har vishay par kitaabein likhin, jinmein National Book Award-winning *Technics and Civilization* aur *City in History* shamil hain. Mumford ne is tarah dikhaya ki kisi bhi formal higher education ke bina ek intellectual polymath banna kaafi sambhav hai.

Ek holistic educational anubhav pradan karne ke liye sthapna sansthaon par nirbhar nahi kiya ja sakta. Ek universal mind viksit karne ke liye khud par nirbhar hona chahiye. Andar dekhna aksar intellectual, financial aur spiritual aazadi ka akela raasta hai. Individuality aur autonomy ke ahasas ko phir se khojna hyperspecialisation ke matrix se bachne ki kunjee hai.

Self-learning (ya ‘autodidactism’) prospective polymath ke liye ek vyavaharik — kuch keh sakte hain pasandida — raasta hai. Asal mein, itihaas mein anant polymaths formal learning process se niraash hue hain (mukhya roop se kyunki yeh aparyapt aur claustrophobically specialised tha), aur unhone standard curriculum ke baahar se apna gyaan aur skills dhoondhe.

Kul milakar, aatm-shiksha ka yeh roop shayad sabse asli (aur isliye shayad sabse prabhavshali) seekhne ki prakriya hai. Iski ignition ke roop mein kaam karne wale kisi samajik, sansthagath ya parivarik dabaav ke bina, yeh ek aantarik roop se self-motivated pursuit hai aur shuddh pehal aur curiosity par nirbhar karta hai; apne khud ke liye seekhne ki ichha. Sabse mahatvapurna baat, autodidacts shayad hi kabhi specialise karte hain, kyunki woh jaldi hi ehsaas kar lete hain ki domain isolation aur compartmentalisation ek krutrim rachna hai — gyaan ke formal disseminators dwara manufactured — jo manav samajh ki kshamta ko mahatvapurna roop se sankuchit karti hai.

Autodidact aur vidwan hip-hop artist Akala, jinhone bina kisi bachelor’s degree ke major U.K. universities se do honorary doctorates praapt kiye hain, self-learning ko intellectual freedom ka sabse behtareen raasta maante hain:

> Rajneetik roop se niyantrit academies ki seemaon ke baahar, vishesh roop se jaankari ke itne vyapak prasaar ke is daur mein, dimaag padhai ke apne raaste ko aage badhane ke liye aazad hain, jise unhe 'sazish' ya 'paagalpan' kaha jata hai usse a-pratibandhit, woh zyada khule taur par sochne aur taboos ko todne ke liye aazad hain. Adhyayan ka yeh roop academy ki sakhti aur anushasan ka pratisthapan nahi hai balki ek aatm-thopa gaya anushasan hai jo student dwara khud nirdeshit jawab dhoondhta hai na ki 'teacher' dwara.

Aaj online sroton (tutorials aur lectures ke videos, encyclopaedias, e-books, blogs, forums, social media aur ityaadi) ke zariya jaankari ki sugamta ke sath-sath sasti kitaabon ki uplabdhta aur kai libraries ke khulne ko dekhte hue self-learning bahut aasan ho gaya hai. Kai muft digital platforms ka uday duniya ke door-daraaz ke ilaqon mein ujjeval lekin seemit yuva dimaagon ko muft educational materials aur tutorials praapt karne ki anumati deta hai jo duniya ki kisi bhi pramukh university ke barabar shiksha ki anumati dete hain.

Asal mein, hum e-learning mein ek global boom ke gawah ban rahe hain: popular platforms ke udaharanon mein W3Schools, Khan Academy, University of the People, Open University, Academic Earth, Lumosity Brain Training, Mind Gym, Gems Education, edX, Skillshare, Udacity, Udemy, TeacherTube, MIT OpenCourseWare aur CK-12 shamil hain. Yeh ek aashawadi trajectory hai, lekin do mahatvapurna seemaon wali: pehle, yeh kabhi bhi ideas ke bhaautik aadan-pradan ke liye ek prabhavshali substitute ke roop mein kaam nahi kar sakti, aur doosra, koi bhi platform ek aisa unifying framework pradan nahi karta jo alag-alag kshetron ko jodta ho. Students ko synthesis aur integration apne aap karne ke liye chhod diya jata hai.

---

### A Polymathic Curriculum (Ek Polymathic Curriculum)

> Disciplines roop badalte hain, aakar lete hain aur apna charitra lete hain jaise-jaise ideas aur gyaan viksit hote hain . . . woh un sabhi tareeqon se jude hue hain jinhe dekhne se bachhon ko vartaman mein discourage kiya jata hai.
> — Ken Robinson

Ek aisi duniya mein jahan gyaan (kam se kam theoretically) hamari ungliyon par uplabdh hai, school curriculum ko na sirf vyapakta ke liye, balki shayad usse bhi zyada mahatvapurna context ke liye gehrai ka tyag karna chahiye. Intellectual exploration ke tareeqe jaise critical thinking, creative thinking, contextual thinking, speed-reading, emotional intelligence, decision-making, internalisation aur mental resilience ko mamooli 'information transmission' se bada pedagogical focus hona chahiye.

Nimnlikhit ko pre-university curriculum (16- se 18-saal ke bachhon ke liye) viksit karne ke liye ek 'systems approach' ke roop mein prastut kiya gaya hai — shayad International Baccalaureate (IB) ki tarah. Autodidactic adult ke liye, ise unke jeevan mein kisi bhi point par adhyayan ke liye ek framework ke roop mein upayog kiya ja sakta hai. Jaise ki woh listed form mein darshaye gaye hain, vishay aise lagte hain jaise woh alag aur codify kiye gaye hain, lekin aadarsh roop se unhe ek poore, jude hue roop mein prastut kiya jana chahiye — ek cosmic constellation ya ek neural network ki tarah — jo har cheez ki aantarik connectivity ko uchit roop se darshata hai. Vishay manav sthiti ke aath bhiyaadi pehluon ke anusar sangathit hain: Nature, Society, Mind, Body, Survival, Work aur Expression aur Transcendence. Koi kram ya hierarchy nahi hai kyunki woh bhi aapas mein jude hue hain aur ek doosre ke liye barabar mahatva ke hain. Uddeshya perspective haasil karna hai. Yeh perspective phir student ko is baat ke baare mein ek achhi tarah se soochit chunav karne ki anumati de sakta hai ki woh kya mahsoos karta hai ki use bhavishya ke adhyayan mein kis par focus karna chahiye. Woh 'mahatvapurna' aur 'aakarshak' kya hai iski apni samriddh samajh viksit karenge.

Gyaan ka har hissa haasil karne par, student ko prashnon ki ek shrinkhla par vichar karne ke liye encourage kiya jayega is point tak ki zaroori learning methods instinctive ban jayein: yeh kyun mahatvapurna hai? Yeh meri zindagi mein kaise fit baitha hai? Iska har doosri cheez ke sath kya connection hai? Yeh mujhe kya naye insights deta hai? Yeh meri zindagi ko kaise badha sakta hai? Main doosron ki madad karne ke liye iska upayog kaise kar sakta hoon? Aage ki jaanch ke yogya kya ho sakta hai? Kshetra mein mera sambhavid yogdan kya ho sakta hai?

---

### Transcendence (Pardeshita / Adhyatmikta)

* **Cosmology:** Brāhmāṇḍa aur iske uddeshya par observations
* **Existentialism:** Manav jeevan ke arth aur chetna ki shuruat par ideas
* **Introspection:** Andaruni yatra aur meditation ki kala ki jaanch karna
* **World traditions:** Mukhya world religions aur spiritual traditions ka adhyayan
* **Morality:** Moral compass, samay ke sath iska vikas aur sthaan ke anusar antar
* **Eschatology:** Afterlife par atkalen (speculations) aur postulations
* **Love:** History aur philosophy, context aur rishte ki prakriti ke anusar; abhivyakti, anukoolan, literature mein

---

### Nature (Prakriti)

* **Physics:** Energy, force, matter aur motion
* **Geography:** Geology, natural disasters, atmosphere, physics, astronomy, environment
* **Botany:** Podhe, vanaspati, horticulture
* **Chemistry:** Composition, structure, matter ke gun aur badlav
* **Zoology:** Animal kingdom, alag-alag species
* **Green living:** Prakriti ke beech jeene wale insaano par practical tools, methods aur ethics

---

### Society (Samaj)

* **Human history:** World human history (sabhi jaani-maani narratives aur perspectives)
* **Human geography:** Migration, population, pandemics
* **International relations:** Geopolitics, international organisations
* **Social organisation:** Socialism, democracy, communalism, feudalism aur ityaadi
* **Justice:** Duniya bhar mein aur itihaas mein legal systems
* **Humanitarianism:** Charity, disaster relief, gareebi unmoolan
* **Gender:** Samanta, antar, history aur philosophy
* **Globality:** Duniya ki bhashayein aur sanskritiyan
* **Future:** Science aur technology (superintelligence), social organisation mein trends aur scenarios
* **Challenges and solutions:** Global warming, nuclear proliferation, atyadhik gareebi, endemics aur bimariyan, natural disasters, yuddh, terrorism aur crime

---

### Mind (Dimaag)

* **Cognitive science:** Neuroanatomy aur psychology
* **Thinking methods:** Critical thinking, lateral thinking, strategic thinking, cognitive bias, cognitive exercises
* **Learning methods:** Reading, mnemonics, discourse, synthesis
* **Sources of knowledge:** Duniya bhar ki epistemological traditions ke anusar gyaan ke multiple sroton ki jaanch
* **History of ideas:** Vividh world traditions mein ideas aur philosophies ke itihaas ka sarvekshan
* **Mathematics:** Logic, geometry, algebra aur calculus

---

### Body (Sharir)

* **Human anatomy:** Insaani sharir, iske karya, potential aur seemaon ko samajhna
* **Nutrition:** Vividh khane mein nutrients ki pehchan aur sharirik aur manasik performance par unke sakaratmak aur nakaratmak prabhav
* **Physical training:** Exercise ke vividh uddeshyon aur tareeqon ko explore karna
* **Sports:** Alag-alag sharirik karyon ki zaroorat wale vividh sports ka adhyayan aur abhyas
* **Sex:** Uddeshya, parinaam aur performance
* **Hygiene:** Sharir, niwaas aur kaam ki jagah ki zaroori safai

---

### Survival (Aatma-Raksha / Jeevit Rehna)

* **Administration:** Patrachar (correspondence), logistics aur financial planning ka prabhavshali prabandhan
* **Arithmetic:** Rozmarra ki mathematical samasyaon ke samadhan
* **Emergency training:** Saadhan-sampannata (resourcefulness), first aid, sthiti ki jagrukta, crisis management, self-defence
* **Handiwork:** Basic plumbing, decorating, DIY, safai, driving
* **Family planning:** Protection/contraception ka upayog
* **Digital and tech:** Sabhi mukhya digital devices, apps, software ka prabhavshali upayog
* **Information:** Digital space ka prabhavshali navigation, news consumption ke tareeqe, media landscape ki ethics aur politics

---

### Work (Kaam)

* **Economics:** Macro/micro, corporatism, consumerism, vividh economic models (neoclassical, neoliberal, Marxist, Islamic, communal aur ityaadi)
* **Professional landscape:** Yeh samajhna ki koi aarthik roop se kaise tik sakta hai aur pragati kar sakta hai, vyaktigat roop se viksit ho sakta hai saath hi logon ki lives mein ek mahatvapurna yogdan de sakta hai; sambhavid career paths aur bhavishya ki sambhavnaon ka sarvekshan
* **Organisational skills:** Project management, workflow efficiency
* **Leadership:** Decision-making, prabhav aur prerna, risk-taking aur holistic synthesis
* **Teamwork:** Sahyog, sahkaryata, hamdardi, synergy, functionalism, samvaad, emotional intelligence
* **Entrepreneurship:** Risk vishleshan, market landscaping, business modelling/planning, vikas
* **Self-development:** Bhashayein, mind training, reading, vocational education

---

### Expression (Abhivyakti)

* **Creative thinking:** Ek method aur abhyas ke roop mein creativity ki art aur science ka sarvekshan
* **Aesthetics:** Sundarta ki philosophy aur iska itihaas
* **Visual art:** Painting, drawing, sculpture, photography aur design se sambandhit theory, history, abhyas aur creativity
* **Music:** World music, dance mein theory, history, abhyas aur creativity
* **Literature:** World literature ki theory, history, abhyas aur creativity
* **Film/theatre:** World film/theatre ki theory, history, abhyas aur creativity

---

## Occupation (PESHA / KAAM)

### Revising the Notion of ‘Work’ (Work Ki Dharana Ka Sodhan)

Education ka ultimate objective, phir, employers ke aashwasan ke liye simply ek dashakon-lambi initiation ceremony hone se badal kar, jeevan aur manav sthiti ka 360-degree exploration ban jata hai. ‘Kamaane ke liye seekhna’ aur ‘seekhne ke liye seekhna’ ke beech koi dwidha (dichotomy) nahi hai.

Jaise pehle charcha ki gayi thi, ‘survive karne ke liye specialise karein’ ki modern dharana ko sakht sodhan (serious revision) ki zaroorat hai. Woh gatividhi jismein koi survival aur ik इकट्ठा karne ke saadhan pradan karne ke liye shamil hota hai use kisi ke samay ka zyadatar hissa lene ki zaroorat nahi hai, unhe paribhashit karne ki baat toh door hai. Yahan tak ki traditional samajon mein hunter-gatherers, jinhe kai logon ne maana ki woh survival ke liye ek lagatar sangharsh mein the, vaastav mein khana haasil karne par dhyan kendrit karne mein haftah mein sirf do se teen din ke barabar bitate the, aur use bhi ek kaam (chore) ke bajaye ek ‘tyohar’ ya ek ceremony ke roop mein zyada maana jata tha. Kisi ke jeevan-kaal mein kisi gatividhi ke bade hisse ko us ek cheez par bitaye jaane ka poora vichar jo unhe khilata hai ek modern, capitalist construct hai.

Work aur leisure ka algaav hamesha itna sakht nahi tha. ‘Work’ ko un sabhi gatividhiyon ko samet na chahiye — paid ya unpaid, intellectual ya practical — jiska manav jeevan mein survival ya vikas mulya hai. Yir-Yoront indigenous Australian tribe, udaharan ke liye, ke paas abhi bhi ek shabd (*woq*) hai jo vaastav mein kisi bhi aisi cheez ko sandarbhit karta hai jise karne ki zaroorat hai — chahe woh hunting ho, ritualistic ceremony, recreation ya procreation ho. Yeh manav gatividhi ke kai kshetron ko milata hai, unhe ek ekrat, interconnected jeevan-shaili ke roop mein treat karta hai. Historian Felipe Fernández-Armesto humein yaad dilate hain ki prehistoric, Stone Age samajon mein ‘leisure ko work se alag karne ki, ya kisi bhi class ya dono mein se kisi bhi sex ko is tak vishesh pahonch ke sath visheshadhikar dene ki koi zaroorat ya mauqa nahi tha.’

Ek isi tarah ke tareeqe se, polymaths zaroori roop se apne kaam ko conventional sense mein ‘careers’ ya ‘professions’ ke roop mein nahi dekhte — woh aksar apni gatividhiyon (paid ya unpaid) ko ‘pursuits,’ ya ‘projects,’ ya ‘opportunities,’ ya ‘ventures’ ya ‘initiatives’ ke roop mein sandarbhit karenge. Woh pradarshit karte hain ki ‘work’ ko ek kaam (chore) hone ki zaroorat nahi hai, balki romanshak adventures ki ek shrinkhla hona chahiye. Phir bhi ‘life ke liye ek field’ ka model abhi bhi hamare samajon mein swikrut norm hai; hum logon ke ‘apna niche dhoondhne’ aur uske sath tike rehne ke sath junooni bane hue hain. Hamare day jobs abhi bhi woh label bane hue hain jiske dwara hamare poore astitva ko mahsoos aur paribhashit kiya jata hai.

---

### The Twenty-First-Century Career Landscape (21st-Century Ka Career Paridrishya)

Work ki duniya asahniya roop se jatil ho gayi hai. Professional duniya ka ped bahu-sankhyak shakhaon, up-shakhaon aur tehniyon ko samet ne ke liye atyadhik badh gaya hai, har ek ek job title ka pratinidhitv karta hai. Professions ke sath-sath ‘fields’ ko aaj phir se paribhashit kiya ja raha hai. ‘Merchant’ ka apekshakrit purana concept, udaharan ke liye, ne ab kai naye roop le liye hain. Mool roop se, merchants logon ke ek aise class ke roop mein ubhare jinhone artisan ya farmer se commercial bojh hata diya, baad wale ko unki respective skills par focus karne ki anumati dete hue, kisi bhi bhatkaav ya dabaav se mukt. Aaj yeh merchants literary agents, art agents, sports agents, speaking agents, real estate agents, yahan tak ki recruitment agents ke roop mein aate hain, jin sabhi ne khud ko ek doosre se poori tarah se alag maana hai.

Kisi ki polymathy ka mulyankan karne mein, humne ab tak traditional professions ya ‘fields of accomplishment’ par vichar kiya hai jinmein scientist, artist, athlete, musician, politician, soldier, writer, architect, physician, priest, poet, lawyer, diplomat aur ityaadi shamil hain. Yeh vaastav mein kaal-atheeth (timeless) hain. Lekin aaj, naye fields ubhare hain jinake andar vyaktiyon ke paas shreshthta haasil karne ki jagah hai, tarkasangat roop se traditional kshetron ke barabar levels tak. Yeh beshak 21st century mein polymathy ki prakriti ko prabhavit karta hai.

In naye fields ya professions mein se zyadatar — jo mool roop se information age aur corporation ke uday ke parinaamswarup paida hue hain — ne extinct professions jaise courtier, alchemist, gladiator aur messenger ko insolvency practitioner, management consultant, public relations agent, human resources manager, customer services representative, property developer, logistics manager aur charity coordinator jaise logon se replace kar diya hai. Haal hi mein, humne app developer, AI engineer, social media marketer aur data scientist ka uday dekha hai. ‘Reality TV star,’ ‘blogger,’ ‘royal pundit,’ ‘gossip columnist’ aur ‘socialite’ ke baare mein kya? Inmein se kitne woh hain jise David Graeber ‘bullshit jobs’ kehte hain — kam samajik mulya wale nirarthak occupations jo logon ko lambe samay tak kaam karne aur unhe ‘unke apne projects, pleasures, visions aur ideas’ ko aage badhane se bhatkane ke liye design kiye gaye hain — yeh vivadaaspad hai.

Iske alawa, 21st century ke vishesh professions ne professional landscape ko poori tarah se badal diya hai, jaise technology ke ghatatmak vikas se ubharne wale occupations ne kiya hai. Kis hadd tak is tarah ke occupations polymathic repertoire mein jode jaane ke liye ‘distinct fields of accomplishment’ ke roop mein qualify karte hain yeh vivadaaspad hai, lekin kam se kam vichar kiya jana chahiye.
