Aur phir, beshak, hamare paas computerisation aur job automation ki ghatatmak prakriya hai, jiska kaafi hissa industrial revolution ke baad se ho raha hai. Oxford scholars Frey aur Osborne ne ek adhyayan kiya yeh dikhane ke liye ki un jobs par bhi jinhe hum ‘safe,’ ‘human-centric’ maante hain automation ka khatra hoga:

> Jabki 19th century ki manufacturing technologies ne tasks ke saralikaran ke zariya kaafi hadd tak skilled labour ko substitute kiya, 20th century ki Computer Revolution ne middle-income jobs ko khali karne (hollowing-out) ka kaaran banaya. . . . Jabki computerisation itihaas roop se explicit rule-based gatividhiyon se jude routine tasks tak seemit raha hai, big data ke algorithms ab pattern recognition par nirbhar domains mein tezi se pravesh kar rahe hain aur non-routine cognitive tasks ki ek vyapak range mein labour ko aasaani se substitute kar sakte hain. Iske alawa, advanced robots behtar senses aur dexterity haasil kar rahe hain, jo unhe manual tasks ke ek vyapak scope ko perform karne ki anumati deta hai. Isse industries aur occupations mein work ki prakriti badalne ki sambhavna hai.

Is baat ko dhyan mein rakhte hue, kisi ko professional life aur aam taur par ‘work’ ke prati apne drishtikon mein creative hona chahiye. Ise ek aise tareeqe se design kiya jana chahiye jo autonomy, contribution aur vyaktigat vikas ki anumati deta hai. Humein education phase ke dauran viksit values, methods aur drishtikon ke prati sachet rehna chahiye, aur sachha rehna chahiye.

Ubharte hue polymath ke liye, phir, modern professional life mein teen sambhavid raaste hain jo mel khate hain: successive career changes, simultaneous (portfolio) careers aur polymathic professions.

---

### Career Changes (Career Badlav)

Harvard psychologist Dan Gilbert zor dete hain ki hum sabhi is bhram mein hain ki hamara vyaktigat itihaas bas abhi khatam hua hai, ki hum haal hi mein woh log ban gaye hain jo hum apni baaki zindagi ke liye hamesha banne wale the. ‘Log apni zindagiyon mein badlav ko kam aankte hain’ woh humein yaad dilate hue kehte hain ki hamari zindagi mein ek sthir cheez, vaastav mein, badlav hai. ‘Manav jeev vikas ke kaam (works in progress) hain jo galati se sochte hain ki woh poore ho gaye hain.’

Hamari raayein, mindsets, priorities aur objectives, chahe kitne bhi gaadhe hon, zaroori roop se fluid hain. Lagatar badlav ki sthiti mein hone par, woh apni prakriti se dynamic aur evolutionary hain — woh apariharyata roop se badalte hain. Jaise Gilbert ise rakhte hain, ‘samay ek shaktishali taqat hai, yeh hamari pasand ko badal deta hai, yeh hamare values ko phir se aakar deta hai, yeh hamare vyaktitva ko badal deta hai.’ Isse, hum yeh nishkarsh nikal sakte hain ki hum sabse zyada sambhavna hai ki apni zindagi ke alag-alag stages par alag-alag cheezein chahenge. Chahe yeh pyaar, adhyatmikta, sports, parivar, sex, technology, bhaautik kabze, jaanwaron, yatra ya khane se juda ho, hamari priorities aur preferences umar ke sath aur paristhitiyon ke anusar badalti hain. Yeh mool roop se hamare dimaag ki aantarik plasticity ke kaaran hai. Is baat ko dhyan mein rakhte hue, logon ko, kam se kam theoretically, apni zindagi ke kisi bhi diye gaye daur mein un cheezon ko aage badhane ki aazadi honi chahiye jo unke liye sabse zyada maayne rakhti hain.

Yeh zindagi ko aakarshak aur vividh banata hai, lekin ek shaktishali haqeeqat ko bhi ughadta hai: ki polymathy hamesha ek sambhavna hai. Isliye niyamit career badlav koi chonkane wali cheez nahi honi chahiye, na hi unpar aanch aani chahiye. Is baat ko dekhte hue ki aantarik talents aur interests nishchit gyaan aur anubhavon ki prati-kriya ke roop mein jaagte hain, hum kabhi nahi jaan sakte ki kis samay kya humein aakarshit karega ya kab kaun si skills ya gyaan unlock ya haasil honge. Albert Schweitzer ne apne 30s mein medicine padhna shuru kiya, Takeshi Kitano ne apne 40s mein apni pehli feature film banayi, Rabindranath Tagore ne apne 60s mein painting ke liye apna shauk khoja aur Paul Newman apne 70s mein ek racing champion bane. Polymath ke liye, umar sach mein sirf ek number hai.

Serial career change ka idea nishchit roop se naya nahi hai. 19th century mein, William Pember Reeves ne, New Zealand mein paida hue aur padhe-likhe whites ki pehli peedhi se, ek athlete ke roop mein shuruat ki, phir ek lawyer, phir diplomat (High Commissioner to London), phir banker (Chairman of the New Zealand Central Bank), phir journalist (Editor of the Canterbury Times), phir poet (*The Passing of the Forest* aur *A Colonist in his Garden* jaise prasiddh poems publish kiye) aur historian (*History of New Zealand* likhi) bane isse pehle ki woh ek sath Minister of Labour, Education aur Justice ke roop mein seva karne ke liye politics mein pravesh karte.

Aaj, hamare hyper-specialised workplaces mein, career badlav ke liye ek badhti hui bhookh hai. U.K. mein aaj, udaharan ke liye, U.K. mein 10 mein se lagbhag 1 vyakti ka apna career badalne ka irada hai, jo sujhatta hai ki lagbhag 2.5 million log aisa karne par vichar kar sakte hain. 20-somethings ke 51 percent pehle se hi apne career ke chunav par afsos jatate hain aur ek alag chunenge. Iske alawa, U.K. workforce ke 45 percent bade career badlav par vichar kar rahe the. Yadyapi yeh adhyayan ek recession ke mandraate hue hi kiya gaya tha, recession jaisi vaastaviktaayein working life mein a-samanya nahi hain. Ek cheez ko badalne ka mauqa diye jaane par, 25 percent adults kisi bhi doosri cheez se upar ek naya job chunenge.

Lekin jabki bade career badlav vaastav mein zyadatar logon ki zindagiyon mein ek haqeeqat ban gaye hain, yeh dhyan diya jana chahiye ki do ‘kamyab,’ asambandhit careers hona polymath hone ke barabar nahi hai; ‘poly’ bhashayi roop se do se zyada ka matlab rakhta hai, aur bahut kam logon ke paas saaf taur par alag kshetron mein teen ya zyada kamyab careers rahe hain. Iske alawa, career badlav ke data ko record karna kathin hai. American Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) ne, udaharan ke liye, kabhi yeh andaza lagane ki koshish nahi ki hai ki log apne working lives ke dauran kitni baar careers badalte hain kyunki yeh sunishchit karna kathin hai ki vaastav mein ‘career change’ kya banata hai. Polymath ke liye ek career change field ka badlav ke sath-sath job mein badlav bhi hai.

Kuch career badlav aam hain, aur yadyapi kabhi-kabhi unmein saaf taur par alag kshetron mein chhalaang shamil ho sakti hai, badlav aksar aam samajh bante hain. Soldiers diplomats aur phir politicians ban jaate hain; fashion models actors aur phir filmmakers ban jaate hain, athletes coaches aur phir commentators ban jaate hain. Doosre career change trends mein yeh tathya shamil hai ki kai professions — jaise ki ek athlete, ek singer, ek actor aur ek musician ka — ek shuruati shuruat (aur aksar, ek shuruati ant) ka aashwasan dete hain, jo swabhavik roop se ek career change ko utprerit karta hai. Lekin jabki ‘child stars’ ya prodigies ke kam umar mein kaafi kuch haasil karne ki sambhavna hoti hai, bahut kam vaastav mein adulthood mein apne careers ko banaye rakhte hain (jaisa ki former prodigy Alison Quark ne apni kitaab *Hothouse Kids: The Dilemma of the Gifted Child* mein paaya). Aamtaur par chhote careers jaise ki sports, entertainment aur military mein baad mein doosre careers aate hain, jo zyadatar mamlon mein gehre se jude hote hain — ek footballer coach ya commentator ban jata hai, ek soldier military adviser ya spy ban jata hai, ek film director producer ya screenwriter ban jata hai.

Ek aur aam pravritti: kisi vishesh kshetra mein ek kamyab career viksit karne ke baad, kai log us kshetra se juda ek business shuru karte hain; toh ek doctor ya ek lawyer ya ek engineer ya ek journalist, baad mein ek businessman bhi ban jata hai, jiske liye beshak ek poori tarah se alag skill set ki zaroorat hoti hai. Yeh isliye hai kyunki log aam taur par parichit kshetra mein rehna pasand karte hain, ya ki jab woh career change ki ichha karte hain, toh mauqe unke apne kshetra ke andar sabse zyada vaastavik roop se prachur hote hain.

Yahi wajah hai ki ‘inter-field’ polymaths — woh jo apne vyapak kshetra ke andar versatility pradarshit karte hain, jaise science, sport, ya art — shayad sabse aam prakar hain. J.P.R. Williams, Sócrates (20th-century ke Brazilian footballer!), Imran Khan aur George Foreman jaise athletes anokhe (anomalies) hain; woh ek taaza shuruat karte hain aur ek poori tarah se asambandhit interest ko aage badhane ke mauqe ka upayog karte hain, chahe chetan roop se ya kisi life-shifting anubhav ya trigger event (jaise maut ya bimari, ek adhyatmik jaagruti, ek samajik ya rajneetik kaaran) ke kaaran.

Kisi vishesh kshetra mein kamyab hona aatmakankshi polymath ke liye shraap aur vardaan dono ho sakta hai. Ek taraf kamyabi kisi vyakti ke kalank/stigma ya pigeonholing ka kaaran banti hai, jisse unhe kisi doosre kshetra mein mauqa diya jana lagbhag asambhav ho jata hai. Unhe ‘occupational closure’ dwara bhi roka jata hai — har ‘profession’ ko ‘insiders’ dwara amateurs ke roop mein dekhe jaane wale outsiders se bachane ke liye uthayi gayi baadh dwara pravesh mein ek rukawat. Doosri taraf, paise, contacts, mahaarat aur aam taur par mauqe jo ek kshetra mein kamyabi se aate hain kai doosri sambhavnaon ke darwaze khol sakte hain jo doosri tarah se uplabdh nahi hote. Ek kshetra mein kamyabi, yaani, kai doosre kshetron mein lagatar kamyabi ka ek chakra pradan kar sakti hai. Ek bodybuilder ya martial artist achanak ek A-list Hollywood actor ban sakta hai. Wahi actor phir achanak ek labhdayak business opportunity offer kiya ja sakta hai. Business se banaya gaya paisa aur Hollywood star ke roop mein haasil ki gayi popularity phir use political office ke liye ek majboot abhiyan chalane ki anumati de sakti hai. Yeh ghatna, jise ‘accumulative advantage’ ya Matthew Effect ke roop mein jaana jata hai, yahi wajah hai ki kamyab businessmen ullekhniya philanthropists aur hobbyists ban jaayenge, celebrity singers ko mukhya acting roles milenge aur unka ‘brand’ unhe apna khud ka kamyab fashion range shuru karne ki anumati dega.

Oprah Winfrey, jinhe aaj duniya ki sabse prabhavshali mahilaon mein se ek maana jata hai, ne shuruat mein ek talk show host ke roop mein kamyabi haasil ki, jisne unhe ek actress banne ki taraf badhaya, jisne film production ki taraf badhaya, jisne doosre business ventures ki taraf badhaya, jisne kai doosre charitable ventures ki taraf badhaya, jisne social activism ki taraf badhaya (aur jo aakhirkar unhe presidency tak le ja sakta hai) aur ityaadi. Simply apni shohrat aur public profile ke kaaran (unke spasht talents ka zikr na karte hue), Stephen Fry ko shayad product endorsements, guest editing, TV presenting, public speaking, business start-ups aur unke anubhav se poori tarah se asambandhit har tarah ke doosre projects se lekar an-ginit prastav mil rahe hain. Jaise Juli Crockett, American boxer-turned-musician-turned-playwright-turned-academic swikar karti hain, ‘har cheez har doosri cheez ko khilati hai.’

Vyakti ke liye yeh adbhut hai lekin kisi samaj ya samuday ke liye Matthew effect vaastav mein socio-economic asamanta (inequality) ke mukhya kaaranon mein se ek hai — elite networks ka ek duschakra jo kamyabi aur uplabdhi ko logon ke ek chhote, chune hue group tak seemit rakhta hai kyunki woh har ek aakhirkar ek doosre ke careers ka samarthan aur suvidhajanak banate hain. Isliye yeh hairani ki baat nahi hai ki vishwa itihaas mein polymaths ki majority — kuch avsarwadi, doosre anichha se system mein khinche gaye — elite class se the (ya aakhirkar shamil hue).

Yeh sach hai ki zyadatar log ek ‘extreme’ career change (jo kaam ke kshetra ko poori tarah se badal deta hai) ki sambhavna ko daravani aur risky dono paate hain. Curiosity ke ahasas ke bajaye an-jaane ka darr hota hai. Yeh darr kam se kam aanshik roop se is aam dharana ke kaaran maujood hai ki jaise-jaise hum bade hote hain, hamari utpadakta (productivity), intelligence aur creativity ghat-ti hai. Yeh kisi ki naye sir se kuch seekhne ki kshamta mein vishwas ke khone ki taraf le jata hai. Jabki sahi hai agar is premise par aadharit ho ki vyakti ke paas ek linear, ajeevak career ya specialisation hai, yeh dharana un logon ke liye nahi tikti jo vividhta laate hain.

Cognitive scientists sochte the ki jaise-jaise hum bade hote hain, hamare dimaag badlav ke anusar adjust hone ki apni kshamta kho dete hain. Ab neuroscientists ne khoj liya hai ki dimaag poori zindagi kuch plasticity banaye rakhta hai, aur kisi bhi umar mein kami ke aas-pas kaam karna seekh sakta hai. Psychologist Keith Simonton ne paaya ki jabki creative output vridhavastha mein ghat-ta hai (ek career mein linear progression ke zariya diminishing returns ke parinaamswarup), un vyaktiyon ke liye jo apne kaam ke kshetra ko poori tarah se badalte hain, yeh trend khatam kiya ja sakta hai ya ulat bhi kiya ja sakta hai. Middle-aged specialist ke liye, apne astitva ke doosre pakshon ko explore karne ke liye utsukh lekin hichkichate hue, yeh ek aashawadi tathya hai.

Asal mein, Yuval Noah Harari ke anusar, *Homo Deus* aur *21 Lessons for the 21st Century* ke lekhak, har kuch saalon mein khud ko phir se paribhashit karna ek vikalp nahi, balki ek zaroorat hogi:

> ‘2050 ki duniya ke sath bane rehne ke liye, aapko na keval naye ideas aur products ka aavishkar karne ki zaroorat hogi — aapko sabse upar baar-baar khud ko phir se invent karne ki zaroorat hogi.’

Physical, cognitive aur circumstantial badlav ki apariharyata ka matlab hai ki yeh tathya ki log apni zindagi ke alag-alag samayon par apne talents aur interests ko pradarshit kar sakte hain koi chonkane wali baat nahi honi chahiye. Aisa badlav ek andaruni ya baahari anubhav ka parinaam ho sakta tha, paristhitiyon ka ek jawab ya sirf shuddh prerna. Ki ek lawyer achanak apne career ke charam par history of art ke liye ek junoon viksit karta hai use aakarshak nahi dekha jana chahiye. Ki ek musician 50 saal ki umar mein ek mathematics program mein ya 60 saal ki umar mein ek mechanical apprenticeship mein dakhila leta hai use ek praakritik sambhavna ke roop mein swikar kiya jana chahiye. Malcolm Gladwell ke paas *Entrepreneur Magazine* ke sath apne interview mein dene ke liye kuch aakarshak salah thi:

> Sabse mahatvapurna cheez yeh hai ki kabhi bhi apne baare mein aisa faisla na lein jo aapke vikalpon ko seemit karta ho. Self-conceptions shaktishali roop se seemit karne wale hote hain. Khud ko paribhashit karne ke kaam mein, aap badlav ke mauqon ko band karna shuru kar dete hain, aur yeh mujhe ek bahut hi bewakoofana cheez lagti hai agar aap 85 saal ke nahi hain.

---

### Portfolio Careers (Portfolio Careers)

> Hamari working identity ek chhipa hua khazana nahi hai, jo hamare astitva ke bilkul kendra mein khoje jaane ka intazar kar raha hai — balki yeh kai sambhavnaon se bani hai . . . hum kai selves hain.
> — Herminia Ibarra

Anukramik roop se (sequentially) multiple career badlavon se guzarna aapke andar ke polymath ko mukt karne ka ek raasta hai, doosra ek hi samay mein (simultaneously) multiple careers ko aage badhana hai. Aaj is baad wale raaste ko aksar ‘portfolio career’ ke roop mein sandarbhit kiya jata hai; kisi bhi diye gaye samay par ‘projects’ ya jobs ka ek portfolio rakhne ka ek system. Kyunki ek hi kshetra mein ek sath jobs non-competition clauses jaise karakon ke kaaran shayad hi kabhi sambhav hote hain, portfolio careerists aksar vividh aapatat: asambandhit domains mein kaam karte hain jo unke interests, abilities aur qualifications se mel khate hain. Yeh West mein working middle class ke beech ek fashionable trend hai lekin vaastav mein saalon se duniya bhar mein sangharsh karne walon ke liye zaroorat ki zindagi rahi hai.

Guzara karne ke liye, vishesh roop se developing world mein, logon ko ashadharan roop se saadhan-sampann aur creative banna pada hai. Yahi wajah hai ki developing world ke sheharon mein rehne wale average vyakti (male ya female) ke paas ek sath multiple jobs hone ki ek majboot pravritti hoti hai. Addis Ababa mein ek taxi-driving, jewellery-selling hotel receptionist, ya Colombo mein ek street-busking, tennis-coaching lifeguard se milna aam baat hai. Poverty line par log entrepreneurial banne ke liye majboor hain — woh ‘hustle’ karne ke liye majboor hain; unke paas maujood kisi bhi talent ko monetise karein, chahe woh street crowds ka manoranjan karne ke liye kitchen utensils ke sath drums bajana ho ya kisi bhi cheez ko bechne ke liye apne interpersonal aur trading abilities ka upayog karna ho jo unke haath lag sake. Aisi jobs se judi aparyaptta ya a-sthirta ke kaaran, unke paas ek sath unme se kai ko aage badhane ke alawa thoda vikalp hai is umeed mein ki ek aadha-uchit aamdani haasil ki ja sake.

Sirf haal hi mein developed world mein, vishesh roop se working middle classes ke beech, log aisi occupational diversification ke labhon ko samajhna shuru kar rahe hain. Daniel Pink ne apni kitaab *Free Agent Nation* mein dekha ki America ka career landscape badal raha tha aur jald hi freelancers ya portfolio careerists dwara haavi hoga. Bill Bridges ki kitaab *Jobshift* ne isi tarah ke tarka diye, Marci Alboher ki *One Person/Multiple Careers* ne United States mein aam logon ke beech is badhte trend ko rekhankit kiya aur haal hi mein Emilie Wapnick ki *How to be Everything* aur Emma Gannon ki *The Multi-Hyphen Method* ne millennials se portfolio career design ki badhti maang ka jawab diya hai. 21st century hone ke naate, revenue ke sambhavid srot guna ho gaye hain. Kuch logon ke liye, ‘gig economy’ aarthik a-suraksha ka matlab rakhti hai. Doosron ke liye, yeh ek mauqa hai.

Portfolio careers ko encourage karne wale samajon ya systems ko kabhi-kabhi apmaan-janak roop se ‘gig economies’ ke roop mein sandarbhit kiya jata hai aur job insecurity aur employer exploitation se joda jata hai. Yeh aksar sach hota hai, yahi wajah hai ki aisa career route zaroorat ya norm ke bajaye ek vikalp ke roop mein uplabdh hona chahiye.

World-leading management thinker Charles Handy, halanki, zor dete hain ki ek ‘portfolio worker’ hona kathin aarthik samayon mein ek chatur survival strategy hai kyunki yeh berozgar hone ke risk ko kam karti hai. Barrie Hopson, *10 Steps to Creating a Portfolio Career* ke co-author, pushti karte hain ki kai jobs ki ek jeevan-shaili ek financial aur professional safety net ki anumati deti hai. Unhone paaya ki unke adhyayan mein zyadatar portfolio careerists ne apne portfolio career ke do saalon ke andar usse zyada kamaya jitna unhone kabhi full-time employee ke roop mein kamaya tha aur sample mein 46 portfolio careerists mein se sirf ek pichhle do saalon mein single-track career par wapas lauta. Mahatvapurna roop se, unke sabhi participants ne daava kiya ki woh aisi career lifestyle se zyada khush the kyunki yeh ek kaafi zyada aatm-santhoshti-dayak work-life blend pradan karti thi. Asal mein, jaise upar pradarshit kiya gaya hai, vyaktigat aatm-siddhi survival ke liye financial labh jitni hi (aur kuch ke liye, zyada) mahatvapurna ho sakti hai.

Yeh koi hairani ki baat nahi hai. Hamara aantarik bahumukhi-pan (multifacetedness) abhyas kiye jaane ke adhikaar ke liye tarasta hai. Apni kitaab *How to Find Fulfilling Work* mein, cultural thinker Roman Krznaric ne zor diya ki ‘ek hi samay mein kai careers ko aage badhana samriddh hone aur hamare multiple selves ke prati sachha hone ka ek tareeqa hai.’ Aatm-siddhi vishesh roop se tab kamzor hoti hai jab log kisi vishesh kshetra mein bahut zyada samay bitakar diminishing returns ka anubhav karte hain. Alboher ne nishkarsh nikala ki vividhta ki aantarik ichha ko santusht karne ke alawa, multiple careers multiple income streams pradan karte hain aur ‘un logon mein aam burnout ke khilaf ek tonic ke roop mein kaam karte hain jo vishesh roop se ek income endeavour ko aage badhate hain.’

Portfolio careers ko samaj ke alag-alag segments dwara alag-alag tareeqon se apnaya jata hai. Celebrities (vishesh roop se arts aur entertainment ki duniya mein wale), udaharan ke liye, aksar portfolio careers rakhte hain — kabhi-kabhi visheshadhikar praapt dilettantes ke roop mein interests ko aage badhane ke liye, lekin mukhya roop se ek be-reham, a-sthir industry mein survival ke saadhan ke roop mein. American popular culture, udaharan ke liye, filhaal Lenny Kravitz, Will.i.am, Jamie Foxx, Madonna, Jennifer Lopez, Donald Glover aur Pharrell Williams jaise versatile artists dwara haavi hai, jinmein se har ek ne music, film, fashion aur business mein kamyabi haasil ki hai.

Woh apni gatividhiyon mein vividhta laate hain zaroori roop se isliye nahi kyunki unke paas jo kuch bhi unke dimaag ko lubhata hai use karne ka luxury hai (halanki kabhi-kabhi aisa hota hai), balki kyunki woh pehchante hain ki entertainment industry ki chamak aur daulat bahut aksar kam samay tak tikti hai, aur isliye creative, entrepreneurial aur vividh hona tikne (aur jeevan-shaili ko banaye rakhne) ka sabse pakka tareeqa hai. Asal mein, unmein se kai ek ‘brand’ banane par focus karte hain jise phir commercial initiatives ki ek vyapak range par laagu kiya ja sakta hai — un initiatives mein unki vyaktigat bhagidari ki degree chahe jo bhi ho. Asal mein, *Wired* ke co-founder Kevin Kelly ke anusar, ek creator ko apne brand ke aas-pas ek labhdayak business banane ke liye prasiddh hone ki bhi zaroorat nahi hai — unhe sirf 1,000 true fans ki zaroorat hai jo unke dwaara produce kiye gaye lagbhag kisi bhi cheez ko khareedenge kyunki woh kisi vishesh product ke bajaye vyakti ke prati vafadar hain. Toh ek polymath sambhavid roop se apni paintings, books, music, perfume aur fashion ko ek hi fan base ko bech sakta hai aur ek achhi rozi-roti kama sakta hai.

Toh kya ek portfolio career aapke liye hai? Yeh is baat par nirbhar karta hai ki kya aap kaam ke sambandh mein ek vishesh manoshtithi apna sakte hain. Portfolio careerists ke paas conventional careerists ke muqable ek alag psyche hoti hai. Woh apne kaam ko traditional sense mein ‘careers’ ya ‘professions’ ke roop mein nahi dekhte — woh iske bajaye apni vividh gatividhiyon ko ‘pursuits,’ ‘projects,’ ‘opportunities,’ ‘ventures,’ ya ‘initiatives’ ke roop mein sandarbhit karenge. Asal mein industrial age se pehle ‘job’ shabd ka matlab simply ek vishesh ‘task’ ya ‘undertaking’ tha aur sirf haal hi mein ek sangathan mein ek lambe samay ke (ya ajeevak) role ya kisi vishesh kshetra ke prati ek exclusive samarpan ka paryay ban gaya hai.

Yeh ishara karte hue ki har ‘undertaking’ ek ajeevak career se mahatvapurna roop se chhota hai, yeh time-scale ko khula chhodta hai. Iska matlab yeh bhi hai ki har project poora din, saptah, mahina ya saal nahi le sakta. Yahi wajah hai ki Luke Johnson, serial entrepreneur jiski zindagi TV production, pizza business, financial advisory aur management writing se bani hai, khud ko ek ‘projector’ kehna pasand karte hain. Jaise ek freelance journalist, consultant, artist ya sports coach poori tarah se kaam sunishchit karne ke liye ek sath multiple projects paida karne ka in-charge hota hai, ek portfolio worker wahi karta hai, lekin sabhi projects ke ek career ya specialism se jude hone ke bajaye, projects har ek alag kshetra mein ek alag talent ya interest se jude hote hain.

Toh ek portfolio careerist ke roop mein, aapko aksar ehsaas hoga ki jeevan-shaili mein additional labh hai. Aap gatividhi ke asambandhit kshetron ke beech ke connections aur synergies se labhanvit hone mein saksham honge — ideas, contacts, resources jinhe ek project se khincha ja sakta hai doosre ko majboot karne ke liye. Varying degrees ki bhagidari ke sath, entrepreneur-scholar-philanthropist Azeem Ibrahim ek sath lagbhag 40 projects ko juggle karte hain, jinmein businesses, charities, academic institutions aur non-profit organisations shamil hain. ‘Maine paaya hai ki main doosron ko zyada prabhavshali banane ke liye alag-alag projects se ideas aur strategies udhaar lene mein saksham raha hoon,’ woh kehte hain.

Benjamin Dunlap, jinke rozmarra ke kaam mein administration, Asian literature, ballet aur libretti jaise vividh kshetr shamil hain, ne khoja hai ki yeh jeevan-shaili ‘aisi mind-sets paida kar sakti hai jo badle mein a-pratyashit connections ki khoj ki taraf le jaati hain’ yeh zor dete hue ki ‘jinke talents aur interests unhe pursuits ki ek badi vividhta mein shamil hone ke liye le jaate hain woh is prakriya mein apne liye aise mauqe paida kar sakte hain jinse woh doosri tarah se nahi milte.’ Apni zindagi mein kaafi aksar Dunlap ne paaya hai ki ‘aapatat: asambandhit ventures ne pehle se a-bhavishyavani-yogy vikalp banane ke liye combine kiya hai, aur, us arth mein, koi tarka de sakta hai ki ek wayward enthusiast hona kabhi-kabhi ek practical mulya rakh sakta hai.’

Toh yeh portfolio career lifestyle ke zariya hi hai ki hum apne andar ke aantarik bahumukhi-pan ko apne working lives ke zariya phalne-phoolne ki anumati de sakte hain. Yeh is tareeqe se hai ki hum, career specialist Krznaric ke anusar, ‘hum kaun hain uske kai pakshon ko viksit karne mein saksham honge, hamari identity ke vividh pankhudiyon ko poori tarah se khulne ki anumati dete hue.’ Aisa karte hue, hume a-pratyashit connections milte hain aur hum hamare kai pursuits mein creative breakthroughs karne mein saksham hote hain jo doosri tarah se kalpana se pare hote.

---

### Polymathic Professions (Polymathic Professions)

> Jab log uchit roop se vyast hote hain, toh unka manoranjan unke kaam se nikalta hai.
> — John Ruskin

Isambard Kingdom Brunel ek ajeevak engineer the. Lekin zyadatar engineers ke vipreet, woh behadd alag cheezein banane ke liye apni skills ka upayog karne mein saksham the: bridges (Bristol mein Clifton Suspension Bridge samet), tunnels (Thames Tunnel), steamships (SS Great Britain jaise transatlantic ships), railway networks (shaandar Great Western Railway), imaratein aur dockyards (Renkioi Hospital). Brunel ke zyadatar designs ko innovation ke krantikari kaarname maana jata hai; woh aaj bhi upayog mein hain. Asal mein, unhe modern history ke sabse bade engineers mein se ek maana jata hai.

Hum jaante hain ki itihaas roop se, polymaths ko aksar ek platform ki zaroorat rahi hai, yaani ek samajik ya professional sthiti ke roop mein, jo unhe multiple fields ko explore karne aur yogdan dene ke liye ek launch pad pradan karti hai. Yeh unka job tha, unka mool occupation, jisne aantarik roop se unhe polymathic hone ki anumati di (ya zaroorat rakhi). Brunel ke liye, yeh ek engineer ke roop mein unka job tha.

Peeche samay mein aise ‘occupations’ ya ‘workplaces’ the jo multidisciplinarity, interdisciplinarity aur isliye polymathy ko encourage karte the, jaise Renaissance ke Venetian guilds (painting, sculpture, architecture aur engineering) aur Cordoba ya Baghdad ke royal courts (culture, politics, scholarship). Jaise pehle charcha ki gayi thi, aise job ka ek typical udaharan courtier ki position rahi hai — jo beshak West African griot, Arab vizier aur European gentleman jaise kai roopon mein aayi.

Lekin Enlightenment ke baad se — aur vishesh roop se Industrial Revolution aur corporatism aur state bureaucracies ke uday ke baad — royal court (aur isliye polymathic courtiers) pichhle samay ki cheez ban gaye aur ek naya polymathic playing field uski jagah le liya. Jabki mukhya roop se us hyper-specialised culture ke liye ek catalyst ke roop mein kaam karte hue jismein hum ab jeete hain, corporate sector khud vaastav mein ek ubharta hua generic field ban raha hai jo kabhi-kabhi apne khud ke polymaths produce karta hai ek isi tarah ke tareeqe se jaise science aur art ne hamesha kiya hai.

Aaj, polymathy ke liye kam se kam chhah sambhavid corporate platforms hain:

* Business administrator ya ‘chief executives’ — woh jo ek functioning business ke kai pehluon (finance aur accounts, legal, marketing aur communications, information technology, business development, logistics aur ityaadi) mein nipunata pradarshit karte hain;
* Serial entrepreneur, jo saaf taur par alag kshetron ya sectors mein vividh start-ups shuru karta hai aur jo har business ko kamyabi se chalane mein sakriya roop se bhaag leta hai (ya vaastav mein aage rehta hai), parinaamswarup har respective sector par ek expert ban jata hai;
* Venture capitalist ya ‘business angel’ jo investor ke roop mein (active ya passive) alag-alag sectors mein aur vividh prakar ke kai businesses mein shamil hota hai;
* Business consultant, jo strategic levels par alag-alag sectors mein corporations ko salah deta hai;
* Board Member, jiske paas vividh sangathnatmak boards ki executive aur non-executive membership hoti hai.

Saath hi, Charles Handy ne ‘interim managers’ ke ek badhte trend ko dekha jo ek badi badlav ki taraf le jaane wali interim avadhi mein ek sangathan ki ‘caretaking’ karte hue chhote periods ke liye company se company (sector aur type ke parwah kiye bina) koodte the. Doosre employees ‘job rotation’ policies se labhanvit hote hain jo unhe engaged aur stimulated rakhne ki talash karti hain. Beshak, in corporate generalists mein se bahut kam genuine polymaths ke roop mein qualify honge (woh vaastav mein ek durlabh prajati hain is baat ko dekhte hue ki kam hi plain generalism se polymathy tak ki rekha ko paar karne mein saksham hain), lekin yeh sujhana a-sateek hoga ki aise platforms polymathy ke liye vyavaharik raaste nahi hain. Asal mein entrepreneur aur *Zero to One* ke bestselling author Peter Thiel ne sujhav diya hai ki sabse kamyab 21st-century business leaders polymaths hone ki pravritti rakhte hain:

> ‘Duniya ke bahut se world-class entrepreneurs . . . woh specialists nahi hain, woh polymaths ke paas kuch hain. Toh udaharan ke liye Mark Zuckerberg ke sath, woh bahut si cheezon ke baare mein ek chonkane wali matra mein samajh ke sath baat karne mein saksham hain . . . woh ek Facebook product ke details ke baare mein baat kar sakte the, jis tareeqe se log social media ke baare mein sochte hain, psychology, jis tareeqe se culture shift ho raha hai, company ka management . . . yeh technology ke bade itihaas mein kaise fit baitha hai . . . yeh polymath-like intellect jaisa kaafi zyada hai . . . jis tarah ki board conversations hamare paas pichhle 13–14 saalon mein rahi hain . . . yeh bas yeh crazy range rahi hai.’

Doosre professional platforms bhi polymathy ke aam raaste ke roop mein kaam karte hain. Ek journalist apne career ke dauran finance se, music, religion tak apna editorial ya reporting focus badal sakta hai, jaise ek politician apni working life ke dauran alag-alag departments (jaise health, economy, arts aur culture, sport) ke minister ke roop mein seva kar sakta hai. Lekin occupational diversification zaroori roop se khud field ke roop mein nahi aani chahiye. Iske bajaye, sirf ek kshetra mein mahaarat ko vividh tareeqon se abhyas ya upayog kiya ja sakta hai, professional channels ke ek prachurta ke zariya jo khud har ek alag cognitive skills aur intelligences ki zaroorat rakhte hain.

Vichar karein, udaharan ke liye, ek psychologist jiski exclusive specialty psychology hai: woh adhyayan karti hai (ek scholar ke roop mein), kitaabein likhti hai (ek writer ke roop mein), students ko padhati hai (ek teacher ke roop mein), policy par sarkar ko salah deti hai (ek consultant ke roop mein), ek specialist publication ke liye op-eds aur articles likhti hai (ek columnist ke roop mein), public lectures deti hai (ek orator ke roop mein), aur psychology par ek TV documentary prastut karti hai (ek presenter ke roop mein) aur ek psychotherapy clinic shuru karti hai (ek businesswoman ke roop mein). Aisa karte hue, usne ek intellectual domain ke apne gyaan mein yogdan diya hai ya laagu kiya hai vividh professional domains mein.

Kai full-time workers part-time jobs mein shamil hote hain, ya apni aamdani ko poora karne ke liye apne khud ke business par kaam kar rahe hote hain. Aisa ek ‘side hustle’ employer dwara bhi encourage kiya ja sakta hai jo apne employees ko doosre kshetron ko explore karne ki anumati dene ke sangathnatmak labhon ko pehchanta hai. Kuch toh apni khud ki company ki policies ke andar is idea ko shaamil karte hain. Iska ek classic udaharan Google ka 20 percent time rule hai, jo employees ko unke mool kaam gatividhi se na-jude projects par apne samay ka 20 percent samarpit karne ki anumati deta hai. Jaise Eric Schmidt (former) Google ke CEO kehte hain, ‘yeh employees ko garima ke sath-sath kuch vikalp bhi pradan karta hai.’ Asal mein Google ke kai sabse kamyab initiatives is initiative se paida hue the. Ek udaharan Google Arts and Culture platform hai, jo duniya bhar ke sanskritik sansthaon se ucha quality visual art content ko digitise, publish aur curate karta hai duniya ke sabse bade art ko logon ke liye zyada sugam banane ke ek prayas mein.

Professional jobs ke alawa aise intellectual fields bhi hain jo multidisciplinary aur interdisciplinary thinkers ke liye platforms ke roop mein kaam karte hain. Philosophy, udaharan ke liye, polymaths ke liye saadhan aur ant dono sabit hui hai; ek platform (jaanch ke tareeqe ke roop mein) aur ek destination (ek nirupit vishwa-drishti ke roop mein). Shayad abhi haal hi tak, philosophy (religious aur secular dono) padhai ke ek ‘discipline’ ke bajaye jaanch ke ek universal method ke roop mein apne roop mein — hamesha polymathy ke liye ek majboot platform rahi hai. Jaise polymath aur philosopher Raymond Tallis ne kaha, ‘Philosophy ke astitva mein hone ka akela bahana yeh hai ki yeh atyadhik generality ka hai.’

Isliye isme thoda hairani hai, ki pehle ke kai polymaths (nischit roop se medieval period se pehle) philosophers the. Jabki pehle, vyakti polymaths ban jaate the kyunki woh philosophers the, humne haal hi mein vyaktiyon ko philosophers bante dekhna shuru kiya hai kyunki woh polymaths the. Toh jabki Aristotle aur Al-Farabi philosophers hone ke naate, vividh sciences aur humanities ka adhyayan karte the, Wittgenstein, Schweitzer aur Roerich jaise logon ne doosre kshetron mein anubhavon aur uplabdhiyon ka ek vividh set ik इकट्ठा karne ke baad apne darshanik yogdan diye.

Sach yeh hai, pesha chahe jo bhi ho, ise ya toh monomathically ya polymathically apnaya ja sakta hai — mindset aur drishtikon par nirbhar karte hue. Har occupation, profession ya intellectual field — chahe yeh kitna bhi vishesh roop se polymathic ho, ya hone ka potential rakhta ho — utni hi aasaani se hyper-specialisation ke liye ek platform mein badla ja sakta hai. Ek entrepreneur apne business ke sath itna vyast ho sakta hai ki woh an-jaan kshetra mein phir se shuru karne ki majboori kho deta hai. Ek philosopher (aur aaj kal aksar karta hai) philosophical method ke ek pehlu jaise logic ya metaphysics mein gehrai se jaane mein poori zindagi bitaye sakta hai. Ek journalist ek poora career vishesh roop se Danish royal family par report karne mein bitaye sakta hai aur kuch nahi.

Lekin ise us tareeqe se hone ki zaroorat nahi hai: chahe aap ek economist, lawyer, artist, physicist, ya politician hon, sabse pehle vishay ke ‘aaspas padhne’, peripheral fields mein bhaag lene, unhe synthesise karne aur apne core field ki apni samajh ko badhane ke liye prayas kiye ja sakte hain. Wahi professions ke liye jaata hai: yeh vishwas karna atyadhik mahatvapurna hai ki aapke mool job ke alawa aapke adhyayan aur pursuits aakhirkar aapke mool specialty mein aapki samajh aur performance ko badhane ka kaam karenge bajaye iske ki aapko isse bhatkayein. Vividhta mein ekta dhoondhna mahatvapurna hai. Toh us case mein ki kisi ka profession khud ek polymathic hai, iska ajeevak ya exclusive hona ab koi samasya nahi rehta — job khud, Story Musgrave ko quote karne ke liye, polymath ka ‘playing field’ ban jata hai. Sansehp mein, polymathic professions seemit samay mein adhikatam, vividh uplabdhi sunishchit karne ka, ya ‘ek teer se kai shikaar karne’ ka ek aam tareeqa hain.

Jo log prati-sparddhi labh (competitive advantage) ke kaaranon se specialise karte hain woh paayenge ki baad wala vaastav mein ek polymathic drishtikon ke zariya sabse behtareen dhang se haasil kiya ja sakta hai. Yeh isliye hai kyunki competition ko linear hone ki zaroorat nahi hai. Asal mein, linear specialisation ke zariya compete karna kathin ho jata hai, jahan diminishing returns apariharyata hain. Competition ke liye sabse behtareen drishtikon creativity hai, linearity nahi. Linear, mechanistic competition ke sath junooni ek corporate culture ka samna karte hue, Steve Jobs ne ek baar apne employees se kaha tha: ‘Hum competition ko dekhkar yeh nahi keh sakte ki hum ise behtar karne wale hain; humein unhe dekhkar yeh kehna hoga ki hum ise alag tarah se karne wale hain.’ Ek anokhi specialty banane ke liye vividhta laakar, aap khud ko doosron se alag kar sakte hain, yahan tak ki ek specialist vatavaran mein bhi. Pigeonholed hone se inkaar karein: iske bajaye, apne khud ke pigeonholes banayein jinhe aap apni marzi se frequent kar sakein. Lekin yahan kunjee hai: ‘apna niche dhoondhna’ ka matlab zaroori roop se ‘specialise karna’ nahi hai.

Ek ‘niche’ aakhirkar identity ka ek roop hai. Log lagatar apne liye ek anokhi identity sthapit karne ki talash kar rahe hain — aur polymathy identity formation ka sabse behtareen roop hai. Yeh kisi ko ek aisa anokha-pan banane ki anumati deta hai jo specialist dwara itni teevrata se pursued ek se aage nikal jata hai. Zyadatar log zindagi mein bahut shuruat mein hi apni identity par faisla karte hain, aur phir baad mein kiya gaya har kaam us identity ko challenge karne, shift karne, ya viksit karne ke bajaye majboot karne ka kaam karta hai. Wahi raaye-on ke liye jaata hai. Lekin identities ko तराशा (carved out) jaane se pehle banaya jata hai. Jaise ek bade athlete ne ek baar bodybuilding ke baare mein kaha tha, mitti ki murt तराशने (carve) ke liye, aapke paas pehle sthaan par mitti ka ek tukda hona chahiye. Udaharan ke liye, agar aapke paas physics, economics aur art jaise vividh vishayon ki achhi samajh thi, toh aap ek aisi specialty carven kar sakte hain jo anokhi hai — shayad Dalí ki atomic vision mein specialise karne wale ek art dealer ban kar ya post-World War II American economy par atomic physics aur visual art ke prabhavon ki tulna karte hue ek Ph.D. karke. Is tarah aap ek specialty ko craft karne ke liye diversity ki taqat ka upayog kar rahe hain.

Teen circles ki kalpana karein, do bottom par aur ek top par, sabhi ativyapit (overlapping). Shaded area jahan teeno ek doosre ke sath ativyapit hote hain woh polymath dwara banayi gayi specialty hai. ‘Fields ke beech ke ilaqe ki mining aapko ek anokha career path carven karne ki anumati dega, ek aisa jo aapke apne interests aur inclinations ke hisaab se custom fit hai,’ Robert Greene kehte hain, *Mastery* ke lekhak. Unhone Yoky Matsuoka ka udaharan dekha, woh computer scientist jinhone ek naya interdisciplinary field — ‘neurobotics’ vyavaharik roop se banane ke liye sports, physiology, mechanical engineering aur neurology mein apne interests aur talents ko milaya. Sabse behtareen niche multiple, aapatat: asambandhit fields ke intersection par paaya jane wala hai. Greene nishkarsh nikalte hain ki utne hi skills aur gyaan ke roopon ko viksit karna jitna kisi bhi diye gaye kshetra mein master banne ki prakriya ka ek abhinna hissa hai: ‘Bhavishya un logon ka hai jo gyaan ke roopon aur alag-alag skills ko milakar sakte hain. Maine jin sabhi modern masters ka interview liya woh iska udaharan hain.’

---

### Blessings from Above? (Upar Se Aashirwaad?)

Kisi bhi idea ka sabse aam utprerak (catalyst) beshak paisa (ya iska saathi, taqat) hai, yahi wajah hai ki ameer, shaktishali patrons saalon se polymathic parampara ko zinda rakhne mein sahayak rahe hain. Jabki pehle yeh nirankush samraat (despotic monarchs), dharmik aur shaikshanik adhikari aur ameer merchants the, woh ab technological visionaries, philanthropists, corporate executives, media tycoons, celebrities aur sarakaron ke roop mein aate hain — jinmein se sabhi achhi niyat wale nahi hain.

Aaj, ‘establishment’ ek samaj mein sabse shaktishali aur prabhavshali sansthaon aur actors ko samet ti hai: sarakarein, corporations (financial aur technological samet), judiciary, dharmik sansthan, military, media, entrepreneurs, philanthropists, academic institutions, popular celebrities (sports aur entertainment mein) aur shayad mutthi bhar activists. Har segment mein pramukh hastiyon ko samoohit kiya ja sakta hai aur samuhik roop se ‘modern elite’ ke roop mein sandarbhit kiya ja sakta hai. Yeh aksar is ‘Davos guest list’ ke aam-sehamati (chahe jaan-boojhkar coordinated ho ya praakritik swikriti dwara) ke sath hai ki ek idea ya ek system ko global roop se phelne ki anumati di ja sakti hai.

Lekin jaise French historian Jules Michelet ne dekha, itihaas aksar janta ke hakshep (interventions) dwara nishchayatmak roop se aakar leta hai. Aur beshak, ideas ko jungali aag ki tarah phelne ke liye hamesha top-down backing ki zaroorat nahi hoti, jaise Malcolm Gladwell ki bestseller *The Tipping Point* pradarshit karti hai. Ideas ke paas apna khud ka dimaag hota hai, aur kabhi-kabhi aag jalane ke liye sirf ek utprerak (catalyst), ek chingari ki zaroorat hoti hai. Hamari samajik dhancha ki kranti ke zariya manav potential ko mukt karne ke modern ideas mein, udaharan ke liye, Venus Project shamil hai, jo maujooda money-based system ko ek resource-based system se badalane ka prastav rakhta hai. Project is tarah vyakti ki aisi cheez karke simply ‘rozi-roti kamane’ se aazadi ki kalpana karta hai jo uske multiple talents aur interests ko kachra hone ka kaaran banti hai. Phir bhi project ek chhoti-paimane ki, vyakti-sanchalit pehal bana hua hai jo kabhi sach mein scale nahi hua hai. Toh tathya bana hua hai ki jab tak ya jab tak maujooda money-based, capitalistic system ko badla nahi jata, ameer vyaktiyon aur sansthaon par aksar polymaths ke mulya mein vishwās karne aur ise badhava dene ke liye nirbhar kiya jayega.

---

### An Opportunity (Ek Mauqa)

Global asamanta (inequality) dukhad roop se badh rahi hai, lekin ise ulatne ke mauqe bhi badh rahe hain. Manav itihaas mein pehli baar, media aur technology ke globalisation ne aam vyaktiyon ki ek tezi se badhti sankhya ko gyaan ke sath-sath mauqon tak pahonch rakhne ki anumati di hai. Internet coverage, mobile phone penetration, kitaabon ki uplabdhta aur kifayata, mass education, digital media — gyaan ke sabhi mahatvapurna transmitters — duniya bhar mein abhootpoorv levels tak pahonch gaye hain. Jaise Wikipedia ke founder Jimmy Wales kehte hain: ‘ivory tower academic ka classic stereotype, gyaan ka ucha purohit, sach mein udda diya ja raha hai . . . aur Wikipedia ke baare mein aakarshak cheezon mein se ek yeh pehchan hai ki talent samaj mein zyada vyapak roop se baanta gaya hai.’

Isne itihaas ke zyadatar doosre points ki tulna mein (vyaktigat level par) zyada commercial, artistic aur intellectual autonomy ki anumati di hai. Yeh chhote aur madhyam aakar ke businesses, civil society groups, independent film producers, record labels, TV news channels, newspapers aur publishers ki sankhya mein mahatvapurna badhotari mein murt roop leta hai, jinmein se kai self-funded, anti-establishment hain, aur conventional models ka palan nahi karte hain. Iske alawa, artificial intelligence (AI), robotics, cybernetics, biotechnology, nanotechnology, Internet of Things (IoT), Brain-Brain Interfaces, virtual reality, 3D printing aur biomedical engineering jaise transformational technologies ghatatmak roop se (exponentially) viksit hongi aur manav performance aur gyaan ko badhane aur anukoolit (optimise) karne ka mauqa pradan karengi. Jaise bade neuroscientist Miguel Nicolelis kehte hain, ‘Hum technology ko apne hisse ke roop mein absorb karenge — technology humein kabhi absorb nahi karegi. Yeh simply asambhav hai.’ Yeh ek mauqa hai, agar hum sirf iska faayda utha sakein.

---

## Programming Our Future (Hamare Bhavishya Ko Program Karna)

Ray Kurzweil — duniya ke pramukh technological polymaths mein se ek — ek transhumanist hain. Unka manna hai ki is century mein bahut jald, hamare kai zindagiyon ke andar, hum manav vikas ke agle, aur shayad aakhri stage ka anubhav karenge. Yeh woh point hoga jis par insaan organic biology ko langh jayenge. Singularity, jaisa ki yeh jaana jane laga hai, nikat bhavishya mein ek sambhavid point hai jab superintelligent machines (nanotechnology ke zariya) aisa roop lengi, jo unhe hamari biological anatomy ke sath ek aise tareeqe se fuse hone ki anumati deta hai jo mool roop se humein part-human part-machine bana dega. Yeh brain-machine interface ka ek roop hai.

Kurzweil zor dete hain ki 2030s tak nanobots nervous system ke andar se virtual reality banakar manav anubhav ko kaafi hadd tak badhane ke liye biological neurons ke sath interact karenge. Dimaag ki capillaries mein inject kiye gaye yeh billions nanobots manav intelligence ko bhi kaafi hadd tak badhayenge. Machine intelligence ke sath standard biological intelligence ka abhinna fusion ka matlab hoga ki insaan kai cognitive skills viksit karne aur badi speed aur efficiency ke sath jaankari ki vishaal matra ko process karne mein saksham honge. ‘Cloud mein apne neocortex ko vistarit karke,’ woh kehte hain, ‘hum multiple fields mein mahaarat haasil karne mein saksham honge jo innovate karne ki hamari kshamta ko kaafi hadd tak badhayega.’ Polymathise karne ki kshamta isliye ek abhootpoorv level tak badhegi.

Hamare paas vartaman mein hamare neocortex mein 300 million neocortical modules hain, dimaag ka woh ilaqa jo hum jise ‘soch’ ke roop mein pehchante hain uske kaafi hisse ke liye zimmedar hai. In modules mein se har ek ek pattern ko seekh sakta hai, pehchan sakta hai aur yaad rakh sakta hai. Yeh modules khud ko jatil hierarchies mein jodte hain. Hamara neocortex hamari apni soch ke aadhar par in hierarchies ko khud banata hai. Lekin hamare system mein superintelligent nanobots ke injection ke sath, khel badal jata hai. Kurzweil logic samjhate hain:

> Yaad karein ki pichhli baar kya hua tha jab humne aur neocortex joda tha jab hum humanoids bane (aur frontal cortex viksit hua)? Humne language aur art aur science ka aavishkar kiya. Jab hum cloud mein phir se additional neocortex jodte hain, toh hum abstraction ke additional levels jodenge. Parinaam aaj ke hamare art aur technology se bhi zyada gehre abhivyakti ke saadhanon ka aavishkar hoga. Yeh vistar ab ek fixed enclosure (hamare skulls) dwara seemit nahi hoga aur ek aise information processing substrate ka upayog kar raha hoga jo dimaag dwara upayog kiye jane wale se millions guna tezi se kaam karta hai. Yeh ghatatmak roop se (exponentially) badhne ke liye aazad hoga, aakhirkar hamari intelligence ko billions-fold badhate hue (woh "Singularity" ki meri dharana hai).

Singularity par atyadhik projections is ‘posthuman’ ko amar, badalane yogya genes aur 100 quadrillion synapses wale aur parallel mein chalne wale multiple realities wale ke roop mein dekhte hain. Yeh ek lagbhag a-kalpanik vaastavikta hai, lekin transhumanist scientists ke ek badhte movement ke anusar, kya vishwās nahi karna hai? Kurzweil banaye rakhte hain ki ‘hum jab chahein cloud mein apne neocortical extensions ko share karne ya jab chahein unhe private rakhne mein saksham honge, is tarah apni individuality ko banaye rakhte hue.’ Woh zor dete hain ki, homogenous robots banne se door, yeh humein ‘aaj se bhi zyada anokha’ banayega. Yeh, woh daava karte hain, polymathy ka bhavishya hai.

Kya yeh ‘polymathic cyborgs’ bhavishya ke maalik honge ya nahi yeh vivadaaspad hai. Physicist Stephen Hawking jaise aadaraniya vaigyanik sehamat the ki yeh sambhavna ‘science-fact hai, science-fiction nahi,’ halanki Noam Chomsky aur Miguel Nicolelis jaise doosre prasiddh cognitive scientists aur neuroscientists ke paas iske baare mein unke aarakshan hain. Kisi bhi maamle mein, ek cheez poori tarah se saaf hai: humein ek buniyaadi badlav ki zaroorat hai jismein insaani dimaag khud kaam karta hai. Haal ke saalon mein neuroscience mein hue zabardast pragati ko superintelligent machines ke vikas ke muqable insaani dimaag ke anukoolan (optimisation) ko prathmikta deni chahiye. Yeh kyun mahatvapurna hai? Kyunki yeh insaani dimaag hai jo aakhirkar machines ko program karne ke liye zimmedar hoga!

Machine learning tezi se human learning ke liye sexy substitute ban raha hai. AI ka vikas aaj human intelligence ko poshit karne se zyada resources aur dhyan praapt kar raha hai. Phir bhi aane wale saalon mein, yeh insaani dimaag hain jinake paas apni prajati ke itihaas mein shayad sabse badi chunauti aur zimmedari hai, machines ko un values ke liye program karna jo hum unke paas chahte hain, taaki bhavishya mein superintelligence ka koi bhi system ek vinashkari taqat ke bajaye ek rachnatmak taqat bane. Oxford University ke Future of Humanity Institute ke Anders Sandberg phir se pushti karte hain ‘agle kuch dashakon ke andar humein aisa code banana hoga jo human values ko darshata hai.’ Aur humein tatkalikta ke level ko samajhna chahiye. ‘Agar hum iske baare mein kuch karne wale hain,’ historian aur futurist Yuval Noah Harari kehte hain, ‘humein iske baare mein abhi kuch karna chahiye. 30 saalon mein bahut der ho jayegi.’

Lekin kaun in core values par faisla karta hai? Kaun vaastavikta ki sabse sateek tasveer par faisla karta hai? Kaun hamari prajati ke bhagya ko tay karne ke liye kaafi qualified hai? Iske liye, humein khud duniya ki aantarik jatilta ki ek behtar samajh ki zaroorat hai. Aur jabki polymathy naitikta (morality) se correlate ho sakti hai ya nahi, yeh nishchit roop se zyada spashtata pradan kar sakti hai. Yeh ultimate objectivity ka vaada nahi kar sakti, lekin iski taraf ek prabhavshali raaste ke roop mein kaam kar sakti hai.

Toh kya hum ‘beings’ banna chahte hain — Jason Bourne aur Terminator ke beech kahin — ya actual, optimised human beings? Agar baad wala, toh humein apni cognition aur apni chetna ko agle level par le jana chahiye. Sach mein, hamare bhavishya ke primary owners cyborgs ya superintelligent machines nahi hain (jo secondary owners ho sakte hain), balki vaastav mein woh insaan hain jinake paas yeh tay karne ke liye drishtikon, creativity aur critical intelligence hai ki yeh machines manavta ke vikas mein kya sabse behtareen bhoomika nibha sakti hain — aur unhe usi anusar program karein. Polymaths isliye hamari sabse achhi umeed hain. ‘Mujhe nahi lagta ki machines badi samasyaon ko solve karne wali hain, insaan karenge, machines ki madad se,’ neuroscientist Daniel Levitin zor dete hain.

Phir bhi yeh machines ko kaun program karta hai yeh bhi bhavishya ko prabhavit nahi karega; yeh woh hai jo insaano ko program karta hai. Aur main prastut karta hoon ki yeh insaan hain jinhe khud ko program karna chahiye. Is kitaab ka uddeshya is cognitive revolution ko jalaana hai, taaki har koi — Gambian entrepreneur, Norwegian farmer, American mother, Bolivian soldier aur Tibetan merchant — aakhirkar bhavishya mein apna hissa daava kar sake.

---

# Chapter 7 - Twenty-First-Century Polymaths (IKKEESVEIN SADI KE POLYMATHS)

> Polymath endangered (khatre mein) hai, bhale hi vilupt (extinct) nahi.
> — Peter Burke, *A Social History of Knowledge*

---

## A Vanguard of Disruptors (Disruptors Ka Ek Vanguard / Naye Raaste Banane Walon Ka Dasta)

Yogis ko aksar insaano mein sabse zyada specialised maana jata hai. Ek metaphysical vaastavikta ki khoj mein mystics ke roop mein unhe bhaautik vaastavikta ko tyag dena chahiye ya kam se kam khud ko usse mukt karna chahiye. Woh is ek task dwara itne samarpit, itne anushasit, itne khap gaye hote hain ki kisi bhi doosre worldly pursuit mein shreshthta haasil karna a-chintaniya (unthinkable) lagta hai. Prasiddh 21st-century ke Indian mystic Sadhguru Jaggi Vasudev ke anusar, yeh ek trutipoorn dharana (flawed assumption) hai. Sadhguru sabse prasiddh jeevant yogis mein se ek hain. Unhone ‘inner engineering’ program viksit kiya aur apni Isha Foundation ke zariya duniya bhar mein sabse popular meditation retreats mein se ek ki sthapna ki.

Phir bhi yahan kriya ka ek aadmi (man of action) hai — ek social entrepreneur jo golf gambhirta se khelta hai, gastronomy par kitaabein likhta hai aur motor bikes chalane, saanpon ko sambhalne aur pahadon par chadne ki adventure ki zindagi jeeta hai. Yeh wahi hai jise woh ‘wild life’ kehte hain. Yadyapi woh padhai ka aadmi na hone ka daava karte hain, world-renowned neuroscientists, educationalists, journalists aur artists ke sath unki vidwan baatchit ko vyapak roop se broadcast kiya gaya hai. Unhone vishayon ki ek range par aath bhashaon mein ek sau se zyada kitaabein likhi hain.

Samajik, bauddhika aur adhyatmik vikas par specialisation ke haavi paradigm ke prabhav ke baavjood, polymaths ka ek vanguard bana hua hai jo alag tarah se sochne, hone aur kaam karne par zor deta hai. Sadhguru is nasal ka aakarshak roop se pratinidhitv karte hain, jismein thinkers, scientists, artists, statesmen aur entrepreneurs mein se sabse zyada kalpana-shil bhi shamil hain. Woh sabhi alag-alag prakar ke aur alag-alag kaaranon se polymaths hain. Jo woh saajha karte hain woh hai ek a-pratibandhit intelligence, badhi hui creativity, individuality ka ek ahasas, asantusht curiosity aur ekta ki ek vision. Woh Prithvi par sabse versatile insaano mein se hain.

Aaj, pichhle samay ke polymathic philosopher se hamara sabse paas ka link ‘public intellectual’ hai, ek aisa vyakti jo us ek ke alawa jiske liye woh sabse behtareen dhang se jaani jaati hai kai doosre vishayon par tippani karne mein saksham hai: ‘public intellectual ka saar kai cheezon ke baare mein ek aisa drishtikon rakhna hai jo ekrat karta hai aur samajh banata hai,’ philosopher A.C. Grayling kehte hain. ‘Yeh interest ki vyapakta aur ek vichar kiye gaye drishtikon ke upayog ke baare mein hai.’ Jabki kai log is title par daava karte hain, sirf asli public intellectual hi ek sachha polymath hai. Jaise humne dekha hai, polymaths, apni prakriti, jatilta aur pure vividhta dwara sanskritik, dharmik aur rajneetik paramparaon ke ek spectrum se ubharte hain.

Slovene leftist Slavoj Žižek ne, udaharan ke liye, psychoanalysis, theology, opera, politics, existentialist philosophy, sociology aur film theory jaise vividh vishayon par kitaabein likhi hain. British conservative Roger Scruton ne — novels aur opera ke alawa — beauty, philosophy, music, politics, architecture, hunting, animal rights, sex aur ecology jaise vividh vishayon par kitaabein likhi hain.

GDI Impuls duniya ke pramukh thinkers ki ek pradhikrut list publish karta hai. Iske editor ke anusar, duniya ‘specialists’ ke sath itni junooni hai ki top 100 mein listed logon mein se kisi ko bhi polymaths ke roop mein shrenibaddh nahi kiya ja sakta tha. List mein jagah banane wale akele polymath Vaclav Smil the, jo 155th rank par the. Phir bhi Smil ne economics, history, energy, food, metals aur environment jaise vividh vishayon par kitaabein likhi hain, aur unhe Bill Gates ka favorite author maana jata hai. Jared Diamond ne, ek isi tarah ki nasal, linguistics, biology, physiology, geography, anthropology, zoology aur sociology mein apni mahaarat ko synthesise karke manav itihaas mein patterns ko samjhane ki koshish ki hai — ek polymathy jo unki award-winning bestseller *Guns, Germs, and Steel* mein pradarshit hui hai. Academic institutions aur publishers se specialists ki maang ke baavjood, Smil aur Diamond jaise intellectuals seemaon ko aage badhana jaari rakhte hain. Unki kamyabi aur breakthroughs unke interdisciplinary drishtikon ke mulya ko pradarshit karte hain.

Aaj ke kai scientific polymaths information technology aur artificial intelligence (AI) ke kshetron se aane ki pravritti rakhte hain jinhe anatomy, neurobiology, cognitive psychology, mechanical engineering, computer programming, quantum physics, linguistics aur mathematics jaise vividh (aur sambandhit) vaigyanik kshetron ke gehre gyaan ki zaroorat hoti hai. AI ke visionary pioneers aantarik roop se science ke synthesisers hain. Technologist aur futurist Ray Kurzweil ne apni krantikari 2005 ki kitaab *The Singularity is Near* mein inmein se kai disciplines ko ek sath laya jisne bhavishya mein information technologies mein ghatatmak pragati ki purvanuman lagayi thi. Elon Musk ek serial entrepreneur hain jo solar energy, automobile, space travel, neurotechnology aur digital money exchange samet science, technology aur engineering ke alag-alag sectors mein badi companies banane ke liye vividh sciences ke apne gyaan ka upayog karte hain.

Arts — visual, literary aur performing — hamesha se polymaths ka ghar raha hai, kyunki woh creative expression ke multiple saadhan pradan karte hain. Ziryab, Andrade aur Tagore ‘complete artist’ ke classic udaharan hain. Jabki kai log aaj is title ka daava karte hain, kuch asli logon mein American Bob Dylan jaise log shamil hain jo na sirf sabse zyada saraye gaye jeevant musicians, singers aur poets mein se ek hain, balki ek kushal painter bhi hain, aur Joni Mitchell, jo painting, music, poetry aur choreography (ballet) mein shreshthta haasil karti hain.

Hollywood actor Viggo Mortensen ne *Lord of the Rings* aur *A History of Violence* jaise blockbusters mein kaam kiya hai. Woh ek prasiddh composer bhi hain jinhone *Lord of the Rings* ke score mein yogdan diya, ek painter jinka kaam international roop se pradarshit kiya gaya hai aur ek poet jo (ek polyglot hone ke naate) kai bhashaon mein publish kar chuke hain.

Twentieth century mein Cocteau, Pasolini, Parks, Ray aur Kiarostami jaise logon dwara dikhaye gaye anusar, film polymathic exploration ke liye ek shaktishali platform ke roop mein kaam kar sakti hai. Modern American filmmaking ke ‘Renaissance Man’ kahe gaye, director David Lynch popular surrealism ke bina kisi vivad ke pita hain. Unke on-screen masterpieces mein *The Elephant Man*, *Dune*, *Blue Velvet* aur *Mulholland Drive* shamil hain. Lekin woh ek kushal painter, sculptor aur photographer, screenwriter, composer, set designer aur actor ke sath-sath creativity par transcendental meditation ke prabhav ke baare mein ek mukhya kitaab ke lekhak bhi hain.

Japan mein, Takeshi Kitano ne acting aur stand-up comedy mein apne career ki shuruat ki lekin jaldi hi *HANA-BI* jaise masterpieces produce karne ke liye film-making ki taraf apna dhyan moda. Ek motorcycle accident ke baad, Kitano ne painting shuru ki aur unka kaam kitaabon mein publish hua hai, galleries mein pradarshit hua hai, album covers par upayog kiya gaya hai aur films mein feature kiya gaya hai. Kitano ek prachur poet, film critic aur theorist aur kai novels ke lekhak bhi hain jinhe screen ke liye adapt kiya gaya hai. Isse bhi zyada, woh ek popular video game ke designer aur ek TV talk show ke host hain.

Dubai ke Ruler, Sheikh Mohammed bin Rashid Al Maktoum shayad aaj ek ‘polymathic monarch’ ka sabse paas ka udaharan hain. Region mein sabse mahatvapurna statesmen mein se ek hone ke alawa, woh apni peedhi ke sabse behtareen Nabati poets mein se ek hain, saath hi ek world champion equestrian, ek commissioned army officer aur region ke sabse prabhavshali business leaders mein se ek hain. Woh arts ke ek shauqeen patron aur duniya mein sabse aage sakriya philanthropists mein se ek bhi hain. Isi tarah, Charles, Prince of Wales ne multiple interests aur uplabdhiyan pradarshit ki hain jinne unhe haal hi mein Palazzo Strozzi ka Renaissance Man of the Year Award dilaya. Unhe world religions, architecture, education, sports, art, agriculture, nature aur literature se jude unke kaam ke liye sammaanit kiya gaya hai. Jab unhone haal hi mein Lifetime Achievement ke liye GQ Man of the Year Award jeeta, toh unhone kaha, ‘Main paata hoon ki bahut si cheezein hain jinhe karne ya jinse ladne ki zaroorat hai.’ Hum Denmark ki Queen Margrethe II ko bhi polymathic monarchs ke is club mein shamil kar sakte hain. ‘Europe ki sabse intellectual monarch’ ke roop mein jaane-maane, unhone archaeology, political science aur economics ka adhyayan kiya aur woh ek polyglot translator ke sath-sath ek kushal painter aur fashion designer bhi hain.

Jaise monarchs ne dikhaya hai, philanthropy polymath ke liye expression ka ek anokha zariya ho sakti hai. Aaj ke kai philanthropists ameer entrepreneurs hain. British businessman Richard Branson ne music, aviation, telecommunications, sports aur space jaise vividh sectors mein multi-million-pound businesses ko kamyabi se shuru kiya hai aur chalaya hai. Woh vartaman mein vividh sectors mein 200 se zyada businesses ke maalik hain, jinmein se kai mein woh seedhe taur par shamil hain aur kai doosre humanitarian, ecological aur arts projects mein bhi yogdan dete hain. Isse bhi zyada, unhone ek hot air balloon mein duniya ka chakkar lagaya hai, ek boat aur ek amphibian vehicle dono mein world record time mein English Channel ko paar kiya hai aur Australia mein andhere mein golf ka ek world-record round kamyabi se poora kiya hai.

Twentieth century ke ant tak mahilaon ko kanooni aur samajik roop se property maana jata tha, log nahi. Female emancipation Western samaj mein ek apekshakrit haal hi ki ghatna hai aur lambe samay se chale aa rahe gender bias ki virasat, bhale hi tezi se khatam ho rahi ho, abhi bhi mahsoos ki jaati hai. Iske baavjood, kuch mahilaon ne interest aur talent dwara sanchalit hokar multiple fields mein shreshthta haasil ki hai. Humne female scholar-activist ka uday dekha hai, udaharan ke liye Puerto Rican academic aur social activist Antonia Darder jo education ki theory aur history mein apne yogdan ke liye sabse behtareen dhang se jaane jaati hain, lekin ek behtareen painter aur poet bhi hain.

Indian activist Vandana Shiva ke paas ek scientific background hai aur woh climate change, human rights aur gender samet vividh kaaranon ki ek champion (aur expert) hain. Kai mahilaayein business aur science mein bhi bade kaarname poore kar rahi hain. African-American Mae Jemison ne, udaharan ke liye, ek dancer ke roop mein shuruat ki lekin ek engineer, physician, NASA astronaut aur tech entrepreneur banin.
